--- FILE: frontend/app/[lang]/dato/[slug]/page.tsx ---
import React from "react";
import Link from "next/link";
import { notFound } from "next/navigation";
import type { Metadata } from "next";
import { ArrowLeft, Tag } from "lucide-react";
import type { Locale } from "@/lib/types";
import { getIndicatorBySlug, getAllIndicatorSlugs } from "@/lib/data";
import { routes, CATEGORY_LABELS, t } from "@/lib/i18n";
import { IndicatorVisualization } from "@/components/shared/IndicatorVisualization";
import SourceCard from "@/components/shared/SourceCard";
import ContextNotesCard from "@/components/shared/ContextNotesCard";
import { ConceptGuideProvider } from "@/components/shared/guide/ConceptGuideContext";
import ConceptGuideSection from "@/components/shared/guide/ConceptGuideSection";

interface PageProps {
  params: Promise<{ lang: string; slug: string }>;
}

export async function generateStaticParams() {
  const slugs = await getAllIndicatorSlugs();
  const params: Array<{ lang: string; slug: string }> = [];
  for (const lang of ["es", "en"]) {
    for (const slug of slugs) {
      params.push({ lang, slug });
    }
  }
  return params;
}

export async function generateMetadata({ params }: PageProps): Promise<Metadata> {
  const resolvedParams = await params;
  const lang = (resolvedParams?.lang === "en" ? "en" : "es") as Locale;
  const slug = resolvedParams?.slug;

  const indicator = await getIndicatorBySlug(slug, lang);
  if (!indicator) {
    return {
      title: lang === "en" ? "Indicator Not Found | Factor.es" : "Indicador no encontrado | Factor.es",
    };
  }

  const question = indicator.question[lang] || indicator.question.es;
  const summary = indicator.contextNotes[lang]?.[0] || indicator.contextNotes.es[0] || question;

  return {
    title: `${question} | Factor.es`,
    description: summary,
    openGraph: {
      title: `${question} | Factor.es`,
      description: summary,
    },
  };
}

export default async function IndicatorPage({ params }: PageProps) {
  const resolvedParams = await params;
  const lang = (resolvedParams?.lang === "en" ? "en" : "es") as Locale;
  const slug = resolvedParams?.slug;

  const indicator = await getIndicatorBySlug(slug, lang);
  if (!indicator) {
    notFound();
  }

  const question = indicator.question[lang] || indicator.question.es;
  const categoryLabel = CATEGORY_LABELS[indicator.category]?.[lang] ?? indicator.category;
  const backHref = routes.home(lang);

  return (
    <ConceptGuideProvider guide={(indicator as any).conceptGuide}>
      <article className="space-y-6 animate-in fade-in duration-200">
        <div>
          <Link
            href={backHref}
            className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 text-xs font-semibold text-slate-300 hover:text-white bg-slate-900 hover:bg-slate-800 border border-slate-800 px-3.5 py-2 rounded-full transition-colors shadow-xs"
          >
            <ArrowLeft className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
            <span>{t("backToExplorer", lang)}</span>
          </Link>
        </div>

        <div>
          <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-1 bg-blue-950 text-blue-300 border border-blue-800/80 rounded-lg text-xs font-semibold">
            <Tag className="w-3 h-3 text-blue-400" />
            {categoryLabel}
          </span>
        </div>

        <header className="space-y-2">
          <h1 className="text-xl sm:text-2xl font-bold text-white leading-snug tracking-tight">
            {question}
          </h1>
        </header>

        <IndicatorVisualization indicator={indicator} locale={lang} />

        {(indicator as any).conceptGuide && <ConceptGuideSection lang={lang} />}

        <ContextNotesCard
          notes={indicator.contextNotes[lang] || indicator.contextNotes.es}
          lang={lang}
          locale={lang}
        />

        <SourceCard
          sources={indicator.sources}
          lastUpdated={indicator.lastUpdated}
          locale={lang}
          lang={lang}
        />
      </article>
    </ConceptGuideProvider>
  );
}

import React from "react";
import Header from "@/components/shared/Header";
import Footer from "@/components/shared/Footer";
import type { Locale } from "@/lib/types";

export async function generateStaticParams() {
  return [{ lang: "es" }, { lang: "en" }];
}

interface LayoutProps {
  children: React.ReactNode;
  params: Promise<{ lang: string }>;
}

export default async function LangLayout({ children, params }: LayoutProps) {
  const resolvedParams = await params;
  const lang = (resolvedParams?.lang === "en" ? "en" : "es") as Locale;

  return (
    <>
      <Header locale={lang} />
      <main className="flex-1 max-w-5xl w-full mx-auto px-4 py-6">
        {children}
      </main>
      <Footer locale={lang} />
    </>
  );
}

import { getDirectoryIndex } from "@/lib/data";
import DirectoryExplorer from "@/components/directory/DirectoryExplorer";
import type { Locale } from "@/lib/types";

interface PageProps {
  params: Promise<{ lang: string }>;
}

export async function generateStaticParams() {
  return [{ lang: "es" }, { lang: "en" }];
}

export default async function HomePage({ params }: PageProps) {
  const resolvedParams = await params;
  const lang = (resolvedParams?.lang === "en" ? "en" : "es") as Locale;
  const initialItems = await getDirectoryIndex(lang);
  return <DirectoryExplorer initialItems={initialItems} locale={lang} />;
}

export default function RootPage() {
  return (
    <html lang="es">
      <head>
        <meta httpEquiv="refresh" content="0; url=/es/" />
        <script
          dangerouslySetInnerHTML={{
            __html: `if (typeof window !== "undefined") { window.location.href = "/es/"; }`,
          }}
        />
      </head>
      <body>
        <p className="p-4 text-xs text-slate-400">
          Redirecting to <a href="/es/" className="underline">/es/</a>...
        </p>
      </body>
    </html>
  );
}

--- FILE: frontend/components/admin-registers/AdminRegistersChart.tsx ---
"use client";

import React, { useState } from "react";
import {
  ChevronLeft,
  ChevronRight,
  Info,
  Table,
  ChevronDown,
  ChevronUp,
  Scale,
  Users,
} from "lucide-react";
import type { Lang } from "@/lib/types";
import {
  getAvailableYears,
  getLatestYear,
  buildGroupSummary,
  buildGroupAgeBrackets,
  getTgssBreakdown,
  type AdminRegisterYearRecord,
  type AdminSexFilter,
  type GroupSummaryCardData,
} from "./adminRegisterSelectors";

const NATIONALS_COLOR = "#38BDF8";
const FOREIGNERS_COLOR = "#F59E0B";
const UNEMPLOYMENT_COLOR = "#818CF8";
const PENSIONS_COLOR = "#C084FC";

interface AdminRegistersChartProps {
  records: AdminRegisterYearRecord[];
  lang?: Lang;
}

function formatHeadcount(num: number | null | undefined, lang: Lang): string {
  if (num === null || num === undefined || Number.isNaN(num)) return "—";
  if (num >= 1_000_000) {
    const val = (num / 1_000_000).toFixed(2);
    return lang === "en" ? `${val} M` : `${val.replace(".", ",")} M`;
  }
  if (num >= 1_000) {
    const val = (num / 1_000).toFixed(1);
    return lang === "en" ? `${val} k` : `${val.replace(".", ",")} mil`;
  }
  return num.toLocaleString(lang === "en" ? "en-US" : "es-ES");
}

function formatCurrencyEur(num: number | null | undefined, lang: Lang): string {
  if (num === null || num === undefined || Number.isNaN(num)) return "—";
  const intPart = Math.round(num).toLocaleString(lang === "en" ? "en-US" : "es-ES");
  return `${intPart} €`;
}

function formatBillionsEur(eur: number | null, lang: Lang): string {
  if (eur === null || !Number.isFinite(eur)) return "—";
  const billions = eur / 1_000_000_000;
  if (billions >= 1) {
    const formatted = billions.toFixed(1);
    return lang === "en" ? `€${formatted}B` : `${formatted.replace(".", ",")} MM€`;
  }
  const millions = Math.round(eur / 1_000_000);
  return lang === "en"
    ? `€${millions.toLocaleString("en-US")}M`
    : `${millions.toLocaleString("es-ES")} M€`;
}

function GroupBalanceCard({
  summary,
  lang,
}: {
  summary: GroupSummaryCardData;
  lang: Lang;
}) {
  const isSpanish = summary.nationality === "spanish";
  const accentColor = isSpanish ? NATIONALS_COLOR : FOREIGNERS_COLOR;

  const title = isSpanish
    ? lang === "en"
      ? "Spanish Nationality"
      : "Nacionalidad Española"
    : lang === "en"
    ? "Foreign Nationality"
    : "Nacionalidad Extranjera";

  const maxFlow = Math.max(
    summary.totalWagesEur ?? 0,
    summary.unemploymentCostEur ?? 0,
    summary.pensionsCostEur ?? 0,
    1
  );

  const getPct = (val: number | null) =>
    val !== null && val > 0 ? Math.min(100, Math.max(3, (val / maxFlow) * 100)) : 0;

  return (
    <div className="bg-slate-950/70 border border-slate-800 rounded-2xl p-4 sm:p-5 flex flex-col justify-between space-y-4">
      {/* Header */}
      <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-2 pb-2.5 border-b border-slate-800/80">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
          <span
            className="w-2.5 h-2.5 rounded-full shrink-0"
            style={{ backgroundColor: accentColor }}
          />
          <h3 className="text-sm font-bold text-white">{title}</h3>
        </div>
        {summary.salariedHeadcount !== null && (
          <span className="text-xs font-mono text-slate-300 bg-slate-900 border border-slate-800 px-2.5 py-0.5 rounded-full">
            {formatHeadcount(summary.salariedHeadcount, lang)}{" "}
            {lang === "en" ? "salaried" : "asalariados"}
          </span>
        )}
      </div>

      {/* Identical 3 Flow Bars for Both Cohorts */}
      <div className="space-y-3">
        {/* 1. Masa salarial bruta */}
        <div className="space-y-1">
          <div className="flex items-center justify-between text-xs">
            <span className="text-slate-300 font-medium">
              {lang === "en" ? "Gross wage bill" : "Masa salarial bruta"}
            </span>
            <span className="font-mono font-bold text-white tabular-nums">
              {formatBillionsEur(summary.totalWagesEur, lang)}
            </span>
          </div>
          <div className="h-2.5 w-full bg-slate-900 rounded-full overflow-hidden border border-slate-800">
            <div
              className="h-full rounded-full transition-all duration-500"
              style={{ width: `${getPct(summary.totalWagesEur)}%`, backgroundColor: accentColor }}
            />
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* 2. Coste desempleo */}
        <div className="space-y-1">
          <div className="flex items-center justify-between text-xs">
            <span className="text-slate-300 font-medium">
              {lang === "en" ? "Unemployment cost" : "Coste desempleo"}
              {summary.unemploymentRecipients !== null && (
                <span className="text-slate-500 font-mono text-[11px] ml-1.5">
                  ({formatHeadcount(summary.unemploymentRecipients, lang)})
                </span>
              )}
            </span>
            <span className="font-mono font-bold text-indigo-300 tabular-nums">
              {formatBillionsEur(summary.unemploymentCostEur, lang)}
            </span>
          </div>
          <div className="h-2.5 w-full bg-slate-900 rounded-full overflow-hidden border border-slate-800">
            <div
              className="h-full rounded-full transition-all duration-500"
              style={{
                width: `${getPct(summary.unemploymentCostEur)}%`,
                backgroundColor: UNEMPLOYMENT_COLOR,
              }}
            />
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* 3. Coste pensiones */}
        <div className="space-y-1">
          <div className="flex items-center justify-between text-xs">
            <span className="text-slate-300 font-medium">
              {lang === "en" ? "Pensions cost" : "Coste pensiones"}
              {summary.pensionRecipients !== null && (
                <span className="text-slate-500 font-mono text-[11px] ml-1.5">
                  ({formatHeadcount(summary.pensionRecipients, lang)})
                </span>
              )}
            </span>
            <span className="font-mono font-bold text-purple-300 tabular-nums">
              {formatBillionsEur(summary.pensionsCostEur, lang)}
            </span>
          </div>
          <div className="h-2.5 w-full bg-slate-900 rounded-full overflow-hidden border border-slate-800">
            <div
              className="h-full rounded-full transition-all duration-500"
              style={{
                width: `${getPct(summary.pensionsCostEur)}%`,
                backgroundColor: PENSIONS_COLOR,
              }}
            />
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* 4. Ratio */}
      <div className="pt-2.5 border-t border-slate-800/80 flex items-center justify-between gap-2 text-xs">
        <span className="text-slate-400">
          {lang === "en"
            ? "Ratio (Wages / Unemployment + Pensions):"
            : "Ratio (Masa salarial / Desempleo + Pensiones):"}
        </span>
        <span
          className="font-mono font-bold px-2.5 py-0.5 rounded-lg border text-sm tabular-nums"
          style={{
            color: accentColor,
            borderColor: `${accentColor}44`,
            backgroundColor: `${accentColor}12`,
          }}
        >
          {summary.wageToBenefitRatio !== null
            ? `${summary.wageToBenefitRatio.toFixed(2).replace(".", lang === "en" ? "." : ",")}x`
            : "—"}
        </span>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

export function AdminRegistersChart({
  records,
  lang = "es",
}: AdminRegistersChartProps) {
  const years = getAvailableYears(records);
  const [selectedYear, setSelectedYear] = useState<string>(() => getLatestYear(records));
  const [sexFilter, setSexFilter] = useState<AdminSexFilter>("total");
  const [activeGroup, setActiveGroup] = useState<"spanish" | "foreign">("foreign");
  const [showAuditTable, setShowAuditTable] = useState<boolean>(false);

  const activeYear = years.includes(selectedYear) ? selectedYear : getLatestYear(records);
  const currentIdx = years.indexOf(activeYear);
  const canPrev = currentIdx > 0;
  const canNext = currentIdx !== -1 && currentIdx < years.length - 1;

  const currentRecord =
    records.find((r) => r.year === activeYear) ?? records[records.length - 1];

  if (!currentRecord) return null;

  const spanishSummary = buildGroupSummary(currentRecord, "spanish", sexFilter);
  const foreignSummary = buildGroupSummary(currentRecord, "foreign", sexFilter);
  const ageDetail = buildGroupAgeBrackets(currentRecord, activeGroup, sexFilter);
  const tgssInfo = getTgssBreakdown(currentRecord);

  const activeAccent = activeGroup === "spanish" ? NATIONALS_COLOR : FOREIGNERS_COLOR;

  return (
    <div className="space-y-6">
      <section className="bg-slate-900 border border-slate-800 rounded-2xl p-4 sm:p-6 shadow-xl space-y-5">
        <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-center justify-between gap-4 pb-4 border-b border-slate-800">
          <div className="space-y-1">
            <h2 className="text-base sm:text-lg font-bold text-white tracking-tight flex items-center gap-2">
              <Scale className="w-5 h-5 text-sky-400" />
              <span>
                {lang === "en"
                  ? "Direct Monetary Flows by Group: Gross Wages vs. Unemployment & Pensions"
                  : "Flujos Monetarios Directos por Grupo: Masa Salarial frente a Desempleo y Pensiones"}
              </span>
            </h2>
            <p className="text-xs text-slate-400">
              {lang === "en"
                ? `Each group is evaluated on its own scale from official AEAT Form 190 registers (${activeYear})`
                : `Cada grupo se evalúa en su propia escala a partir de registros oficiales AEAT Modelo 190 (${activeYear})`}
            </p>
          </div>

          <div className="flex items-center gap-2 flex-wrap">
            <div className="inline-flex items-center bg-slate-950 border border-slate-800 rounded-xl p-1 gap-1">
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => canPrev && setSelectedYear(years[currentIdx - 1])}
                disabled={!canPrev}
                aria-label={lang === "en" ? "Previous year" : "Año anterior"}
                className="p-1 rounded-lg text-slate-400 hover:text-white hover:bg-slate-800 disabled:opacity-30 disabled:cursor-not-allowed transition-colors"
              >
                <ChevronLeft className="w-4 h-4" />
              </button>
              <select
                value={activeYear}
                onChange={(e) => setSelectedYear(e.target.value)}
                aria-label={lang === "en" ? "Select year" : "Seleccionar año"}
                className="bg-transparent text-white font-mono font-bold text-sm px-2 py-0.5 focus:outline-none cursor-pointer"
              >
                {years.map((y) => (
                  <option key={y} value={y} className="bg-slate-900 text-white">
                    {y}
                  </option>
                ))}
              </select>
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => canNext && setSelectedYear(years[currentIdx + 1])}
                disabled={!canNext}
                aria-label={lang === "en" ? "Next year" : "Año siguiente"}
                className="p-1 rounded-lg text-slate-400 hover:text-white hover:bg-slate-800 disabled:opacity-30 disabled:cursor-not-allowed transition-colors"
              >
                <ChevronRight className="w-4 h-4" />
              </button>
            </div>

            <div
              role="group"
              aria-label={lang === "en" ? "Sex filter" : "Filtro de sexo"}
              className="inline-flex items-center p-1 bg-slate-950 border border-slate-800 rounded-xl"
            >
              {(["total", "male", "female"] as AdminSexFilter[]).map((s) => (
                <button
                  key={s}
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => setSexFilter(s)}
                  className={`px-3 py-1 rounded-lg text-xs font-semibold transition-all ${
                    sexFilter === s
                      ? "bg-slate-800 text-white"
                      : "text-slate-400 hover:text-slate-200"
                  }`}
                >
                  {s === "total"
                    ? "Total"
                    : s === "male"
                    ? lang === "en"
                      ? "Men"
                      : "Hombres"
                    : lang === "en"
                    ? "Women"
                    : "Mujeres"}
                </button>
              ))}
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>

        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 lg:grid-cols-2 gap-4">
          <GroupBalanceCard summary={spanishSummary} lang={lang} />
          <GroupBalanceCard summary={foreignSummary} lang={lang} />
        </div>

        <div className="bg-slate-950/80 border border-slate-800/80 rounded-xl p-3 flex items-start gap-2.5 text-xs text-slate-400 leading-relaxed">
          <Info className="w-4 h-4 text-blue-400 shrink-0 mt-0.5" />
          <span>
            {lang === "en"
              ? "Scope note: Official tax registers (AEAT Form 190) report annual gross wages and direct monetary benefits (unemployment and pensions). Indirect taxes (VAT) and individual in-kind public services (healthcare, education) are not broken down by nationality in National Accounts."
              : "Alcance de los datos: Los registros tributarios oficiales (AEAT Modelo 190) miden rentas salariales brutas y prestaciones monetarias directas (desempleo y pensiones). Los impuestos indirectos (IVA) y el gasto individual en servicios en especie (sanidad, educación) no se desglosan por nacionalidad en la Contabilidad Nacional."}
          </span>
        </div>
      </section>

      <section className="bg-slate-900 border border-slate-800 rounded-2xl p-4 sm:p-6 shadow-xl space-y-5">
        <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-center justify-between gap-4 pb-4 border-b border-slate-800">
          <div className="space-y-1">
            <h3 className="text-base font-bold text-white flex items-center gap-2">
              <Users className="w-4 h-4 text-sky-400" />
              <span>
                {lang === "en"
                  ? `Life-Cycle Structure by Age Bracket (${activeYear})`
                  : `Estructura por Tramos de Edad (${activeYear})`}
              </span>
            </h3>
            <p className="text-xs text-slate-400">
              {lang === "en"
                ? "Select a cohort to inspect gross wages, unemployment/pensions cost, and internal ratios across age bands"
                : "Selecciona un colectivo para inspeccionar su masa salarial, coste de desempleo/pensiones y ratio interna en cada tramo de edad"}
            </p>
          </div>

          <div
            role="group"
            aria-label={lang === "en" ? "Select cohort" : "Seleccionar colectivo"}
            className="inline-flex items-center p-1 bg-slate-950 border border-slate-800 rounded-xl self-start sm:self-auto"
          >
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => setActiveGroup("spanish")}
              className={`px-3.5 py-1.5 rounded-lg text-xs font-semibold transition-all flex items-center gap-1.5 ${
                activeGroup === "spanish"
                  ? "bg-slate-800 text-white shadow-xs"
                  : "text-slate-400 hover:text-slate-200"
              }`}
            >
              <span
                className="w-2 h-2 rounded-full"
                style={{ backgroundColor: NATIONALS_COLOR }}
              />
              <span>{lang === "en" ? "Spanish Nationality" : "Nacionalidad Española"}</span>
            </button>
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => setActiveGroup("foreign")}
              className={`px-3.5 py-1.5 rounded-lg text-xs font-semibold transition-all flex items-center gap-1.5 ${
                activeGroup === "foreign"
                  ? "bg-slate-800 text-white shadow-xs"
                  : "text-slate-400 hover:text-slate-200"
              }`}
            >
              <span
                className="w-2 h-2 rounded-full"
                style={{ backgroundColor: FOREIGNERS_COLOR }}
              />
              <span>{lang === "en" ? "Foreign Nationality" : "Nacionalidad Extranjera"}</span>
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>

        {ageDetail.rows.length === 0 ? (
          <div className="bg-slate-950/60 border border-slate-800 rounded-xl p-6 text-center text-xs text-slate-400">
            {lang === "en"
              ? "No age-band breakdown available for this selection."
              : "No hay desglose por tramos de edad disponible para esta selección."}
          </div>
        ) : (
          <div className="space-y-2.5">
            {ageDetail.rows.map((row) => {
              const barPct = ageDetail.hasBenefitsData
                ? ageDetail.maxRatio > 0 && row.ratio !== null
                  ? Math.min(100, Math.max(3, (row.ratio / ageDetail.maxRatio) * 100))
                  : 0
                : ageDetail.maxWageEur > 0
                ? Math.min(100, Math.max(3, (row.averageAnnualWageEur / ageDetail.maxWageEur) * 100))
                : 0;

              return (
                <div
                  key={row.id}
                  className="bg-slate-950/60 border border-slate-800/80 rounded-xl p-3.5 hover:border-slate-700 transition-all space-y-2"
                >
                  <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-center justify-between gap-2 text-xs">
                    <div className="flex items-center gap-2.5">
                      <span className="font-mono font-bold text-white bg-slate-900 border border-slate-800 px-2.5 py-0.5 rounded">
                        {row.label}
                      </span>
                      <span className="text-slate-400 font-mono">
                        {formatHeadcount(row.headcount, lang)}{" "}
                        {lang === "en" ? "workers" : "asalariados"}
                      </span>
                    </div>

                    <div className="flex items-center gap-3 flex-wrap font-mono tabular-nums">
                      <span>
                        <span className="text-slate-400">
                          {lang === "en" ? "Wages: " : "Salarios: "}
                        </span>
                        <strong className="text-white">
                          {formatBillionsEur(row.totalWagesEur, lang)}
                        </strong>
                      </span>

                      {row.unemploymentCostEur !== null && (
                        <span>
                          <span className="text-slate-400">
                            {lang === "en" ? "Unemp.: " : "Desempleo: "}
                          </span>
                          <strong className="text-indigo-300">
                            {formatBillionsEur(row.unemploymentCostEur, lang)}
                          </strong>
                        </span>
                      )}

                      {row.pensionsCostEur !== null && (
                        <span>
                          <span className="text-slate-400">
                            {lang === "en" ? "Pensions: " : "Pensiones: "}
                          </span>
                          <strong className="text-purple-300">
                            {formatBillionsEur(row.pensionsCostEur, lang)}
                          </strong>
                        </span>
                      )}

                      {row.ratio !== null && (
                        <span
                          className="font-bold px-2 py-0.5 rounded border"
                          style={{
                            color: activeAccent,
                            borderColor: `${activeAccent}44`,
                            backgroundColor: `${activeAccent}12`,
                          }}
                        >
                          {row.ratio.toFixed(1).replace(".", lang === "en" ? "." : ",")}x
                        </span>
                      )}
                    </div>
                  </div>

                  <div className="h-2 w-full bg-slate-900 rounded-full overflow-hidden border border-slate-800">
                    <div
                      className="h-full rounded-full transition-all duration-300"
                      style={{
                        width: `${barPct}%`,
                        backgroundColor: activeAccent,
                      }}
                    />
                  </div>
                </div>
              );
            })}
          </div>
        )}
      </section>

      <section className="bg-slate-900 border border-slate-800 rounded-2xl p-4 sm:p-5 shadow-xl">
        <button
          type="button"
          onClick={() => setShowAuditTable(!showAuditTable)}
          className="w-full flex items-center justify-between text-xs font-semibold text-slate-300 hover:text-white transition-colors cursor-pointer"
        >
          <span className="flex items-center gap-2">
            <Table className="w-4 h-4 text-sky-400" />
            <span>
              {lang === "en"
                ? `Inspect Raw Official Tables (AEAT & TGSS · ${activeYear})`
                : `Ver Tablas Numéricas Completas de Fuentes Oficiales (AEAT y TGSS · ${activeYear})`}
            </span>
          </span>
          {showAuditTable ? (
            <ChevronUp className="w-4 h-4 text-slate-400" />
          ) : (
            <ChevronDown className="w-4 h-4 text-slate-400" />
          )}
        </button>

        {showAuditTable && (
          <div className="mt-4 pt-4 border-t border-slate-800 space-y-6">
            <div className="space-y-2 overflow-x-auto">
              <div className="text-xs font-bold text-white">
                {lang === "en"
                  ? `AEAT Salaried Workers — ${activeGroup === "spanish" ? "Spanish" : "Foreign"} Nationality (${activeYear})`
                  : `AEAT Asalariados — Nacionalidad ${activeGroup === "spanish" ? "Española" : "Extranjera"} (${activeYear})`}
              </div>
              <table className="w-full text-left text-xs font-mono">
                <thead>
                  <tr className="border-b border-slate-800 text-slate-400">
                    <th className="py-2 px-2">{lang === "en" ? "Age Band" : "Tramo de Edad"}</th>
                    <th className="py-2 px-2 text-right">{lang === "en" ? "Headcount" : "Asalariados"}</th>
                    <th className="py-2 px-2 text-right">{lang === "en" ? "Total Wages (€)" : "Masa Salarial (€)"}</th>
                    <th className="py-2 px-2 text-right">{lang === "en" ? "Mean Annual (€)" : "Salario Medio (€)"}</th>
                  </tr>
                </thead>
                <tbody className="divide-y divide-slate-800/60 text-slate-200">
                  {ageDetail.rows.map((r) => (
                    <tr key={r.id} className="hover:bg-slate-950/60">
                      <td className="py-2 px-2 font-bold text-slate-100">{r.label}</td>
                      <td className="py-2 px-2 text-right tabular-nums">
                        {r.headcount.toLocaleString(lang === "en" ? "en-US" : "es-ES")}
                      </td>
                      <td className="py-2 px-2 text-right tabular-nums">
                        {Math.round(r.totalWagesEur).toLocaleString(lang === "en" ? "en-US" : "es-ES")} €
                      </td>
                      <td className="py-2 px-2 text-right tabular-nums font-bold text-white">
                        {formatCurrencyEur(r.averageAnnualWageEur, lang)}
                      </td>
                    </tr>
                  ))}
                </tbody>
              </table>
            </div>

            {tgssInfo.byCountry.length > 0 && (
              <div className="space-y-2 overflow-x-auto pt-2 border-t border-slate-800/80">
                <div className="flex items-center justify-between text-xs">
                  <span className="font-bold text-white">
                    {lang === "en"
                      ? `TGSS Foreign Social Security Affiliates by Country (Top 15 · ${currentRecord.tgss?.referencePeriod ?? activeYear})`
                      : `TGSS Afiliados Extranjeros por País (Top 15 · ${currentRecord.tgss?.referencePeriod ?? activeYear})`}
                  </span>
                  {tgssInfo.totalForeign !== null && (
                    <span className="font-mono text-amber-400 font-bold">
                      Total: {tgssInfo.totalForeign.toLocaleString(lang === "en" ? "en-US" : "es-ES")}
                    </span>
                  )}
                </div>
                <table className="w-full text-left text-xs font-mono">
                  <thead>
                    <tr className="border-b border-slate-800 text-slate-400">
                      <th className="py-2 px-2">{lang === "en" ? "Country" : "País"}</th>
                      <th className="py-2 px-2 text-right">{lang === "en" ? "Affiliates" : "Afiliados"}</th>
                      <th className="py-2 px-2 text-right">{lang === "en" ? "Share" : "Cuota"}</th>
                    </tr>
                  </thead>
                  <tbody className="divide-y divide-slate-800/60 text-slate-200">
                    {tgssInfo.byCountry.slice(0, 15).map((c) => (
                      <tr key={c.country} className="hover:bg-slate-950/60">
                        <td className="py-2 px-2 font-sans text-slate-200">{c.country}</td>
                        <td className="py-2 px-2 text-right tabular-nums">
                          {c.affiliates.toLocaleString(lang === "en" ? "en-US" : "es-ES")}
                        </td>
                        <td className="py-2 px-2 text-right tabular-nums text-slate-400">
                          {c.shareOfTotalPct.toFixed(1).replace(".", lang === "en" ? "." : ",")}%
                        </td>
                      </tr>
                    ))}
                  </tbody>
                </table>
              </div>
            )}
          </div>
        )}
      </section>
    </div>
  );
}

export default AdminRegistersChart;

--- FILE: frontend/components/admin-registers/adminRegisterSelectors.ts ---
export type AdminSexFilter = "total" | "male" | "female";
export type AdminNationalityKey = "spanish" | "foreign" | "total";

export interface AeatAgeMetrics {
  headcount?: number;
  recipients?: number;
  paymentsPerPerson?: number;
  totalWagesEur?: number;
  averageAnnualWageEur?: number;
  totalBenefitsEur?: number;
  averageBenefitEur?: number;
  totalPensionsEur?: number;
  averagePensionEur?: number;
}

export type AeatAgeBandMap = Record<string, AeatAgeMetrics>;

export interface AeatPopulationData {
  spanish?: Partial<Record<AdminSexFilter, AeatAgeBandMap>>;
  foreign?: Partial<Record<AdminSexFilter, AeatAgeBandMap>>;
  total?: Partial<Record<AdminSexFilter, AeatAgeBandMap>>;
}

export interface AeatYearPayload {
  salariedWorkers?: AeatPopulationData;
  unemploymentRecipients?: AeatPopulationData;
  pensionRecipients?: AeatPopulationData;
}

export interface TgssYearPayload {
  referencePeriod: string;
  periodsAvailable: string[];
  affiliatesByCountryAndRegime: Record<string, Record<string, number>>;
}

export interface AdminRegisterYearRecord {
  year: string;
  aeat?: AeatYearPayload | null;
  tgss?: TgssYearPayload | null;
}

export interface GroupSummaryCardData {
  nationality: "spanish" | "foreign";
  salariedHeadcount: number | null;
  totalWagesEur: number | null;
  unemploymentCostEur: number | null;
  unemploymentRecipients: number | null;
  pensionsCostEur: number | null;
  pensionRecipients: number | null;
  totalBenefitsAndPensionsEur: number | null;
  wageToBenefitRatio: number | null;
}

export interface AgeBracketDetailRow {
  id: string;
  label: string;
  headcount: number;
  paymentsPerPerson: number | null;
  totalWagesEur: number;
  averageAnnualWageEur: number;
  shareOfGroupWagesPct: number;
  unemploymentCostEur: number | null;
  pensionsCostEur: number | null;
  totalReceivedEur: number | null;
  ratio: number | null;
}

export interface TgssCountryRow {
  country: string;
  affiliates: number;
  shareOfTotalPct: number;
}

export function getAvailableYears(records: AdminRegisterYearRecord[]): string[] {
  if (!Array.isArray(records)) return [];
  return records
    .filter((r) => r && r.aeat)
    .map((r) => r.year)
    .sort((a, b) => Number(a) - Number(b));
}

export function getLatestYear(records: AdminRegisterYearRecord[]): string {
  const years = getAvailableYears(records);
  return years[years.length - 1] ?? "2025";
}

function getTotalEntry(map: AeatAgeBandMap | undefined): AeatAgeMetrics | undefined {
  if (!map) return undefined;
  if (map["Total"]) return map["Total"];
  const totalKey = Object.keys(map).find((k) => k.toLowerCase().startsWith("total"));
  return totalKey ? map[totalKey] : undefined;
}

function getAgeMap(
  popData: AeatPopulationData | undefined,
  nationality: AdminNationalityKey,
  sex: AdminSexFilter
): AeatAgeBandMap | undefined {
  if (!popData) return undefined;
  return (
    popData[nationality]?.[sex] ??
    popData[nationality]?.total ??
    popData.total?.[sex] ??
    popData.total?.total
  );
}

export function buildGroupSummary(
  record: AdminRegisterYearRecord | undefined,
  nationality: "spanish" | "foreign",
  sex: AdminSexFilter
): GroupSummaryCardData {
  const salMap = getAgeMap(record?.aeat?.salariedWorkers, nationality, sex);
  const unempMap = getAgeMap(record?.aeat?.unemploymentRecipients, nationality, sex);
  const penMap = getAgeMap(record?.aeat?.pensionRecipients, nationality, sex);

  const salTotal = getTotalEntry(salMap);
  const unempTotal = getTotalEntry(unempMap);
  const penTotal = getTotalEntry(penMap);

  const salariedHeadcount = salTotal?.headcount ?? null;
  const totalWagesEur = salTotal?.totalWagesEur ?? null;
  const unemploymentCostEur = unempTotal?.totalBenefitsEur ?? null;
  const unemploymentRecipients = unempTotal?.recipients ?? null;
  const pensionsCostEur = penTotal?.totalPensionsEur ?? null;
  const pensionRecipients = penTotal?.recipients ?? null;

  const hasAnyBenefit = unemploymentCostEur !== null || pensionsCostEur !== null;
  const totalBenefitsAndPensionsEur = hasAnyBenefit
    ? (unemploymentCostEur ?? 0) + (pensionsCostEur ?? 0)
    : null;

  const wageToBenefitRatio =
    totalWagesEur !== null &&
    totalBenefitsAndPensionsEur !== null &&
    totalBenefitsAndPensionsEur > 0
      ? totalWagesEur / totalBenefitsAndPensionsEur
      : null;

  return {
    nationality,
    salariedHeadcount,
    totalWagesEur,
    unemploymentCostEur,
    unemploymentRecipients,
    pensionsCostEur,
    pensionRecipients,
    totalBenefitsAndPensionsEur,
    wageToBenefitRatio,
  };
}

export function buildGroupAgeBrackets(
  record: AdminRegisterYearRecord | undefined,
  nationality: "spanish" | "foreign",
  sex: AdminSexFilter
): {
  rows: AgeBracketDetailRow[];
  hasBenefitsData: boolean;
  maxWageEur: number;
  maxRatio: number;
} {
  const salMap = getAgeMap(record?.aeat?.salariedWorkers, nationality, sex);
  const unempMap = getAgeMap(record?.aeat?.unemploymentRecipients, nationality, sex);
  const penMap = getAgeMap(record?.aeat?.pensionRecipients, nationality, sex);

  if (!salMap) {
    return { rows: [], hasBenefitsData: false, maxWageEur: 0, maxRatio: 0 };
  }

  const groupTotalWages = getTotalEntry(salMap)?.totalWagesEur ?? 0;
  const hasBenefitsData = Boolean(unempMap || penMap);

  const rows: AgeBracketDetailRow[] = [];
  let maxWageEur = 0;
  let maxRatio = 0;

  for (const [ageLabel, metrics] of Object.entries(salMap)) {
    if (ageLabel.toLowerCase().startsWith("total")) continue;
    const headcount = metrics.headcount ?? 0;
    const totalWagesEur = metrics.totalWagesEur ?? 0;
    const averageAnnualWageEur = metrics.averageAnnualWageEur ?? 0;

    if (averageAnnualWageEur > maxWageEur) {
      maxWageEur = averageAnnualWageEur;
    }

    const uMetrics = unempMap?.[ageLabel];
    const pMetrics = penMap?.[ageLabel];
    const unemploymentCostEur = uMetrics?.totalBenefitsEur ?? null;
    const pensionsCostEur = pMetrics?.totalPensionsEur ?? null;

    const totalReceivedEur =
      unemploymentCostEur !== null || pensionsCostEur !== null
        ? (unemploymentCostEur ?? 0) + (pensionsCostEur ?? 0)
        : null;

    const ratio =
      totalReceivedEur !== null && totalReceivedEur > 0
        ? totalWagesEur / totalReceivedEur
        : null;

    if (ratio !== null && ratio > maxRatio) {
      maxRatio = ratio;
    }

    rows.push({
      id: ageLabel,
      label: ageLabel,
      headcount,
      paymentsPerPerson: metrics.paymentsPerPerson ?? null,
      totalWagesEur,
      averageAnnualWageEur,
      shareOfGroupWagesPct:
        groupTotalWages > 0 ? (totalWagesEur / groupTotalWages) * 100 : 0,
      unemploymentCostEur,
      pensionsCostEur,
      totalReceivedEur,
      ratio,
    });
  }

  return { rows, hasBenefitsData, maxWageEur, maxRatio };
}

export function getTgssBreakdown(record: AdminRegisterYearRecord | undefined): {
  totalForeign: number | null;
  byCountry: TgssCountryRow[];
} {
  const map = record?.tgss?.affiliatesByCountryAndRegime;
  if (!map) return { totalForeign: null, byCountry: [] };

  const regimeSet = new Set<string>();
  Object.values(map).forEach((regObj) => {
    Object.keys(regObj).forEach((r) => regimeSet.add(r));
  });
  const regimes = Array.from(regimeSet);
  const totalRegimeKey =
    regimes.find((r) => r.toLowerCase().includes("total")) ?? regimes[0];

  let totalForeign: number | null = null;
  const totalEntry = map["TOTAL"] ?? map["Total"];
  if (totalEntry && totalRegimeKey && typeof totalEntry[totalRegimeKey] === "number") {
    totalForeign = totalEntry[totalRegimeKey];
  }

  const rows: TgssCountryRow[] = [];
  for (const [country, regObj] of Object.entries(map)) {
    if (country.toUpperCase() === "TOTAL") continue;
    const val = totalRegimeKey ? regObj[totalRegimeKey] : undefined;
    if (typeof val === "number" && val > 0) {
      rows.push({
        country,
        affiliates: val,
        shareOfTotalPct: totalForeign && totalForeign > 0 ? (val / totalForeign) * 100 : 0,
      });
    }
  }

  rows.sort((a, b) => b.affiliates - a.affiliates);
  return { totalForeign, byCountry: rows };
}

--- FILE: frontend/components/detail/ChartRenderer.tsx ---
"use client";

import dynamic from "next/dynamic";
import type { DatoItem, Lang } from "@/lib/types";
import { isFiscalBalanceItem, isNationalityIndicatorsItem } from "@/lib/types";
import { ChartSkeleton } from "@/components/visualizations/ChartSkeleton";
import InteractiveChart from "./InteractiveChart";

const FiscalBalanceView = dynamic(
  () => import("./fiscal/FiscalBalanceView"),
  { loading: () => <ChartSkeleton /> }
);

const NationalityIndicatorsView = dynamic(
  () => import("./nationality/NationalityIndicatorsView"),
  { ssr: false, loading: () => <ChartSkeleton /> }
);

const AdminRegistersChart = dynamic(
  () => import("./admin-registers/AdminRegistersChart"),
  { loading: () => <ChartSkeleton /> }
);

const ImmigrationExplorer = dynamic(
  () => import("@/components/visualizations/immigration/ImmigrationExplorer"),
  { loading: () => <ChartSkeleton /> }
);

interface ChartRendererProps {
  item: DatoItem | any;
  lang?: Lang;
  locale?: Lang;
}

export function ChartRenderer({ item, lang, locale = "es" }: ChartRendererProps) {
  const activeLang = lang || locale || "es";

  if (isFiscalBalanceItem(item)) {
    return <FiscalBalanceView item={item} lang={activeLang} locale={activeLang} />;
  }

  if (item?.kind === "admin-registers-nationality") {
    return <AdminRegistersChart records={item.annualRecords || []} lang={activeLang} />;
  }

  if (isNationalityIndicatorsItem(item)) {
    return <NationalityIndicatorsView item={item} lang={activeLang} locale={activeLang} />;
  }

  if (item.chartType === "immigration-flow" || "annualBlocks" in item || "blocks" in item) {
    return <ImmigrationExplorer indicator={item} locale={activeLang} />;
  }

  return <InteractiveChart item={item} lang={activeLang} locale={activeLang} />;
}

export default ChartRenderer;

"use client";

import type { DatoItem, Lang } from "@/lib/types";
import { ConceptGuideProvider } from "./guide/ConceptGuideContext";
import ConceptGuideSection from "./guide/ConceptGuideSection";
import ChartRenderer from "./ChartRenderer";
import ContextNotesCard from "./ContextNotesCard";
import SourceCard from "./SourceCard";

interface DatoClientViewProps {
  dato: DatoItem | any;
  lang?: Lang;
  locale?: Lang;
}

export function DatoClientView({ dato, lang, locale = "es" }: DatoClientViewProps) {
  const activeLang = lang || locale || "es";
  const notesArray = Array.isArray(dato.contextNotes)
    ? dato.contextNotes
    : typeof dato.contextNotes === "object" && dato.contextNotes !== null
    ? dato.contextNotes[activeLang] || dato.contextNotes.es
    : [];

  return (
    <ConceptGuideProvider guide={dato.conceptGuide}>
      <div className="space-y-6">
        <ChartRenderer item={dato} lang={activeLang} locale={activeLang} />
        {dato.conceptGuide && <ConceptGuideSection lang={activeLang} />}
        <ContextNotesCard notes={notesArray} lang={activeLang} locale={activeLang} />
        <SourceCard
          sources={dato.sources || (dato.source ? [dato.source] : [])}
          lastUpdated={dato.lastUpdated || dato.last_updated}
          lang={activeLang}
          locale={activeLang}
        />
      </div>
    </ConceptGuideProvider>
  );
}

--- FILE: frontend/components/detail/InteractiveChart.tsx ---
"use client";

import { useMemo } from "react";
import {
  ResponsiveContainer,
  LineChart,
  Line,
  BarChart,
  Bar,
  XAxis,
  YAxis,
  Tooltip,
  CartesianGrid,
  type TooltipProps,
} from "recharts";
import type { DatoItem, Lang } from "@/lib/types";
import { formatNumber, formatPercent } from "@/lib/format";

interface InteractiveChartProps {
  item: DatoItem;
  lang?: Lang;
  locale?: Lang;
}

interface CustomTooltipProps extends TooltipProps<number, string> {
  unit: string;
  lang: Lang;
}

function StandardTooltip({ active, payload, label, unit, lang }: CustomTooltipProps) {
  if (!active || !payload || !payload.length) return null;
  const entry = payload[0];
  const val = Number(entry.value) || 0;

  const isPercent = unit.includes("%");
  const formattedVal = isPercent ? formatPercent(val, lang) : formatNumber(val, lang);

  return (
    <div className="bg-slate-900 text-white p-3 rounded-xl shadow-2xl border border-slate-700 text-xs space-y-1 font-sans">
      <div className="font-semibold text-slate-300">{label}</div>
      <div className="text-sm font-bold text-blue-400 font-mono tabular-nums">
        {formattedVal} {unit ? `(${unit})` : ""}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

export function InteractiveChart({ item, lang, locale = "es" }: InteractiveChartProps) {
  const activeLang = lang || locale || "es";
  const { chartType, unit } = item;

  // Read data points safely (from dataPoints array or points array)
  const rawPoints = (item as any).dataPoints || (item as any).points || [];

  const data = useMemo(() => {
    return rawPoints.map((pt: any) => ({
      period: pt.year || pt.period || "",
      value: typeof pt.value === "number" ? pt.value : Number(pt.value) || 0,
    }));
  }, [rawPoints]);

  if (!data || data.length === 0) {
    return (
      <div className="bg-slate-900 border border-slate-800 rounded-2xl p-12 text-center text-slate-400 text-xs">
        No hay datos suficientes para mostrar la gráfica.
      </div>
    );
  }

  const isBar = chartType === "bar";

  return (
    <div className="bg-slate-900 border border-slate-800 rounded-2xl p-4 sm:p-6 shadow-xl space-y-3">
      <div className="w-full h-[360px] sm:h-[420px]">
        <ResponsiveContainer width="100%" height="100%">
          {isBar ? (
            <BarChart data={data} margin={{ top: 12, right: 16, left: -10, bottom: 8 }}>
              <CartesianGrid strokeDasharray="3 3" stroke="#1E293B" vertical={false} />
              <XAxis
                dataKey="period"
                stroke="#64748B"
                tick={{ fill: "#94A3B8", fontSize: 11, fontFamily: "monospace" }}
                axisLine={{ stroke: "#334155" }}
                tickLine={false}
              />
              <YAxis
                stroke="#64748B"
                tick={{ fill: "#94A3B8", fontSize: 11, fontFamily: "monospace" }}
                axisLine={{ stroke: "#334155" }}
                domain={[0, (dataMax: number) => Math.ceil(dataMax * 1.1)]}
                tickFormatter={(v: number) => formatNumber(v, activeLang)}
              />
              <Tooltip
                content={<StandardTooltip unit={unit} lang={activeLang} />}
              />
              <Bar dataKey="value" fill="#3B82F6" radius={[4, 4, 0, 0]} />
            </BarChart>
          ) : (
            <LineChart data={data} margin={{ top: 12, right: 16, left: -10, bottom: 8 }}>
              <CartesianGrid strokeDasharray="3 3" stroke="#1E293B" vertical={false} />
              <XAxis
                dataKey="period"
                stroke="#64748B"
                tick={{ fill: "#94A3B8", fontSize: 11, fontFamily: "monospace" }}
                axisLine={{ stroke: "#334155" }}
                tickLine={false}
              />
              <YAxis
                stroke="#64748B"
                tick={{ fill: "#94A3B8", fontSize: 11, fontFamily: "monospace" }}
                axisLine={{ stroke: "#334155" }}
                domain={[
                  (dataMin: number) => (dataMin < 0 ? Math.floor(dataMin * 1.1) : 0),
                  (dataMax: number) => Math.ceil(dataMax * 1.1),
                ]}
                tickFormatter={(v: number) => formatNumber(v, activeLang)}
              />
              <Tooltip
                content={<StandardTooltip unit={unit} lang={activeLang} />}
              />
              <Line
                type="monotone"
                dataKey="value"
                stroke="#3B82F6"
                strokeWidth={3}
                dot={{ r: 3, fill: "#3B82F6" }}
                activeDot={{ r: 6, fill: "#60a5fa" }}
              />
            </LineChart>
          )}
        </ResponsiveContainer>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

export default InteractiveChart;

--- FILE: frontend/components/detail/MethodologicalNotes.tsx ---
import type { Locale } from "@/lib/types";
import { t } from "@/lib/i18n";

interface MethodologicalNotesProps {
  notes: string[];
  locale: Locale;
}

export function MethodologicalNotes({ notes, locale }: MethodologicalNotesProps) {
  if (!notes || notes.length === 0) return null;

  return (
    <section className="bg-slate-900 rounded-2xl border border-slate-800 p-5 space-y-3 shadow-xl">
      <h3 className="text-xs font-bold text-slate-300 uppercase tracking-wider flex items-center gap-2">
        <span className="w-2 h-2 rounded-full bg-blue-500" />
        {t("methodologicalNotesTitle", locale)}
      </h3>
      <ul className="space-y-2 text-xs text-slate-400">
        {notes.map((note, idx) => (
          <li key={idx} className="flex items-start gap-2">
            <span className="text-blue-400 font-mono mt-0.5">•</span>
            <span className="leading-relaxed">{note}</span>
          </li>
        ))}
      </ul>
    </section>
  );
}

export default MethodologicalNotes;

--- FILE: frontend/components/detail/SourceCard.tsx ---
import { ShieldCheck, Calendar, ExternalLink, AlertCircle } from "lucide-react";
import type { Indicator, Locale } from "@/lib/types";
import type { Lang } from "@/lib/types";
import { sanitizeHttpUrl } from "@/lib/url";
import { t } from "@/lib/i18n";

interface SourceCardProps {
  sources?: Indicator["sources"] | any[];
  lastUpdated?: string;
  locale?: Locale;
  lang?: Lang;
}

export function SourceCard({ sources, lastUpdated, locale, lang }: SourceCardProps) {
  const activeLocale: Locale = (lang || locale || "es") as Locale;

  if (!sources || sources.length === 0) {
    return (
      <div className="bg-slate-900 border border-slate-800 rounded-2xl p-5 shadow-sm space-y-4">
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between flex-wrap gap-2">
          <span className="text-[11px] uppercase tracking-wider font-bold text-slate-400 flex items-center gap-1.5">
            <ShieldCheck className="w-4 h-4 text-blue-400" />
            {t("sourceTitle", activeLocale)}
          </span>
        </div>
        <div className="p-5 rounded-xl border border-dashed border-slate-800 bg-slate-950/50 text-center space-y-2">
          <AlertCircle className="w-5 h-5 text-amber-400 mx-auto" />
          <p className="text-xs font-semibold text-white">
            {t("noDataYet", activeLocale)}
          </p>
        </div>
      </div>
    );
  }

  const uniqueInstitutions = Array.from(
    new Set(
      sources.map((s: any) =>
        typeof s.institution === "object"
          ? s.institution[activeLocale] || s.institution.es
          : s.institution
      ).filter(Boolean)
    )
  );
  const headerInstitution =
    uniqueInstitutions.length > 1
      ? uniqueInstitutions.join(" / ")
      : typeof sources[0]?.institution === "object"
      ? sources[0]?.institution[activeLocale] || sources[0]?.institution.es
      : sources[0]?.institution ?? "";

  const subtitleText =
    sources.length === 1
      ? t("sourcesSubtitleSingle", activeLocale)
      : t("sourcesSubtitleMultiple", activeLocale);

  return (
    <section className="bg-slate-900 border border-slate-800 rounded-2xl p-5 shadow-xl space-y-4">
      <div className="flex items-center justify-between flex-wrap gap-2">
        <span className="text-[11px] uppercase tracking-wider font-bold text-slate-400 flex items-center gap-1.5">
          <ShieldCheck className="w-4 h-4 text-blue-400" />
          {sources.length > 1 ? t("sourcesTitle", activeLocale) : t("sourceTitle", activeLocale)}
        </span>
      </div>

      <div className="space-y-1">
        <p className="text-sm font-semibold text-white">{headerInstitution}</p>
        <p className="text-xs text-slate-400">{subtitleText}</p>
      </div>

      <div className="space-y-3">
        {sources.map((s: any, idx: number) => {
          const safeUrl = sanitizeHttpUrl(s.url);
          const instText =
            typeof s.institution === "object"
              ? s.institution[activeLocale] || s.institution.es
              : s.institution;
          const titleText =
            typeof s.title === "object"
              ? s.title[activeLocale] || s.title.es
              : s.title;
          const shortName =
            typeof s.shortName === "object"
              ? s.shortName?.[activeLocale] || s.shortName?.es || titleText
              : s.shortName || titleText;

          const effectiveDate = s.lastUpdated ?? lastUpdated;

          return (
            <div
              key={`${s.tableId ?? "src"}-${idx}`}
              className="p-4 rounded-xl border transition-all flex flex-col sm:flex-row items-start sm:items-center justify-between gap-4 bg-slate-950/50 border-slate-800 hover:border-slate-700"
            >
              <div className="space-y-1.5">
                <div className="flex items-center gap-2 flex-wrap">
                  {uniqueInstitutions.length > 1 && (
                    <span className="text-[11px] font-semibold text-amber-400 bg-amber-950/80 px-2 py-0.5 rounded border border-amber-800/80">
                      {instText}
                    </span>
                  )}
                  <span className="text-xs font-bold text-white">{titleText}</span>
                  {s.period && (
                    <span className="text-[11px] font-semibold text-blue-400 bg-blue-950 px-2 py-0.5 rounded border border-blue-800/80 font-mono">
                      {s.period}
                    </span>
                  )}
                </div>

                <div className="flex items-center gap-3 text-[11px] text-slate-400 flex-wrap">
                  {s.tableId && (
                    <span className="font-mono text-slate-300 font-semibold">
                      {t("officialTable", activeLocale)} {s.tableId}
                    </span>
                  )}
                  {effectiveDate && (
                    <span className="flex items-center gap-1">
                      <Calendar className="w-3 h-3 text-slate-500" />
                      {t("lastUpdated", activeLocale)}: {effectiveDate}
                    </span>
                  )}
                </div>
              </div>

              {safeUrl ? (
                <a
                  href={safeUrl}
                  target="_blank"
                  rel="noopener noreferrer"
                  className="inline-flex items-center gap-2 text-xs font-semibold text-blue-300 hover:text-white bg-blue-950 hover:bg-blue-900 px-4 py-2.5 rounded-xl transition-colors border border-blue-800/80 shrink-0 w-full sm:w-auto justify-center"
                >
                  <span>{shortName}</span>
                  <ExternalLink className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                </a>
              ) : (
                <span className="text-xs text-slate-500 italic">URL no disponible</span>
              )}
            </div>
          );
        })}
      </div>
    </section>
  );
}

export default SourceCard;

--- FILE: frontend/components/detail/admin-registers/AdminRegistersChart.tsx ---
export { AdminRegistersChart as default, AdminRegistersChart } from "../../admin-registers/AdminRegistersChart";

--- FILE: frontend/components/detail/admin-registers/adminRegisterSelectors.ts ---
export * from "../../admin-registers/adminRegisterSelectors";

--- FILE: frontend/components/detail/fiscal/FiscalAnatomySplit.tsx ---
"use client";

import { useState } from "react";
import { ChevronDown, ChevronRight, Info, Scale } from "lucide-react";
import type { AnnualBalanceRecord, FiscalTaxonomy, FiscalUnitId, Lang } from "@/lib/types";
import {
  buildAnatomyBreakdown,
  getLatestCofogYear,
  formatFiscalValue,
  type AnatomyItem,
} from "./fiscalSelectors";
import { getTaxonColor, DEFICIT_BRIDGE, SURPLUS_BRIDGE } from "./fiscalPalette";
import { t } from "@/lib/i18n";
import ConceptHelpTrigger from "@/components/detail/guide/ConceptHelpTrigger";

interface FiscalAnatomySplitProps {
  record: AnnualBalanceRecord;
  taxonomy: FiscalTaxonomy;
  unit: FiscalUnitId;
  balances: AnnualBalanceRecord[];
  onSelectYear: (year: string) => void;
  lang?: Lang;
}

export function FiscalAnatomySplit({
  record,
  taxonomy,
  unit,
  balances,
  onSelectYear,
  lang = "es",
}: FiscalAnatomySplitProps) {
  const breakdown = buildAnatomyBreakdown(record, taxonomy, unit);
  const otherUnit: FiscalUnitId = unit === "PC_GDP" ? "MIO_EUR" : "PC_GDP";
  const otherMetrics = record.metrics[otherUnit];

  // Default expanded taxa codes: D5 and GF10, GF01
  const [expandedCodes, setExpandedCodes] = useState<Record<string, boolean>>({
    D5: true,
    GF10: true,
    GF01: true,
  });

  const toggleExpand = (code: string) => {
    setExpandedCodes((prev) => ({ ...prev, [code]: !prev[code] }));
  };

  const allTaxaCodes = [
    ...breakdown.revenues.filter((r) => r.children.length > 0).map((r) => r.code),
    ...breakdown.expenditures.filter((e) => e.children.length > 0).map((e) => e.code),
  ];

  const isAllExpanded = allTaxaCodes.every((c) => expandedCodes[c]);

  const toggleAll = () => {
    if (isAllExpanded) {
      setExpandedCodes({});
    } else {
      const next: Record<string, boolean> = {};
      allTaxaCodes.forEach((c) => {
        next[c] = true;
      });
      setExpandedCodes(next);
    }
  };

  const latestCofogYear = getLatestCofogYear(balances);

  const totalRevVal = record.metrics[unit].totals.revenue;
  const otherTotalRevVal = otherMetrics.totals.revenue;

  const totalExpVal = record.metrics[unit].totals.expenditure;
  const otherTotalExpVal = otherMetrics.totals.expenditure;

  return (
    <div className="space-y-4">
      {/* Top Accordion Action Row */}
      <div className="flex items-center justify-end">
        <button
          onClick={toggleAll}
          className="text-xs font-semibold text-blue-400 hover:text-blue-300 transition-colors flex items-center gap-1"
        >
          {isAllExpanded ? t("collapseAll", lang) : t("expandAll", lang)}
        </button>
      </div>

      {/* 2-Column Responsive Layout */}
      <div className="grid grid-cols-1 lg:grid-cols-2 gap-6">
        {/* LEFT COLUMN: REVENUES (Ingresos) */}
        <div className="bg-slate-900 border border-slate-800 rounded-2xl p-4 sm:p-5 space-y-4">
          {/* Column Header */}
          <div className="border-b border-slate-800 pb-3 flex items-center justify-between">
            <div>
              <h4 className="text-sm font-bold text-white uppercase tracking-wider flex items-center gap-2">
                <span className="w-2.5 h-2.5 rounded-full bg-sky-400" />
                <span>Ingresos Públicos (TR)</span>
              </h4>
              <p className="text-xs text-slate-400 font-mono tabular-nums mt-0.5">
                {formatFiscalValue(otherTotalRevVal, otherUnit, lang, { compact: true })}
              </p>
            </div>
            <div className="text-right">
              <span className="text-lg font-bold text-sky-400 font-mono tabular-nums">
                {formatFiscalValue(totalRevVal, unit, lang, { compact: true })}
              </span>
            </div>
          </div>

          {/* Revenues List */}
          <div className="space-y-2.5">
            {breakdown.revenues.map((item) => {
              const isExpanded = !!expandedCodes[item.code];
              const hasChildren = item.children.length > 0;
              const color = getTaxonColor(item.code);

              return (
                <div
                  key={item.code}
                  className="bg-slate-950/60 border border-slate-800/80 rounded-xl p-3 space-y-2 transition-all hover:border-slate-700"
                >
                  {/* Item Main Row */}
                  <div
                    onClick={() => hasChildren && toggleExpand(item.code)}
                    className={`flex items-center justify-between gap-3 ${
                      hasChildren ? "cursor-pointer select-none" : ""
                    }`}
                  >
                    <div className="flex items-center gap-2 min-w-0 flex-1">
                      {hasChildren ? (
                        <button
                          aria-expanded={isExpanded}
                          className="text-slate-400 hover:text-white transition-colors"
                        >
                          {isExpanded ? (
                            <ChevronDown className="w-4 h-4" />
                          ) : (
                            <ChevronRight className="w-4 h-4" />
                          )}
                        </button>
                      ) : (
                        <span className="w-4" />
                      )}

                      <span
                        className="w-2.5 h-2.5 rounded-full shrink-0"
                        style={{ backgroundColor: color }}
                      />

                      <span
                        className="text-xs font-semibold text-slate-200 truncate"
                        title={item.label}
                      >
                        {item.shortLabel}
                      </span>

                      <span className="text-[10px] font-mono text-slate-400 px-1.5 py-0.5 bg-slate-800 rounded">
                        {item.code}
                      </span>
                      <ConceptHelpTrigger code={item.code} lang={lang} />
                    </div>

                    <div className="text-right shrink-0 font-mono tabular-nums text-xs">
                      <span className="font-bold text-white">
                        {formatFiscalValue(item.value, unit, lang, { compact: true })}
                      </span>
                      <span className="text-slate-400 text-[11px] ml-1.5">
                        ({item.sharePct.toFixed(1)}%)
                      </span>
                    </div>
                  </div>

                  {/* Proportional Bar */}
                  <div className="w-full h-1.5 bg-slate-800 rounded-full overflow-hidden">
                    <div
                      className="h-full rounded-full transition-all duration-300"
                      style={{
                        width: `${Math.min(100, Math.max(0, item.sharePct))}%`,
                        backgroundColor: color,
                      }}
                    />
                  </div>

                  {/* Accordion Sub-list for Children */}
                  {hasChildren && isExpanded && (
                    <div className="pl-6 pt-1 space-y-1.5 border-t border-slate-800/60 mt-2">
                      {item.children.map((child) => (
                        <div
                          key={child.code}
                          className="flex items-center justify-between gap-2 text-xs font-mono tabular-nums"
                        >
                          <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-slate-400 min-w-0 flex-1">
                            <span className="text-[10px] text-slate-400 font-semibold shrink-0">
                              [{child.code}]
                            </span>
                            <span className="truncate min-w-0 flex-1">{child.label}</span>
                            <ConceptHelpTrigger code={child.code} lang={lang} />
                          </div>
                          <div className="text-right text-slate-300 shrink-0 text-[11px]">
                            <span>
                              {formatFiscalValue(child.value, unit, lang, { compact: true })}
                            </span>
                            <span className="text-slate-400 ml-1">
                              ({child.shareOfParentPct.toFixed(0)}%)
                            </span>
                          </div>
                        </div>
                      ))}
                    </div>
                  )}
                </div>
              );
            })}

            {/* Accounting Bridge Row: Deficit if B.9 < 0 */}
            {breakdown.bridgeItem && breakdown.bridgeItem.side === "revenues" && (
              <div className="border-2 border-dashed border-rose-500/40 bg-rose-950/20 rounded-xl p-3 flex items-center justify-between text-xs font-mono tabular-nums">
                <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                  <span
                    className="w-2.5 h-2.5 rounded-full shrink-0"
                    style={{ backgroundColor: DEFICIT_BRIDGE }}
                  />
                  <span className="font-bold text-rose-300">
                    + Necesidad de financiación (Déficit B.9)
                  </span>
                </div>
                <div className="text-right">
                  <span className="font-bold text-rose-400">
                    {formatFiscalValue(breakdown.bridgeItem.value, unit, lang, {
                      compact: true,
                    })}
                  </span>
                  <span className="text-rose-400/70 ml-1">
                    ({breakdown.bridgeItem.sharePct.toFixed(1)}%)
                  </span>
                </div>
              </div>
            )}
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* RIGHT COLUMN: EXPENDITURES (Gastos por función COFOG) */}
        <div className="bg-slate-900 border border-slate-800 rounded-2xl p-4 sm:p-5 space-y-4">
          {/* Column Header */}
          <div className="border-b border-slate-800 pb-3 flex items-center justify-between">
            <div>
              <h4 className="text-sm font-bold text-white uppercase tracking-wider flex items-center gap-2">
                <span className="w-2.5 h-2.5 rounded-full bg-indigo-400" />
                <span>Gastos Públicos (COFOG TE)</span>
              </h4>
              <p className="text-xs text-slate-400 font-mono tabular-nums mt-0.5">
                {formatFiscalValue(otherTotalExpVal, otherUnit, lang, { compact: true })}
              </p>
            </div>
            <div className="text-right">
              <span className="text-lg font-bold text-indigo-400 font-mono tabular-nums">
                {formatFiscalValue(totalExpVal, unit, lang, { compact: true })}
              </span>
            </div>
          </div>

          {/* Graceful Handling when cofogAvailable === false */}
          {!breakdown.cofogAvailable ? (
            <div className="bg-slate-950/80 border border-dashed border-amber-500/40 rounded-xl p-6 text-center space-y-4 my-auto">
              <div className="w-10 h-10 rounded-full bg-amber-500/10 border border-amber-500/30 flex items-center justify-center mx-auto text-amber-400">
                <Info className="w-5 h-5" />
              </div>
              <div className="space-y-1">
                <h5 className="text-sm font-bold text-white">
                  {t("cofogNotPublished", lang)}
                </h5>
                <p className="text-xs text-slate-400 max-w-sm mx-auto">
                  Eurostat e IGAE publican los desgloses funcionales COFOG con un año de desfase respecto a la contabilidad nacional trimestral.
                </p>
              </div>

              {latestCofogYear && (
                <button
                  onClick={() => onSelectYear(latestCofogYear)}
                  className="inline-flex items-center gap-2 px-4 py-2 bg-amber-500/20 hover:bg-amber-500/30 text-amber-300 border border-amber-500/40 rounded-xl text-xs font-bold transition-all shadow-md"
                >
                  <Scale className="w-4 h-4" />
                  <span>
                    {t("viewLatestCofogYear", lang)} ({latestCofogYear})
                  </span>
                </button>
              )}
            </div>
          ) : (
            /* Expenditures List */
            <div className="space-y-2.5">
              {breakdown.expenditures.map((item) => {
                const isExpanded = !!expandedCodes[item.code];
                const hasChildren = item.children.length > 0;
                const color = getTaxonColor(item.code);

                return (
                  <div
                    key={item.code}
                    className="bg-slate-950/60 border border-slate-800/80 rounded-xl p-3 space-y-2 transition-all hover:border-slate-700"
                  >
                    {/* Main Row */}
                    <div
                      onClick={() => hasChildren && toggleExpand(item.code)}
                      className={`flex items-center justify-between gap-3 ${
                        hasChildren ? "cursor-pointer select-none" : ""
                      }`}
                    >
                      <div className="flex items-center gap-2 min-w-0 flex-1">
                        {hasChildren ? (
                          <button
                            aria-expanded={isExpanded}
                            className="text-slate-400 hover:text-white transition-colors"
                          >
                            {isExpanded ? (
                              <ChevronDown className="w-4 h-4" />
                            ) : (
                              <ChevronRight className="w-4 h-4" />
                            )}
                          </button>
                        ) : (
                          <span className="w-4" />
                        )}

                        <span
                          className="w-2.5 h-2.5 rounded-full shrink-0"
                          style={{ backgroundColor: color }}
                        />

                        <span
                          className="text-xs font-semibold text-slate-200 truncate"
                          title={item.label}
                        >
                          {item.shortLabel}
                        </span>

                        <span className="text-[10px] font-mono text-slate-400 px-1.5 py-0.5 bg-slate-800 rounded">
                          {item.code}
                        </span>
                        <ConceptHelpTrigger code={item.code} lang={lang} />
                      </div>

                      <div className="text-right shrink-0 font-mono tabular-nums text-xs">
                        <span className="font-bold text-white">
                          {formatFiscalValue(item.value, unit, lang, { compact: true })}
                        </span>
                        <span className="text-slate-400 text-[11px] ml-1.5">
                          ({item.sharePct.toFixed(1)}%)
                        </span>
                      </div>
                    </div>

                    {/* Proportional Bar */}
                    <div className="w-full h-1.5 bg-slate-800 rounded-full overflow-hidden">
                      <div
                        className="h-full rounded-full transition-all duration-300"
                        style={{
                          width: `${Math.min(100, Math.max(0, item.sharePct))}%`,
                          backgroundColor: color,
                        }}
                      />
                    </div>

                    {/* Accordion Sub-list for Highlights */}
                    {hasChildren && isExpanded && (
                      <div className="pl-6 pt-1 space-y-1.5 border-t border-slate-800/60 mt-2">
                        {item.children.map((child) => (
                          <div
                            key={child.code}
                            className="flex items-center justify-between gap-2 text-xs font-mono tabular-nums"
                          >
                            <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-slate-400 min-w-0">
                              <span className="text-[10px] text-slate-400 font-semibold shrink-0">
                                [{child.code}]
                              </span>
                              <span className="truncate">{child.label}</span>
                              <ConceptHelpTrigger code={child.code} lang={lang} />
                            </div>
                            <div className="text-right text-slate-300 shrink-0 text-[11px]">
                              <span>
                                {formatFiscalValue(child.value, unit, lang, {
                                  compact: true,
                                })}
                              </span>
                              <span className="text-slate-400 ml-1">
                                ({child.shareOfParentPct.toFixed(0)}%)
                              </span>
                            </div>
                          </div>
                        ))}
                      </div>
                    )}
                  </div>
                );
              })}

              {/* Accounting Bridge Row: Surplus if B.9 > 0 */}
              {breakdown.bridgeItem && breakdown.bridgeItem.side === "expenditures" && (
                <div className="border-2 border-dashed border-emerald-500/40 bg-emerald-950/20 rounded-xl p-3 flex items-center justify-between text-xs font-mono tabular-nums">
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                    <span
                      className="w-2.5 h-2.5 rounded-full shrink-0"
                      style={{ backgroundColor: SURPLUS_BRIDGE }}
                    />
                    <span className="font-bold text-emerald-300">
                      + Capacidad de financiación (Superávit B.9)
                    </span>
                  </div>
                  <div className="text-right">
                    <span className="font-bold text-emerald-400">
                      {formatFiscalValue(breakdown.bridgeItem.value, unit, lang, {
                        compact: true,
                      })}
                    </span>
                    <span className="text-emerald-400/70 ml-1">
                      ({breakdown.bridgeItem.sharePct.toFixed(1)}%)
                    </span>
                  </div>
                </div>
              )}
            </div>
          )}
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Uncollectible Adjustment Note if present */}
      {breakdown.uncollectibleAdjustment !== null && (
        <div className="text-[11px] text-slate-400 flex items-center gap-2 px-3 py-2 bg-slate-900 border border-slate-800 rounded-xl">
          <Info className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-slate-400 shrink-0" />
          <span>
            Ajuste de recaudación incierta (SEC 2010): D.995 / D.91 ajuste por impuestos no cobrados ={" "}
            {formatFiscalValue(breakdown.uncollectibleAdjustment, unit, lang, { signed: true })}.
          </span>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

export default FiscalAnatomySplit;

--- FILE: frontend/components/detail/fiscal/FiscalBalanceView.tsx ---
"use client";

import { useState } from "react";
import type { FiscalBalanceItem, FiscalUnitId, Lang } from "@/lib/types";
import { getAvailableYears } from "./fiscalSelectors";
import FiscalControlBar from "./FiscalControlBar";
import FiscalMacroChart from "./FiscalMacroChart";
import FiscalAnatomySplit from "./FiscalAnatomySplit";
import FiscalSankeyDiagram from "./FiscalSankeyDiagram";
import FiscalEraComparison from "./FiscalEraComparison";
import { t } from "@/lib/i18n";

interface FiscalBalanceViewProps {
  item: FiscalBalanceItem;
  lang?: Lang;
  locale?: Lang;
}

type AnatomyTab = "split" | "sankey" | "compare";

export function FiscalBalanceView({
  item,
  lang,
  locale = "es",
}: FiscalBalanceViewProps) {
  const activeLang = lang || locale || "es";
  const { annualBalances, taxonomy } = item;

  const years = getAvailableYears(annualBalances);
  const defaultYear = years.length > 0 ? years[years.length - 1] : "2025";

  const [unit, setUnit] = useState<FiscalUnitId>("PC_GDP");
  const [selectedYear, setSelectedYear] = useState<string>(defaultYear);
  const [anatomyTab, setAnatomyTab] = useState<AnatomyTab>("split");

  const currentRecord =
    annualBalances.find((b) => b.year === selectedYear) ||
    annualBalances[annualBalances.length - 1];

  if (!currentRecord) return null;

  return (
    <div className="space-y-6 animate-in fade-in duration-200">
      {/* Layer 0: Control Bar & KPI Cards */}
      <FiscalControlBar
        balances={annualBalances}
        selectedYear={selectedYear}
        onSelectYear={setSelectedYear}
        unit={unit}
        onSelectUnit={setUnit}
        lang={activeLang}
      />

      {/* Layer 1: Macro Historical Explorer (1995-Present) */}
      <FiscalMacroChart
        balances={annualBalances}
        taxonomy={taxonomy}
        unit={unit}
        selectedYear={selectedYear}
        onSelectYear={setSelectedYear}
        lang={activeLang}
      />

      {/* Layer 2: Anatomy & Structural Subviews */}
      <div className="space-y-4">
        {/* Section Header & Tab Switcher */}
        <div className="bg-slate-900 border border-slate-800 rounded-2xl p-4 sm:p-5 flex flex-col sm:flex-row items-stretch sm:items-center justify-between gap-3 shadow-xl">
          <div>
            <h3 className="text-base font-bold text-white flex items-center gap-2">
              <span>{t("budgetAnatomy", activeLang)}</span>
              <span className="text-xs font-mono font-bold text-blue-400 bg-blue-950 border border-blue-800/80 px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full">
                {selectedYear}
              </span>
            </h3>
            <p className="text-xs text-slate-400 mt-0.5">
              Inspecciona la estructura detallada de ingresos tributarios y gastos por función social.
            </p>
          </div>

          {/* Tab Switcher */}
          <div className="inline-flex p-1 bg-slate-950 border border-slate-800 rounded-xl text-xs font-semibold shrink-0">
            <button
              onClick={() => setAnatomyTab("split")}
              className={`px-3.5 py-1.5 rounded-lg transition-all ${
                anatomyTab === "split"
                  ? "bg-blue-600 text-white shadow-xs"
                  : "text-slate-400 hover:text-white"
              }`}
            >
              {t("tabBreakdown", activeLang)}
            </button>
            <button
              onClick={() => setAnatomyTab("sankey")}
              className={`px-3.5 py-1.5 rounded-lg transition-all ${
                anatomyTab === "sankey"
                  ? "bg-blue-600 text-white shadow-xs"
                  : "text-slate-400 hover:text-white"
              }`}
            >
              {t("tabFlow", activeLang)}
            </button>
            <button
              onClick={() => setAnatomyTab("compare")}
              className={`px-3.5 py-1.5 rounded-lg transition-all ${
                anatomyTab === "compare"
                  ? "bg-blue-600 text-white shadow-xs"
                  : "text-slate-400 hover:text-white"
              }`}
            >
              {t("tabCompare", activeLang)}
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Selected Layer 2 Subview */}
        {anatomyTab === "split" && (
          <FiscalAnatomySplit
            record={currentRecord}
            taxonomy={taxonomy}
            unit={unit}
            balances={annualBalances}
            onSelectYear={setSelectedYear}
            lang={activeLang}
          />
        )}
