        {anatomyTab === "sankey" && (
          <FiscalSankeyDiagram
            record={currentRecord}
            taxonomy={taxonomy}
            unit={unit}
            balances={annualBalances}
            onSelectYear={setSelectedYear}
            onSwitchToSplit={() => setAnatomyTab("split")}
            lang={activeLang}
          />
        )}

        {anatomyTab === "compare" && (
          <FiscalEraComparison
            balances={annualBalances}
            taxonomy={taxonomy}
            unit={unit}
            lang={activeLang}
          />
        )}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

export default FiscalBalanceView;

--- FILE: frontend/components/detail/fiscal/FiscalControlBar.tsx ---
"use client";

import { ChevronLeft, ChevronRight, Scale, TrendingUp, TrendingDown } from "lucide-react";
import type { AnnualBalanceRecord, FiscalUnitId, Lang } from "@/lib/types";
import {
  getAvailableYears,
  getYearMetrics,
  formatFiscalValue,
  formatFiscalDelta,
} from "./fiscalSelectors";
import { t } from "@/lib/i18n";
import ConceptHelpTrigger from "@/components/detail/guide/ConceptHelpTrigger";

interface FiscalControlBarProps {
  balances: AnnualBalanceRecord[];
  selectedYear: string;
  onSelectYear: (year: string) => void;
  unit: FiscalUnitId;
  onSelectUnit: (unit: FiscalUnitId) => void;
  lang?: Lang;
}

export function FiscalControlBar({
  balances,
  selectedYear,
  onSelectYear,
  unit,
  onSelectUnit,
  lang = "es",
}: FiscalControlBarProps) {
  const years = getAvailableYears(balances);
  const currentIdx = years.indexOf(selectedYear);

  const yearMetrics = getYearMetrics(balances, selectedYear, unit);
  const otherUnit: FiscalUnitId = unit === "PC_GDP" ? "MIO_EUR" : "PC_GDP";
  const otherYearMetrics = getYearMetrics(balances, selectedYear, otherUnit);

  if (!yearMetrics) return null;

  const { record, metrics, prevMetrics } = yearMetrics;
  const otherMetrics = otherYearMetrics?.metrics;
  const otherPrevMetrics = otherYearMetrics?.prevMetrics;

  const canPrev = currentIdx > 0;
  const canNext = currentIdx !== -1 && currentIdx < years.length - 1;

  const handlePrev = () => {
    if (canPrev) onSelectYear(years[currentIdx - 1]);
  };

  const handleNext = () => {
    if (canNext) onSelectYear(years[currentIdx + 1]);
  };

  // Revenues KPI calculations
  const revVal = metrics.totals.revenue;
  const otherRevVal = otherMetrics?.totals.revenue ?? 0;
  const revYoY = prevMetrics ? revVal - prevMetrics.totals.revenue : null;

  // Expenditures KPI calculations
  const expVal = metrics.totals.expenditure;
  const otherExpVal = otherMetrics?.totals.expenditure ?? 0;
  const expYoY = prevMetrics ? expVal - prevMetrics.totals.expenditure : null;

  // Balance KPI calculations
  const balVal = metrics.totals.balance;
  const otherBalVal = otherMetrics?.totals.balance ?? 0;
  const balYoY = prevMetrics ? balVal - prevMetrics.totals.balance : null;

  const isSurplus = balVal >= 0;

  return (
    <div className="bg-slate-900 border border-slate-800 rounded-2xl p-4 sm:p-5 shadow-xl space-y-4">
      {/* Top Controls Row */}
      <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row items-stretch sm:items-center justify-between gap-3">
        {/* Unit Switcher */}
        <div
          role="group"
          aria-label="Selección de unidad fiscal"
          className="inline-flex items-center p-1 bg-slate-950 border border-slate-800 rounded-xl"
        >
          <button
            onClick={() => onSelectUnit("PC_GDP")}
            className={`px-3.5 py-1.5 rounded-lg text-xs font-semibold transition-all inline-flex items-center gap-1 ${
              unit === "PC_GDP"
                ? "bg-blue-600 text-white shadow-sm"
                : "text-slate-400 hover:text-slate-200"
            }`}
          >
            <span>{t("unitPcGdp", lang)}</span>
          </button>
          <button
            onClick={() => onSelectUnit("MIO_EUR")}
            className={`px-3.5 py-1.5 rounded-lg text-xs font-semibold transition-all ${
              unit === "MIO_EUR"
                ? "bg-blue-600 text-white shadow-sm"
                : "text-slate-400 hover:text-slate-200"
            }`}
          >
            {t("unitMioEur", lang)}
          </button>
          <ConceptHelpTrigger code="PC_GDP" lang={lang} className="ml-1.5" />
          <ConceptHelpTrigger code="S13" lang={lang} className="ml-1" />
        </div>

        {/* Year Stepper */}
        <div className="flex items-center justify-center gap-2 bg-slate-950 border border-slate-800 rounded-xl px-2 py-1">
          <button
            onClick={handlePrev}
            disabled={!canPrev}
            aria-label={t("previousYear", lang)}
            className="p-1.5 rounded-lg text-slate-400 hover:text-white disabled:opacity-30 disabled:cursor-not-allowed hover:bg-slate-800 transition-colors"
          >
            <ChevronLeft className="w-4 h-4" />
          </button>

          <select
            value={selectedYear}
            onChange={(e) => onSelectYear(e.target.value)}
            className="bg-transparent text-white font-mono font-bold text-sm py-1 px-2 focus:outline-none cursor-pointer"
          >
            {years.map((y) => {
              const rec = balances.find((b) => b.year === y);
              const hasCofog = rec?.cofogAvailable ?? true;
              return (
                <option key={y} value={y} className="bg-slate-900 text-white">
                  {y} {!hasCofog ? `(${lang === "es" ? "sin COFOG" : "no COFOG"})` : ""}
                </option>
              );
            })}
          </select>

          <button
            onClick={handleNext}
            disabled={!canNext}
            aria-label={t("nextYear", lang)}
            className="p-1.5 rounded-lg text-slate-400 hover:text-white disabled:opacity-30 disabled:cursor-not-allowed hover:bg-slate-800 transition-colors"
          >
            <ChevronRight className="w-4 h-4" />
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* 3-Column Macro KPI Strip */}
      <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-3 gap-3">
        {/* KPI 1: Revenues */}
        <div className="bg-slate-950/70 border border-slate-800/80 rounded-xl p-3.5 space-y-1">
          <div className="text-[11px] font-semibold tracking-wider text-slate-400 uppercase flex items-center justify-between">
            <span>{t("totalRevenue", lang)}</span>
            <ConceptHelpTrigger code="TR" lang={lang} />
          </div>
          <div className="text-xl sm:text-2xl font-bold text-sky-400 font-mono tabular-nums">
            {formatFiscalValue(revVal, unit, lang, { compact: true })}
          </div>
          <div className="text-xs text-slate-400 flex items-center justify-between font-mono tabular-nums pt-0.5 border-t border-slate-800/60">
            <span>{formatFiscalValue(otherRevVal, otherUnit, lang, { compact: true })}</span>
            {revYoY !== null && (
              <span className="text-slate-300">
                {formatFiscalDelta(revYoY, unit, lang)} {t("yoyChange", lang)}
              </span>
            )}
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* KPI 2: Expenditures */}
        <div className="bg-slate-950/70 border border-slate-800/80 rounded-xl p-3.5 space-y-1">
          <div className="text-[11px] font-semibold tracking-wider text-slate-400 uppercase flex items-center justify-between">
            <span>{t("totalExpenditure", lang)}</span>
            <ConceptHelpTrigger code="TE" lang={lang} />
          </div>
          <div className="text-xl sm:text-2xl font-bold text-indigo-400 font-mono tabular-nums">
            {formatFiscalValue(expVal, unit, lang, { compact: true })}
          </div>
          <div className="text-xs text-slate-400 flex items-center justify-between font-mono tabular-nums pt-0.5 border-t border-slate-800/60">
            <span>{formatFiscalValue(otherExpVal, otherUnit, lang, { compact: true })}</span>
            {expYoY !== null && (
              <span className="text-slate-300">
                {formatFiscalDelta(expYoY, unit, lang)} {t("yoyChange", lang)}
              </span>
            )}
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* KPI 3: Fiscal Balance */}
        <div
          className={`bg-slate-950/70 border rounded-xl p-3.5 space-y-1 ${
            isSurplus ? "border-emerald-500/30" : "border-rose-500/30"
          }`}
        >
          <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
            <span className="text-[11px] font-semibold tracking-wider text-slate-400 uppercase flex items-center gap-1">
              <span>{t("fiscalBalance", lang)}</span>
              <ConceptHelpTrigger code="B9" lang={lang} />
            </span>
            <span
              className={`inline-flex items-center gap-1 px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full text-[10px] font-bold ${
                isSurplus
                  ? "bg-emerald-950 text-emerald-400 border border-emerald-800/80"
                  : "bg-rose-950 text-rose-400 border border-rose-800/80"
              }`}
            >
              {isSurplus ? <TrendingUp className="w-3 h-3" /> : <TrendingDown className="w-3 h-3" />}
              {isSurplus ? t("surplus", lang) : t("deficit", lang)}
            </span>
          </div>
          <div
            className={`text-xl sm:text-2xl font-bold font-mono tabular-nums ${
              isSurplus ? "text-emerald-400" : "text-rose-400"
            }`}
          >
            {formatFiscalValue(balVal, unit, lang, { signed: true, compact: true })}
          </div>
          <div className="text-xs text-slate-400 flex items-center justify-between font-mono tabular-nums pt-0.5 border-t border-slate-800/60">
            <span>
              {formatFiscalValue(otherBalVal, otherUnit, lang, { signed: true, compact: true })}
            </span>
            {balYoY !== null && (
              <span className="text-slate-300">
                {formatFiscalDelta(balYoY, unit, lang)} {t("yoyChange", lang)}
              </span>
            )}
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

export default FiscalControlBar;

--- FILE: frontend/components/detail/fiscal/FiscalEraComparison.tsx ---
"use client";

import { useState } from "react";
import { Info, ArrowRight } from "lucide-react";
import type { AnnualBalanceRecord, FiscalTaxonomy, FiscalUnitId, Lang } from "@/lib/types";
import {
  computeEraComparison,
  getAvailableYears,
  getLatestCofogYear,
  formatFiscalValue,
  formatFiscalDelta,
} from "./fiscalSelectors";
import { getTaxonColor } from "./fiscalPalette";
import { t } from "@/lib/i18n";

interface FiscalEraComparisonProps {
  balances: AnnualBalanceRecord[];
  taxonomy: FiscalTaxonomy;
  unit: FiscalUnitId;
  lang?: Lang;
}

type Side = "expenditures" | "revenues";

export function FiscalEraComparison({
  balances,
  taxonomy,
  unit,
  lang = "es",
}: FiscalEraComparisonProps) {
  const years = getAvailableYears(balances);
  const latestCofog = getLatestCofogYear(balances) ?? years[years.length - 1];

  const defaultYearA = years.includes("2007") ? "2007" : years[0];
  const defaultYearB = latestCofog;

  const [yearA, setYearA] = useState<string>(defaultYearA);
  const [yearB, setYearB] = useState<string>(defaultYearB);
  const [side, setSide] = useState<Side>("expenditures");

  const era = computeEraComparison(balances, taxonomy, yearA, yearB, side, unit);

  return (
    <div className="bg-slate-900 border border-slate-800 rounded-2xl p-4 sm:p-6 space-y-6">
      {/* Controls Header */}
      <div className="flex flex-col md:flex-row items-stretch md:items-center justify-between gap-4 border-b border-slate-800 pb-4">
        {/* Presets & Year Dropdowns */}
        <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-3">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2 bg-slate-950 border border-slate-800 rounded-xl px-3 py-1 text-xs">
            <span className="text-slate-400 font-semibold">{t("compareFrom", lang)}:</span>
            <select
              value={yearA}
              onChange={(e) => setYearA(e.target.value)}
              className="bg-transparent text-white font-mono font-bold focus:outline-none cursor-pointer"
            >
              {years.map((y) => (
                <option key={y} value={y} className="bg-slate-900 text-white">
                  {y}
                </option>
              ))}
            </select>
          </div>

          <ArrowRight className="w-4 h-4 text-slate-500 shrink-0 hidden sm:block" />

          <div className="flex items-center gap-2 bg-slate-950 border border-slate-800 rounded-xl px-3 py-1 text-xs">
            <span className="text-slate-400 font-semibold">{t("compareTo", lang)}:</span>
            <select
              value={yearB}
              onChange={(e) => setYearB(e.target.value)}
              className="bg-transparent text-white font-mono font-bold focus:outline-none cursor-pointer"
            >
              {years.map((y) => (
                <option key={y} value={y} className="bg-slate-900 text-white">
                  {y}
                </option>
              ))}
            </select>
          </div>

          {/* Neutral Presets */}
          <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-xs font-semibold">
            <span className="text-slate-400 text-[11px] uppercase mr-1">Presets:</span>
            {years.includes("1995") && (
              <button
                onClick={() => {
                  setYearA("1995");
                  setYearB(latestCofog);
                }}
                className={`px-2.5 py-1 rounded-lg border transition-all ${
                  yearA === "1995" && yearB === latestCofog
                    ? "bg-blue-600 text-white border-blue-500"
                    : "bg-slate-950 text-slate-300 border-slate-800 hover:text-white"
                }`}
              >
                1995 → {latestCofog}
              </button>
            )}
            {years.includes("2007") && (
              <button
                onClick={() => {
                  setYearA("2007");
                  setYearB(latestCofog);
                }}
                className={`px-2.5 py-1 rounded-lg border transition-all ${
                  yearA === "2007" && yearB === latestCofog
                    ? "bg-blue-600 text-white border-blue-500"
                    : "bg-slate-950 text-slate-300 border-slate-800 hover:text-white"
                }`}
              >
                2007 → {latestCofog}
              </button>
            )}
            {years.includes("2019") && (
              <button
                onClick={() => {
                  setYearA("2019");
                  setYearB(latestCofog);
                }}
                className={`px-2.5 py-1 rounded-lg border transition-all ${
                  yearA === "2019" && yearB === latestCofog
                    ? "bg-blue-600 text-white border-blue-500"
                    : "bg-slate-950 text-slate-300 border-slate-800 hover:text-white"
                }`}
              >
                2019 → {latestCofog}
              </button>
            )}
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Side Toggle: Expenditures vs Revenues */}
        <div className="inline-flex p-1 bg-slate-950 border border-slate-800 rounded-xl text-xs font-semibold shrink-0">
          <button
            onClick={() => setSide("expenditures")}
            className={`px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg transition-all ${
              side === "expenditures"
                ? "bg-indigo-600 text-white shadow-xs"
                : "text-slate-400 hover:text-white"
            }`}
          >
            Gastos (COFOG)
          </button>
          <button
            onClick={() => setSide("revenues")}
            className={`px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg transition-all ${
              side === "revenues"
                ? "bg-sky-600 text-white shadow-xs"
                : "text-slate-400 hover:text-white"
            }`}
          >
            Ingresos
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Inflation warning notice for MIO_EUR */}
      {unit === "MIO_EUR" && (
        <div className="bg-slate-950 border border-amber-500/30 rounded-xl p-3 flex items-center gap-2 text-xs text-amber-300">
          <Info className="w-4 h-4 text-amber-400 shrink-0" />
          <span>{t("nominalEurWarning", lang)}</span>
        </div>
      )}

      {/* Missing COFOG years warning */}
      {era.missingCofogYears.length > 0 ? (
        <div className="bg-slate-950 border border-slate-800 rounded-xl p-8 text-center space-y-2">
          <Info className="w-6 h-6 text-amber-400 mx-auto" />
          <h5 className="text-sm font-bold text-white">
            {t("cofogNotPublished", lang)}
          </h5>
          <p className="text-xs text-slate-400">
            No se puede realizar la comparativa de gastos COFOG porque no hay datos disponibles para el año {era.missingCofogYears.join(", ")}.
          </p>
        </div>
      ) : (
        /* Diverging Horizontal Bar Chart */
        <div className="space-y-3">
          <div className="flex items-center justify-between text-xs font-semibold text-slate-400 border-b border-slate-800 pb-2">
            <span>Partida</span>
            <div className="flex items-center gap-12 font-mono">
              <span>{yearA} → {yearB}</span>
              <span className="w-24 text-right">Variación Estr. ({unit === "PC_GDP" ? "pp PIB" : "M€"})</span>
            </div>
          </div>

          <div className="space-y-2">
            {era.items.map((item) => {
              const color = getTaxonColor(item.code);
              const maxScale = era.maxAbsDelta > 0 ? era.maxAbsDelta : 1;
              const barWidthPct = Math.min(100, (Math.abs(item.delta) / maxScale) * 100);
              const isPositive = item.delta >= 0;

              return (
                <div
                  key={item.code}
                  className="bg-slate-950/70 border border-slate-800/80 rounded-xl p-3 space-y-2 hover:border-slate-700 transition-all"
                >
                  <div className="flex items-center justify-between text-xs min-w-0">
                    <div className="flex items-center gap-2 min-w-0 flex-1">
                      <span
                        className="w-2.5 h-2.5 rounded-full shrink-0"
                        style={{ backgroundColor: color }}
                      />
                      <span className="font-semibold text-slate-200 truncate">
                        {item.shortLabel}
                      </span>
                      <span className="text-[10px] font-mono text-slate-400 bg-slate-800 px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded">
                        {item.code}
                      </span>
                    </div>

                    <div className="flex items-center gap-6 font-mono tabular-nums text-xs shrink-0">
                      <span className="text-slate-400">
                        {formatFiscalValue(item.valueA, unit, lang, { compact: true })} →{" "}
                        <strong className="text-slate-200">
                          {formatFiscalValue(item.valueB, unit, lang, { compact: true })}
                        </strong>
                      </span>

                      <span
                        className={`font-bold w-24 text-right ${
                          isPositive ? "text-sky-400" : "text-amber-400"
                        }`}
                      >
                        {formatFiscalDelta(item.delta, unit, lang)}
                      </span>
                    </div>
                  </div>

                  {/* Diverging Bar Track */}
                  <div className="relative w-full h-2 bg-slate-800/80 rounded-full overflow-hidden flex items-center">
                    <div className="absolute left-1/2 top-0 bottom-0 w-0.5 bg-slate-600 z-10" />
                    <div className="w-full flex h-full">
                      {/* Left Side (Negative Deltas) */}
                      <div className="w-1/2 flex justify-end">
                        {!isPositive && (
                          <div
                            className="h-full bg-amber-500 rounded-l-full transition-all duration-300"
                            style={{ width: `${barWidthPct}%` }}
                          />
                        )}
                      </div>
                      {/* Right Side (Positive Deltas) */}
                      <div className="w-1/2 flex justify-start">
                        {isPositive && (
                          <div
                            className="h-full bg-sky-400 rounded-r-full transition-all duration-300"
                            style={{ width: `${barWidthPct}%` }}
                          />
                        )}
                      </div>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                </div>
              );
            })}
          </div>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

export default FiscalEraComparison;

--- FILE: frontend/components/detail/fiscal/FiscalMacroChart.tsx ---
"use client";

import { useState, useMemo } from "react";
import {
  ResponsiveContainer,
  ComposedChart,
  Line,
  BarChart,
  Bar,
  AreaChart,
  Area,
  XAxis,
  YAxis,
  Tooltip,
  CartesianGrid,
  ReferenceLine,
  Cell,
  type TooltipProps,
} from "recharts";
import type { AnnualBalanceRecord, FiscalTaxonomy, FiscalUnitId, Lang } from "@/lib/types";
import {
  buildMacroTimeSeries,
  buildCompositionSeries,
  formatFiscalValue,
  getLatestCofogYear,
} from "./fiscalSelectors";
import {
  REVENUE_COLOR,
  EXPENDITURE_COLOR,
  SURPLUS_COLOR,
  DEFICIT_COLOR,
  getTaxonColor,
} from "./fiscalPalette";
import { t } from "@/lib/i18n";
import ConceptHelpTrigger from "@/components/detail/guide/ConceptHelpTrigger";

interface FiscalMacroChartProps {
  balances: AnnualBalanceRecord[];
  taxonomy: FiscalTaxonomy;
  unit: FiscalUnitId;
  selectedYear: string;
  onSelectYear: (year: string) => void;
  lang?: Lang;
}

type MacroTab = "gap" | "rev-comp" | "exp-comp";
type ScaleMode = "unit" | "percent";

function MacroTooltip({
  active,
  payload,
  label,
  unit,
  balances,
  lang = "es",
}: TooltipProps<number, string> & {
  unit: FiscalUnitId;
  balances: AnnualBalanceRecord[];
  lang?: Lang;
}) {
  if (!active || !payload || !payload.length || !label) return null;

  const yearStr = String(label);
  const rec = balances.find((b) => b.year === yearStr);
  const m = rec?.metrics?.[unit];

  if (!m) return null;

  const rev = m.totals.revenue;
  const exp = m.totals.expenditure;
  const bal = m.totals.balance;
  const isSurplus = bal >= 0;

  return (
    <div className="bg-slate-950 border border-slate-700 p-3 rounded-xl shadow-2xl text-xs space-y-2 max-w-xs font-sans">
      <div className="font-bold text-white border-b border-slate-800 pb-1 flex items-center justify-between">
        <span>
          {t("yearLabel", lang)} {yearStr}
        </span>
        <span className="text-slate-400 font-mono font-normal">
          {unit === "PC_GDP" ? t("unitPcGdp", lang) : t("unitMioEur", lang)}
        </span>
      </div>

      <div className="space-y-1 font-mono tabular-nums text-[11px]">
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-4">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-sky-400">
            <span className="w-2 h-2 rounded-full bg-sky-400" />
            <span>Ingresos (TR):</span>
          </div>
          <span className="font-bold text-white">
            {formatFiscalValue(rev, unit, lang, { compact: true })}
          </span>
        </div>

        <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-4">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-indigo-400">
            <span className="w-2 h-2 rounded-full bg-indigo-400" />
            <span>Gastos (TE):</span>
          </div>
          <span className="font-bold text-white">
            {formatFiscalValue(exp, unit, lang, { compact: true })}
          </span>
        </div>

        <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-4 pt-1 border-t border-slate-800">
          <div
            className={`flex items-center gap-1.5 ${
              isSurplus ? "text-emerald-400" : "text-rose-400"
            }`}
          >
            <span
              className={`w-2 h-2 rounded-full ${
                isSurplus ? "bg-emerald-400" : "bg-rose-400"
              }`}
            />
            <span>Saldo (B.9):</span>
          </div>
          <span
            className={`font-bold ${
              isSurplus ? "text-emerald-400" : "text-rose-400"
            }`}
          >
            {formatFiscalValue(bal, unit, lang, { signed: true, compact: true })}
          </span>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

export function FiscalMacroChart({
  balances,
  taxonomy,
  unit,
  selectedYear,
  onSelectYear,
  lang = "es",
}: FiscalMacroChartProps) {
  const [activeTab, setActiveTab] = useState<MacroTab>("gap");
  const [scaleMode, setScaleMode] = useState<ScaleMode>("unit");
  const [isolatedTaxon, setIsolatedTaxon] = useState<string | null>(null);

  const macroData = useMemo(
    () => buildMacroTimeSeries(balances, unit),
    [balances, unit]
  );

  const compData = useMemo(() => {
    if (activeTab === "gap") return [];
    const side = activeTab === "rev-comp" ? "revenues" : "expenditures";
    return buildCompositionSeries(balances, taxonomy, side, unit, scaleMode);
  }, [balances, taxonomy, activeTab, unit, scaleMode]);

  const activeSideTaxa = useMemo(() => {
    if (activeTab === "gap") return [];
    return activeTab === "rev-comp" ? taxonomy.revenues : taxonomy.expenditures;
  }, [taxonomy, activeTab]);

  const handleChartClick = (e: any) => {
    if (e && e.activeLabel) {
      onSelectYear(String(e.activeLabel));
    }
  };

  const latestRec = balances[balances.length - 1];

  return (
    <div className="bg-slate-900 border border-slate-800 rounded-2xl p-4 sm:p-6 shadow-xl space-y-4">
      {/* Header & Controls */}
      <div className="flex flex-col md:flex-row items-stretch md:items-center justify-between gap-3 border-b border-slate-800/80 pb-4">
        <div>
          <h3 className="text-base font-bold text-white flex items-center gap-2">
            <span>{t("historicalEvolution", lang)}</span>
            <ConceptHelpTrigger code="SEC2010" lang={lang} />
          </h3>
          <p className="text-xs text-slate-400 mt-0.5 flex items-center gap-1.5">
            <span>{t("clickYearHint", lang)}</span>
            {activeTab === "exp-comp" && <ConceptHelpTrigger code="COFOG" lang={lang} />}
          </p>
        </div>

        <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
          {/* Main Mode Tabs */}
          <div className="inline-flex p-1 bg-slate-950 border border-slate-800 rounded-xl text-xs font-semibold">
            <button
              onClick={() => {
                setActiveTab("gap");
                setIsolatedTaxon(null);
              }}
              className={`px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg transition-all ${
                activeTab === "gap"
                  ? "bg-blue-600 text-white shadow-xs"
                  : "text-slate-400 hover:text-white"
              }`}
            >
              {t("revenueVsExpenditure", lang)}
            </button>
            <button
              onClick={() => {
                setActiveTab("rev-comp");
                setIsolatedTaxon(null);
              }}
              className={`px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg transition-all ${
                activeTab === "rev-comp"
                  ? "bg-blue-600 text-white shadow-xs"
                  : "text-slate-400 hover:text-white"
              }`}
            >
              {t("revenueComposition", lang)}
            </button>
            <button
              onClick={() => {
                setActiveTab("exp-comp");
                setIsolatedTaxon(null);
              }}
              className={`px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg transition-all ${
                activeTab === "exp-comp"
                  ? "bg-blue-600 text-white shadow-xs"
                  : "text-slate-400 hover:text-white"
              }`}
            >
              {t("expenditureComposition", lang)}
            </button>
          </div>

          {/* Scale Toggle for Composition modes */}
          {activeTab !== "gap" && (
            <div className="inline-flex p-1 bg-slate-950 border border-slate-800 rounded-xl text-xs font-semibold">
              <button
                onClick={() => setScaleMode("unit")}
                className={`px-2.5 py-1 rounded-lg transition-all ${
                  scaleMode === "unit"
                    ? "bg-slate-700 text-white"
                    : "text-slate-400 hover:text-white"
                }`}
              >
                {unit === "PC_GDP" ? "% PIB" : "M€"}
              </button>
              <button
                onClick={() => setScaleMode("percent")}
                className={`px-2.5 py-1 rounded-lg transition-all ${
                  scaleMode === "percent"
                    ? "bg-slate-700 text-white"
                    : "text-slate-400 hover:text-white"
                }`}
              >
                % Total
              </button>
            </div>
          )}
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* MODE 1: GAP (Revenues vs Expenditure + Synchronized Balance Strip) */}
      {activeTab === "gap" && (
        <div className="space-y-2">
          {/* Upper Gap Chart */}
          <div className="w-full h-72 sm:h-80">
            <ResponsiveContainer width="100%" height="100%">
              <ComposedChart
                syncId="fiscal-macro"
                data={macroData}
                onClick={handleChartClick}
                margin={{ top: 10, right: 10, left: -20, bottom: 0 }}
              >
                <CartesianGrid strokeDasharray="3 3" stroke="#1E293B" vertical={false} />
                <XAxis
                  dataKey="year"
                  stroke="#64748B"
                  tick={{ fill: "#94A3B8", fontSize: 11, fontFamily: "monospace" }}
                  axisLine={{ stroke: "#334155" }}
                  tickLine={false}
                />
                <YAxis
                  domain={["auto", "auto"]}
                  stroke="#64748B"
                  tick={{ fill: "#94A3B8", fontSize: 11, fontFamily: "monospace" }}
                  axisLine={{ stroke: "#334155" }}
                  tickFormatter={(val: number) =>
                    formatFiscalValue(val, unit, lang, { compact: true })
                  }
                />
                <Tooltip
                  content={
                    <MacroTooltip unit={unit} balances={balances} lang={lang} />
                  }
                />
                <ReferenceLine
                  x={selectedYear}
                  stroke="#94A3B8"
                  strokeDasharray="4 4"
                  strokeWidth={1.5}
                />
                <Line
                  type="monotone"
                  dataKey="revenue"
                  name="Ingresos (TR)"
                  stroke={REVENUE_COLOR}
                  strokeWidth={2.5}
                  dot={false}
                  activeDot={{ r: 5, fill: REVENUE_COLOR }}
                />
                <Line
                  type="monotone"
                  dataKey="expenditure"
                  name="Gastos (TE)"
                  stroke={EXPENDITURE_COLOR}
                  strokeWidth={2.5}
                  dot={false}
                  activeDot={{ r: 5, fill: EXPENDITURE_COLOR }}
                />
              </ComposedChart>
            </ResponsiveContainer>
          </div>

          {/* Lower Synchronized Balance Strip */}
          <div className="w-full h-28 pt-2 border-t border-slate-800/60">
            <ResponsiveContainer width="100%" height="100%">
              <BarChart
                syncId="fiscal-macro"
                data={macroData}
                onClick={handleChartClick}
                margin={{ top: 5, right: 10, left: -20, bottom: 0 }}
              >
                <CartesianGrid strokeDasharray="3 3" stroke="#1E293B" vertical={false} />
                <XAxis dataKey="year" hide />
                <YAxis
                  domain={["auto", "auto"]}
                  stroke="#64748B"
                  tick={{ fill: "#94A3B8", fontSize: 10, fontFamily: "monospace" }}
                  tickFormatter={(val: number) => (val === 0 ? "0" : `${val}`)}
                />
                <ReferenceLine y={0} stroke="#475569" strokeWidth={1} />
                <ReferenceLine
                  x={selectedYear}
                  stroke="#94A3B8"
                  strokeDasharray="4 4"
                />
                <Bar dataKey="balance" radius={[2, 2, 0, 0]}>
                  {macroData.map((entry) => {
                    const isSelected = entry.year === selectedYear;
                    const fill = entry.balance >= 0 ? SURPLUS_COLOR : DEFICIT_COLOR;
                    return (
                      <Cell
                        key={entry.year}
                        fill={fill}
                        opacity={isSelected ? 1.0 : 0.75}
                        cursor="pointer"
                      />
                    );
                  })}
                </Bar>
              </BarChart>
            </ResponsiveContainer>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      {/* MODE 2 & 3: Composition Area Chart */}
      {activeTab !== "gap" && (
        <div className="space-y-3">
          <div className="w-full h-80 sm:h-96">
            <ResponsiveContainer width="100%" height="100%">
              <AreaChart
                data={compData}
                onClick={handleChartClick}
                margin={{ top: 10, right: 10, left: -20, bottom: 0 }}
              >
                <CartesianGrid strokeDasharray="3 3" stroke="#1E293B" vertical={false} />
                <XAxis
                  dataKey="year"
                  stroke="#64748B"
                  tick={{ fill: "#94A3B8", fontSize: 11, fontFamily: "monospace" }}
                  axisLine={{ stroke: "#334155" }}
                  tickLine={false}
                />
                <YAxis
                  stroke="#64748B"
                  tick={{ fill: "#94A3B8", fontSize: 11, fontFamily: "monospace" }}
                  axisLine={{ stroke: "#334155" }}
                  tickFormatter={(val: number) =>
                    scaleMode === "percent"
                      ? `${val.toFixed(0)}%`
                      : formatFiscalValue(val, unit, lang, { compact: true })
                  }
                />
                <Tooltip
                  content={({ active, payload, label }) => {
                    if (!active || !payload || !payload.length || !label) return null;
                    return (
                      <div className="bg-slate-950 border border-slate-700 p-3 rounded-xl shadow-2xl text-xs space-y-1.5 max-w-xs font-sans">
                        <div className="font-bold text-white border-b border-slate-800 pb-1 flex items-center justify-between">
                          <span>Año {label}</span>
                          <span className="text-slate-400 font-mono text-[11px]">
                            {scaleMode === "percent" ? "% del Total" : unit}
                          </span>
                        </div>
                        <div className="space-y-1 font-mono tabular-nums text-[11px]">
                          {payload.map((entry) => {
                            if (entry.value === null || entry.value === undefined)
                              return null;
                            const taxon = activeSideTaxa.find(
                              (t) => t.code === entry.dataKey
                            );
                            const valNum = Number(entry.value);
                            return (
                              <div
                                key={String(entry.dataKey)}
                                className="flex items-center justify-between gap-3"
                              >
                                <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 truncate">
                                  <span
                                    className="w-2.5 h-2.5 rounded-full shrink-0"
                                    style={{ backgroundColor: entry.color }}
                                  />
                                  <span className="truncate text-slate-300">
                                    {taxon?.shortLabel ?? entry.dataKey}
                                  </span>
                                </div>
                                <span className="font-bold text-white">
                                  {scaleMode === "percent"
                                    ? `${valNum.toFixed(1)}%`
                                    : formatFiscalValue(valNum, unit, lang, {
                                        compact: true,
                                      })}
                                </span>
                              </div>
                            );
                          })}
                        </div>
                      </div>
                    );
                  }}
                />
                <ReferenceLine
                  x={selectedYear}
                  stroke="#94A3B8"
                  strokeDasharray="4 4"
                  strokeWidth={1.5}
                />
                {activeSideTaxa.map((t) => {
                  const color = getTaxonColor(t.code);
                  const isDimmed = isolatedTaxon !== null && isolatedTaxon !== t.code;
                  return (
                    <Area
                      key={t.code}
                      type="monotone"
                      dataKey={t.code}
                      name={t.shortLabel}
                      stackId="1"
                      stroke={color}
                      fill={color}
                      fillOpacity={isDimmed ? 0.08 : 0.85}
                      strokeOpacity={isDimmed ? 0.2 : 1.0}
                      connectNulls={false}
                    />
                  );
                })}
              </AreaChart>
            </ResponsiveContainer>
          </div>

          {/* Clickable Legend Pills */}
          <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2 pt-2 border-t border-slate-800">
            {activeSideTaxa.map((t) => {
              const color = getTaxonColor(t.code);
              const isSelected = isolatedTaxon === t.code;
              return (
                <button
                  key={t.code}
                  onClick={() =>
                    setIsolatedTaxon(isSelected ? null : t.code)
                  }
                  className={`inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-2.5 py-1 rounded-full text-xs font-semibold transition-all ${
                    isSelected
                      ? "bg-slate-700 text-white ring-1 ring-white/40"
                      : isolatedTaxon !== null
                      ? "bg-slate-950/40 text-slate-500 opacity-60"
                      : "bg-slate-950 text-slate-300 hover:text-white border border-slate-800"
                  }`}
                >
                  <span
                    className="w-2.5 h-2.5 rounded-full"
                    style={{ backgroundColor: color }}
                  />
                  <span>{t.shortLabel}</span>
                  <span className="font-mono text-[10px] opacity-60">({t.code})</span>
                </button>
              );
            })}

            {isolatedTaxon && (
              <button
                onClick={() => setIsolatedTaxon(null)}
                className="px-2.5 py-1 rounded-full text-xs font-semibold text-blue-400 hover:text-blue-300 transition-colors"
              >
                Restablecer
              </button>
            )}
          </div>

          {/* Footnote for missing COFOG in recent years */}
          {activeTab === "exp-comp" && !latestRec.cofogAvailable && (
            <p className="text-[11px] text-slate-400 italic">
              * Nota: El desglose por funciones COFOG finaliza en {getLatestCofogYear(balances)} (el ejercicio {latestRec.year} aún no cuenta con desglose funcional por la IGAE/Eurostat).
            </p>
          )}
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

export default FiscalMacroChart;

--- FILE: frontend/components/detail/fiscal/FiscalSankeyDiagram.tsx ---
"use client";

import { useState } from "react";
import { Info, Monitor, ArrowRight } from "lucide-react";
import type { AnnualBalanceRecord, FiscalTaxonomy, FiscalUnitId, Lang } from "@/lib/types";
import {
  buildSankeyGraph,
  getLatestCofogYear,
  formatFiscalValue,
  type SankeyNode,
} from "./fiscalSelectors";
import { t } from "@/lib/i18n";

interface FiscalSankeyDiagramProps {
  record: AnnualBalanceRecord;
  taxonomy: FiscalTaxonomy;
  unit: FiscalUnitId;
  balances: AnnualBalanceRecord[];
  onSelectYear: (year: string) => void;
  onSwitchToSplit: () => void;
  lang?: Lang;
}

export function FiscalSankeyDiagram({
  record,
  taxonomy,
  unit,
  balances,
  onSelectYear,
  onSwitchToSplit,
  lang = "es",
}: FiscalSankeyDiagramProps) {
  const [hoveredCode, setHoveredCode] = useState<string | null>(null);

  const graph = buildSankeyGraph(record, taxonomy, unit, 920, 520);
  const latestCofogYear = getLatestCofogYear(balances);

  if (!graph.cofogAvailable) {
    return (
      <div className="bg-slate-900 border border-slate-800 rounded-2xl p-8 text-center space-y-4">
        <div className="w-12 h-12 rounded-full bg-amber-500/10 border border-amber-500/30 flex items-center justify-center mx-auto text-amber-400">
          <Info className="w-6 h-6" />
        </div>
        <div className="space-y-1">
          <h5 className="text-base font-bold text-white">
            {t("cofogNotPublished", lang)}
          </h5>
          <p className="text-xs text-slate-400 max-w-md mx-auto">
            El diagrama de flujo Sankey requiere el desglose por funciones COFOG de los gastos.
          </p>
        </div>
        {latestCofogYear && (
          <button
            onClick={() => onSelectYear(latestCofogYear)}
            className="inline-flex items-center gap-2 px-4 py-2 bg-amber-500/20 hover:bg-amber-500/30 text-amber-300 border border-amber-500/40 rounded-xl text-xs font-bold transition-all shadow-md"
          >
            <span>
              {t("viewLatestCofogYear", lang)} ({latestCofogYear})
            </span>
          </button>
        )}
      </div>
    );
  }

  const activeHoveredNode = graph.nodes.find((n) => n.code === hoveredCode);

  return (
    <div className="bg-slate-900 border border-slate-800 rounded-2xl p-4 sm:p-6 space-y-4">
      {/* Mobile Notice Banner */}
      <div className="md:hidden bg-slate-950 border border-slate-800 rounded-xl p-3 flex items-center justify-between gap-3 text-xs">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-slate-300">
          <Monitor className="w-4 h-4 text-blue-400 shrink-0" />
          <span>El diagrama de flujo se aprecia mejor en pantallas anchas.</span>
        </div>
        <button
          onClick={onSwitchToSplit}
          className="px-2.5 py-1 bg-slate-800 hover:bg-slate-700 text-white rounded-lg text-xs font-semibold shrink-0 transition-colors"
        >
          {t("tabBreakdown", lang)}
        </button>
      </div>

      {/* Detail Hover Card Banner */}
      <div className="min-h-[52px] bg-slate-950/80 border border-slate-800/80 rounded-xl p-3 flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-3 text-xs font-mono">
        {activeHoveredNode ? (
          <>
            <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
              <span
                className="w-3 h-3 rounded-full"
                style={{ backgroundColor: activeHoveredNode.color }}
              />
              <span className="font-bold text-white font-sans">
                {activeHoveredNode.label}
              </span>
              <span className="text-slate-400">[{activeHoveredNode.code}]</span>
            </div>

            <div className="flex items-center gap-4 text-slate-200">
              <span>
                Valor:{" "}
                <strong className="text-white">
                  {formatFiscalValue(activeHoveredNode.value, unit, lang, {
                    compact: true,
                  })}
                </strong>
              </span>
              <span>
                Cuota:{" "}
                <strong className="text-blue-400">
                  {activeHoveredNode.sharePct.toFixed(1)}%
                </strong>
              </span>
            </div>
          </>
        ) : (
          <div className="text-slate-400 flex items-center gap-2 font-sans italic text-xs">
            <Info className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-blue-400" />
            <span>Pasa el cursor por cualquier partida o cinta para resaltar el flujo financiero.</span>
          </div>
        )}
      </div>

      {/* Pure SVG Sankey Diagram */}
      <div className="w-full overflow-x-auto">
        <svg
          viewBox="0 0 920 520"
          className="w-full h-auto min-w-[700px] select-none"
        >
          {/* Section Headers */}
          <text x="10" y="16" fill="#94A3B8" fontSize="12" fontWeight="bold" fontFamily="sans-serif">
            INGRESOS Y FINANCIACIÓN
          </text>
          <text
            x="460"
            y="16"
            fill="#94A3B8"
            fontSize="12"
            fontWeight="bold"
            textAnchor="middle"
            fontFamily="sans-serif"
          >
            NÚCLEO AAPP S.13
          </text>
          <text
            x="910"
            y="16"
            fill="#94A3B8"
            fontSize="12"
            fontWeight="bold"
            textAnchor="end"
            fontFamily="sans-serif"
          >
            GASTOS POR FUNCIÓN COFOG
          </text>

          {/* Render Flow Links (Ribbons) */}
          {graph.links.map((link) => {
            const isHovered =
              hoveredCode !== null &&
              (link.sourceId.includes(hoveredCode) || link.targetId.includes(hoveredCode));

            const opacity =
              hoveredCode === null ? 0.45 : isHovered ? 0.85 : 0.08;

            return (
              <path
                key={link.id}
                d={link.path}
                fill={link.color}
                opacity={opacity}
                className="transition-all duration-200 cursor-pointer"
                onMouseEnter={() => {
                  const code = link.id.replace(/^link-(left|right)-/, "");
                  setHoveredCode(code);
                }}
                onMouseLeave={() => setHoveredCode(null)}
              />
            );
          })}

          {/* Render Nodes */}
          {graph.nodes.map((node) => {
            const isHovered = hoveredCode === node.code;
            const isDimmed = hoveredCode !== null && !isHovered;

            return (
              <g
                key={node.id}
                transform={`translate(${node.x}, ${node.y})`}
                onMouseEnter={() => setHoveredCode(node.code)}
                onMouseLeave={() => setHoveredCode(null)}
                className="cursor-pointer"
              >
                {/* Node Rect */}
                <rect
                  width={node.width}
                  height={node.height}
                  rx={4}
                  fill={node.color}
                  opacity={isDimmed ? 0.3 : 1.0}
                  stroke={isHovered ? "#FFFFFF" : "transparent"}
                  strokeWidth={2}
                  className="transition-all duration-200"
                />

                {/* Node Label Text */}
                {node.height >= 14 && (
                  <text
                    x={
                      node.column === 0
                        ? node.width + 8
                        : node.column === 2
                        ? -8
                        : node.width / 2
                    }
                    y={node.height / 2 + 4}
                    textAnchor={
                      node.column === 0
                        ? "start"
                        : node.column === 2
                        ? "end"
                        : "middle"
                    }
                    fill={isDimmed ? "#64748B" : "#F8FAFC"}
                    fontSize={node.column === 1 ? "11" : "11"}
                    fontWeight="600"
                    fontFamily="sans-serif"
                    className="pointer-events-none transition-colors"
                  >
                    {node.shortLabel} ({node.sharePct.toFixed(0)}%)
                  </text>
                )}
              </g>
            );
          })}
        </svg>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

export default FiscalSankeyDiagram;

--- FILE: frontend/components/detail/fiscal/fiscalPalette.ts ---
export const REVENUE_COLOR = "#38BDF8";
export const EXPENDITURE_COLOR = "#818CF8";
export const SURPLUS_COLOR = "#10B981";
export const DEFICIT_COLOR = "#F43F5E";

export const DEFICIT_BRIDGE = "#F43F5E";
export const SURPLUS_BRIDGE = "#10B981";

export const TAXON_COLORS: Record<string, string> = {
  // Revenues (ESA 2010)
  D61: "#0EA5E9",
  D5: "#2563EB",
  D2: "#06B6D4",
  NON_TAX_RESIDUAL: "#64748B",
  D91: "#14B8A6",
  DEFICIT_BRIDGE: "#F43F5E",

  // Expenditures (COFOG 1999)
  GF10: "#6366F1",
  GF07: "#10B981",
  GF01: "#F59E0B",
  GF09: "#3B82F6",
  GF04: "#8B5CF6",
  GF03: "#EC4899",
  GF08: "#14B8A6",
  GF02: "#94A3B8",
  GF05: "#22C55E",
  GF06: "#FB923C",
  SURPLUS_BRIDGE: "#10B981",
};

export function getTaxonColor(code: string): string {
  if (TAXON_COLORS[code]) {
    return TAXON_COLORS[code];
  }
  // Deterministic fallback color based on code string
  let hash = 0;
  for (let i = 0; i < code.length; i++) {
    hash = code.charCodeAt(i) + ((hash << 5) - hash);
  }
  const hue = Math.abs(hash) % 360;
  return `hsl(${hue}, 65%, 55%)`;
}

--- FILE: frontend/components/detail/fiscal/fiscalSelectors.ts ---
import type {
  AnnualBalanceRecord,
  FiscalUnitId,
  FiscalTaxonomy,
  FiscalUnitMetrics,
  Lang,
} from "@/lib/types";
import { getTaxonColor, DEFICIT_BRIDGE, SURPLUS_BRIDGE, REVENUE_COLOR, EXPENDITURE_COLOR } from "./fiscalPalette";

export interface AnatomyChildItem {
  code: string;
  label: string;
  value: number;
  shareOfParentPct: number;
  shareOfTotalPct: number;
}

export interface AnatomyItem {
  code: string;
  label: string;
  shortLabel: string;
  value: number;
  sharePct: number;
  children: AnatomyChildItem[];
}

export interface BridgeItem {
  side: "revenues" | "expenditures";
  type: "deficit" | "surplus";
  value: number;
  sharePct: number;
}

export interface AnatomyBreakdownResult {
  revenues: AnatomyItem[];
  expenditures: AnatomyItem[];
  bridgeItem: BridgeItem | null;
  uncollectibleAdjustment: number | null;
  cofogAvailable: boolean;
}

export interface EraComparisonItem {
  code: string;
  label: string;
  shortLabel: string;
  valueA: number;
  valueB: number;
  delta: number;
  sharePctA: number;
  sharePctB: number;
}

export interface EraComparisonResult {
  items: EraComparisonItem[];
  maxAbsDelta: number;
  missingCofogYears: string[];
}

export interface SankeyNode {
  id: string;
  code: string;
  label: string;
  shortLabel: string;
  value: number;
  sharePct: number;
  x: number;
  y: number;
  width: number;
  height: number;
  color: string;
  column: 0 | 1 | 2;
  subNotes?: string[];
  isBridge?: boolean;
}

export interface SankeyLink {
  id: string;
  sourceId: string;
  targetId: string;
  value: number;
  color: string;
  path: string;
}

export interface SankeyGraphResult {
  nodes: SankeyNode[];
  links: SankeyLink[];
  cofogAvailable: boolean;
}

/**
 * Returns chronologically sorted list of years (ascending).
 */
export function getAvailableYears(balances: AnnualBalanceRecord[]): string[] {
  if (!balances || balances.length === 0) return [];
  return balances
    .map((b) => b.year)
    .sort((a, b) => parseInt(a, 10) - parseInt(b, 10));
}

/**
 * Scans from newest to oldest and returns latest year where cofogAvailable === true and metrics exist.
 */
export function getLatestCofogYear(balances: AnnualBalanceRecord[]): string | null {
  if (!balances || balances.length === 0) return null;
  const sorted = [...balances].sort((a, b) => parseInt(b.year, 10) - parseInt(a.year, 10));
  for (const b of sorted) {
    if (b.cofogAvailable && b.metrics?.PC_GDP?.expenditures !== null) {
      return b.year;
    }
  }
  return null;
}

/**
 * Finds requested record and its unit metrics + previous year metrics for YoY calculations.
 */
export function getYearMetrics(
  balances: AnnualBalanceRecord[],
  year: string,
  unit: FiscalUnitId
): { record: AnnualBalanceRecord; metrics: FiscalUnitMetrics; prevMetrics: FiscalUnitMetrics | null } | null {
  if (!balances || balances.length === 0) return null;
  const sorted = [...balances].sort((a, b) => parseInt(a.year, 10) - parseInt(b.year, 10));

  let idx = sorted.findIndex((b) => b.year === year);
  if (idx === -1) {
    idx = sorted.length - 1; // Fallback to latest
  }

  const record = sorted[idx];
  const metrics = record.metrics[unit];
  const prevMetrics = idx > 0 ? sorted[idx - 1].metrics[unit] : null;

  return { record, metrics, prevMetrics };
}

/**
 * Formats a fiscal numeric value based on unit (PC_GDP or MIO_EUR) and locale.
 */
export function formatFiscalValue(
  value: number,
  unit: FiscalUnitId,
  lang: Lang = "es",
  opts?: { signed?: boolean; compact?: boolean }
): string {
  if (value === undefined || value === null || !Number.isFinite(value)) {
    return "—";
  }

  const isEs = lang === "es";

  if (unit === "PC_GDP") {
    const formattedNum = Math.abs(value).toLocaleString(isEs ? "es-ES" : "en-US", {
      minimumFractionDigits: 1,
      maximumFractionDigits: 1,
    });
    const unitSuffix = isEs ? "% PIB" : "% GDP";
    const sign = value < 0 ? "-" : opts?.signed && value > 0 ? "+" : "";
    return `${sign}${formattedNum} ${unitSuffix}`;
  }

  // unit === "MIO_EUR"
  // Eurostat MIO_EUR values are already in millions of euros (e.g. 661500 = 661,5 MM€).
  const absVal = Math.abs(value);
  const sign = value < 0 ? "-" : opts?.signed && value > 0 ? "+" : "";

  if (opts?.compact && absVal >= 1000) {
    const billions = absVal / 1000;
    const formattedB = billions.toLocaleString(isEs ? "es-ES" : "en-US", {
      minimumFractionDigits: 1,
      maximumFractionDigits: 1,
    });
    return isEs ? `${sign}${formattedB} MM€` : `${sign}€${formattedB}B`;
  }

  const formattedM = Math.round(absVal).toLocaleString(isEs ? "es-ES" : "en-US");
  return isEs ? `${sign}${formattedM} M€` : `${sign}€${formattedM}M`;
}

/**
 * Formats a YoY or era delta value with explicit sign.
 */
export function formatFiscalDelta(
  delta: number,
  unit: FiscalUnitId,
  lang: Lang = "es"
): string {
  if (delta === undefined || delta === null || !Number.isFinite(delta)) {
    return "—";
  }

  const isEs = lang === "es";
  const sign = delta > 0 ? "+" : delta < 0 ? "-" : "";

  if (unit === "PC_GDP") {
    const abs = Math.abs(delta).toLocaleString(isEs ? "es-ES" : "en-US", {
      minimumFractionDigits: 1,
      maximumFractionDigits: 1,
    });
    return `${sign}${abs} pp`;
  }

  // MIO_EUR
  const absVal = Math.abs(delta);
  if (absVal >= 1000) {
    const billions = (absVal / 1000).toLocaleString(isEs ? "es-ES" : "en-US", {
      minimumFractionDigits: 1,
      maximumFractionDigits: 1,
    });
    return isEs ? `${sign}${billions} MM€` : `${sign}€${billions}B`;
  }

  const millions = Math.round(absVal).toLocaleString(isEs ? "es-ES" : "en-US");
  return isEs ? `${sign}${millions} M€` : `${sign}€${millions}M`;
}

/**
 * Builds array of macro time series points for Layer 1.
 */
export function buildMacroTimeSeries(
  balances: AnnualBalanceRecord[],
  unit: FiscalUnitId
) {
  if (!balances) return [];
  const sorted = [...balances].sort((a, b) => parseInt(a.year, 10) - parseInt(b.year, 10));
  return sorted.map((b) => {
    const m = b.metrics[unit];
    const revenue = m.totals.revenue;
    const expenditure = m.totals.expenditure;
    const balance = m.totals.balance;
    return {
      year: b.year,
      revenue,
      expenditure,
      balance,
      isSurplus: balance >= 0,
      cofogAvailable: b.cofogAvailable,
    };
  });
}

/**
 * Builds stacked composition series for Recharts AreaChart.
 */
export function buildCompositionSeries(
  balances: AnnualBalanceRecord[],
  taxonomy: FiscalTaxonomy,
  side: "revenues" | "expenditures",
  unit: FiscalUnitId,
  scaleMode: "unit" | "percent"
) {
  if (!balances || !taxonomy) return [];
  const sorted = [...balances].sort((a, b) => parseInt(a.year, 10) - parseInt(b.year, 10));
  const taxa = taxonomy[side];

  return sorted.map((b) => {
    const m = b.metrics[unit];
    const row: Record<string, any> = { year: b.year };

    if (side === "expenditures" && (!b.cofogAvailable || !m.expenditures)) {
      taxa.forEach((t) => {
        row[t.code] = null;
      });
    } else {
      const dataMap = side === "revenues" ? m.revenues : m.expenditures;
      const columnTotal = side === "revenues" ? m.totals.revenue : m.totals.expenditure;

      taxa.forEach((t) => {
        const entry = dataMap ? dataMap[t.code] : null;
        if (!entry || entry.total === undefined || entry.total === null) {
          row[t.code] = null;
        } else {
          row[t.code] =
            scaleMode === "percent" && columnTotal > 0
              ? (entry.total / columnTotal) * 100
              : entry.total;
        }
      });
    }

    return row;
  });
}

/**
 * Builds top-level breakdown for Layer 2 Anatomy Split view.
 */
export function buildAnatomyBreakdown(
  record: AnnualBalanceRecord,
  taxonomy: FiscalTaxonomy,
  unit: FiscalUnitId
): AnatomyBreakdownResult {
  const defaultResult: AnatomyBreakdownResult = {
    revenues: [],
    expenditures: [],
    bridgeItem: null,
    uncollectibleAdjustment: null,
    cofogAvailable: record.cofogAvailable,
  };

  if (!record || !taxonomy) return defaultResult;
  const metrics = record.metrics[unit];
  if (!metrics) return defaultResult;

  const totalRev = metrics.totals.revenue;
  const totalExp = metrics.totals.expenditure;
  const balance = metrics.totals.balance;

  // 1. Revenues Breakdown
  const revenues: AnatomyItem[] = taxonomy.revenues
    .map((taxon) => {
      const entry = metrics.revenues ? metrics.revenues[taxon.code] : null;
      const val = entry?.total ?? 0;
      const sharePct = totalRev > 0 ? (val / totalRev) * 100 : 0;

      const children: AnatomyChildItem[] = taxon.children.map((child) => {
        const childVal = entry?.breakdown ? entry.breakdown[child.code] ?? 0 : 0;
        return {
          code: child.code,
          label: child.label,
          value: childVal,
          shareOfParentPct: val > 0 ? (childVal / val) * 100 : 0,
          shareOfTotalPct: totalRev > 0 ? (childVal / totalRev) * 100 : 0,
        };
      });

      return {
        code: taxon.code,
        label: taxon.label,
        shortLabel: taxon.shortLabel,
        value: val,
        sharePct,
        children,
      };
    })
    .sort((a, b) => b.value - a.value);

  // 2. Expenditures Breakdown
  let expenditures: AnatomyItem[] = [];
  if (record.cofogAvailable && metrics.expenditures) {
    expenditures = taxonomy.expenditures
      .map((taxon) => {
        const entry = metrics.expenditures ? metrics.expenditures[taxon.code] : null;
        const val = entry?.total ?? 0;
        const sharePct = totalExp > 0 ? (val / totalExp) * 100 : 0;

        const children: AnatomyChildItem[] = taxon.highlights.map((child) => {
          const childVal = entry?.highlights ? entry.highlights[child.code] ?? 0 : 0;
          return {
            code: child.code,
            label: child.label,
            value: childVal,
            shareOfParentPct: val > 0 ? (childVal / val) * 100 : 0,
            shareOfTotalPct: totalExp > 0 ? (childVal / totalExp) * 100 : 0,
          };
        });

        return {
          code: taxon.code,
          label: taxon.label,
          shortLabel: taxon.shortLabel,
          value: val,
          sharePct,
          children,
        };
      })
      .sort((a, b) => b.value - a.value);
  }

  // 3. Accounting Bridge Item
  let bridgeItem: BridgeItem | null = null;
  if (balance < 0) {
    // Deficit: added to Revenue side to balance
    const absDef = Math.abs(balance);
    bridgeItem = {
      side: "revenues",
      type: "deficit",
      value: absDef,
      sharePct: totalRev > 0 ? (absDef / totalRev) * 100 : 0,
    };
  } else if (balance > 0) {
    // Surplus: added to Expenditure side to balance
    bridgeItem = {
      side: "expenditures",
      type: "surplus",
      value: balance,
      sharePct: totalExp > 0 ? (balance / totalExp) * 100 : 0,
    };
  }

  const uncollectibleAdjustment =
    Math.abs(metrics.totals.uncollectibleAdjustment ?? 0) > 0.05
      ? metrics.totals.uncollectibleAdjustment ?? null
      : null;

  return {
    revenues,
    expenditures,
    bridgeItem,
    uncollectibleAdjustment,
    cofogAvailable: record.cofogAvailable,
  };
}

/**
 * Computes era comparison between Year A and Year B.
 */
export function computeEraComparison(
  balances: AnnualBalanceRecord[],
  taxonomy: FiscalTaxonomy,
  yearA: string,
  yearB: string,
  side: "revenues" | "expenditures",
  unit: FiscalUnitId
): EraComparisonResult {
  const missingCofogYears: string[] = [];

  const recA = balances.find((b) => b.year === yearA);
  const recB = balances.find((b) => b.year === yearB);

  if (!recA || !recB || !taxonomy) {
    return { items: [], maxAbsDelta: 0, missingCofogYears };
  }

  if (side === "expenditures") {
    if (!recA.cofogAvailable) missingCofogYears.push(yearA);
    if (!recB.cofogAvailable) missingCofogYears.push(yearB);
  }

  if (missingCofogYears.length > 0) {
    return { items: [], maxAbsDelta: 0, missingCofogYears };
  }

  const metricsA = recA.metrics[unit];
  const metricsB = recB.metrics[unit];
  const dataA = side === "revenues" ? metricsA.revenues : metricsA.expenditures;
  const dataB = side === "revenues" ? metricsB.revenues : metricsB.expenditures;
  const totalA = side === "revenues" ? metricsA.totals.revenue : metricsA.totals.expenditure;
  const totalB = side === "revenues" ? metricsB.totals.revenue : metricsB.totals.expenditure;

  const taxa = taxonomy[side];

  let maxAbsDelta = 0;

  const items: EraComparisonItem[] = taxa.map((taxon) => {
    const valA = dataA?.[taxon.code]?.total ?? 0;
    const valB = dataB?.[taxon.code]?.total ?? 0;
    const delta = valB - valA;
    if (Math.abs(delta) > maxAbsDelta) {
      maxAbsDelta = Math.abs(delta);
    }
    return {
      code: taxon.code,
      label: taxon.label,
      shortLabel: taxon.shortLabel,
      valueA: valA,
      valueB: valB,
      delta,
      sharePctA: totalA > 0 ? (valA / totalA) * 100 : 0,
      sharePctB: totalB > 0 ? (valB / totalB) * 100 : 0,
    };
  });

  items.sort((a, b) => b.delta - a.delta);

  return { items, maxAbsDelta, missingCofogYears };
}

/**
 * Pure deterministic SVG geometry calculator for 3-Column Sankey Diagram.
 */
export function buildSankeyGraph(
  record: AnnualBalanceRecord,
  taxonomy: FiscalTaxonomy,
  unit: FiscalUnitId,
  width: number,
  height: number
): SankeyGraphResult {
  if (!record || !taxonomy || !record.cofogAvailable || !record.metrics[unit].expenditures) {
    return { nodes: [], links: [], cofogAvailable: false };
  }

  const metrics = record.metrics[unit];
  const totalRev = metrics.totals.revenue;
  const totalExp = metrics.totals.expenditure;
  const balance = metrics.totals.balance;

  const nodeWidth = 22;
  const paddingTop = 28;
  const paddingBottom = 28;
  const availableH = height - paddingTop - paddingBottom;
  const nodeGap = 10;

  // Prepare Column 0 (Revenues + Deficit Bridge if B.9 < 0)
  type RawSankeyNode = {
    id: string;
    code: string;
    label: string;
    shortLabel: string;
    value: number;
    sharePct: number;
    color: string;
    column: 0 | 1 | 2;
    subNotes: string[];
    isBridge?: boolean;
  };

  const rawRevNodes: RawSankeyNode[] = taxonomy.revenues
    .map((t) => {
      const val = metrics.revenues?.[t.code]?.total ?? 0;
      const subNotes: string[] = [];
      const entry = metrics.revenues?.[t.code];
      if (entry?.breakdown) {
        Object.entries(entry.breakdown).forEach(([c, v]) => {
          const childMeta = t.children.find((ch) => ch.code === c);
          if (childMeta && v > 0) {
            subNotes.push(`${childMeta.label.split("(")[0].trim()}: ${v}`);
          }
        });
      }
      return {
        id: `node-rev-${t.code}`,
        code: t.code,
        label: t.label,
        shortLabel: t.shortLabel,
        value: val,
        sharePct: totalRev > 0 ? (val / totalRev) * 100 : 0,
        color: getTaxonColor(t.code),
        column: 0 as const,
        subNotes,
      };
    })
    .sort((a, b) => b.value - a.value);

  if (balance < 0) {
    const absDef = Math.abs(balance);
    rawRevNodes.push({
      id: "node-bridge-deficit",
      code: "DEFICIT_BRIDGE",
      label: "Necesidad de financiación (Déficit B.9)",
      shortLabel: "Déficit B.9",
      value: absDef,
      sharePct: totalRev > 0 ? (absDef / totalRev) * 100 : 0,
      color: DEFICIT_BRIDGE,
      column: 0 as const,
      subNotes: ["Financiación mediante endeudamiento neto"],
      isBridge: true,
    });
  }

  const col0Total = rawRevNodes.reduce((sum, n) => sum + n.value, 0);

  // Layout Column 0
  const col0Gaps = (rawRevNodes.length - 1) * nodeGap;
  const col0HeightBudget = availableH - col0Gaps;

  let currentY0 = paddingTop;
  const col0Nodes: SankeyNode[] = rawRevNodes.map((n) => {
    const nodeH = col0Total > 0 ? Math.max(8, (n.value / col0Total) * col0HeightBudget) : 8;
    const node: SankeyNode = {
      ...n,
      x: 0,
      y: currentY0,
      width: nodeWidth,
      height: nodeH,
    };
    currentY0 += nodeH + nodeGap;
    return node;
  });

  // Prepare Column 2 (Expenditures + Surplus Bridge if B.9 > 0)
  const rawExpNodes: RawSankeyNode[] = taxonomy.expenditures
    .map((t) => {
      const val = metrics.expenditures?.[t.code]?.total ?? 0;
      const subNotes: string[] = [];
      const entry = metrics.expenditures?.[t.code];
      if (entry?.highlights) {
        Object.entries(entry.highlights).forEach(([c, v]) => {
          const childMeta = t.highlights.find((ch) => ch.code === c);
          if (childMeta && v > 0) {
            subNotes.push(`${childMeta.label.split("(")[0].trim()}: ${v}`);
          }
        });
      }
      return {
        id: `node-exp-${t.code}`,
        code: t.code,
        label: t.label,
        shortLabel: t.shortLabel,
        value: val,
        sharePct: totalExp > 0 ? (val / totalExp) * 100 : 0,
        color: getTaxonColor(t.code),
        column: 2 as const,
        subNotes,
      };
    })
    .sort((a, b) => b.value - a.value);

  if (balance > 0) {
    rawExpNodes.push({
      id: "node-bridge-surplus",
      code: "SURPLUS_BRIDGE",
      label: "Capacidad de financiación (Superávit B.9)",
      shortLabel: "Superávit B.9",
      value: balance,
      sharePct: totalExp > 0 ? (balance / totalExp) * 100 : 0,
      color: SURPLUS_BRIDGE,
      column: 2 as const,
      subNotes: ["Capacidad neta de desendeudamiento"],
      isBridge: true,
    });
  }

  const col2Total = rawExpNodes.reduce((sum, n) => sum + n.value, 0);

  // Layout Column 2
  const col2Gaps = (rawExpNodes.length - 1) * nodeGap;
  const col2HeightBudget = availableH - col2Gaps;
  const xCol2 = width - nodeWidth;

  let currentY2 = paddingTop;
  const col2Nodes: SankeyNode[] = rawExpNodes.map((n) => {
    const nodeH = col2Total > 0 ? Math.max(8, (n.value / col2Total) * col2HeightBudget) : 8;
    const node: SankeyNode = {
      ...n,
      x: xCol2,
      y: currentY2,
      width: nodeWidth,
      height: nodeH,
    };
    currentY2 += nodeH + nodeGap;
    return node;
  });

  // Column 1 Hub: Central Government S.13
  const hubX = Math.round(width / 2 - nodeWidth / 2);
  const hubNode: SankeyNode = {
    id: "node-hub-s13",
    code: "S13",
    label: "Administraciones Públicas (Consolidado AAPP S.13)",
    shortLabel: "AAPP Consolidado",
    value: Math.max(totalRev, totalExp),
    sharePct: 100,
    x: hubX,
    y: paddingTop,
    width: nodeWidth,
    height: availableH,
    color: "#64748B",
    column: 1,
    subNotes: [
      `Ingresos Totales: ${totalRev}`,
      `Gastos Totales: ${totalExp}`,
      `Saldo Fiscal B.9: ${balance >= 0 ? "+" : ""}${balance}`,
    ],
  };

  const nodes: SankeyNode[] = [...col0Nodes, hubNode, ...col2Nodes];
  const links: SankeyLink[] = [];

  // Left Links: Col 0 -> Col 1 Hub
  let hubLeftY = paddingTop;
  col0Nodes.forEach((srcNode) => {
    const x0 = srcNode.x + srcNode.width;
    const y0 = srcNode.y;
    const h = srcNode.height;
    const x1 = hubNode.x;
    const y1 = hubLeftY;
    const cx = (x0 + x1) / 2;

    const path = `M ${x0} ${y0} C ${cx} ${y0}, ${cx} ${y1}, ${x1} ${y1} L ${x1} ${y1 + h} C ${cx} ${
      y1 + h
    }, ${cx} ${y0 + h}, ${x0} ${y0 + h} Z`;

    links.push({
      id: `link-left-${srcNode.code}`,
      sourceId: srcNode.id,
      targetId: hubNode.id,
      value: srcNode.value,
      color: srcNode.color,
      path,
    });

    hubLeftY += h;
  });

  // Right Links: Col 1 Hub -> Col 2
  let hubRightY = paddingTop;
  col2Nodes.forEach((tgtNode) => {
    const x0 = hubNode.x + hubNode.width;
    const y0 = hubRightY;
    const h = tgtNode.height;
    const x1 = tgtNode.x;
    const y1 = tgtNode.y;
    const cx = (x0 + x1) / 2;

    const path = `M ${x0} ${y0} C ${cx} ${y0}, ${cx} ${y1}, ${x1} ${y1} L ${x1} ${y1 + h} C ${cx} ${
      y1 + h
    }, ${cx} ${y0 + h}, ${x0} ${y0 + h} Z`;

    links.push({
      id: `link-right-${tgtNode.code}`,
      sourceId: hubNode.id,
      targetId: tgtNode.id,
      value: tgtNode.value,
      color: tgtNode.color,
      path,
    });

    hubRightY += h;
  });

  return { nodes, links, cofogAvailable: true };
}

--- FILE: frontend/components/detail/guide/ConceptGuideContext.tsx ---
"use client";

import React, {
  createContext,
  useContext,
  useMemo,
  useState,
  useCallback,
  useRef,
  useEffect,
} from "react";
import type { ConceptGuide, GlossaryTerm } from "@/lib/types";

interface ConceptGuideContextValue {
  guide: ConceptGuide | null;
  getTerm: (code: string) => GlossaryTerm | undefined;
  activeConceptCode: string | null;
  highlightedConceptCode: string | null;
  focusConcept: (code: string) => void;
  clearActiveConcept: () => void;
}

const ConceptGuideContext = createContext<ConceptGuideContextValue>({
  guide: null,
  getTerm: () => undefined,
  activeConceptCode: null,
  highlightedConceptCode: null,
  focusConcept: () => {},
  clearActiveConcept: () => {},
});

export interface ConceptGuideProviderProps {
  guide?: ConceptGuide | null;
  children: React.ReactNode;
}

export function ConceptGuideProvider({ guide = null, children }: ConceptGuideProviderProps) {
  const [activeConceptCode, setActiveConceptCode] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [highlightedConceptCode, setHighlightedConceptCode] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const highlightTimerRef = useRef<number | null>(null);

  const termMap = useMemo(() => {
    const map = new Map<string, GlossaryTerm>();
    if (guide?.terms) {
      for (const term of guide.terms) {
        map.set(term.code, term);
        map.set(term.code.trim().toUpperCase(), term);
        map.set(term.displayCode, term);
        map.set(term.displayCode.trim().toUpperCase(), term);
      }
    }
    return map;
  }, [guide]);

  const getTerm = useCallback(
    (code: string) => {
      if (!code) return undefined;
      return (
        termMap.get(code) ||
        termMap.get(code.trim().toUpperCase()) ||
        termMap.get(code.replace(".", "")) ||
        termMap.get(code.replace(".", "").toUpperCase())
      );
    },
    [termMap]
  );

  const clearActiveConcept = useCallback(() => {
    setActiveConceptCode(null);
    setHighlightedConceptCode(null);
  }, []);

  const focusConcept = useCallback((code: string) => {
    setActiveConceptCode(code);
    setHighlightedConceptCode(code);

    if (highlightTimerRef.current !== null) {
      window.clearTimeout(highlightTimerRef.current);
    }

    highlightTimerRef.current = window.setTimeout(() => {
      setHighlightedConceptCode(null);
      highlightTimerRef.current = null;
    }, 2200);

    requestAnimationFrame(() => {
      const el = document.getElementById(`concept-${code}`);
      if (el) {
        const prefersReducedMotion = window.matchMedia("(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)").matches;
        el.scrollIntoView({
          behavior: prefersReducedMotion ? "auto" : "smooth",
          block: "center",
        });
      }
    });
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    return () => {
      if (highlightTimerRef.current !== null) {
        window.clearTimeout(highlightTimerRef.current);
      }
    };
  }, []);

  const value = useMemo(
    () => ({
      guide,
      getTerm,
      activeConceptCode,
      highlightedConceptCode,
      focusConcept,
      clearActiveConcept,
    }),
    [guide, getTerm, activeConceptCode, highlightedConceptCode, focusConcept, clearActiveConcept]
  );

  return (
    <ConceptGuideContext.Provider value={value}>
      {children}
    </ConceptGuideContext.Provider>
  );
}

export function useConceptGuide() {
  return useContext(ConceptGuideContext);
}

--- FILE: frontend/components/detail/guide/ConceptGuideSection.tsx ---
"use client";

import React, { useState, useMemo, useEffect } from "react";
import {
  BookOpen,
  Search,
  ChevronDown,
  ChevronRight,
  Info,
  ArrowRight,
  Filter,
} from "lucide-react";
import type { Lang, GlossaryTerm } from "@/lib/types";
import { useConceptGuide } from "./ConceptGuideContext";
import { t } from "@/lib/i18n";

interface ConceptGuideSectionProps {
  lang?: Lang;
}

export function ConceptGuideSection({ lang = "es" }: ConceptGuideSectionProps) {
  const { guide, activeConceptCode, highlightedConceptCode, focusConcept } = useConceptGuide();

  const [selectedCategory, setSelectedCategory] = useState<string>("all");
  const [searchQuery, setSearchQuery] = useState<string>("");
  const [expandedCodes, setExpandedCodes] = useState<Record<string, boolean>>({});

  // Sync activeConceptCode from context (e.g., when triggered from a popover or chart)
  useEffect(() => {
    if (activeConceptCode) {
      setExpandedCodes((prev) => ({ ...prev, [activeConceptCode]: true }));
      // Reset filter and search if active concept is hidden
      setSelectedCategory("all");
      setSearchQuery("");
    }
  }, [activeConceptCode]);

  if (!guide) return null;

  const { title, subtitle, quickKeys, categories, terms } = guide;

  // Filter terms by selected category and search query
  const filteredTerms = useMemo(() => {
    return terms.filter((term) => {
      // Category check
      if (selectedCategory !== "all" && term.categoryId !== selectedCategory) {
        return false;
      }
      // Search check
      if (searchQuery.trim()) {
        const q = searchQuery.toLowerCase().trim();
        const codeMatch = term.code.toLowerCase().includes(q);
        const displayMatch = term.displayCode.toLowerCase().includes(q);
        const titleMatch = term.title.toLowerCase().includes(q);
        const shortMatch = term.shortDefinition.toLowerCase().includes(q);
        const expMatch = term.explanation.toLowerCase().includes(q);
        return codeMatch || displayMatch || titleMatch || shortMatch || expMatch;
      }
      return true;
    });
  }, [terms, selectedCategory, searchQuery]);

  const toggleExpand = (code: string) => {
    setExpandedCodes((prev) => ({ ...prev, [code]: !prev[code] }));
  };

  const isAllExpanded =
    filteredTerms.length > 0 &&
    filteredTerms.every((term) => expandedCodes[term.code]);

  const toggleAll = () => {
    if (isAllExpanded) {
      setExpandedCodes({});
    } else {
      const next: Record<string, boolean> = {};
      filteredTerms.forEach((term) => {
        next[term.code] = true;
      });
      setExpandedCodes(next);
    }
  };

  // Term counts per category for filter pills
  const categoryCounts = useMemo(() => {
    const map = new Map<string, number>();
    map.set("all", terms.length);
    for (const term of terms) {
      map.set(term.categoryId, (map.get(term.categoryId) || 0) + 1);
    }
    return map;
  }, [terms]);

  return (
    <section className="bg-slate-900 border border-slate-800 rounded-2xl p-5 sm:p-6 space-y-6 shadow-xl animate-in fade-in duration-200">
      {/* Section Header */}
      <div className="border-b border-slate-800 pb-4 space-y-1">
        <h2 className="text-base sm:text-lg font-bold text-white flex items-center gap-2">
          <BookOpen className="w-5 h-5 text-blue-400 shrink-0" />
          <span>{title}</span>
        </h2>
        <p className="text-xs text-slate-400 leading-relaxed max-w-3xl">
          {subtitle}
        </p>
      </div>

      {/* PART 1: Quick-Reading Keys (Claves) */}
      {quickKeys && quickKeys.length > 0 && (
        <div className="space-y-3">
          <h3 className="text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-slate-300">
            {t("quickKeysHeading", lang)}
          </h3>

          <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 gap-3.5">
            {quickKeys.map((keyCard) => (
              <div
                key={keyCard.id}
                className="bg-slate-950/60 border border-slate-800 rounded-xl p-4 space-y-2 hover:border-slate-700 transition-all"
              >
                <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                  <span className="text-[10px] font-mono font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-blue-300 bg-blue-950/80 border border-blue-800/70 px-2 py-0.5 rounded">
                    {keyCard.badge}
                  </span>
                  <h4 className="text-sm font-bold text-white leading-tight">
                    {keyCard.title}
                  </h4>
                </div>

                <p className="text-xs text-slate-300 leading-relaxed">
                  {keyCard.summary}
                </p>

                {keyCard.example && (
                  <p className="text-[11px] text-slate-400 border-l-2 border-slate-700 pl-2.5 pt-0.5 italic">
                    {keyCard.example}
                  </p>
                )}
              </div>
            ))}
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      {/* PART 2: Interactive Technical Glossary */}
      <div className="space-y-4 pt-2 border-t border-slate-800/80">
        <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row items-stretch sm:items-center justify-between gap-3">
          <h3 className="text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-slate-300">
            {t("glossaryHeading", lang)}
          </h3>

          <button
            onClick={toggleAll}
            className="text-xs font-semibold text-blue-400 hover:text-blue-300 transition-colors self-end sm:self-auto"
          >
            {isAllExpanded ? t("collapseAll", lang) : t("expandAll", lang)}
          </button>
        </div>

        {/* Filter & Search Bar */}
        <div className="flex flex-col md:flex-row items-stretch md:items-center justify-between gap-3">
          {/* Category Filter Pills */}
          <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-1.5">
            <button
              onClick={() => setSelectedCategory("all")}
              className={`px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg text-xs font-semibold transition-all ${
