  const incomeKpi: KpiCardData = {
    titleKey: "metricMedianIncome",
    foreignValue: formatCurrencyEur(medForInc, locale),
    nationalValue: formatCurrencyEur(medNatInc, locale),
    foreignSubtext: locale === "en" ? "Foreign median" : "Extranjeros (mediana)",
    nationalSubtext: locale === "en" ? "Spanish median" : "Españoles (mediana)",
    yoyDelta: incDelta !== null ? formatDelta(incDelta, "€", 0, locale) : null,
    notice: definition === "birthplace"
      ? (locale === "en" ? "EU-SILC published by passport" : "ECV oficial por pasaporte")
      : undefined,
  };

  return { popKpi, workingAgeKpi, employmentKpi, incomeKpi };
}

export function buildRepresentationComparison(
  record: NationalityAnnualRecord | undefined,
  definition: PopulationDefinition = "citizenship"
): RepresentationComparisonRow[] {
  if (!record) return [];
  const slice = record.definitions?.[definition];
  const demoFor = slice?.demographics?.foreigners;
  const demoNat = slice?.demographics?.nationals;
  const demoTot = slice?.demographics?.total;

  const laborFor = slice?.laborMarket?.foreigners;
  const laborTot = slice?.laborMarket?.total;

  const popShare = demoFor?.shareOfTotalPct ?? 0;
  const workShare = demoFor?.shareOfWorkingAgePct ?? 0;
  const empShare = laborFor?.shareOfEmployedPct ?? 0;

  return [
    {
      labelKey: "totalPopulation",
      foreignSharePct: popShare,
      nationalSharePct: demoNat?.shareOfTotalPct ?? (100 - popShare),
      foreignCount: demoFor?.population,
      totalCount: demoTot?.population,
    },
    {
      labelKey: "workingAgeShare",
      foreignSharePct: workShare,
      nationalSharePct: demoNat?.shareOfWorkingAgePct ?? (100 - workShare),
      foreignCount: demoFor?.ageBands.workingAge15to64,
      totalCount: demoTot?.ageBands.workingAge15to64,
    },
    {
      labelKey: "employedShare",
      foreignSharePct: empShare,
      nationalSharePct: laborTot ? Math.max(0, 100 - empShare) : 0,
      foreignCount: laborFor?.employedThousands ? Math.round(laborFor.employedThousands * 1000) : undefined,
      totalCount: laborTot?.employedThousands ? Math.round(laborTot.employedThousands * 1000) : undefined,
    },
  ];
}

export function buildLaborTimeSeries(
  records: NationalityAnnualRecord[],
  definition: PopulationDefinition,
  metric: LaborMetricKey,
  includeEuSplit: boolean = false
): LaborTimeSeriesRow[] {
  if (!Array.isArray(records)) return [];

  return records.map((rec) => {
    const labor = rec.definitions?.[definition]?.laborMarket;
    const row: LaborTimeSeriesRow = {
      year: rec.year,
      nationals: labor?.nationals?.[metric] ?? null,
      foreigners: labor?.foreigners?.[metric] ?? null,
      total: labor?.total?.[metric] ?? null,
    };

    if (includeEuSplit) {
      row.euForeigners = labor?.euForeigners?.[metric] ?? null;
      row.nonEuForeigners = labor?.nonEuForeigners?.[metric] ?? null;
    }

    return row;
  });
}

export function buildNaturalizationsSeries(
  records: NationalityAnnualRecord[]
): NaturalizationPoint[] {
  if (!Array.isArray(records)) return [];

  let cumulative = 0;
  return records.map((rec) => {
    const val = rec.naturalizations;
    if (val !== null && val !== undefined) {
      cumulative += val;
    }
    return {
      year: rec.year,
      naturalizations: val,
      cumulativeSinceStart: cumulative,
    };
  });
}

export function buildLivingConditionsSeries(
  records: NationalityAnnualRecord[],
  metric: LivingConditionsMetricKey,
  includeEuSplit: boolean = false
): LivingConditionsTimeSeriesRow[] {
  if (!Array.isArray(records)) return [];

  return records.map((rec) => {
    const liv = rec.livingConditions;
    const natVal = liv?.nationals?.[metric] ?? null;
    const forVal = liv?.foreigners?.[metric] ?? null;

    let ratio: number | null = null;
    if (natVal !== null && forVal !== null && natVal > 0) {
      ratio = roundTo((forVal / natVal) * 100, 1);
    }

    const row: LivingConditionsTimeSeriesRow = {
      year: rec.year,
      nationals: natVal,
      foreigners: forVal,
      total: liv?.total?.[metric] ?? null,
      ratioPct: ratio,
    };

    if (includeEuSplit) {
      row.euForeigners = liv?.euForeigners?.[metric] ?? null;
      row.nonEuForeigners = liv?.nonEuForeigners?.[metric] ?? null;
    }

    return row;
  });
}

function roundTo(val: number, decimals: number): number {
  const p = Math.pow(10, decimals);
  return Math.round(val * p) / p;
}

--- FILE: frontend/components/directory/DirectoryExplorer.tsx ---
"use client";

import { useState, useMemo } from "react";
import { Search, X, AlertCircle } from "lucide-react";
import type { DirectoryIndexItem, Category, Locale } from "@/lib/types";
import { CATEGORY_LABELS, t } from "@/lib/i18n";
import DirectorySection from "./DirectorySection";

const CATEGORIES: Category[] = [
  "pensions-demography",
  "economy-employment",
  "housing",
  "healthcare",
];

interface DirectoryExplorerProps {
  initialItems: DirectoryIndexItem[];
  locale: Locale;
}

export function DirectoryExplorer({ initialItems, locale }: DirectoryExplorerProps) {
  const [searchQuery, setSearchQuery] = useState("");
  const [selectedCategory, setSelectedCategory] = useState<Category | "all">("all");

  const getCategoryCount = (cat: Category | "all") => {
    if (cat === "all") return initialItems.length;
    return initialItems.filter((i) => i.category === cat).length;
  };

  const sections = useMemo(() => {
    const q = searchQuery.toLowerCase().trim();

    return CATEGORIES.map((cat) => {
      if (selectedCategory !== "all" && selectedCategory !== cat) {
        return { category: cat, items: [] };
      }

      const matching = initialItems.filter((item) => {
        if (item.category !== cat) return false;
        if (!q) return true;

        const question = item.question[locale].toLowerCase();
        const summary = item.searchSummary[locale].toLowerCase();
        const source = item.primarySource[locale].toLowerCase();
        const id = item.id.toLowerCase();

        return (
          question.includes(q) ||
          summary.includes(q) ||
          source.includes(q) ||
          id.includes(q)
        );
      });

      return { category: cat, items: matching };
    }).filter((sec) => sec.items.length > 0);
  }, [initialItems, selectedCategory, searchQuery, locale]);

  const totalFilteredCount = sections.reduce((sum, sec) => sum + sec.items.length, 0);

  return (
    <div className="space-y-6 animate-in fade-in duration-200">
      {/* Top Editorial Tagline */}
      <header className="pt-2 pb-1 text-center sm:text-left">
        <p className="text-sm text-slate-400 max-w-xl">{t("tagline", locale)}</p>
      </header>

      {/* Integrated Search Bar */}
      <div className="relative">
        <div className="absolute inset-y-0 left-0 pl-3.5 flex items-center pointer-events-none text-slate-400">
          <Search className="w-4 h-4" />
        </div>
        <input
          type="text"
          value={searchQuery}
          onChange={(e) => setSearchQuery(e.target.value)}
          placeholder={t("searchPlaceholder", locale)}
          className="w-full pl-10 pr-10 py-3 bg-slate-900 border border-slate-800 rounded-2xl text-sm text-white placeholder-slate-500 focus:outline-hidden focus:border-blue-500 focus:ring-1 focus:ring-blue-500 transition-all shadow-md"
        />
        {searchQuery && (
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => setSearchQuery("")}
            className="absolute inset-y-0 right-0 pr-3.5 flex items-center text-slate-400 hover:text-white transition-colors"
            title={t("clearSearch", locale)}
          >
            <X className="w-4 h-4" />
          </button>
        )}
      </div>

      {/* Interactive Category Filter Bar */}
      <nav className="overflow-x-auto no-scrollbar py-1" aria-label="Category filters">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2 min-w-max">
          {(["all", ...CATEGORIES] as const).map((cat) => {
            const count = getCategoryCount(cat);
            const isActive = selectedCategory === cat;
            const label = CATEGORY_LABELS[cat]?.[locale] ?? cat;

            return (
              <button
                type="button"
                key={cat}
                onClick={() => setSelectedCategory(cat)}
                className={`px-3.5 py-1.5 rounded-full text-xs font-semibold transition-all flex items-center gap-2 border ${
                  isActive
                    ? "bg-blue-600 border-blue-500 text-white shadow-md shadow-blue-900/30"
                    : "bg-slate-900 border-slate-800 text-slate-400 hover:text-slate-200 hover:border-slate-700"
                }`}
              >
                <span>{label}</span>
                <span
                  className={`text-[10px] px-1.5 py-0.2 rounded-full font-mono ${
                    isActive ? "bg-blue-700 text-white" : "bg-slate-800 text-slate-400"
                  }`}
                >
                  {count}
                </span>
              </button>
            );
          })}
        </div>
      </nav>

      {/* Directory Sections or Empty State */}
      {totalFilteredCount === 0 ? (
        <div className="bg-slate-900/50 border border-slate-800 rounded-2xl p-8 text-center space-y-3">
          <AlertCircle className="w-6 h-6 text-amber-400 mx-auto" />
          <p className="text-sm font-semibold text-white">
            {t("noResults", locale)} &ldquo;{searchQuery}&rdquo;
          </p>
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => {
              setSearchQuery("");
              setSelectedCategory("all");
            }}
            className="text-xs text-blue-400 hover:underline font-semibold"
          >
            {t("clearSearch", locale)}
          </button>
        </div>
      ) : (
        <div className="space-y-6">
          {sections.map((sec) => (
            <DirectorySection
              key={sec.category}
              category={sec.category}
              items={sec.items}
              locale={locale}
            />
          ))}
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

export default DirectoryExplorer;

--- FILE: frontend/components/directory/DirectoryRow.tsx ---
import Link from "next/link";
import { ChevronRight } from "lucide-react";
import type { DirectoryIndexItem, Locale } from "@/lib/types";
import { routes } from "@/lib/i18n";
import { formatIndicatorValue } from "@/lib/format";
import Sparkline from "./Sparkline";

interface DirectoryRowProps {
  item: DirectoryIndexItem;
  locale: Locale;
}

export function DirectoryRow({ item, locale }: DirectoryRowProps) {
  const question = item.question[locale];
  const unit = item.unit[locale];
  const primarySource = item.primarySource[locale];
  const href = routes.indicator(locale, item.id);

  const rawCompact = formatIndicatorValue(item.latestValue, item.valueFormat, locale, true);
  const compactVal = unit.startsWith("%") && !rawCompact.endsWith("%") ? `${rawCompact}%` : rawCompact;
  const exactVal = formatIndicatorValue(item.latestValue, item.valueFormat, locale, false);

  return (
    <Link
      href={href}
      className="group flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-center justify-between bg-slate-900/60 hover:bg-slate-900 border border-slate-800/90 hover:border-slate-700 rounded-xl px-4 py-3.5 transition-all cursor-pointer gap-3 sm:gap-4 no-underline"
    >
      {/* Left Column: Question, Source and Time Horizon */}
      <div className="space-y-1 flex-1 min-w-0">
        <h4 className="text-sm font-semibold text-[#F1F5F9] group-hover:text-[#3B82F6] transition-colors leading-snug truncate">
          {question}
        </h4>
        <div className="text-xs text-[#94A3B8] font-medium flex items-center gap-1.5 font-mono">
          <span>{primarySource}</span>
          <span>·</span>
          <span>{item.timeHorizon}</span>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Right Column: Sparkline and Numeric Value */}
      <div className="flex items-center justify-between sm:justify-end gap-4 shrink-0">
        {item.sparkline.length > 1 && (
          <div className="hidden md:block">
            <Sparkline data={item.sparkline} width={90} height={28} color="#3B82F6" />
          </div>
        )}

        <div className="text-left sm:text-right">
          <div
            className="text-base font-bold text-white font-mono tabular-nums tracking-tight"
            title={`${exactVal} ${unit}`}
          >
            {compactVal}
          </div>
          <div className="text-[11px] text-[#64748B] font-medium truncate max-w-[120px]">
            {unit}
          </div>
        </div>

        <div className="w-6 h-6 rounded-full bg-white/[0.04] group-hover:bg-blue-600/20 text-[#64748B] group-hover:text-[#3B82F6] flex items-center justify-center transition-all">
          <ChevronRight className="w-3.5 h-3.5 group-hover:translate-x-0.5 transition-transform" />
        </div>
      </div>
    </Link>
  );
}

export default DirectoryRow;

--- FILE: frontend/components/directory/DirectorySection.tsx ---
"use client";

import { useState } from "react";
import { ChevronDown, ChevronUp } from "lucide-react";
import type { DirectoryIndexItem, Category, Locale } from "@/lib/types";
import { CATEGORY_LABELS, CATEGORY_ICONS, t } from "@/lib/i18n";
import DirectoryRow from "./DirectoryRow";

interface DirectorySectionProps {
  category: Category;
  items: DirectoryIndexItem[];
  locale: Locale;
}

export function DirectorySection({ category, items, locale }: DirectorySectionProps) {
  const [isCollapsed, setIsCollapsed] = useState(false);

  if (items.length === 0) return null;

  const translatedCategory = CATEGORY_LABELS[category]?.[locale] ?? category;
  const countLabel =
    items.length === 1 ? t("indicatorCount", locale) : t("indicatorsCount", locale);
  const icon = CATEGORY_ICONS[category] ?? "📁";

  return (
    <section id={`section-${category}`} className="scroll-mt-24 space-y-3">
      {/* Semantic Single Button Header */}
      <button
        type="button"
        aria-expanded={!isCollapsed}
        onClick={() => setIsCollapsed(!isCollapsed)}
        className="w-full flex items-center justify-between py-3 px-3.5 rounded-xl bg-white/[0.03] border border-white/[0.06] hover:bg-white/[0.05] transition-all text-left cursor-pointer"
      >
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2.5">
          <span className="text-base" aria-hidden="true">
            {icon}
          </span>
          <h3 className="text-xs font-black uppercase tracking-wider text-[#F1F5F9]">
            {translatedCategory}
          </h3>
          <span className="text-[10px] font-bold text-[#64748B] bg-white/[0.06] px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full font-mono">
            {items.length} {countLabel}
          </span>
        </div>

        <span className="text-[#94A3B8] hover:text-white transition-colors">
          {isCollapsed ? <ChevronDown className="w-4 h-4" /> : <ChevronUp className="w-4 h-4" />}
        </span>
      </button>

      {/* Rows Container */}
      {!isCollapsed && (
        <div className="space-y-1.5 pl-0.5">
          {items.map((item) => (
            <DirectoryRow key={item.id} item={item} locale={locale} />
          ))}
        </div>
      )}
    </section>
  );
}

export default DirectorySection;

--- FILE: frontend/components/directory/Sparkline.tsx ---
interface SparklineProps {
  data: number[];
  width?: number;
  height?: number;
  color?: string;
}

export function Sparkline({
  data,
  width = 90,
  height = 28,
  color = "#3B82F6",
}: SparklineProps) {
  if (!data || data.length < 2) return null;

  const min = Math.min(...data);
  const max = Math.max(...data);
  const range = max - min || 1;
  const padding = 2;
  const usableHeight = height - padding * 2;
  const usableWidth = width - padding * 2;

  const points = data
    .map((val, idx) => {
      const x = padding + (idx / (data.length - 1)) * usableWidth;
      const y = height - padding - ((val - min) / range) * usableHeight;
      return `${x.toFixed(1)},${y.toFixed(1)}`;
    })
    .join(" ");

  return (
    <svg
      width={width}
      height={height}
      className="overflow-visible inline-block shrink-0"
      aria-hidden="true"
    >
      <polyline
        fill="none"
        stroke={color}
        strokeWidth={1.75}
        strokeLinecap="round"
        strokeLinejoin="round"
        points={points}
      />
    </svg>
  );
}

export default Sparkline;

--- FILE: frontend/components/fiscal/FiscalAnatomySplit.tsx ---
"use client";

import { useState } from "react";
import { ChevronDown, ChevronRight, Info, Scale } from "lucide-react";
import type { AnnualBalanceRecord, FiscalTaxonomy, FiscalUnitId, Lang } from "@/lib/types";
import {
  buildAnatomyBreakdown,
  getLatestCofogYear,
  formatFiscalValue,
  type AnatomyItem,
} from "./fiscalSelectors";
import { getTaxonColor, DEFICIT_BRIDGE, SURPLUS_BRIDGE } from "./fiscalPalette";
import { t } from "@/lib/i18n";
import ConceptHelpTrigger from "@/components/detail/guide/ConceptHelpTrigger";

interface FiscalAnatomySplitProps {
  record: AnnualBalanceRecord;
  taxonomy: FiscalTaxonomy;
  unit: FiscalUnitId;
  balances: AnnualBalanceRecord[];
  onSelectYear: (year: string) => void;
  lang?: Lang;
}

export function FiscalAnatomySplit({
  record,
  taxonomy,
  unit,
  balances,
  onSelectYear,
  lang = "es",
}: FiscalAnatomySplitProps) {
  const breakdown = buildAnatomyBreakdown(record, taxonomy, unit);
  const otherUnit: FiscalUnitId = unit === "PC_GDP" ? "MIO_EUR" : "PC_GDP";
  const otherMetrics = record.metrics[otherUnit];

  // Default expanded taxa codes: D5 and GF10, GF01
  const [expandedCodes, setExpandedCodes] = useState<Record<string, boolean>>({
    D5: true,
    GF10: true,
    GF01: true,
  });

  const toggleExpand = (code: string) => {
    setExpandedCodes((prev) => ({ ...prev, [code]: !prev[code] }));
  };

  const allTaxaCodes = [
    ...breakdown.revenues.filter((r) => r.children.length > 0).map((r) => r.code),
    ...breakdown.expenditures.filter((e) => e.children.length > 0).map((e) => e.code),
  ];

  const isAllExpanded = allTaxaCodes.every((c) => expandedCodes[c]);

  const toggleAll = () => {
    if (isAllExpanded) {
      setExpandedCodes({});
    } else {
      const next: Record<string, boolean> = {};
      allTaxaCodes.forEach((c) => {
        next[c] = true;
      });
      setExpandedCodes(next);
    }
  };

  const latestCofogYear = getLatestCofogYear(balances);

  const totalRevVal = record.metrics[unit].totals.revenue;
  const otherTotalRevVal = otherMetrics.totals.revenue;

  const totalExpVal = record.metrics[unit].totals.expenditure;
  const otherTotalExpVal = otherMetrics.totals.expenditure;

  return (
    <div className="space-y-4">
      {/* Top Accordion Action Row */}
      <div className="flex items-center justify-end">
        <button
          onClick={toggleAll}
          className="text-xs font-semibold text-blue-400 hover:text-blue-300 transition-colors flex items-center gap-1"
        >
          {isAllExpanded ? t("collapseAll", lang) : t("expandAll", lang)}
        </button>
      </div>

      {/* 2-Column Responsive Layout */}
      <div className="grid grid-cols-1 lg:grid-cols-2 gap-6">
        {/* LEFT COLUMN: REVENUES (Ingresos) */}
        <div className="bg-slate-900 border border-slate-800 rounded-2xl p-4 sm:p-5 space-y-4">
          {/* Column Header */}
          <div className="border-b border-slate-800 pb-3 flex items-center justify-between">
            <div>
              <h4 className="text-sm font-bold text-white uppercase tracking-wider flex items-center gap-2">
                <span className="w-2.5 h-2.5 rounded-full bg-sky-400" />
                <span>Ingresos Públicos (TR)</span>
              </h4>
              <p className="text-xs text-slate-400 font-mono tabular-nums mt-0.5">
                {formatFiscalValue(otherTotalRevVal, otherUnit, lang, { compact: true })}
              </p>
            </div>
            <div className="text-right">
              <span className="text-lg font-bold text-sky-400 font-mono tabular-nums">
                {formatFiscalValue(totalRevVal, unit, lang, { compact: true })}
              </span>
            </div>
          </div>

          {/* Revenues List */}
          <div className="space-y-2.5">
            {breakdown.revenues.map((item) => {
              const isExpanded = !!expandedCodes[item.code];
              const hasChildren = item.children.length > 0;
              const color = getTaxonColor(item.code);

              return (
                <div
                  key={item.code}
                  className="bg-slate-950/60 border border-slate-800/80 rounded-xl p-3 space-y-2 transition-all hover:border-slate-700"
                >
                  {/* Item Main Row */}
                  <div
                    onClick={() => hasChildren && toggleExpand(item.code)}
                    className={`flex items-center justify-between gap-3 ${
                      hasChildren ? "cursor-pointer select-none" : ""
                    }`}
                  >
                    <div className="flex items-center gap-2 min-w-0 flex-1">
                      {hasChildren ? (
                        <button
                          aria-expanded={isExpanded}
                          className="text-slate-400 hover:text-white transition-colors"
                        >
                          {isExpanded ? (
                            <ChevronDown className="w-4 h-4" />
                          ) : (
                            <ChevronRight className="w-4 h-4" />
                          )}
                        </button>
                      ) : (
                        <span className="w-4" />
                      )}

                      <span
                        className="w-2.5 h-2.5 rounded-full shrink-0"
                        style={{ backgroundColor: color }}
                      />

                      <span
                        className="text-xs font-semibold text-slate-200 truncate"
                        title={item.label}
                      >
                        {item.shortLabel}
                      </span>

                      <span className="text-[10px] font-mono text-slate-400 px-1.5 py-0.5 bg-slate-800 rounded">
                        {item.code}
                      </span>
                      <ConceptHelpTrigger code={item.code} lang={lang} />
                    </div>

                    <div className="text-right shrink-0 font-mono tabular-nums text-xs">
                      <span className="font-bold text-white">
                        {formatFiscalValue(item.value, unit, lang, { compact: true })}
                      </span>
                      <span className="text-slate-400 text-[11px] ml-1.5">
                        ({item.sharePct.toFixed(1)}%)
                      </span>
                    </div>
                  </div>

                  {/* Proportional Bar */}
                  <div className="w-full h-1.5 bg-slate-800 rounded-full overflow-hidden">
                    <div
                      className="h-full rounded-full transition-all duration-300"
                      style={{
                        width: `${Math.min(100, Math.max(0, item.sharePct))}%`,
                        backgroundColor: color,
                      }}
                    />
                  </div>

                  {/* Accordion Sub-list for Children */}
                  {hasChildren && isExpanded && (
                    <div className="pl-6 pt-1 space-y-1.5 border-t border-slate-800/60 mt-2">
                      {item.children.map((child) => (
                        <div
                          key={child.code}
                          className="flex items-center justify-between gap-2 text-xs font-mono tabular-nums"
                        >
                          <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-slate-400 min-w-0 flex-1">
                            <span className="text-[10px] text-slate-400 font-semibold shrink-0">
                              [{child.code}]
                            </span>
                            <span className="truncate min-w-0 flex-1">{child.label}</span>
                            <ConceptHelpTrigger code={child.code} lang={lang} />
                          </div>
                          <div className="text-right text-slate-300 shrink-0 text-[11px]">
                            <span>
                              {formatFiscalValue(child.value, unit, lang, { compact: true })}
                            </span>
                            <span className="text-slate-400 ml-1">
                              ({child.shareOfParentPct.toFixed(0)}%)
                            </span>
                          </div>
                        </div>
                      ))}
                    </div>
                  )}
                </div>
              );
            })}

            {/* Accounting Bridge Row: Deficit if B.9 < 0 */}
            {breakdown.bridgeItem && breakdown.bridgeItem.side === "revenues" && (
              <div className="border-2 border-dashed border-rose-500/40 bg-rose-950/20 rounded-xl p-3 flex items-center justify-between text-xs font-mono tabular-nums">
                <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                  <span
                    className="w-2.5 h-2.5 rounded-full shrink-0"
                    style={{ backgroundColor: DEFICIT_BRIDGE }}
                  />
                  <span className="font-bold text-rose-300">
                    + Necesidad de financiación (Déficit B.9)
                  </span>
                </div>
                <div className="text-right">
                  <span className="font-bold text-rose-400">
                    {formatFiscalValue(breakdown.bridgeItem.value, unit, lang, {
                      compact: true,
                    })}
                  </span>
                  <span className="text-rose-400/70 ml-1">
                    ({breakdown.bridgeItem.sharePct.toFixed(1)}%)
                  </span>
                </div>
              </div>
            )}
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* RIGHT COLUMN: EXPENDITURES (Gastos por función COFOG) */}
        <div className="bg-slate-900 border border-slate-800 rounded-2xl p-4 sm:p-5 space-y-4">
          {/* Column Header */}
          <div className="border-b border-slate-800 pb-3 flex items-center justify-between">
            <div>
              <h4 className="text-sm font-bold text-white uppercase tracking-wider flex items-center gap-2">
                <span className="w-2.5 h-2.5 rounded-full bg-indigo-400" />
                <span>Gastos Públicos (COFOG TE)</span>
              </h4>
              <p className="text-xs text-slate-400 font-mono tabular-nums mt-0.5">
                {formatFiscalValue(otherTotalExpVal, otherUnit, lang, { compact: true })}
              </p>
            </div>
            <div className="text-right">
              <span className="text-lg font-bold text-indigo-400 font-mono tabular-nums">
                {formatFiscalValue(totalExpVal, unit, lang, { compact: true })}
              </span>
            </div>
          </div>

          {/* Graceful Handling when cofogAvailable === false */}
          {!breakdown.cofogAvailable ? (
            <div className="bg-slate-950/80 border border-dashed border-amber-500/40 rounded-xl p-6 text-center space-y-4 my-auto">
              <div className="w-10 h-10 rounded-full bg-amber-500/10 border border-amber-500/30 flex items-center justify-center mx-auto text-amber-400">
                <Info className="w-5 h-5" />
              </div>
              <div className="space-y-1">
                <h5 className="text-sm font-bold text-white">
                  {t("cofogNotPublished", lang)}
                </h5>
                <p className="text-xs text-slate-400 max-w-sm mx-auto">
                  Eurostat e IGAE publican los desgloses funcionales COFOG con un año de desfase respecto a la contabilidad nacional trimestral.
                </p>
              </div>

              {latestCofogYear && (
                <button
                  onClick={() => onSelectYear(latestCofogYear)}
                  className="inline-flex items-center gap-2 px-4 py-2 bg-amber-500/20 hover:bg-amber-500/30 text-amber-300 border border-amber-500/40 rounded-xl text-xs font-bold transition-all shadow-md"
                >
                  <Scale className="w-4 h-4" />
                  <span>
                    {t("viewLatestCofogYear", lang)} ({latestCofogYear})
                  </span>
                </button>
              )}
            </div>
          ) : (
            /* Expenditures List */
            <div className="space-y-2.5">
              {breakdown.expenditures.map((item) => {
                const isExpanded = !!expandedCodes[item.code];
                const hasChildren = item.children.length > 0;
                const color = getTaxonColor(item.code);

                return (
                  <div
                    key={item.code}
                    className="bg-slate-950/60 border border-slate-800/80 rounded-xl p-3 space-y-2 transition-all hover:border-slate-700"
                  >
                    {/* Main Row */}
                    <div
                      onClick={() => hasChildren && toggleExpand(item.code)}
                      className={`flex items-center justify-between gap-3 ${
                        hasChildren ? "cursor-pointer select-none" : ""
                      }`}
                    >
                      <div className="flex items-center gap-2 min-w-0 flex-1">
                        {hasChildren ? (
                          <button
                            aria-expanded={isExpanded}
                            className="text-slate-400 hover:text-white transition-colors"
                          >
                            {isExpanded ? (
                              <ChevronDown className="w-4 h-4" />
                            ) : (
                              <ChevronRight className="w-4 h-4" />
                            )}
                          </button>
                        ) : (
                          <span className="w-4" />
                        )}

                        <span
                          className="w-2.5 h-2.5 rounded-full shrink-0"
                          style={{ backgroundColor: color }}
                        />

                        <span
                          className="text-xs font-semibold text-slate-200 truncate"
                          title={item.label}
                        >
                          {item.shortLabel}
                        </span>

                        <span className="text-[10px] font-mono text-slate-400 px-1.5 py-0.5 bg-slate-800 rounded">
                          {item.code}
                        </span>
                        <ConceptHelpTrigger code={item.code} lang={lang} />
                      </div>

                      <div className="text-right shrink-0 font-mono tabular-nums text-xs">
                        <span className="font-bold text-white">
                          {formatFiscalValue(item.value, unit, lang, { compact: true })}
                        </span>
                        <span className="text-slate-400 text-[11px] ml-1.5">
                          ({item.sharePct.toFixed(1)}%)
                        </span>
                      </div>
                    </div>

                    {/* Proportional Bar */}
                    <div className="w-full h-1.5 bg-slate-800 rounded-full overflow-hidden">
                      <div
                        className="h-full rounded-full transition-all duration-300"
                        style={{
                          width: `${Math.min(100, Math.max(0, item.sharePct))}%`,
                          backgroundColor: color,
                        }}
                      />
                    </div>

                    {/* Accordion Sub-list for Highlights */}
                    {hasChildren && isExpanded && (
                      <div className="pl-6 pt-1 space-y-1.5 border-t border-slate-800/60 mt-2">
                        {item.children.map((child) => (
                          <div
                            key={child.code}
                            className="flex items-center justify-between gap-2 text-xs font-mono tabular-nums"
                          >
                            <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-slate-400 min-w-0">
                              <span className="text-[10px] text-slate-400 font-semibold shrink-0">
                                [{child.code}]
                              </span>
                              <span className="truncate">{child.label}</span>
                              <ConceptHelpTrigger code={child.code} lang={lang} />
                            </div>
                            <div className="text-right text-slate-300 shrink-0 text-[11px]">
                              <span>
                                {formatFiscalValue(child.value, unit, lang, {
                                  compact: true,
                                })}
                              </span>
                              <span className="text-slate-400 ml-1">
                                ({child.shareOfParentPct.toFixed(0)}%)
                              </span>
                            </div>
                          </div>
                        ))}
                      </div>
                    )}
                  </div>
                );
              })}

              {/* Accounting Bridge Row: Surplus if B.9 > 0 */}
              {breakdown.bridgeItem && breakdown.bridgeItem.side === "expenditures" && (
                <div className="border-2 border-dashed border-emerald-500/40 bg-emerald-950/20 rounded-xl p-3 flex items-center justify-between text-xs font-mono tabular-nums">
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                    <span
                      className="w-2.5 h-2.5 rounded-full shrink-0"
                      style={{ backgroundColor: SURPLUS_BRIDGE }}
                    />
                    <span className="font-bold text-emerald-300">
                      + Capacidad de financiación (Superávit B.9)
                    </span>
                  </div>
                  <div className="text-right">
                    <span className="font-bold text-emerald-400">
                      {formatFiscalValue(breakdown.bridgeItem.value, unit, lang, {
                        compact: true,
                      })}
                    </span>
                    <span className="text-emerald-400/70 ml-1">
                      ({breakdown.bridgeItem.sharePct.toFixed(1)}%)
                    </span>
                  </div>
                </div>
              )}
            </div>
          )}
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Uncollectible Adjustment Note if present */}
      {breakdown.uncollectibleAdjustment !== null && (
        <div className="text-[11px] text-slate-400 flex items-center gap-2 px-3 py-2 bg-slate-900 border border-slate-800 rounded-xl">
          <Info className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-slate-400 shrink-0" />
          <span>
            Ajuste de recaudación incierta (SEC 2010): D.995 / D.91 ajuste por impuestos no cobrados ={" "}
            {formatFiscalValue(breakdown.uncollectibleAdjustment, unit, lang, { signed: true })}.
          </span>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

export default FiscalAnatomySplit;

--- FILE: frontend/components/fiscal/FiscalBalanceView.tsx ---
"use client";

import { useState } from "react";
import type { FiscalBalanceItem, FiscalUnitId, Lang } from "@/lib/types";
import { getAvailableYears } from "./fiscalSelectors";
import FiscalControlBar from "./FiscalControlBar";
import FiscalMacroChart from "./FiscalMacroChart";
import FiscalAnatomySplit from "./FiscalAnatomySplit";
import FiscalSankeyDiagram from "./FiscalSankeyDiagram";
import FiscalEraComparison from "./FiscalEraComparison";
import { t } from "@/lib/i18n";

interface FiscalBalanceViewProps {
  item: FiscalBalanceItem;
  lang?: Lang;
  locale?: Lang;
}

type AnatomyTab = "split" | "sankey" | "compare";

export function FiscalBalanceView({
  item,
  lang,
  locale = "es",
}: FiscalBalanceViewProps) {
  const activeLang = lang || locale || "es";
  const { annualBalances, taxonomy } = item;

  const years = getAvailableYears(annualBalances);
  const defaultYear = years.length > 0 ? years[years.length - 1] : "2025";

  const [unit, setUnit] = useState<FiscalUnitId>("PC_GDP");
  const [selectedYear, setSelectedYear] = useState<string>(defaultYear);
  const [anatomyTab, setAnatomyTab] = useState<AnatomyTab>("split");

  const currentRecord =
    annualBalances.find((b) => b.year === selectedYear) ||
    annualBalances[annualBalances.length - 1];

  if (!currentRecord) return null;

  return (
    <div className="space-y-6 animate-in fade-in duration-200">
      {/* Layer 0: Control Bar & KPI Cards */}
      <FiscalControlBar
        balances={annualBalances}
        selectedYear={selectedYear}
        onSelectYear={setSelectedYear}
        unit={unit}
        onSelectUnit={setUnit}
        lang={activeLang}
      />

      {/* Layer 1: Macro Historical Explorer (1995-Present) */}
      <FiscalMacroChart
        balances={annualBalances}
        taxonomy={taxonomy}
        unit={unit}
        selectedYear={selectedYear}
        onSelectYear={setSelectedYear}
        lang={activeLang}
      />

      {/* Layer 2: Anatomy & Structural Subviews */}
      <div className="space-y-4">
        {/* Section Header & Tab Switcher */}
        <div className="bg-slate-900 border border-slate-800 rounded-2xl p-4 sm:p-5 flex flex-col sm:flex-row items-stretch sm:items-center justify-between gap-3 shadow-xl">
          <div>
            <h3 className="text-base font-bold text-white flex items-center gap-2">
              <span>{t("budgetAnatomy", activeLang)}</span>
              <span className="text-xs font-mono font-bold text-blue-400 bg-blue-950 border border-blue-800/80 px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full">
                {selectedYear}
              </span>
            </h3>
            <p className="text-xs text-slate-400 mt-0.5">
              Inspecciona la estructura detallada de ingresos tributarios y gastos por función social.
            </p>
          </div>

          {/* Tab Switcher */}
          <div className="inline-flex p-1 bg-slate-950 border border-slate-800 rounded-xl text-xs font-semibold shrink-0">
            <button
              onClick={() => setAnatomyTab("split")}
              className={`px-3.5 py-1.5 rounded-lg transition-all ${
                anatomyTab === "split"
                  ? "bg-blue-600 text-white shadow-xs"
                  : "text-slate-400 hover:text-white"
              }`}
            >
              {t("tabBreakdown", activeLang)}
            </button>
            <button
              onClick={() => setAnatomyTab("sankey")}
              className={`px-3.5 py-1.5 rounded-lg transition-all ${
                anatomyTab === "sankey"
                  ? "bg-blue-600 text-white shadow-xs"
                  : "text-slate-400 hover:text-white"
              }`}
            >
              {t("tabFlow", activeLang)}
            </button>
            <button
              onClick={() => setAnatomyTab("compare")}
              className={`px-3.5 py-1.5 rounded-lg transition-all ${
                anatomyTab === "compare"
                  ? "bg-blue-600 text-white shadow-xs"
                  : "text-slate-400 hover:text-white"
              }`}
            >
              {t("tabCompare", activeLang)}
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Selected Layer 2 Subview */}
        {anatomyTab === "split" && (
          <FiscalAnatomySplit
            record={currentRecord}
            taxonomy={taxonomy}
            unit={unit}
            balances={annualBalances}
            onSelectYear={setSelectedYear}
            lang={activeLang}
          />
        )}

        {anatomyTab === "sankey" && (
          <FiscalSankeyDiagram
            record={currentRecord}
            taxonomy={taxonomy}
            unit={unit}
            balances={annualBalances}
            onSelectYear={setSelectedYear}
            onSwitchToSplit={() => setAnatomyTab("split")}
            lang={activeLang}
          />
        )}

        {anatomyTab === "compare" && (
          <FiscalEraComparison
            balances={annualBalances}
            taxonomy={taxonomy}
            unit={unit}
            lang={activeLang}
          />
        )}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

export default FiscalBalanceView;

--- FILE: frontend/components/fiscal/FiscalControlBar.tsx ---
"use client";

import { ChevronLeft, ChevronRight, Scale, TrendingUp, TrendingDown } from "lucide-react";
import type { AnnualBalanceRecord, FiscalUnitId, Lang } from "@/lib/types";
import {
  getAvailableYears,
  getYearMetrics,
  formatFiscalValue,
  formatFiscalDelta,
} from "./fiscalSelectors";
import { t } from "@/lib/i18n";
import ConceptHelpTrigger from "@/components/detail/guide/ConceptHelpTrigger";

interface FiscalControlBarProps {
  balances: AnnualBalanceRecord[];
  selectedYear: string;
  onSelectYear: (year: string) => void;
  unit: FiscalUnitId;
  onSelectUnit: (unit: FiscalUnitId) => void;
  lang?: Lang;
}

export function FiscalControlBar({
  balances,
  selectedYear,
  onSelectYear,
  unit,
  onSelectUnit,
  lang = "es",
}: FiscalControlBarProps) {
  const years = getAvailableYears(balances);
  const currentIdx = years.indexOf(selectedYear);

  const yearMetrics = getYearMetrics(balances, selectedYear, unit);
  const otherUnit: FiscalUnitId = unit === "PC_GDP" ? "MIO_EUR" : "PC_GDP";
  const otherYearMetrics = getYearMetrics(balances, selectedYear, otherUnit);

  if (!yearMetrics) return null;

  const { record, metrics, prevMetrics } = yearMetrics;
  const otherMetrics = otherYearMetrics?.metrics;
  const otherPrevMetrics = otherYearMetrics?.prevMetrics;

  const canPrev = currentIdx > 0;
  const canNext = currentIdx !== -1 && currentIdx < years.length - 1;

  const handlePrev = () => {
    if (canPrev) onSelectYear(years[currentIdx - 1]);
  };

  const handleNext = () => {
    if (canNext) onSelectYear(years[currentIdx + 1]);
  };

  // Revenues KPI calculations
  const revVal = metrics.totals.revenue;
  const otherRevVal = otherMetrics?.totals.revenue ?? 0;
  const revYoY = prevMetrics ? revVal - prevMetrics.totals.revenue : null;

  // Expenditures KPI calculations
  const expVal = metrics.totals.expenditure;
  const otherExpVal = otherMetrics?.totals.expenditure ?? 0;
  const expYoY = prevMetrics ? expVal - prevMetrics.totals.expenditure : null;

  // Balance KPI calculations
  const balVal = metrics.totals.balance;
  const otherBalVal = otherMetrics?.totals.balance ?? 0;
  const balYoY = prevMetrics ? balVal - prevMetrics.totals.balance : null;

  const isSurplus = balVal >= 0;

  return (
    <div className="bg-slate-900 border border-slate-800 rounded-2xl p-4 sm:p-5 shadow-xl space-y-4">
      {/* Top Controls Row */}
      <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row items-stretch sm:items-center justify-between gap-3">
        {/* Unit Switcher */}
        <div
          role="group"
          aria-label="Selección de unidad fiscal"
          className="inline-flex items-center p-1 bg-slate-950 border border-slate-800 rounded-xl"
        >
          <button
            onClick={() => onSelectUnit("PC_GDP")}
            className={`px-3.5 py-1.5 rounded-lg text-xs font-semibold transition-all inline-flex items-center gap-1 ${
              unit === "PC_GDP"
                ? "bg-blue-600 text-white shadow-sm"
                : "text-slate-400 hover:text-slate-200"
            }`}
          >
            <span>{t("unitPcGdp", lang)}</span>
          </button>
          <button
            onClick={() => onSelectUnit("MIO_EUR")}
            className={`px-3.5 py-1.5 rounded-lg text-xs font-semibold transition-all ${
              unit === "MIO_EUR"
                ? "bg-blue-600 text-white shadow-sm"
                : "text-slate-400 hover:text-slate-200"
            }`}
          >
            {t("unitMioEur", lang)}
          </button>
          <ConceptHelpTrigger code="PC_GDP" lang={lang} className="ml-1.5" />
          <ConceptHelpTrigger code="S13" lang={lang} className="ml-1" />
        </div>

        {/* Year Stepper */}
        <div className="flex items-center justify-center gap-2 bg-slate-950 border border-slate-800 rounded-xl px-2 py-1">
          <button
            onClick={handlePrev}
            disabled={!canPrev}
            aria-label={t("previousYear", lang)}
            className="p-1.5 rounded-lg text-slate-400 hover:text-white disabled:opacity-30 disabled:cursor-not-allowed hover:bg-slate-800 transition-colors"
          >
            <ChevronLeft className="w-4 h-4" />
          </button>

          <select
            value={selectedYear}
            onChange={(e) => onSelectYear(e.target.value)}
            className="bg-transparent text-white font-mono font-bold text-sm py-1 px-2 focus:outline-none cursor-pointer"
          >
            {years.map((y) => {
              const rec = balances.find((b) => b.year === y);
              const hasCofog = rec?.cofogAvailable ?? true;
              return (
                <option key={y} value={y} className="bg-slate-900 text-white">
                  {y} {!hasCofog ? `(${lang === "es" ? "sin COFOG" : "no COFOG"})` : ""}
                </option>
              );
            })}
          </select>

          <button
            onClick={handleNext}
            disabled={!canNext}
            aria-label={t("nextYear", lang)}
            className="p-1.5 rounded-lg text-slate-400 hover:text-white disabled:opacity-30 disabled:cursor-not-allowed hover:bg-slate-800 transition-colors"
          >
            <ChevronRight className="w-4 h-4" />
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* 3-Column Macro KPI Strip */}
      <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-3 gap-3">
        {/* KPI 1: Revenues */}
        <div className="bg-slate-950/70 border border-slate-800/80 rounded-xl p-3.5 space-y-1">
          <div className="text-[11px] font-semibold tracking-wider text-slate-400 uppercase flex items-center justify-between">
            <span>{t("totalRevenue", lang)}</span>
            <ConceptHelpTrigger code="TR" lang={lang} />
          </div>
          <div className="text-xl sm:text-2xl font-bold text-sky-400 font-mono tabular-nums">
            {formatFiscalValue(revVal, unit, lang, { compact: true })}
          </div>
          <div className="text-xs text-slate-400 flex items-center justify-between font-mono tabular-nums pt-0.5 border-t border-slate-800/60">
            <span>{formatFiscalValue(otherRevVal, otherUnit, lang, { compact: true })}</span>
            {revYoY !== null && (
              <span className="text-slate-300">
                {formatFiscalDelta(revYoY, unit, lang)} {t("yoyChange", lang)}
              </span>
            )}
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* KPI 2: Expenditures */}
        <div className="bg-slate-950/70 border border-slate-800/80 rounded-xl p-3.5 space-y-1">
          <div className="text-[11px] font-semibold tracking-wider text-slate-400 uppercase flex items-center justify-between">
            <span>{t("totalExpenditure", lang)}</span>
            <ConceptHelpTrigger code="TE" lang={lang} />
          </div>
          <div className="text-xl sm:text-2xl font-bold text-indigo-400 font-mono tabular-nums">
            {formatFiscalValue(expVal, unit, lang, { compact: true })}
          </div>
          <div className="text-xs text-slate-400 flex items-center justify-between font-mono tabular-nums pt-0.5 border-t border-slate-800/60">
            <span>{formatFiscalValue(otherExpVal, otherUnit, lang, { compact: true })}</span>
            {expYoY !== null && (
              <span className="text-slate-300">
                {formatFiscalDelta(expYoY, unit, lang)} {t("yoyChange", lang)}
              </span>
            )}
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* KPI 3: Fiscal Balance */}
        <div
          className={`bg-slate-950/70 border rounded-xl p-3.5 space-y-1 ${
            isSurplus ? "border-emerald-500/30" : "border-rose-500/30"
          }`}
        >
          <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
            <span className="text-[11px] font-semibold tracking-wider text-slate-400 uppercase flex items-center gap-1">
              <span>{t("fiscalBalance", lang)}</span>
              <ConceptHelpTrigger code="B9" lang={lang} />
            </span>
            <span
              className={`inline-flex items-center gap-1 px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full text-[10px] font-bold ${
                isSurplus
                  ? "bg-emerald-950 text-emerald-400 border border-emerald-800/80"
                  : "bg-rose-950 text-rose-400 border border-rose-800/80"
              }`}
            >
              {isSurplus ? <TrendingUp className="w-3 h-3" /> : <TrendingDown className="w-3 h-3" />}
              {isSurplus ? t("surplus", lang) : t("deficit", lang)}
            </span>
          </div>
          <div
            className={`text-xl sm:text-2xl font-bold font-mono tabular-nums ${
              isSurplus ? "text-emerald-400" : "text-rose-400"
            }`}
          >
            {formatFiscalValue(balVal, unit, lang, { signed: true, compact: true })}
          </div>
          <div className="text-xs text-slate-400 flex items-center justify-between font-mono tabular-nums pt-0.5 border-t border-slate-800/60">
            <span>
              {formatFiscalValue(otherBalVal, otherUnit, lang, { signed: true, compact: true })}
            </span>
            {balYoY !== null && (
              <span className="text-slate-300">
                {formatFiscalDelta(balYoY, unit, lang)} {t("yoyChange", lang)}
              </span>
            )}
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

export default FiscalControlBar;

--- FILE: frontend/components/fiscal/FiscalEraComparison.tsx ---
"use client";

import { useState } from "react";
import { Info, ArrowRight } from "lucide-react";
import type { AnnualBalanceRecord, FiscalTaxonomy, FiscalUnitId, Lang } from "@/lib/types";
import {
  computeEraComparison,
  getAvailableYears,
  getLatestCofogYear,
  formatFiscalValue,
  formatFiscalDelta,
} from "./fiscalSelectors";
import { getTaxonColor } from "./fiscalPalette";
import { t } from "@/lib/i18n";

interface FiscalEraComparisonProps {
  balances: AnnualBalanceRecord[];
  taxonomy: FiscalTaxonomy;
  unit: FiscalUnitId;
  lang?: Lang;
}

type Side = "expenditures" | "revenues";

export function FiscalEraComparison({
  balances,
  taxonomy,
  unit,
  lang = "es",
}: FiscalEraComparisonProps) {
  const years = getAvailableYears(balances);
  const latestCofog = getLatestCofogYear(balances) ?? years[years.length - 1];

  const defaultYearA = years.includes("2007") ? "2007" : years[0];
  const defaultYearB = latestCofog;

  const [yearA, setYearA] = useState<string>(defaultYearA);
  const [yearB, setYearB] = useState<string>(defaultYearB);
  const [side, setSide] = useState<Side>("expenditures");

  const era = computeEraComparison(balances, taxonomy, yearA, yearB, side, unit);

  return (
    <div className="bg-slate-900 border border-slate-800 rounded-2xl p-4 sm:p-6 space-y-6">
      {/* Controls Header */}
      <div className="flex flex-col md:flex-row items-stretch md:items-center justify-between gap-4 border-b border-slate-800 pb-4">
        {/* Presets & Year Dropdowns */}
        <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-3">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2 bg-slate-950 border border-slate-800 rounded-xl px-3 py-1 text-xs">
            <span className="text-slate-400 font-semibold">{t("compareFrom", lang)}:</span>
            <select
              value={yearA}
              onChange={(e) => setYearA(e.target.value)}
              className="bg-transparent text-white font-mono font-bold focus:outline-none cursor-pointer"
            >
              {years.map((y) => (
                <option key={y} value={y} className="bg-slate-900 text-white">
                  {y}
                </option>
              ))}
            </select>
          </div>

          <ArrowRight className="w-4 h-4 text-slate-500 shrink-0 hidden sm:block" />

          <div className="flex items-center gap-2 bg-slate-950 border border-slate-800 rounded-xl px-3 py-1 text-xs">
            <span className="text-slate-400 font-semibold">{t("compareTo", lang)}:</span>
            <select
              value={yearB}
              onChange={(e) => setYearB(e.target.value)}
              className="bg-transparent text-white font-mono font-bold focus:outline-none cursor-pointer"
            >
              {years.map((y) => (
                <option key={y} value={y} className="bg-slate-900 text-white">
                  {y}
                </option>
              ))}
            </select>
          </div>

          {/* Neutral Presets */}
          <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-xs font-semibold">
            <span className="text-slate-400 text-[11px] uppercase mr-1">Presets:</span>
            {years.includes("1995") && (
              <button
                onClick={() => {
                  setYearA("1995");
                  setYearB(latestCofog);
                }}
                className={`px-2.5 py-1 rounded-lg border transition-all ${
                  yearA === "1995" && yearB === latestCofog
                    ? "bg-blue-600 text-white border-blue-500"
                    : "bg-slate-950 text-slate-300 border-slate-800 hover:text-white"
                }`}
              >
                1995 → {latestCofog}
              </button>
            )}
            {years.includes("2007") && (
              <button
                onClick={() => {
                  setYearA("2007");
                  setYearB(latestCofog);
                }}
                className={`px-2.5 py-1 rounded-lg border transition-all ${
                  yearA === "2007" && yearB === latestCofog
                    ? "bg-blue-600 text-white border-blue-500"
                    : "bg-slate-950 text-slate-300 border-slate-800 hover:text-white"
                }`}
              >
                2007 → {latestCofog}
              </button>
            )}
            {years.includes("2019") && (
              <button
                onClick={() => {
                  setYearA("2019");
                  setYearB(latestCofog);
                }}
                className={`px-2.5 py-1 rounded-lg border transition-all ${
                  yearA === "2019" && yearB === latestCofog
                    ? "bg-blue-600 text-white border-blue-500"
                    : "bg-slate-950 text-slate-300 border-slate-800 hover:text-white"
                }`}
              >
                2019 → {latestCofog}
              </button>
            )}
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Side Toggle: Expenditures vs Revenues */}
        <div className="inline-flex p-1 bg-slate-950 border border-slate-800 rounded-xl text-xs font-semibold shrink-0">
          <button
            onClick={() => setSide("expenditures")}
            className={`px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg transition-all ${
              side === "expenditures"
                ? "bg-indigo-600 text-white shadow-xs"
                : "text-slate-400 hover:text-white"
            }`}
          >
            Gastos (COFOG)
          </button>
          <button
            onClick={() => setSide("revenues")}
            className={`px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg transition-all ${
              side === "revenues"
                ? "bg-sky-600 text-white shadow-xs"
                : "text-slate-400 hover:text-white"
            }`}
          >
            Ingresos
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Inflation warning notice for MIO_EUR */}
      {unit === "MIO_EUR" && (
        <div className="bg-slate-950 border border-amber-500/30 rounded-xl p-3 flex items-center gap-2 text-xs text-amber-300">
          <Info className="w-4 h-4 text-amber-400 shrink-0" />
          <span>{t("nominalEurWarning", lang)}</span>
        </div>
      )}

      {/* Missing COFOG years warning */}
      {era.missingCofogYears.length > 0 ? (
        <div className="bg-slate-950 border border-slate-800 rounded-xl p-8 text-center space-y-2">
          <Info className="w-6 h-6 text-amber-400 mx-auto" />
          <h5 className="text-sm font-bold text-white">
            {t("cofogNotPublished", lang)}
          </h5>
          <p className="text-xs text-slate-400">
            No se puede realizar la comparativa de gastos COFOG porque no hay datos disponibles para el año {era.missingCofogYears.join(", ")}.
          </p>
        </div>
      ) : (
        /* Diverging Horizontal Bar Chart */
        <div className="space-y-3">
          <div className="flex items-center justify-between text-xs font-semibold text-slate-400 border-b border-slate-800 pb-2">
            <span>Partida</span>
            <div className="flex items-center gap-12 font-mono">
              <span>{yearA} → {yearB}</span>
              <span className="w-24 text-right">Variación Estr. ({unit === "PC_GDP" ? "pp PIB" : "M€"})</span>
            </div>
          </div>

          <div className="space-y-2">
            {era.items.map((item) => {
              const color = getTaxonColor(item.code);
              const maxScale = era.maxAbsDelta > 0 ? era.maxAbsDelta : 1;
              const barWidthPct = Math.min(100, (Math.abs(item.delta) / maxScale) * 100);
              const isPositive = item.delta >= 0;

              return (
                <div
                  key={item.code}
                  className="bg-slate-950/70 border border-slate-800/80 rounded-xl p-3 space-y-2 hover:border-slate-700 transition-all"
                >
                  <div className="flex items-center justify-between text-xs min-w-0">
                    <div className="flex items-center gap-2 min-w-0 flex-1">
                      <span
                        className="w-2.5 h-2.5 rounded-full shrink-0"
                        style={{ backgroundColor: color }}
                      />
                      <span className="font-semibold text-slate-200 truncate">
                        {item.shortLabel}
                      </span>
                      <span className="text-[10px] font-mono text-slate-400 bg-slate-800 px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded">
                        {item.code}
                      </span>
                    </div>

                    <div className="flex items-center gap-6 font-mono tabular-nums text-xs shrink-0">
                      <span className="text-slate-400">
                        {formatFiscalValue(item.valueA, unit, lang, { compact: true })} →{" "}
                        <strong className="text-slate-200">
                          {formatFiscalValue(item.valueB, unit, lang, { compact: true })}
                        </strong>
                      </span>

                      <span
                        className={`font-bold w-24 text-right ${
                          isPositive ? "text-sky-400" : "text-amber-400"
                        }`}
                      >
                        {formatFiscalDelta(item.delta, unit, lang)}
                      </span>
                    </div>
                  </div>

                  {/* Diverging Bar Track */}
                  <div className="relative w-full h-2 bg-slate-800/80 rounded-full overflow-hidden flex items-center">
                    <div className="absolute left-1/2 top-0 bottom-0 w-0.5 bg-slate-600 z-10" />
                    <div className="w-full flex h-full">
                      {/* Left Side (Negative Deltas) */}
                      <div className="w-1/2 flex justify-end">
                        {!isPositive && (
                          <div
                            className="h-full bg-amber-500 rounded-l-full transition-all duration-300"
                            style={{ width: `${barWidthPct}%` }}
                          />
                        )}
                      </div>
                      {/* Right Side (Positive Deltas) */}
                      <div className="w-1/2 flex justify-start">
                        {isPositive && (
                          <div
                            className="h-full bg-sky-400 rounded-r-full transition-all duration-300"
                            style={{ width: `${barWidthPct}%` }}
                          />
                        )}
                      </div>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                </div>
              );
            })}
          </div>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

export default FiscalEraComparison;

--- FILE: frontend/components/fiscal/FiscalMacroChart.tsx ---
"use client";

import { useState, useMemo } from "react";
import {
  ResponsiveContainer,
  ComposedChart,
  Line,
  BarChart,
  Bar,
  AreaChart,
  Area,
  XAxis,
  YAxis,
  Tooltip,
  CartesianGrid,
  ReferenceLine,
  Cell,
  type TooltipProps,
} from "recharts";
import type { AnnualBalanceRecord, FiscalTaxonomy, FiscalUnitId, Lang } from "@/lib/types";
import {
  buildMacroTimeSeries,
  buildCompositionSeries,
  formatFiscalValue,
  getLatestCofogYear,
} from "./fiscalSelectors";
import {
  REVENUE_COLOR,
  EXPENDITURE_COLOR,
  SURPLUS_COLOR,
  DEFICIT_COLOR,
  getTaxonColor,
} from "./fiscalPalette";
import { t } from "@/lib/i18n";
import ConceptHelpTrigger from "@/components/detail/guide/ConceptHelpTrigger";

interface FiscalMacroChartProps {
  balances: AnnualBalanceRecord[];
  taxonomy: FiscalTaxonomy;
  unit: FiscalUnitId;
  selectedYear: string;
  onSelectYear: (year: string) => void;
  lang?: Lang;
}

type MacroTab = "gap" | "rev-comp" | "exp-comp";
type ScaleMode = "unit" | "percent";

function MacroTooltip({
  active,
  payload,
  label,
  unit,
  balances,
  lang = "es",
}: TooltipProps<number, string> & {
  unit: FiscalUnitId;
  balances: AnnualBalanceRecord[];
  lang?: Lang;
}) {
  if (!active || !payload || !payload.length || !label) return null;

  const yearStr = String(label);
  const rec = balances.find((b) => b.year === yearStr);
  const m = rec?.metrics?.[unit];

  if (!m) return null;

  const rev = m.totals.revenue;
  const exp = m.totals.expenditure;
  const bal = m.totals.balance;
  const isSurplus = bal >= 0;

  return (
    <div className="bg-slate-950 border border-slate-700 p-3 rounded-xl shadow-2xl text-xs space-y-2 max-w-xs font-sans">
      <div className="font-bold text-white border-b border-slate-800 pb-1 flex items-center justify-between">
        <span>
          {t("yearLabel", lang)} {yearStr}
        </span>
        <span className="text-slate-400 font-mono font-normal">
          {unit === "PC_GDP" ? t("unitPcGdp", lang) : t("unitMioEur", lang)}
        </span>
      </div>

      <div className="space-y-1 font-mono tabular-nums text-[11px]">
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-4">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-sky-400">
            <span className="w-2 h-2 rounded-full bg-sky-400" />
            <span>Ingresos (TR):</span>
          </div>
          <span className="font-bold text-white">
            {formatFiscalValue(rev, unit, lang, { compact: true })}
          </span>
        </div>

        <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-4">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-indigo-400">
            <span className="w-2 h-2 rounded-full bg-indigo-400" />
            <span>Gastos (TE):</span>
          </div>
          <span className="font-bold text-white">
            {formatFiscalValue(exp, unit, lang, { compact: true })}
          </span>
        </div>

        <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-4 pt-1 border-t border-slate-800">
          <div
            className={`flex items-center gap-1.5 ${
              isSurplus ? "text-emerald-400" : "text-rose-400"
            }`}
          >
            <span
              className={`w-2 h-2 rounded-full ${
                isSurplus ? "bg-emerald-400" : "bg-rose-400"
              }`}
            />
            <span>Saldo (B.9):</span>
          </div>
          <span
            className={`font-bold ${
              isSurplus ? "text-emerald-400" : "text-rose-400"
            }`}
          >
            {formatFiscalValue(bal, unit, lang, { signed: true, compact: true })}
          </span>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

export function FiscalMacroChart({
  balances,
  taxonomy,
  unit,
  selectedYear,
  onSelectYear,
  lang = "es",
}: FiscalMacroChartProps) {
  const [activeTab, setActiveTab] = useState<MacroTab>("gap");
  const [scaleMode, setScaleMode] = useState<ScaleMode>("unit");
  const [isolatedTaxon, setIsolatedTaxon] = useState<string | null>(null);

  const macroData = useMemo(
    () => buildMacroTimeSeries(balances, unit),
    [balances, unit]
  );

  const compData = useMemo(() => {
    if (activeTab === "gap") return [];
    const side = activeTab === "rev-comp" ? "revenues" : "expenditures";
    return buildCompositionSeries(balances, taxonomy, side, unit, scaleMode);
  }, [balances, taxonomy, activeTab, unit, scaleMode]);

  const activeSideTaxa = useMemo(() => {
    if (activeTab === "gap") return [];
    return activeTab === "rev-comp" ? taxonomy.revenues : taxonomy.expenditures;
  }, [taxonomy, activeTab]);

  const handleChartClick = (e: any) => {
    if (e && e.activeLabel) {
      onSelectYear(String(e.activeLabel));
    }
  };

  const latestRec = balances[balances.length - 1];

  return (
    <div className="bg-slate-900 border border-slate-800 rounded-2xl p-4 sm:p-6 shadow-xl space-y-4">
      {/* Header & Controls */}
      <div className="flex flex-col md:flex-row items-stretch md:items-center justify-between gap-3 border-b border-slate-800/80 pb-4">
        <div>
          <h3 className="text-base font-bold text-white flex items-center gap-2">
            <span>{t("historicalEvolution", lang)}</span>
            <ConceptHelpTrigger code="SEC2010" lang={lang} />
          </h3>
          <p className="text-xs text-slate-400 mt-0.5 flex items-center gap-1.5">
            <span>{t("clickYearHint", lang)}</span>
            {activeTab === "exp-comp" && <ConceptHelpTrigger code="COFOG" lang={lang} />}
          </p>
        </div>

        <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
          {/* Main Mode Tabs */}
          <div className="inline-flex p-1 bg-slate-950 border border-slate-800 rounded-xl text-xs font-semibold">
            <button
              onClick={() => {
                setActiveTab("gap");
                setIsolatedTaxon(null);
              }}
              className={`px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg transition-all ${
                activeTab === "gap"
                  ? "bg-blue-600 text-white shadow-xs"
                  : "text-slate-400 hover:text-white"
              }`}
            >
              {t("revenueVsExpenditure", lang)}
            </button>
            <button
              onClick={() => {
                setActiveTab("rev-comp");
                setIsolatedTaxon(null);
              }}
              className={`px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg transition-all ${
                activeTab === "rev-comp"
                  ? "bg-blue-600 text-white shadow-xs"
                  : "text-slate-400 hover:text-white"
              }`}
            >
              {t("revenueComposition", lang)}
            </button>
            <button
              onClick={() => {
                setActiveTab("exp-comp");
                setIsolatedTaxon(null);
              }}
              className={`px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg transition-all ${
                activeTab === "exp-comp"
                  ? "bg-blue-600 text-white shadow-xs"
                  : "text-slate-400 hover:text-white"
              }`}
            >
              {t("expenditureComposition", lang)}
            </button>
          </div>

          {/* Scale Toggle for Composition modes */}
          {activeTab !== "gap" && (
            <div className="inline-flex p-1 bg-slate-950 border border-slate-800 rounded-xl text-xs font-semibold">
              <button
                onClick={() => setScaleMode("unit")}
                className={`px-2.5 py-1 rounded-lg transition-all ${
                  scaleMode === "unit"
                    ? "bg-slate-700 text-white"
                    : "text-slate-400 hover:text-white"
                }`}
              >
                {unit === "PC_GDP" ? "% PIB" : "M€"}
              </button>
              <button
                onClick={() => setScaleMode("percent")}
                className={`px-2.5 py-1 rounded-lg transition-all ${
                  scaleMode === "percent"
                    ? "bg-slate-700 text-white"
                    : "text-slate-400 hover:text-white"
                }`}
              >
                % Total
              </button>
            </div>
          )}
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* MODE 1: GAP (Revenues vs Expenditure + Synchronized Balance Strip) */}
      {activeTab === "gap" && (
        <div className="space-y-2">
          {/* Upper Gap Chart */}
          <div className="w-full h-72 sm:h-80">
            <ResponsiveContainer width="100%" height="100%">
              <ComposedChart
                syncId="fiscal-macro"
                data={macroData}
                onClick={handleChartClick}
                margin={{ top: 10, right: 10, left: -20, bottom: 0 }}
              >
                <CartesianGrid strokeDasharray="3 3" stroke="#1E293B" vertical={false} />
                <XAxis
                  dataKey="year"
                  stroke="#64748B"
                  tick={{ fill: "#94A3B8", fontSize: 11, fontFamily: "monospace" }}
                  axisLine={{ stroke: "#334155" }}
                  tickLine={false}
                />
                <YAxis
                  domain={["auto", "auto"]}
                  stroke="#64748B"
                  tick={{ fill: "#94A3B8", fontSize: 11, fontFamily: "monospace" }}
                  axisLine={{ stroke: "#334155" }}
                  tickFormatter={(val: number) =>
                    formatFiscalValue(val, unit, lang, { compact: true })
                  }
                />
                <Tooltip
                  content={
                    <MacroTooltip unit={unit} balances={balances} lang={lang} />
                  }
                />
                <ReferenceLine
                  x={selectedYear}
                  stroke="#94A3B8"
                  strokeDasharray="4 4"
                  strokeWidth={1.5}
                />
                <Line
                  type="monotone"
                  dataKey="revenue"
                  name="Ingresos (TR)"
                  stroke={REVENUE_COLOR}
                  strokeWidth={2.5}
                  dot={false}
                  activeDot={{ r: 5, fill: REVENUE_COLOR }}
                />
                <Line
                  type="monotone"
                  dataKey="expenditure"
                  name="Gastos (TE)"
                  stroke={EXPENDITURE_COLOR}
                  strokeWidth={2.5}
                  dot={false}
                  activeDot={{ r: 5, fill: EXPENDITURE_COLOR }}
                />
              </ComposedChart>
            </ResponsiveContainer>
          </div>

          {/* Lower Synchronized Balance Strip */}
          <div className="w-full h-28 pt-2 border-t border-slate-800/60">
            <ResponsiveContainer width="100%" height="100%">
              <BarChart
                syncId="fiscal-macro"
                data={macroData}
                onClick={handleChartClick}
                margin={{ top: 5, right: 10, left: -20, bottom: 0 }}
              >
                <CartesianGrid strokeDasharray="3 3" stroke="#1E293B" vertical={false} />
                <XAxis dataKey="year" hide />
                <YAxis
                  domain={["auto", "auto"]}
                  stroke="#64748B"
                  tick={{ fill: "#94A3B8", fontSize: 10, fontFamily: "monospace" }}
                  tickFormatter={(val: number) => (val === 0 ? "0" : `${val}`)}
                />
                <ReferenceLine y={0} stroke="#475569" strokeWidth={1} />
                <ReferenceLine
                  x={selectedYear}
                  stroke="#94A3B8"
                  strokeDasharray="4 4"
                />
                <Bar dataKey="balance" radius={[2, 2, 0, 0]}>
                  {macroData.map((entry) => {
                    const isSelected = entry.year === selectedYear;
                    const fill = entry.balance >= 0 ? SURPLUS_COLOR : DEFICIT_COLOR;
                    return (
                      <Cell
                        key={entry.year}
                        fill={fill}
                        opacity={isSelected ? 1.0 : 0.75}
                        cursor="pointer"
                      />
                    );
                  })}
                </Bar>
              </BarChart>
            </ResponsiveContainer>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      {/* MODE 2 & 3: Composition Area Chart */}
      {activeTab !== "gap" && (
        <div className="space-y-3">
          <div className="w-full h-80 sm:h-96">
            <ResponsiveContainer width="100%" height="100%">
              <AreaChart
                data={compData}
                onClick={handleChartClick}
                margin={{ top: 10, right: 10, left: -20, bottom: 0 }}
              >
                <CartesianGrid strokeDasharray="3 3" stroke="#1E293B" vertical={false} />
                <XAxis
                  dataKey="year"
                  stroke="#64748B"
                  tick={{ fill: "#94A3B8", fontSize: 11, fontFamily: "monospace" }}
                  axisLine={{ stroke: "#334155" }}
                  tickLine={false}
                />
                <YAxis
                  stroke="#64748B"
                  tick={{ fill: "#94A3B8", fontSize: 11, fontFamily: "monospace" }}
                  axisLine={{ stroke: "#334155" }}
                  tickFormatter={(val: number) =>
                    scaleMode === "percent"
                      ? `${val.toFixed(0)}%`
                      : formatFiscalValue(val, unit, lang, { compact: true })
                  }
                />
                <Tooltip
                  content={({ active, payload, label }) => {
                    if (!active || !payload || !payload.length || !label) return null;
                    return (
                      <div className="bg-slate-950 border border-slate-700 p-3 rounded-xl shadow-2xl text-xs space-y-1.5 max-w-xs font-sans">
                        <div className="font-bold text-white border-b border-slate-800 pb-1 flex items-center justify-between">
                          <span>Año {label}</span>
                          <span className="text-slate-400 font-mono text-[11px]">
                            {scaleMode === "percent" ? "% del Total" : unit}
                          </span>
                        </div>
                        <div className="space-y-1 font-mono tabular-nums text-[11px]">
                          {payload.map((entry) => {
                            if (entry.value === null || entry.value === undefined)
                              return null;
                            const taxon = activeSideTaxa.find(
                              (t) => t.code === entry.dataKey
                            );
                            const valNum = Number(entry.value);
                            return (
                              <div
                                key={String(entry.dataKey)}
                                className="flex items-center justify-between gap-3"
                              >
                                <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 truncate">
                                  <span
                                    className="w-2.5 h-2.5 rounded-full shrink-0"
                                    style={{ backgroundColor: entry.color }}
                                  />
                                  <span className="truncate text-slate-300">
                                    {taxon?.shortLabel ?? entry.dataKey}
                                  </span>
                                </div>
                                <span className="font-bold text-white">
                                  {scaleMode === "percent"
                                    ? `${valNum.toFixed(1)}%`
                                    : formatFiscalValue(valNum, unit, lang, {
                                        compact: true,
                                      })}
                                </span>
                              </div>
                            );
                          })}
                        </div>
                      </div>
                    );
                  }}
                />
                <ReferenceLine
                  x={selectedYear}
                  stroke="#94A3B8"
                  strokeDasharray="4 4"
                  strokeWidth={1.5}
                />
                {activeSideTaxa.map((t) => {
                  const color = getTaxonColor(t.code);
                  const isDimmed = isolatedTaxon !== null && isolatedTaxon !== t.code;
                  return (
                    <Area
                      key={t.code}
                      type="monotone"
                      dataKey={t.code}
                      name={t.shortLabel}
                      stackId="1"
                      stroke={color}
                      fill={color}
                      fillOpacity={isDimmed ? 0.08 : 0.85}
                      strokeOpacity={isDimmed ? 0.2 : 1.0}
                      connectNulls={false}
                    />
                  );
                })}
              </AreaChart>
            </ResponsiveContainer>
          </div>

          {/* Clickable Legend Pills */}
          <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2 pt-2 border-t border-slate-800">
            {activeSideTaxa.map((t) => {
              const color = getTaxonColor(t.code);
              const isSelected = isolatedTaxon === t.code;
              return (
                <button
                  key={t.code}
                  onClick={() =>
                    setIsolatedTaxon(isSelected ? null : t.code)
                  }
                  className={`inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-2.5 py-1 rounded-full text-xs font-semibold transition-all ${
                    isSelected
                      ? "bg-slate-700 text-white ring-1 ring-white/40"
                      : isolatedTaxon !== null
                      ? "bg-slate-950/40 text-slate-500 opacity-60"
                      : "bg-slate-950 text-slate-300 hover:text-white border border-slate-800"
                  }`}
                >
                  <span
                    className="w-2.5 h-2.5 rounded-full"
                    style={{ backgroundColor: color }}
                  />
                  <span>{t.shortLabel}</span>
