                  className="h-full flex items-center justify-center text-slate-950 transition-all duration-300"
                  title={`65+: ${natBands.senior65plusPct}% (${formatHeadcount(natBands.senior65plus, lang)})`}
                >
                  {natBands.senior65plusPct > 8 && `${natBands.senior65plusPct}%`}
                </div>
              </div>
            ) : (
              <div className="h-7 bg-slate-900/50 rounded-lg animate-pulse" />
            )}
          </div>

          {/* Group 2: Extranjeros */}
          <div className="space-y-2">
            <div className="flex items-center justify-between text-xs">
              <span className="font-semibold text-slate-200 flex items-center gap-1.5">
                <span className="w-2 h-2 rounded-full" style={{ backgroundColor: FOREIGNERS_COLOR }} />
                {forGroupLabel} ({formatHeadcount(demoFor?.population, lang)})
              </span>
              <div className="flex items-center gap-1 text-[11px] text-slate-400">
                <span>{t("dependencyRatioLabel", lang)}:</span>
                <span className="font-mono font-bold text-slate-200">{demoFor?.oldAgeDependencyRatio ?? "—"}</span>
                <ConceptHelpTrigger code="DEPENDENCY_RATIO" lang={lang} />
              </div>
            </div>

            {/* Stacked bar */}
            {forBands ? (
              <div className="h-7 w-full bg-slate-900 rounded-lg overflow-hidden flex font-mono text-xs font-bold text-slate-950">
                <div
                  style={{ width: `${forBands.under15Pct}%`, backgroundColor: AGE_BAND_COLORS.under15 }}
                  className="h-full flex items-center justify-center transition-all duration-300"
                  title={`<15: ${forBands.under15Pct}% (${formatHeadcount(forBands.under15, lang)})`}
                >
                  {forBands.under15Pct > 8 && `${forBands.under15Pct}%`}
                </div>
                <div
                  style={{ width: `${forBands.workingAge15to64Pct}%`, backgroundColor: AGE_BAND_COLORS.workingAge15to64 }}
                  className="h-full flex items-center justify-center text-white transition-all duration-300"
                  title={`15–64: ${forBands.workingAge15to64Pct}% (${formatHeadcount(forBands.workingAge15to64, lang)})`}
                >
                  {forBands.workingAge15to64Pct > 12 && `${forBands.workingAge15to64Pct}%`}
                </div>
                <div
                  style={{ width: `${forBands.senior65plusPct}%`, backgroundColor: AGE_BAND_COLORS.senior65plus }}
                  className="h-full flex items-center justify-center text-slate-950 transition-all duration-300"
                  title={`65+: ${forBands.senior65plusPct}% (${formatHeadcount(forBands.senior65plus, lang)})`}
                >
                  {forBands.senior65plusPct > 8 && `${forBands.senior65plusPct}%`}
                </div>
              </div>
            ) : (
              <div className="h-7 bg-slate-900/50 rounded-lg animate-pulse" />
            )}
          </div>

          <div className="text-[11px] text-slate-400/90 leading-relaxed pt-1">
            {lang === "en"
              ? "The high concentration of the foreign population in working ages (15–64) translates into a very low old-age dependency ratio, reducing the country's average demographic aging."
              : "La alta concentración de la población extranjera en tramos de edad laboral activa (15–64) se traduce en una reducida tasa de dependencia de mayores, compensando el envejecimiento demográfico medio del país."}
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Right Column: Representation Comparison + Naturalizations */}
        <div className="space-y-4">
          {/* Representation Bars */}
          <div className="bg-slate-950/60 border border-slate-800/80 rounded-xl p-4 sm:p-5 space-y-3.5">
            <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
              <span className="text-sm font-bold text-white tracking-tight">
                {t("representationTitle", lang)}
              </span>
              <span className="text-xs text-amber-400 font-semibold">
                {forGroupLabel}
              </span>
            </div>

            <div className="space-y-3 pt-1">
              {repRows.map((row) => (
                <div key={row.labelKey} className="space-y-1">
                  <div className="flex items-center justify-between text-xs">
                    <span className="text-slate-300">{t(row.labelKey, lang)}</span>
                    <div className="flex items-center gap-2 font-mono">
                      {row.foreignCount !== undefined && (
                        <span className="text-slate-400 text-[11px]">
                          {formatHeadcount(row.foreignCount, lang)}
                        </span>
                      )}
                      <span className="font-bold text-amber-400 tabular-nums">
                        {formatPercent(row.foreignSharePct, 1, lang)}
                      </span>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                  <div className="h-2.5 w-full bg-slate-900 rounded-full overflow-hidden">
                    <div
                      className="h-full bg-gradient-to-r from-amber-500 to-amber-400 rounded-full transition-all duration-500"
                      style={{ width: `${Math.min(100, Math.max(0, row.foreignSharePct))}%` }}
                    />
                  </div>
                </div>
              ))}
            </div>
          </div>

          {/* Naturalizations Strip */}
          <div className="bg-slate-950/60 border border-slate-800/80 rounded-xl p-4 sm:p-5 space-y-3">
            <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
              <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                <Award className="w-4 h-4 text-purple-400" />
                <span className="text-sm font-bold text-white tracking-tight">
                  {t("naturalizationsTitle", lang)}
                </span>
                <ConceptHelpTrigger code="NATURALIZATION" lang={lang} />
              </div>
              <span className="text-xs text-purple-400 font-mono font-bold">
                2008–{naturalizationsData[naturalizationsData.length - 1]?.year}: {formatHeadcount(latestCumNat, lang)}
              </span>
            </div>

            <div className="flex items-center justify-between text-xs text-slate-400">
              <span>
                {selectedYear}:{" "}
                <strong className="text-white font-mono">
                  {currentNatPoint?.naturalizations !== null && currentNatPoint?.naturalizations !== undefined
                    ? formatExactNumber(currentNatPoint.naturalizations, lang)
                    : (lang === "en" ? "Pending publication" : "Pendiente de publicación")}
                </strong>
              </span>
              <span className="text-[11px] text-slate-400">
                {lang === "en" ? "Click year to inspect" : "Haz clic para cambiar año"}
              </span>
            </div>

            {/* Interactive mini bar chart */}
            <div className="h-10 flex items-end gap-1 pt-1">
              {naturalizationsData.map((d) => {
                const isActive = d.year === selectedYear;
                const hPct = d.naturalizations ? Math.max(12, Math.round((d.naturalizations / maxNatYearVal) * 100)) : 6;
                return (
                  <button
                    key={d.year}
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => onSelectYear(d.year)}
                    title={`${d.year}: ${d.naturalizations ? formatExactNumber(d.naturalizations, lang) : "—"}`}
                    className={`flex-1 rounded-xs transition-all relative group ${
                      isActive
                        ? "bg-purple-400 ring-2 ring-purple-300 ring-offset-1 ring-offset-slate-950"
                        : "bg-purple-900/60 hover:bg-purple-500/80"
                    }`}
                    style={{ height: `${hPct}%` }}
                  >
                    <span className="sr-only">{d.year}</span>
                  </button>
                );
              })}
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </section>
  );
}

export default DemographicStructureView;

--- FILE: frontend/components/nationality/IncomeAndConditionsPanel.tsx ---
"use client";

import React, { useState } from "react";
import {
  ResponsiveContainer,
  LineChart,
  Line,
  XAxis,
  YAxis,
  Tooltip,
  ReferenceLine,
  CartesianGrid,
} from "recharts";
import { Wallet, AlertCircle } from "lucide-react";
import type { NationalityAnnualRecord, PopulationDefinition, Lang } from "@/lib/types";
import {
  buildLivingConditionsSeries,
  getRecordForYear,
  type LivingConditionsMetricKey,
  formatCurrencyEur,
  formatPercent,
} from "./nationalitySelectors";
import {
  NATIONALS_COLOR,
  FOREIGNERS_COLOR,
  EU_FOREIGNERS_COLOR,
  NON_EU_FOREIGNERS_COLOR,
} from "./nationalityPalette";
import { t } from "@/lib/i18n";
import { ConceptHelpTrigger } from "@/components/detail/guide/ConceptHelpTrigger";

interface IncomeAndConditionsPanelProps {
  records: NationalityAnnualRecord[];
  selectedYear: string;
  onSelectYear: (year: string) => void;
  definition: PopulationDefinition;
  lang?: Lang;
}

export function IncomeAndConditionsPanel({
  records,
  selectedYear,
  onSelectYear,
  definition,
  lang = "es",
}: IncomeAndConditionsPanelProps) {
  const [metric, setMetric] = useState<LivingConditionsMetricKey>("medianEquivalisedIncomeEur");

  const currentRecord = getRecordForYear(records, selectedYear);
  const livStats = currentRecord?.livingConditions;

  const data = buildLivingConditionsSeries(records, metric, true);

  const isCurrency = metric !== "atRiskOfPovertyPct";

  const formatYAxis = (val: number) => {
    if (!isCurrency) return `${val}%`;
    if (val >= 1000) return `${(val / 1000).toFixed(0)}k€`;
    return `${val}€`;
  };

  const formatTooltipVal = (val: number | null | undefined) => {
    if (val === null || val === undefined) return "—";
    if (isCurrency) return formatCurrencyEur(val, lang);
    return formatPercent(val, 1, lang);
  };

  const handleChartClick = (e: any) => {
    if (e && e.activeLabel) {
      onSelectYear(String(e.activeLabel));
    }
  };

  // Metric values for selectedYear summary
  const natVal = livStats?.nationals?.[metric];
  const forVal = livStats?.foreigners?.[metric];
  const euVal = livStats?.euForeigners?.[metric];
  const nonEuVal = livStats?.nonEuForeigners?.[metric];

  let incomeRatio: number | null = null;
  if (natVal !== null && natVal !== undefined && forVal !== null && forVal !== undefined && natVal > 0) {
    incomeRatio = Math.round((forVal / natVal) * 1000) / 10;
  }

  return (
    <section className="bg-slate-900 border border-slate-800 rounded-2xl p-4 sm:p-6 shadow-xl space-y-5">
      {/* Header and Controls */}
      <div className="flex flex-col md:flex-row md:items-center justify-between pb-4 border-b border-slate-800 gap-4">
        <div className="space-y-1">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
            <Wallet className="w-5 h-5 text-purple-400" />
            <h2 className="text-base sm:text-lg font-bold text-white tracking-tight">
              {t("livingConditionsTitle", lang)}
            </h2>
            <ConceptHelpTrigger code="EQUIVALISED_INCOME" lang={lang} />
            <ConceptHelpTrigger code="AROP_RATE" lang={lang} />
          </div>
          <div className="text-xs text-slate-400">
            {lang === "en"
              ? "Living Conditions Survey (EU-SILC / INE ECV, adult population 18+)"
              : "Encuesta de Condiciones de Vida (INE ECV / EU-SILC, adultos 18+ años)"}
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Metric Switcher Tabs (only relevant when definition === "citizenship") */}
        {definition === "citizenship" && (
          <div
            role="group"
            aria-label="Selección de métrica de condiciones de vida"
            className="inline-flex items-center p-1 bg-slate-950 border border-slate-800 rounded-xl flex-wrap"
          >
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => setMetric("medianEquivalisedIncomeEur")}
              className={`px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg text-xs font-semibold transition-all ${
                metric === "medianEquivalisedIncomeEur"
                  ? "bg-blue-600 text-white shadow-sm"
                  : "text-slate-400 hover:text-slate-200"
              }`}
            >
              {t("metricMedianIncome", lang)}
            </button>
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => setMetric("meanEquivalisedIncomeEur")}
              className={`px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg text-xs font-semibold transition-all ${
                metric === "meanEquivalisedIncomeEur"
                  ? "bg-blue-600 text-white shadow-sm"
                  : "text-slate-400 hover:text-slate-200"
              }`}
            >
              {t("metricMeanIncome", lang)}
            </button>
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => setMetric("atRiskOfPovertyPct")}
              className={`px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg text-xs font-semibold transition-all ${
                metric === "atRiskOfPovertyPct"
                  ? "bg-blue-600 text-white shadow-sm"
                  : "text-slate-400 hover:text-slate-200"
              }`}
            >
              {t("metricPovertyRate", lang)}
            </button>
          </div>
        )}
      </div>

      {/* When definition === "birthplace", ECV data does not exist by country of birth: show only the notice and NO chart */}
      {definition === "birthplace" ? (
        <div className="bg-slate-950/60 border border-slate-800/80 rounded-xl p-6 sm:p-8 text-center space-y-3">
          <div className="w-10 h-10 rounded-full bg-amber-500/10 border border-amber-500/20 flex items-center justify-center mx-auto text-amber-400">
            <AlertCircle className="w-5 h-5" />
          </div>
          <div className="max-w-xl mx-auto space-y-2">
            <h3 className="text-sm font-bold text-white">
              {lang === "en"
                ? "No official data by country of birth"
                : "Sin datos oficiales por país de nacimiento"}
            </h3>
            <p className="text-xs text-slate-400 leading-relaxed">
              {t("citizenshipOnlyNotice", lang)}
            </p>
          </div>
        </div>
      ) : (
        /* 2-Column Layout (only rendered when definition === "citizenship") */
        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 lg:grid-cols-3 gap-6">
        {/* Left 2 Cols: Historical Line Chart */}
        <div className="lg:col-span-2 space-y-2">
          <div className="flex items-center justify-between text-xs text-slate-400">
            <span className="font-semibold text-slate-300">
              {t("historicalEvolution", lang)} (2008–{data[data.length - 1]?.year})
            </span>
            <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
              <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5">
                <span className="w-2 h-2 rounded-full" style={{ backgroundColor: NATIONALS_COLOR }} />
                {t("groupNationals", lang)}
              </span>
              <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5">
                <span className="w-2 h-2 rounded-full" style={{ backgroundColor: FOREIGNERS_COLOR }} />
                {t("groupForeigners", lang)}
              </span>
            </div>
          </div>

          <div className="h-72 w-full pt-1">
            <ResponsiveContainer width="100%" height="100%">
              <LineChart
                data={data}
                onClick={handleChartClick}
                margin={{ top: 10, right: 10, left: 0, bottom: 0 }}
              >
                <CartesianGrid strokeDasharray="3 3" stroke="#334155" opacity={0.4} />
                <XAxis
                  dataKey="year"
                  stroke="#64748b"
                  fontSize={12}
                  tickLine={false}
                  axisLine={{ stroke: "#334155" }}
                />
                <YAxis
                  stroke="#64748b"
                  fontSize={12}
                  tickLine={false}
                  axisLine={{ stroke: "#334155" }}
                  domain={["auto", "auto"]}
                  tickFormatter={formatYAxis}
                  width={52}
                />
                <Tooltip
                  content={({ active, payload, label }) => {
                    if (!active || !payload || !payload.length) return null;
                    return (
                      <div className="bg-slate-950 border border-slate-800 rounded-xl p-3 shadow-2xl text-xs space-y-1.5 min-w-44">
                        <div className="font-bold text-white border-b border-slate-800 pb-1 flex items-center justify-between">
                          <span>{t("yearLabel", lang)} {label}</span>
                          {label === selectedYear && (
                            <span className="text-[10px] text-blue-400 font-normal">● Activo</span>
                          )}
                        </div>
                        {payload.map((entry: any) => (
                          <div key={entry.name} className="flex items-center justify-between gap-3">
                            <span className="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-slate-300">
                              <span
                                className="w-2 h-2 rounded-full inline-block"
                                style={{ backgroundColor: entry.color }}
                              />
                              {entry.name}:
                            </span>
                            <span className="font-mono font-bold text-white tabular-nums">
                              {formatTooltipVal(entry.value)}
                            </span>
                          </div>
                        ))}
                      </div>
                    );
                  }}
                />

                <ReferenceLine
                  x={selectedYear}
                  stroke="#818CF8"
                  strokeDasharray="4 4"
                  strokeWidth={1.5}
                />

                <Line
                  type="monotone"
                  dataKey="nationals"
                  name={t("groupNationals", lang)}
                  stroke={NATIONALS_COLOR}
                  strokeWidth={2.5}
                  dot={{ r: 2, fill: NATIONALS_COLOR }}
                  activeDot={{ r: 5 }}
                  connectNulls={false}
                />
                <Line
                  type="monotone"
                  dataKey="foreigners"
                  name={t("groupForeigners", lang)}
                  stroke={FOREIGNERS_COLOR}
                  strokeWidth={2.5}
                  dot={{ r: 2, fill: FOREIGNERS_COLOR }}
                  activeDot={{ r: 5 }}
                  connectNulls={false}
                />
              </LineChart>
            </ResponsiveContainer>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Right 1 Col: Summary Card */}
        <div className="bg-slate-950/60 border border-slate-800/80 rounded-xl p-4 sm:p-5 flex flex-col justify-between space-y-4">
          <div>
            <div className="text-xs font-bold text-slate-300 uppercase tracking-wider pb-2 border-b border-slate-800 flex items-center justify-between">
              <span>{t("summaryYear", lang)} {selectedYear}</span>
              <span className="text-[11px] font-normal text-slate-400 lowercase font-mono">
                {metric === "medianEquivalisedIncomeEur" && (lang === "en" ? "median" : "mediana")}
                {metric === "meanEquivalisedIncomeEur" && (lang === "en" ? "mean" : "media")}
                {metric === "atRiskOfPovertyPct" && (lang === "en" ? "poverty" : "pobreza")}
              </span>
            </div>

            <div className="space-y-3 pt-3">
              {/* Españoles */}
              <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
                <span className="text-xs text-slate-300 flex items-center gap-1.5">
                  <span className="w-2 h-2 rounded-full" style={{ backgroundColor: NATIONALS_COLOR }} />
                  {t("groupNationals", lang)}:
                </span>
                <span className="font-mono font-bold text-white text-sm tabular-nums">
                  {formatTooltipVal(natVal)}
                </span>
              </div>

              {/* Extranjeros */}
              <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
                <span className="text-xs text-slate-300 flex items-center gap-1.5">
                  <span className="w-2 h-2 rounded-full" style={{ backgroundColor: FOREIGNERS_COLOR }} />
                  {t("groupForeigners", lang)}:
                </span>
                <span className="font-mono font-bold text-amber-400 text-sm tabular-nums">
                  {formatTooltipVal(forVal)}
                </span>
              </div>

              {/* Ratio */}
              {incomeRatio !== null && (
                <div className="pt-2 border-t border-slate-800/80 flex items-center justify-between text-xs">
                  <span className="text-slate-400">{t("incomeRatioLabel", lang)}:</span>
                  <span className="font-mono font-bold text-purple-300 tabular-nums">
                    {incomeRatio}%
                  </span>
                </div>
              )}

              {/* Desglose UE vs No-UE */}
              {(euVal !== null || nonEuVal !== null) && (
                <div className="pt-3 border-t border-slate-800/80 space-y-1.5 text-xs">
                  <span className="text-[11px] text-slate-400 block font-semibold">
                    {lang === "en" ? "Foreign breakdown:" : "Desglose extranjeros:"}
                  </span>
                  {euVal !== null && euVal !== undefined && (
                    <div className="flex items-center justify-between text-slate-300 pl-1">
                      <span className="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-[11px]">
                        <span className="w-1.5 h-1.5 rounded-full" style={{ backgroundColor: EU_FOREIGNERS_COLOR }} />
                        {t("groupEuForeigners", lang)}:
                      </span>
                      <span className="font-mono font-semibold tabular-nums text-slate-200">
                        {formatTooltipVal(euVal)}
                      </span>
                    </div>
                  )}
                  {nonEuVal !== null && nonEuVal !== undefined && (
                    <div className="flex items-center justify-between text-slate-300 pl-1">
                      <span className="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-[11px]">
                        <span className="w-1.5 h-1.5 rounded-full" style={{ backgroundColor: NON_EU_FOREIGNERS_COLOR }} />
                        {t("groupNonEuForeigners", lang)}:
                      </span>
                      <span className="font-mono font-semibold tabular-nums text-slate-200">
                        {formatTooltipVal(nonEuVal)}
                      </span>
                    </div>
                  )}
                </div>
              )}
            </div>
          </div>

          <div className="text-[11px] text-slate-400/90 leading-relaxed pt-2 border-t border-slate-800/60">
            {lang === "en"
              ? "Equivalised net income measures household disposable income adjusted for household size (modified OECD scale) to allow genuine personal purchasing power comparisons."
              : "La renta neta equivalente mide los ingresos disponibles ajustados por el tamaño familiar (escala modificada OCDE) para comparar el poder adquisitivo individual real."}
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
      )}
    </section>
  );
}

export default IncomeAndConditionsPanel;

--- FILE: frontend/components/nationality/LaborMarketComparisonChart.tsx ---
"use client";

import React, { useState } from "react";
import {
  ResponsiveContainer,
  LineChart,
  Line,
  XAxis,
  YAxis,
  Tooltip,
  Legend,
  ReferenceLine,
  CartesianGrid,
} from "recharts";
import { Briefcase, Info } from "lucide-react";
import type { NationalityAnnualRecord, PopulationDefinition, Lang } from "@/lib/types";
import {
  buildLaborTimeSeries,
  type LaborMetricKey,
  formatPercent,
  formatHeadcount,
} from "./nationalitySelectors";
import {
  NATIONALS_COLOR,
  FOREIGNERS_COLOR,
  EU_FOREIGNERS_COLOR,
  NON_EU_FOREIGNERS_COLOR,
  TOTAL_COLOR,
} from "./nationalityPalette";
import { t } from "@/lib/i18n";
import { ConceptHelpTrigger } from "@/components/detail/guide/ConceptHelpTrigger";

interface LaborMarketComparisonChartProps {
  records: NationalityAnnualRecord[];
  selectedYear: string;
  onSelectYear: (year: string) => void;
  definition: PopulationDefinition;
  lang?: Lang;
}

export function LaborMarketComparisonChart({
  records,
  selectedYear,
  onSelectYear,
  definition,
  lang = "es",
}: LaborMarketComparisonChartProps) {
  const [metric, setMetric] = useState<LaborMetricKey>("employmentRatePct");
  const [includeEuSplit, setIncludeEuSplit] = useState<boolean>(true);

  const isCitizenship = definition === "citizenship";
  const natGroupLabel = isCitizenship ? t("groupNationals", lang) : t("groupNativeBorn", lang);
  const forGroupLabel = isCitizenship ? t("groupForeigners", lang) : t("groupForeignBorn", lang);

  const data = buildLaborTimeSeries(records, definition, metric, includeEuSplit);

  const isRate = metric !== "employedThousands";

  const formatYAxis = (val: number) => {
    if (isRate) return `${val}%`;
    if (val >= 1000) return `${(val / 1000).toFixed(0)}M`;
    return `${val}k`;
  };

  const formatTooltipVal = (val: number | null | undefined) => {
    if (val === null || val === undefined) return "—";
    if (isRate) return formatPercent(val, 1, lang);
    return `${val.toLocaleString(lang === "en" ? "en-US" : "es-ES")} ${lang === "en" ? "thousand" : "miles"}`;
  };

  const handleChartClick = (e: any) => {
    if (e && e.activeLabel) {
      onSelectYear(String(e.activeLabel));
    }
  };

  return (
    <section className="bg-slate-900 border border-slate-800 rounded-2xl p-4 sm:p-6 shadow-xl space-y-5">
      {/* Header and Controls */}
      <div className="flex flex-col md:flex-row md:items-center justify-between pb-4 border-b border-slate-800 gap-4">
        <div className="space-y-1">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
            <Briefcase className="w-5 h-5 text-indigo-400" />
            <h2 className="text-base sm:text-lg font-bold text-white tracking-tight">
              {t("laborMarketTitle", lang)}
            </h2>
          </div>
          <div className="text-xs text-slate-400">
            {lang === "en"
              ? "Harmonized Eurostat Labour Force Survey (LFS / INE EPA, population 15–64)"
              : "Encuesta de Población Activa (EPA armonizada Eurostat, tramo 15–64 años)"}
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Metrics Switcher Tabs */}
        <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 flex-wrap">
          <div
            role="group"
            aria-label="Selección de métrica laboral"
            className="inline-flex items-center p-1 bg-slate-950 border border-slate-800 rounded-xl flex-wrap"
          >
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => setMetric("employmentRatePct")}
              className={`px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg text-xs font-semibold transition-all ${
                metric === "employmentRatePct"
                  ? "bg-blue-600 text-white shadow-sm"
                  : "text-slate-400 hover:text-slate-200"
              }`}
            >
              {t("metricEmploymentRate", lang)}
            </button>
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => setMetric("activityRatePct")}
              className={`px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg text-xs font-semibold transition-all ${
                metric === "activityRatePct"
                  ? "bg-blue-600 text-white shadow-sm"
                  : "text-slate-400 hover:text-slate-200"
              }`}
            >
              {t("metricActivityRate", lang)}
            </button>
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => setMetric("unemploymentRatePct")}
              className={`px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg text-xs font-semibold transition-all ${
                metric === "unemploymentRatePct"
                  ? "bg-blue-600 text-white shadow-sm"
                  : "text-slate-400 hover:text-slate-200"
              }`}
            >
              {t("metricUnemploymentRate", lang)}
            </button>
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => setMetric("employedThousands")}
              className={`px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg text-xs font-semibold transition-all ${
                metric === "employedThousands"
                  ? "bg-blue-600 text-white shadow-sm"
                  : "text-slate-400 hover:text-slate-200"
              }`}
            >
              {t("metricEmployedThousands", lang)}
            </button>
          </div>

          {metric === "employmentRatePct" && <ConceptHelpTrigger code="EMPLOYMENT_RATE" lang={lang} />}
          {metric === "activityRatePct" && <ConceptHelpTrigger code="ACTIVITY_RATE" lang={lang} />}
          {metric === "unemploymentRatePct" && <ConceptHelpTrigger code="UNEMPLOYMENT_RATE" lang={lang} />}
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Sub-bar: EU Drilldown Checkbox */}
      <div className="flex items-center justify-between text-xs text-slate-400">
        <label className="inline-flex items-center gap-2 cursor-pointer select-none hover:text-slate-200 transition-colors">
          <input
            type="checkbox"
            checked={includeEuSplit}
            onChange={(e) => setIncludeEuSplit(e.target.checked)}
            className="w-4 h-4 rounded border-slate-700 bg-slate-950 text-blue-600 focus:ring-blue-500 focus:ring-offset-slate-900"
          />
          <span className="font-semibold text-slate-300">{t("toggleEuSplit", lang)}</span>
          <ConceptHelpTrigger code="EU_VS_NEU" lang={lang} />
        </label>

        <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-[11px] text-slate-400">
          <span className="w-2 h-2 rounded-full" style={{ backgroundColor: NATIONALS_COLOR }} />
          <span>{natGroupLabel}</span>
          <span className="mx-1">·</span>
          <span className="w-2 h-2 rounded-full" style={{ backgroundColor: FOREIGNERS_COLOR }} />
          <span>{forGroupLabel}</span>
          {includeEuSplit && (
            <>
              <span className="mx-1">·</span>
              <span className="w-2 h-2 rounded-full" style={{ backgroundColor: EU_FOREIGNERS_COLOR }} />
              <span>{t("groupEuForeigners", lang)}</span>
              <span className="mx-1">·</span>
              <span className="w-2 h-2 rounded-full" style={{ backgroundColor: NON_EU_FOREIGNERS_COLOR }} />
              <span>{t("groupNonEuForeigners", lang)}</span>
            </>
          )}
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Recharts Chart */}
      <div className="h-80 w-full pt-2">
        <ResponsiveContainer width="100%" height="100%">
          <LineChart
            data={data}
            onClick={handleChartClick}
            margin={{ top: 10, right: 10, left: 0, bottom: 0 }}
          >
            <CartesianGrid strokeDasharray="3 3" stroke="#334155" opacity={0.4} />
            <XAxis
              dataKey="year"
              stroke="#64748b"
              fontSize={12}
              tickLine={false}
              axisLine={{ stroke: "#334155" }}
            />
            <YAxis
              stroke="#64748b"
              fontSize={12}
              tickLine={false}
              axisLine={{ stroke: "#334155" }}
              domain={["auto", "auto"]}
              tickFormatter={formatYAxis}
              width={48}
            />
            <Tooltip
              content={({ active, payload, label }) => {
                if (!active || !payload || !payload.length) return null;
                return (
                  <div className="bg-slate-950 border border-slate-800 rounded-xl p-3 shadow-2xl text-xs space-y-1.5 min-w-44">
                    <div className="font-bold text-white border-b border-slate-800 pb-1 flex items-center justify-between">
                      <span>{t("yearLabel", lang)} {label}</span>
                      {label === selectedYear && (
                        <span className="text-[10px] text-blue-400 font-normal">● Activo</span>
                      )}
                    </div>
                    {payload.map((entry: any) => (
                      <div key={entry.name} className="flex items-center justify-between gap-3">
                        <span className="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-slate-300">
                          <span
                            className="w-2 h-2 rounded-full inline-block"
                            style={{ backgroundColor: entry.color }}
                          />
                          {entry.name}:
                        </span>
                        <span className="font-mono font-bold text-white tabular-nums">
                          {formatTooltipVal(entry.value)}
                        </span>
                      </div>
                    ))}
                  </div>
                );
              }}
            />

            {/* Reference line on active year */}
            <ReferenceLine
              x={selectedYear}
              stroke="#818CF8"
              strokeDasharray="4 4"
              strokeWidth={1.5}
            />

            {/* Main Lines */}
            <Line
              type="monotone"
              dataKey="nationals"
              name={natGroupLabel}
              stroke={NATIONALS_COLOR}
              strokeWidth={2.5}
              dot={{ r: 2, fill: NATIONALS_COLOR }}
              activeDot={{ r: 5 }}
              connectNulls={false}
            />
            <Line
              type="monotone"
              dataKey="foreigners"
              name={forGroupLabel}
              stroke={FOREIGNERS_COLOR}
              strokeWidth={2.5}
              dot={{ r: 2, fill: FOREIGNERS_COLOR }}
              activeDot={{ r: 5 }}
              connectNulls={false}
            />

            {includeEuSplit && (
              <Line
                key="euForeigners"
                type="monotone"
                dataKey="euForeigners"
                name={t("groupEuForeigners", lang)}
                stroke={EU_FOREIGNERS_COLOR}
                strokeWidth={2}
                strokeDasharray="4 4"
                dot={{ r: 2, fill: EU_FOREIGNERS_COLOR }}
                activeDot={{ r: 5 }}
                connectNulls={false}
              />
            )}
            {includeEuSplit && (
              <Line
                key="nonEuForeigners"
                type="monotone"
                dataKey="nonEuForeigners"
                name={t("groupNonEuForeigners", lang)}
                stroke={NON_EU_FOREIGNERS_COLOR}
                strokeWidth={2}
                strokeDasharray="4 4"
                dot={{ r: 2, fill: NON_EU_FOREIGNERS_COLOR }}
                activeDot={{ r: 5 }}
                connectNulls={false}
              />
            )}
          </LineChart>
        </ResponsiveContainer>
      </div>

      {/* Helpful Hint */}
      <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-xs text-slate-400/90 pt-1">
        <Info className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-slate-400 shrink-0" />
        <span>{t("laborClickHint", lang)}</span>
      </div>
    </section>
  );
}

export default LaborMarketComparisonChart;

--- FILE: frontend/components/nationality/NationalityControlBar.tsx ---
"use client";

import React from "react";
import { ChevronLeft, ChevronRight, Users, Briefcase, TrendingUp, Wallet, Info } from "lucide-react";
import type { NationalityAnnualRecord, PopulationDefinition, Lang } from "@/lib/types";
import {
  getAvailableYears,
  getHeadlineKpis,
} from "./nationalitySelectors";
import { NATIONALS_COLOR, FOREIGNERS_COLOR } from "./nationalityPalette";
import { t } from "@/lib/i18n";
import { ConceptHelpTrigger } from "@/components/detail/guide/ConceptHelpTrigger";

interface NationalityControlBarProps {
  records: NationalityAnnualRecord[];
  selectedYear: string;
  onSelectYear: (year: string) => void;
  definition: PopulationDefinition;
  onSelectDefinition: (definition: PopulationDefinition) => void;
  lang?: Lang;
}

export function NationalityControlBar({
  records,
  selectedYear,
  onSelectYear,
  definition,
  onSelectDefinition,
  lang = "es",
}: NationalityControlBarProps) {
  const years = getAvailableYears(records);
  const currentIdx = years.indexOf(selectedYear);

  const canPrev = currentIdx > 0;
  const canNext = currentIdx !== -1 && currentIdx < years.length - 1;

  const handlePrev = () => {
    if (canPrev) onSelectYear(years[currentIdx - 1]);
  };

  const handleNext = () => {
    if (canNext) onSelectYear(years[currentIdx + 1]);
  };

  const kpis = getHeadlineKpis(records, selectedYear, definition, lang);

  const isCitizenship = definition === "citizenship";

  return (
    <div className="bg-slate-900 border border-slate-800 rounded-2xl p-4 sm:p-5 shadow-xl space-y-4">
      {/* Top Controls Row */}
      <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row items-stretch sm:items-center justify-between gap-4">
        {/* Definition Switcher */}
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2 flex-wrap">
          <span className="text-xs font-bold text-slate-400 tracking-wider">
            {t("definitionLabel", lang)}
          </span>
          <div
            role="group"
            aria-label="Selección de definición de población"
            className="inline-flex items-center p-1 bg-slate-950 border border-slate-800 rounded-xl"
          >
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => onSelectDefinition("citizenship")}
              className={`px-3.5 py-1.5 rounded-lg text-xs font-semibold transition-all inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 ${
                isCitizenship
                  ? "bg-blue-600 text-white shadow-sm"
                  : "text-slate-400 hover:text-slate-200"
              }`}
            >
              <span>{t("definitionCitizenship", lang)}</span>
            </button>
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => onSelectDefinition("birthplace")}
              className={`px-3.5 py-1.5 rounded-lg text-xs font-semibold transition-all inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 ${
                !isCitizenship
                  ? "bg-blue-600 text-white shadow-sm"
                  : "text-slate-400 hover:text-slate-200"
              }`}
            >
              <span>{t("definitionBirthplace", lang)}</span>
            </button>
          </div>
          <ConceptHelpTrigger code="CITIZEN_VS_BIRTH" lang={lang} />
        </div>

        {/* Year Stepper */}
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2 self-start sm:self-auto">
          <span className="text-xs font-bold text-slate-400 tracking-wider">
            {t("activeYearLabel", lang)}
          </span>
          <div className="inline-flex items-center bg-slate-950 border border-slate-800 rounded-xl p-1 gap-1">
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={handlePrev}
              disabled={!canPrev}
              aria-label={t("previousYear", lang)}
              className="p-1 rounded-lg text-slate-400 hover:text-white hover:bg-slate-800 disabled:opacity-30 disabled:cursor-not-allowed transition-colors"
            >
              <ChevronLeft className="w-4 h-4" />
            </button>

            <select
              value={selectedYear}
              onChange={(e) => onSelectYear(e.target.value)}
              className="bg-transparent text-white font-mono font-bold text-sm px-2 py-0.5 focus:outline-none cursor-pointer"
            >
              {years.map((y) => (
                <option key={y} value={y} className="bg-slate-900 text-white">
                  {y}
                </option>
              ))}
            </select>

            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={handleNext}
              disabled={!canNext}
              aria-label={t("nextYear", lang)}
              className="p-1 rounded-lg text-slate-400 hover:text-white hover:bg-slate-800 disabled:opacity-30 disabled:cursor-not-allowed transition-colors"
            >
              <ChevronRight className="w-4 h-4" />
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* KPI Cards Grid */}
      <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-4 gap-3 pt-1">
        {/* Card 1: Población */}
        <div className="bg-slate-950/70 border border-slate-800/80 rounded-xl p-3.5 flex flex-col justify-between hover:border-slate-700/80 transition-colors">
          <div className="flex items-center justify-between text-slate-400 mb-1.5">
            <span className="text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-wider flex items-center gap-1.5">
              <Users className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-amber-400" />
              {t(kpis.popKpi.titleKey, lang)}
            </span>
            {kpis.popKpi.yoyDelta && (
              <span className="text-[11px] font-mono px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded bg-slate-800 text-slate-300">
                {kpis.popKpi.yoyDelta}
              </span>
            )}
          </div>
          <div className="space-y-1">
            <div className="text-2xl font-bold text-white font-mono tabular-nums">
              {kpis.popKpi.foreignValue}
            </div>
            <div className="text-xs text-slate-400 font-mono tabular-nums flex items-center justify-between">
              <span>{kpis.popKpi.foreignSubtext}</span>
            </div>
          </div>
          <div className="mt-2 pt-2 border-t border-slate-800/60 flex items-center justify-between text-[11px] text-slate-400">
            <span className="flex items-center gap-1.5">
              <span className="w-2 h-2 rounded-full inline-block" style={{ backgroundColor: NATIONALS_COLOR }} />
              {isCitizenship ? t("groupNationals", lang) : t("groupNativeBorn", lang)}:
            </span>
            <span className="font-mono text-slate-200">{kpis.popKpi.nationalSubtext}</span>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Card 2: Edad Laboral (15-64) */}
        <div className="bg-slate-950/70 border border-slate-800/80 rounded-xl p-3.5 flex flex-col justify-between hover:border-slate-700/80 transition-colors">
          <div className="flex items-center justify-between text-slate-400 mb-1.5">
            <span className="text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-wider flex items-center gap-1.5">
              <Briefcase className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-indigo-400" />
              {t(kpis.workingAgeKpi.titleKey, lang)}
            </span>
            <ConceptHelpTrigger code="WORKING_AGE" lang={lang} />
          </div>
          <div className="space-y-1">
            <div className="text-2xl font-bold text-white font-mono tabular-nums flex items-baseline gap-2">
              <span style={{ color: FOREIGNERS_COLOR }}>{kpis.workingAgeKpi.foreignValue}</span>
              <span className="text-xs text-slate-400 font-normal">vs</span>
              <span style={{ color: NATIONALS_COLOR }}>{kpis.workingAgeKpi.nationalValue}</span>
            </div>
            <div className="text-xs text-slate-400 font-mono flex items-center justify-between">
              <span>{isCitizenship ? t("groupForeigners", lang) : t("groupForeignBorn", lang)}</span>
              <span>{isCitizenship ? t("groupNationals", lang) : t("groupNativeBorn", lang)}</span>
            </div>
          </div>
          <div className="mt-2 pt-2 border-t border-slate-800/60 flex items-center justify-between text-[11px] text-slate-400">
            <span>{lang === "en" ? "Concentration in 15–64" : "Concentración en 15–64"}</span>
            {kpis.workingAgeKpi.yoyDelta && (
              <span className="font-mono text-slate-300">{kpis.workingAgeKpi.yoyDelta}</span>
            )}
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Card 3: Tasa de Empleo */}
        <div className="bg-slate-950/70 border border-slate-800/80 rounded-xl p-3.5 flex flex-col justify-between hover:border-slate-700/80 transition-colors">
          <div className="flex items-center justify-between text-slate-400 mb-1.5">
            <span className="text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-wider flex items-center gap-1.5">
              <TrendingUp className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-sky-400" />
              {t(kpis.employmentKpi.titleKey, lang)}
            </span>
            <ConceptHelpTrigger code="EMPLOYMENT_RATE" lang={lang} />
          </div>
          <div className="space-y-1">
            <div className="text-2xl font-bold text-white font-mono tabular-nums flex items-baseline gap-2">
              <span style={{ color: FOREIGNERS_COLOR }}>{kpis.employmentKpi.foreignValue}</span>
              <span className="text-xs text-slate-400 font-normal">vs</span>
              <span style={{ color: NATIONALS_COLOR }}>{kpis.employmentKpi.nationalValue}</span>
            </div>
            <div className="text-xs text-slate-400 font-mono flex items-center justify-between">
              <span>{isCitizenship ? t("groupForeigners", lang) : t("groupForeignBorn", lang)}</span>
              <span>{isCitizenship ? t("groupNationals", lang) : t("groupNativeBorn", lang)}</span>
            </div>
          </div>
          <div className="mt-2 pt-2 border-t border-slate-800/60 flex items-center justify-between text-[11px] text-slate-400">
            <span>{lang === "en" ? "LFS / EPA 15–64" : "EPA 15–64 años"}</span>
            {kpis.employmentKpi.yoyDelta && (
              <span className="font-mono text-slate-300">{kpis.employmentKpi.yoyDelta}</span>
            )}
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Card 4: Renta Mediana */}
        <div className="bg-slate-950/70 border border-slate-800/80 rounded-xl p-3.5 flex flex-col justify-between hover:border-slate-700/80 transition-colors">
          <div className="flex items-center justify-between text-slate-400 mb-1.5">
            <span className="text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-wider flex items-center gap-1.5">
              <Wallet className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-purple-400" />
              {t(kpis.incomeKpi.titleKey, lang)}
            </span>
            <ConceptHelpTrigger code="EQUIVALISED_INCOME" lang={lang} />
          </div>
          <div className="space-y-1">
            <div className="text-2xl font-bold text-white font-mono tabular-nums flex items-baseline gap-2">
              <span style={{ color: FOREIGNERS_COLOR }}>{kpis.incomeKpi.foreignValue}</span>
              <span className="text-xs text-slate-400 font-normal">vs</span>
              <span style={{ color: NATIONALS_COLOR }}>{kpis.incomeKpi.nationalValue}</span>
            </div>
            <div className="text-xs text-slate-400 font-mono flex items-center justify-between">
              <span>{isCitizenship ? t("groupForeigners", lang) : t("groupForeignBorn", lang)}</span>
              <span>{isCitizenship ? t("groupNationals", lang) : t("groupNativeBorn", lang)}</span>
            </div>
          </div>
          <div className="mt-2 pt-2 border-t border-slate-800/60 flex items-center justify-between text-[11px] text-slate-400">
            {kpis.incomeKpi.notice ? (
              <span className="text-[10px] text-amber-400/90 flex items-center gap-1 truncate" title={kpis.incomeKpi.notice}>
                <Info className="w-3 h-3 shrink-0" />
                <span className="truncate">{kpis.incomeKpi.notice}</span>
              </span>
            ) : (
              <span>{lang === "en" ? "EU-SILC 18+ net eq." : "ECV 18+ neta eq."}</span>
            )}
            {kpis.incomeKpi.yoyDelta && (
              <span className="font-mono text-slate-300">{kpis.incomeKpi.yoyDelta}</span>
            )}
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

export default NationalityControlBar;

--- FILE: frontend/components/nationality/NationalityIndicatorsView.tsx ---
"use client";

import React, { useState } from "react";
import type { NationalityIndicatorsItem, PopulationDefinition, Lang } from "@/lib/types";
import { getLatestCompleteYear } from "./nationalitySelectors";
import NationalityControlBar from "./NationalityControlBar";
import DemographicStructureView from "./DemographicStructureView";
import LaborMarketComparisonChart from "./LaborMarketComparisonChart";
import IncomeAndConditionsPanel from "./IncomeAndConditionsPanel";

interface NationalityIndicatorsViewProps {
  item: NationalityIndicatorsItem;
  lang?: Lang;
  locale?: Lang;
}

export function NationalityIndicatorsView({
  item,
  lang,
  locale = "es",
}: NationalityIndicatorsViewProps) {
  const activeLang = lang || locale || "es";
  const records = item.annualRecords || [];

  const [definition, setDefinition] = useState<PopulationDefinition>("citizenship");
  const [selectedYear, setSelectedYear] = useState<string>(() =>
    getLatestCompleteYear(records, "citizenship")
  );

  return (
    <div className="space-y-6">
      {/* Layer 0: Control Bar & KPI Summary */}
      <NationalityControlBar
        records={records}
        selectedYear={selectedYear}
        onSelectYear={setSelectedYear}
        definition={definition}
        onSelectDefinition={setDefinition}
        lang={activeLang}
      />

      {/* Layer 1: Demographic Structure & Representation */}
      <DemographicStructureView
        records={records}
        selectedYear={selectedYear}
        onSelectYear={setSelectedYear}
        definition={definition}
        lang={activeLang}
      />

      {/* Layer 2: Labor Market Historical Comparison */}
      <LaborMarketComparisonChart
        records={records}
        selectedYear={selectedYear}
        onSelectYear={setSelectedYear}
        definition={definition}
        lang={activeLang}
      />

      {/* Layer 3: Equivalised Net Income & Living Conditions */}
      <IncomeAndConditionsPanel
        records={records}
        selectedYear={selectedYear}
        onSelectYear={setSelectedYear}
        definition={definition}
        lang={activeLang}
      />
    </div>
  );
}

export default NationalityIndicatorsView;

--- FILE: frontend/components/nationality/nationalityPalette.ts ---
/**
 * Tokens de color neutrales para la visualización de indicadores por nacionalidad.
 * Regla de neutralidad radical: Nunca asignar colores semánticos (verde/rojo) como "bueno/malo"
 * a ninguno de los grupos poblacionales.
 */

export const NATIONALS_COLOR = "#38BDF8"; // Sky Blue (Españoles / Nacidos en España)
export const FOREIGNERS_COLOR = "#F59E0B"; // Amber (Extranjeros / Nacidos en el extranjero)
export const EU_FOREIGNERS_COLOR = "#34D399"; // Emerald (Extranjeros UE-27)
export const NON_EU_FOREIGNERS_COLOR = "#FB923C"; // Orange (Extranjeros No-UE)
export const NATURALIZATIONS_COLOR = "#A78BFA"; // Violet (Adquisiciones de nacionalidad)
export const TOTAL_COLOR = "#94A3B8"; // Slate (Total nacional)

export const AGE_BAND_COLORS = {
  under15: "#38BDF8", // Sky Blue (<15 años)
  workingAge15to64: "#818CF8", // Indigo (15–64 años)
  senior65plus: "#C084FC", // Purple (65+ años)
} as const;

export function getGroupColor(groupKey: string): string {
  switch (groupKey) {
    case "nationals":
      return NATIONALS_COLOR;
    case "foreigners":
      return FOREIGNERS_COLOR;
    case "euForeigners":
      return EU_FOREIGNERS_COLOR;
    case "nonEuForeigners":
      return NON_EU_FOREIGNERS_COLOR;
    case "total":
      return TOTAL_COLOR;
    case "naturalizations":
      return NATURALIZATIONS_COLOR;
    default:
      return "#94A3B8";
  }
}

describe("Nationality Selectors & Math", () => {
  it("getAvailableYears returns sorted years", () => {
    const years = getAvailableYears(mockRecords);
    expect(years).toEqual(["2023", "2024"]);
  });

  it("getLatestCompleteYear returns latest complete year", () => {
    const latest = getLatestCompleteYear(mockRecords, "citizenship");
    expect(latest).toBe("2024");
  });

  it("getHeadlineKpis calculates YoY delta properly for 2024", () => {
    const kpis = getHeadlineKpis(mockRecords, "2024", "citizenship", "es");
    // Pop: 13.4% vs 12.5% in 2023 = +0,90 pp
    expect(kpis.popKpi.foreignValue).toBe("13,4%");
    expect(kpis.popKpi.yoyDelta).toBe("+0,90 pp");

    // Working age: 83.1% vs 81.7% in 2023 = +1,4 pp
    expect(kpis.workingAgeKpi.foreignValue).toBe("83,1%");
    expect(kpis.workingAgeKpi.yoyDelta).toBe("+1,4 pp");

    // Employment rate: 62.3% vs 62.0% in 2023 = +0,3 pp
    expect(kpis.employmentKpi.foreignValue).toBe("62,3%");
    expect(kpis.employmentKpi.yoyDelta).toBe("+0,3 pp");

    // Median income: 13.800 € vs 13.500 € = +300 €
    expect(kpis.incomeKpi.foreignValue).toBe("13.800 €");
    expect(kpis.incomeKpi.yoyDelta).toBe("+300 €");
    expect(kpis.incomeKpi.notice).toBeUndefined();
  });

  it("getHeadlineKpis handles birthplace definition and notice", () => {
    const kpis = getHeadlineKpis(mockRecords, "2024", "birthplace", "es");
    expect(kpis.popKpi.foreignValue).toBe("18,1%");
    expect(kpis.incomeKpi.notice).toBe("ECV oficial por pasaporte");
  });

  it("getHeadlineKpis handles missing previous year gracefully without NaN", () => {
    const kpisFirstYear = getHeadlineKpis(mockRecords, "2023", "citizenship", "es");
    expect(kpisFirstYear.popKpi.yoyDelta).toBeNull();
    expect(kpisFirstYear.employmentKpi.yoyDelta).toBeNull();
    expect(kpisFirstYear.incomeKpi.yoyDelta).toBeNull();
  });

  it("buildRepresentationComparison returns 3 share rows without NaN", () => {
    const comp = buildRepresentationComparison(mockRecords[1], "citizenship");
    expect(comp.length).toBe(3);
    expect(comp[0].labelKey).toBe("totalPopulation");
    expect(comp[0].foreignSharePct).toBe(13.4);
    expect(comp[1].labelKey).toBe("workingAgeShare");
    expect(comp[1].foreignSharePct).toBe(16.8);
    expect(comp[2].labelKey).toBe("employedShare");
    expect(comp[2].foreignSharePct).toBe(15.0);
  });

  it("buildLaborTimeSeries formats chart rows and handles EU split", () => {
    const seriesWithEu = buildLaborTimeSeries(mockRecords, "citizenship", "employmentRatePct", true);
    expect(seriesWithEu.length).toBe(2);
    expect(seriesWithEu[1].foreigners).toBe(62.3);
    expect(seriesWithEu[1].nationals).toBe(66.8);
    expect(seriesWithEu[1].euForeigners).toBe(69.5);
    expect(seriesWithEu[1].nonEuForeigners).toBe(59.9);

    const seriesWithoutEu = buildLaborTimeSeries(mockRecords, "citizenship", "employmentRatePct", false);
    expect(seriesWithoutEu[1].euForeigners).toBeUndefined();
  });

  it("buildNaturalizationsSeries calculates cumulative sum", () => {
    const natSeries = buildNaturalizationsSeries(mockRecords);
    expect(natSeries.length).toBe(2);
    expect(natSeries[0].cumulativeSinceStart).toBe(240000);
    expect(natSeries[1].cumulativeSinceStart).toBe(490000);
  });

  it("buildLivingConditionsSeries calculates income ratio", () => {
    const livSeries = buildLivingConditionsSeries(mockRecords, "medianEquivalisedIncomeEur", false);
    expect(livSeries.length).toBe(2);
    // 2024: (13800 / 20400) * 100 = 67.6%
    expect(livSeries[1].ratioPct).toBe(67.6);
  });

  it("pure formatting functions format correctly", () => {
    expect(formatHeadcount(6502282, "es")).toBe("6,50 M");
    expect(formatHeadcount(6502282, "en")).toBe("6.50 M");
    expect(formatPercent(13.4, 1, "es")).toBe("13,4%");
    expect(formatPercent(13.4, 1, "en")).toBe("13.4%");
    expect(formatCurrencyEur(13800, "es")).toBe("13.800 €");
    expect(formatDelta(1.4, "pp", 1, "es")).toBe("+1,4 pp");
    expect(formatDelta(-0.5, "pp", 1, "es")).toBe("-0,5 pp");
  });
});

--- FILE: frontend/components/nationality/nationalitySelectors.ts ---
import type {
  NationalityAnnualRecord,
  PopulationDefinition,
  DemographicGroupStats,
  LaborGroupStats,
  LivingConditionsGroupStats,
} from "@/lib/types";

export type LaborMetricKey =
  | "employmentRatePct"
  | "activityRatePct"
  | "unemploymentRatePct"
  | "employedThousands";

export type LivingConditionsMetricKey =
  | "medianEquivalisedIncomeEur"
  | "meanEquivalisedIncomeEur"
  | "atRiskOfPovertyPct";

export interface KpiCardData {
  titleKey: string;
  foreignValue: string;
  nationalValue: string;
  foreignSubtext: string;
  nationalSubtext: string;
  yoyDelta: string | null;
  notice?: string;
}

export interface RepresentationComparisonRow {
  labelKey: string;
  foreignSharePct: number;
  nationalSharePct: number;
  foreignCount?: number;
  totalCount?: number;
}

export interface LaborTimeSeriesRow {
  year: string;
  nationals: number | null;
  foreigners: number | null;
  euForeigners?: number | null;
  nonEuForeigners?: number | null;
  total: number | null;
}

export interface LivingConditionsTimeSeriesRow {
  year: string;
  nationals: number | null;
  foreigners: number | null;
  euForeigners?: number | null;
  nonEuForeigners?: number | null;
  total: number | null;
  ratioPct: number | null;
}

export interface NaturalizationPoint {
  year: string;
  naturalizations: number | null;
  cumulativeSinceStart: number;
}

/** Formateo compacto en millones o miles con coma decimal española */
export function formatHeadcount(num: number | null | undefined, locale: string = "es"): string {
  if (num === null || num === undefined || Number.isNaN(num)) return "—";
  if (num >= 1_000_000) {
    const val = (num / 1_000_000).toFixed(2);
    return locale === "en" ? `${val} M` : `${val.replace(".", ",")} M`;
  }
  if (num >= 1_000) {
    const val = (num / 1_000).toFixed(1);
    return locale === "en" ? `${val} k` : `${val.replace(".", ",")} mil`;
  }
  return num.toLocaleString(locale === "en" ? "en-US" : "es-ES");
}

export function formatExactNumber(num: number | null | undefined, locale: string = "es"): string {
  if (num === null || num === undefined || Number.isNaN(num)) return "—";
  return num.toLocaleString(locale === "en" ? "en-US" : "es-ES");
}

export function formatPercent(num: number | null | undefined, decimals: number = 1, locale: string = "es"): string {
  if (num === null || num === undefined || Number.isNaN(num)) return "—";
  const fixed = num.toFixed(decimals);
  return `${locale === "en" ? fixed : fixed.replace(".", ",")}%`;
}

export function formatCurrencyEur(num: number | null | undefined, locale: string = "es"): string {
  if (num === null || num === undefined || Number.isNaN(num)) return "—";
  const intPart = Math.round(num).toLocaleString(locale === "en" ? "en-US" : "es-ES");
  return `${intPart} €`;
}

export function formatDelta(
  delta: number | null | undefined,
  unit: string = "pp",
  decimals: number = 1,
  locale: string = "es"
): string {
  if (delta === null || delta === undefined || Number.isNaN(delta)) return "";
  const sign = delta > 0 ? "+" : "";
  const valStr = delta.toFixed(decimals);
  const formatted = locale === "en" ? valStr : valStr.replace(".", ",");
  return `${sign}${formatted} ${unit}`;
}

export function getAvailableYears(records: NationalityAnnualRecord[]): string[] {
  if (!Array.isArray(records)) return [];
  return records.map((r) => r.year).sort((a, b) => Number(a) - Number(b));
}

export function getLatestCompleteYear(
  records: NationalityAnnualRecord[],
  definition: PopulationDefinition = "citizenship"
): string {
  if (!Array.isArray(records) || records.length === 0) return "2024";

  for (let i = records.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
    const r = records[i];
    const slice = r.definitions?.[definition];
    if (slice?.demographics?.foreigners && slice?.laborMarket?.foreigners) {
      return r.year;
    }
  }

  // Fallback a cualquier año con demografía
  for (let i = records.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
    const r = records[i];
    if (r.definitions?.[definition]?.demographics?.foreigners) {
      return r.year;
    }
  }

  return records[records.length - 1]?.year ?? "2024";
}

export function getRecordForYear(
  records: NationalityAnnualRecord[],
  year: string
): NationalityAnnualRecord | undefined {
  return records.find((r) => r.year === year);
}

export function getPreviousRecord(
  records: NationalityAnnualRecord[],
  year: string
): NationalityAnnualRecord | undefined {
  const sorted = [...records].sort((a, b) => Number(a.year) - Number(b.year));
  const idx = sorted.findIndex((r) => r.year === year);
  if (idx > 0) return sorted[idx - 1];
  return undefined;
}

export function getHeadlineKpis(
  records: NationalityAnnualRecord[],
  year: string,
  definition: PopulationDefinition = "citizenship",
  locale: string = "es"
): {
  popKpi: KpiCardData;
  workingAgeKpi: KpiCardData;
  employmentKpi: KpiCardData;
  incomeKpi: KpiCardData;
} {
  const current = getRecordForYear(records, year);
  const prev = getPreviousRecord(records, year);

  const curSlice = current?.definitions?.[definition];
  const prevSlice = prev?.definitions?.[definition];

  const curDemoFor = curSlice?.demographics?.foreigners;
  const curDemoNat = curSlice?.demographics?.nationals;
  const prevDemoFor = prevSlice?.demographics?.foreigners;

  // 1. Población total
  const popShare = curDemoFor?.shareOfTotalPct ?? 0;
  const prevPopShare = prevDemoFor?.shareOfTotalPct;
  const popDelta = prevPopShare !== undefined ? popShare - prevPopShare : null;

  const popKpi: KpiCardData = {
    titleKey: definition === "citizenship" ? "foreignPopulation" : "foreignBornPopulation",
    foreignValue: formatPercent(popShare, 1, locale),
    nationalValue: formatPercent(curDemoNat?.shareOfTotalPct ?? 100 - popShare, 1, locale),
    foreignSubtext: `${formatHeadcount(curDemoFor?.population, locale)} (${locale === "en" ? "of total" : "del total"})`,
    nationalSubtext: `${formatHeadcount(curDemoNat?.population, locale)}`,
    yoyDelta: popDelta !== null ? formatDelta(popDelta, "pp", 2, locale) : null,
  };

  // 2. Edad laboral (15-64)
  const workForPct = curDemoFor?.ageBands.workingAge15to64Pct ?? 0;
  const workNatPct = curDemoNat?.ageBands.workingAge15to64Pct ?? 0;
  const prevWorkForPct = prevDemoFor?.ageBands.workingAge15to64Pct;
  const workDelta = prevWorkForPct !== undefined ? workForPct - prevWorkForPct : null;

  const workingAgeKpi: KpiCardData = {
    titleKey: "workingAgeShare",
    foreignValue: formatPercent(workForPct, 1, locale),
    nationalValue: formatPercent(workNatPct, 1, locale),
    foreignSubtext: locale === "en" ? "Foreigners 15–64" : "Extranjeros (15–64)",
    nationalSubtext: locale === "en" ? "Spanish 15–64" : "Españoles (15–64)",
    yoyDelta: workDelta !== null ? formatDelta(workDelta, "pp", 1, locale) : null,
  };

  // 3. Tasa de empleo (15-64)
  const curLaborFor = curSlice?.laborMarket?.foreigners;
  const curLaborNat = curSlice?.laborMarket?.nationals;
  const prevLaborFor = prevSlice?.laborMarket?.foreigners;

  const empForRate = curLaborFor?.employmentRatePct;
  const empNatRate = curLaborNat?.employmentRatePct;
  const prevEmpForRate = prevLaborFor?.employmentRatePct;
  const empDelta = empForRate !== undefined && prevEmpForRate !== undefined && empForRate !== null && prevEmpForRate !== null
    ? empForRate - prevEmpForRate
    : null;

  const employmentKpi: KpiCardData = {
    titleKey: "metricEmploymentRate",
    foreignValue: formatPercent(empForRate, 1, locale),
    nationalValue: formatPercent(empNatRate, 1, locale),
    foreignSubtext: locale === "en" ? "Foreign 15–64" : "Extranjeros (15–64)",
    nationalSubtext: locale === "en" ? "Spanish 15–64" : "Españoles (15–64)",
    yoyDelta: empDelta !== null ? formatDelta(empDelta, "pp", 1, locale) : null,
  };

  // 4. Renta mediana
  const curLivFor = current?.livingConditions?.foreigners;
  const curLivNat = current?.livingConditions?.nationals;
  const prevLivFor = prev?.livingConditions?.foreigners;

  const medForInc = curLivFor?.medianEquivalisedIncomeEur;
  const medNatInc = curLivNat?.medianEquivalisedIncomeEur;
  const prevMedForInc = prevLivFor?.medianEquivalisedIncomeEur;
  const incDelta = medForInc !== undefined && prevMedForInc !== undefined && medForInc !== null && prevMedForInc !== null
    ? medForInc - prevMedForInc
    : null;

  const incomeKpi: KpiCardData = {
    titleKey: "metricMedianIncome",
    foreignValue: formatCurrencyEur(medForInc, locale),
    nationalValue: formatCurrencyEur(medNatInc, locale),
    foreignSubtext: locale === "en" ? "Foreign median" : "Extranjeros (mediana)",
    nationalSubtext: locale === "en" ? "Spanish median" : "Españoles (mediana)",
    yoyDelta: incDelta !== null ? formatDelta(incDelta, "€", 0, locale) : null,
    notice: definition === "birthplace"
      ? (locale === "en" ? "EU-SILC published by passport" : "ECV oficial por pasaporte")
      : undefined,
  };

  return { popKpi, workingAgeKpi, employmentKpi, incomeKpi };
}

export function buildRepresentationComparison(
  record: NationalityAnnualRecord | undefined,
  definition: PopulationDefinition = "citizenship"
): RepresentationComparisonRow[] {
  if (!record) return [];
  const slice = record.definitions?.[definition];
  const demoFor = slice?.demographics?.foreigners;
  const demoNat = slice?.demographics?.nationals;
  const demoTot = slice?.demographics?.total;

  const laborFor = slice?.laborMarket?.foreigners;
  const laborTot = slice?.laborMarket?.total;

  const popShare = demoFor?.shareOfTotalPct ?? 0;
  const workShare = demoFor?.shareOfWorkingAgePct ?? 0;
  const empShare = laborFor?.shareOfEmployedPct ?? 0;

  return [
    {
      labelKey: "totalPopulation",
      foreignSharePct: popShare,
      nationalSharePct: demoNat?.shareOfTotalPct ?? (100 - popShare),
      foreignCount: demoFor?.population,
      totalCount: demoTot?.population,
    },
    {
      labelKey: "workingAgeShare",
      foreignSharePct: workShare,
      nationalSharePct: demoNat?.shareOfWorkingAgePct ?? (100 - workShare),
      foreignCount: demoFor?.ageBands.workingAge15to64,
      totalCount: demoTot?.ageBands.workingAge15to64,
    },
    {
      labelKey: "employedShare",
      foreignSharePct: empShare,
      nationalSharePct: laborTot ? Math.max(0, 100 - empShare) : 0,
      foreignCount: laborFor?.employedThousands ? Math.round(laborFor.employedThousands * 1000) : undefined,
      totalCount: laborTot?.employedThousands ? Math.round(laborTot.employedThousands * 1000) : undefined,
    },
  ];
}

export function buildLaborTimeSeries(
  records: NationalityAnnualRecord[],
  definition: PopulationDefinition,
  metric: LaborMetricKey,
  includeEuSplit: boolean = false
): LaborTimeSeriesRow[] {
  if (!Array.isArray(records)) return [];

  return records.map((rec) => {
    const labor = rec.definitions?.[definition]?.laborMarket;
    const row: LaborTimeSeriesRow = {
      year: rec.year,
      nationals: labor?.nationals?.[metric] ?? null,
      foreigners: labor?.foreigners?.[metric] ?? null,
      total: labor?.total?.[metric] ?? null,
    };

    if (includeEuSplit) {
      row.euForeigners = labor?.euForeigners?.[metric] ?? null;
      row.nonEuForeigners = labor?.nonEuForeigners?.[metric] ?? null;
    }

    return row;
  });
}

export function buildNaturalizationsSeries(
  records: NationalityAnnualRecord[]
): NaturalizationPoint[] {
  if (!Array.isArray(records)) return [];

  let cumulative = 0;
  return records.map((rec) => {
    const val = rec.naturalizations;
    if (val !== null && val !== undefined) {
      cumulative += val;
    }
    return {
      year: rec.year,
      naturalizations: val,
      cumulativeSinceStart: cumulative,
    };
  });
}

export function buildLivingConditionsSeries(
  records: NationalityAnnualRecord[],
  metric: LivingConditionsMetricKey,
  includeEuSplit: boolean = false
): LivingConditionsTimeSeriesRow[] {
  if (!Array.isArray(records)) return [];

  return records.map((rec) => {
    const liv = rec.livingConditions;
    const natVal = liv?.nationals?.[metric] ?? null;
    const forVal = liv?.foreigners?.[metric] ?? null;

    let ratio: number | null = null;
    if (natVal !== null && forVal !== null && natVal > 0) {
      ratio = roundTo((forVal / natVal) * 100, 1);
    }

    const row: LivingConditionsTimeSeriesRow = {
      year: rec.year,
      nationals: natVal,
      foreigners: forVal,
      total: liv?.total?.[metric] ?? null,
      ratioPct: ratio,
    };

    if (includeEuSplit) {
      row.euForeigners = liv?.euForeigners?.[metric] ?? null;
      row.nonEuForeigners = liv?.nonEuForeigners?.[metric] ?? null;
    }

    return row;
  });
}

function roundTo(val: number, decimals: number): number {
  const p = Math.pow(10, decimals);
  return Math.round(val * p) / p;
}

--- FILE: frontend/components/shared/FiscalBalanceDashboard.tsx ---
import { useState, useMemo } from "react";
import {
  ResponsiveContainer,
  BarChart,
  Bar,
  XAxis,
  YAxis,
  Tooltip,
  CartesianGrid,
  ReferenceLine,
  type TooltipProps,
} from "recharts";
import type { FiscalBalanceIndicator, Locale } from "@/lib/types";
import { ChartCard, CHART_THEME } from "./ChartCard";
import { t } from "@/lib/i18n";
import { formatNumber, formatPercent } from "@/lib/format";

interface FiscalBalanceDashboardProps {
  indicator: FiscalBalanceIndicator;
  locale: Locale;
}

type TabMode = "ratio" | "capita" | "total";

interface FiscalTooltipProps extends TooltipProps<number, string> {
  activeTab: TabMode;
  locale: Locale;
}

function FiscalTooltip({ active, payload, label, activeTab, locale }: FiscalTooltipProps) {
  if (!active || !payload || !payload.length) return null;

  return (
    <div className="bg-slate-900 text-white p-3.5 rounded-xl shadow-2xl border border-slate-700 text-xs max-w-xs space-y-2">
      <div className="flex items-center justify-between border-b border-slate-800 pb-1.5 font-bold">
        <span>
          {t("yearLabel", locale)} {label}
        </span>
        <span className="text-blue-400 font-semibold">
          {activeTab === "ratio"
            ? t("fiscalTabRatio", locale)
            : activeTab === "capita"
            ? t("fiscalTabCapita", locale)
            : t("fiscalTabTotal", locale)}
        </span>
      </div>
      <div className="space-y-1 text-[11px]">
        {payload.map((entry, idx) => {
          const val = Number(entry.value) || 0;
          let displayVal = "";
          if (activeTab === "ratio") {
            const isSurplus = val >= 100;
            displayVal = `${formatPercent(val, locale)} (${
              isSurplus ? t("fiscalSurplus", locale) : t("fiscalDeficit", locale)
            })`;
          } else if (activeTab === "capita") {
            displayVal = `${val >= 0 ? "+" : ""}${formatNumber(val, locale)} €/hab`;
          } else {
            displayVal = `${formatNumber(val, locale)} MM€`;
          }

          return (
            <div key={idx} className="flex items-center justify-between gap-3">
              <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 truncate">
                <span
                  className="w-2.5 h-2.5 rounded-full flex-shrink-0"
                  style={{ backgroundColor: entry.color }}
                />
                <span className="truncate text-slate-300">{entry.name}</span>
              </div>
              <span className="font-semibold text-white tabular-nums font-mono">
                {displayVal}
              </span>
            </div>
          );
        })}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

export default function FiscalBalanceDashboard({
  indicator,
  locale,
}: FiscalBalanceDashboardProps) {
  const [activeTab, setActiveTab] = useState<TabMode>("ratio");

  const points = indicator.points || [];

  const ratioData = useMemo(
    () =>
      points.map((p) => ({
        year: p.year,
        foreignRatio: p.ratio.foreign,
        spanishRatio: p.ratio.spanish,
      })),
    [points]
  );

  const capitaData = useMemo(
    () =>
      points.map((p) => ({
        year: p.year,
        foreignCapita: p.perCapitaEur.foreign,
        spanishCapita: p.perCapitaEur.spanish,
      })),
    [points]
  );

  const totalData = useMemo(
    () =>
      points.map((p) => ({
        year: p.year,
        totalRevenue: p.totalRevenueMmEur,
        totalSpending: p.totalSpendingMmEur,
        deficit: p.deficitMmEur,
      })),
    [points]
  );

  const tabSubtitle =
    activeTab === "ratio"
      ? t("fiscalRatioDesc", locale)
      : activeTab === "capita"
      ? t("fiscalCapitaDesc", locale)
      : t("fiscalTotalDesc", locale);

  const questionText = typeof indicator.question === "object" ? indicator.question[locale] : indicator.question;

  return (
    <ChartCard
      title={
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
          <span className="text-base">⚖️</span>
          <span>{questionText}</span>
        </div>
      }
      subtitle={tabSubtitle}
      headerRight={
        <div className="flex items-center gap-1 bg-slate-800/80 p-1 rounded-xl border border-slate-700/60 text-xs font-bold">
          <button
            onClick={() => setActiveTab("ratio")}
            className={`px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg transition-colors ${
              activeTab === "ratio"
                ? "bg-blue-600 text-white shadow-xs"
                : "text-slate-400 hover:text-white"
            }`}
          >
            {t("fiscalTabRatio", locale)}
          </button>
          <button
            onClick={() => setActiveTab("capita")}
            className={`px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg transition-colors ${
              activeTab === "capita"
                ? "bg-blue-600 text-white shadow-xs"
                : "text-slate-400 hover:text-white"
            }`}
          >
            {t("fiscalTabCapita", locale)}
          </button>
          <button
            onClick={() => setActiveTab("total")}
            className={`px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg transition-colors ${
              activeTab === "total"
                ? "bg-blue-600 text-white shadow-xs"
                : "text-slate-400 hover:text-white"
            }`}
          >
            {t("fiscalTabTotal", locale)}
          </button>
        </div>
      }
    >
      <div className="w-full h-[380px] sm:h-[440px] pt-2">
        <ResponsiveContainer width="100%" height="100%">
          {activeTab === "ratio" ? (
            <BarChart data={ratioData} margin={{ top: 12, right: 16, left: 0, bottom: 8 }}>
              <CartesianGrid {...CHART_THEME.grid} />
              <XAxis dataKey="year" {...CHART_THEME.axis} />
              <YAxis
                {...CHART_THEME.axis}
                domain={[0, (dataMax: number) => Math.ceil(dataMax * 1.15)]}
                tickFormatter={(v: number) => `${v}%`}
              />
              <Tooltip content={<FiscalTooltip activeTab="ratio" locale={locale} />} />
              <ReferenceLine
                y={100}
                stroke="#ef4444"
                strokeDasharray="4 4"
                label={{
                  value: "100% (Equilibrio)",
                  fill: "#ef4444",
                  fontSize: 10,
                  position: "top",
                }}
              />
              <Bar
                dataKey="foreignRatio"
                name={t("fiscalForeignRatio", locale)}
                fill="#10b981"
                radius={[4, 4, 0, 0]}
              />
              <Bar
                dataKey="spanishRatio"
                name={t("fiscalSpanishRatio", locale)}
                fill="#64748b"
                radius={[4, 4, 0, 0]}
              />
            </BarChart>
          ) : activeTab === "capita" ? (
            <BarChart data={capitaData} margin={{ top: 12, right: 16, left: 0, bottom: 8 }}>
              <CartesianGrid {...CHART_THEME.grid} />
              <XAxis dataKey="year" {...CHART_THEME.axis} />
              <YAxis
                {...CHART_THEME.axis}
                domain={[
                  (dataMin: number) => Math.floor(dataMin * 1.15),
                  (dataMax: number) => Math.ceil(dataMax * 1.15),
                ]}
                tickFormatter={(v: number) => `${v} €`}
              />
              <Tooltip content={<FiscalTooltip activeTab="capita" locale={locale} />} />
              <ReferenceLine y={0} stroke="#94a3b8" />
              <Bar
                dataKey="foreignCapita"
                name={t("fiscalForeignCapita", locale)}
                fill="#10b981"
                radius={[4, 4, 0, 0]}
              />
              <Bar
                dataKey="spanishCapita"
                name={t("fiscalSpanishCapita", locale)}
                fill="#64748b"
                radius={[4, 4, 0, 0]}
              />
            </BarChart>
          ) : (
            <BarChart data={totalData} margin={{ top: 12, right: 16, left: 0, bottom: 8 }}>
              <CartesianGrid {...CHART_THEME.grid} />
              <XAxis dataKey="year" {...CHART_THEME.axis} />
              <YAxis
                {...CHART_THEME.axis}
                domain={[0, (dataMax: number) => Math.ceil(dataMax * 1.15)]}
                tickFormatter={(v: number) => `${v} MM€`}
              />
              <Tooltip content={<FiscalTooltip activeTab="total" locale={locale} />} />
              <Bar
                dataKey="totalRevenue"
                name={t("fiscalTotalRevenue", locale)}
                fill="#3b82f6"
                radius={[4, 4, 0, 0]}
              />
              <Bar
                dataKey="totalSpending"
                name={t("fiscalTotalSpending", locale)}
                fill="#8b5cf6"
                radius={[4, 4, 0, 0]}
              />
            </BarChart>
          )}
        </ResponsiveContainer>
      </div>
    </ChartCard>
  );
}
