--- FILE: frontend/components/shared/IndicatorVisualization.tsx ---
"use client";

import dynamic from "next/dynamic";
import type { Indicator, Locale } from "@/lib/types";
import { isFiscalBalanceItem, isNationalityIndicatorsItem } from "@/lib/types";
import { ChartSkeleton } from "./ChartSkeleton";

const TimeSeriesChart = dynamic(() => import("./TimeSeriesChart"), {
  loading: () => <ChartSkeleton />,
});
const FiscalBalanceDashboard = dynamic(() => import("./FiscalBalanceDashboard"), {
  loading: () => <ChartSkeleton />,
});
const FiscalBalanceView = dynamic(() => import("../fiscal/FiscalBalanceView"), {
  loading: () => <ChartSkeleton />,
});
const NationalityIndicatorsView = dynamic(() => import("../nationality/NationalityIndicatorsView"), {
  loading: () => <ChartSkeleton />,
});
const AdminRegistersChart = dynamic(() => import("../admin-registers/AdminRegistersChart"), {
  loading: () => <ChartSkeleton />,
});
const ImmigrationExplorer = dynamic(() => import("./immigration/ImmigrationExplorer"), {
  loading: () => <ChartSkeleton />,
});

export function IndicatorVisualization({
  indicator,
  locale,
}: {
  indicator: Indicator | any;
  locale: Locale;
}) {
  if (isFiscalBalanceItem(indicator as any) || (indicator && "annualBalances" in indicator)) {
    return <FiscalBalanceView item={indicator as any} lang={locale} locale={locale} />;
  }

  if (indicator?.kind === "admin-registers-nationality") {
    return <AdminRegistersChart records={indicator.annualRecords || []} lang={locale} />;
  }

  if (
    isNationalityIndicatorsItem(indicator as any) ||
    indicator?.kind === "nationality-indicators" ||
    indicator?.chartType === "nationality-indicators" ||
    (indicator && "annualRecords" in indicator)
  ) {
    return <NationalityIndicatorsView item={indicator as any} lang={locale} locale={locale} />;
  }

  switch (indicator.kind) {
    case "time-series":
      return <TimeSeriesChart indicator={indicator} locale={locale} />;
    case "fiscal-balance":
      return <FiscalBalanceDashboard indicator={indicator} locale={locale} />;
    case "immigration-flow":
      return <ImmigrationExplorer indicator={indicator} locale={locale} />;
    default: {
      return <TimeSeriesChart indicator={indicator} locale={locale} />;
    }
  }
}

--- FILE: frontend/components/shared/InteractiveChart.tsx ---
"use client";

import { useMemo } from "react";
import {
  ResponsiveContainer,
  LineChart,
  Line,
  BarChart,
  Bar,
  XAxis,
  YAxis,
  Tooltip,
  CartesianGrid,
  type TooltipProps,
} from "recharts";
import type { DatoItem, Lang } from "@/lib/types";
import { formatNumber, formatPercent } from "@/lib/format";

interface InteractiveChartProps {
  item: DatoItem;
  lang?: Lang;
  locale?: Lang;
}

interface CustomTooltipProps extends TooltipProps<number, string> {
  unit: string;
  lang: Lang;
}

function StandardTooltip({ active, payload, label, unit, lang }: CustomTooltipProps) {
  if (!active || !payload || !payload.length) return null;
  const entry = payload[0];
  const val = Number(entry.value) || 0;

  const isPercent = unit.includes("%");
  const formattedVal = isPercent ? formatPercent(val, lang) : formatNumber(val, lang);

  return (
    <div className="bg-slate-900 text-white p-3 rounded-xl shadow-2xl border border-slate-700 text-xs space-y-1 font-sans">
      <div className="font-semibold text-slate-300">{label}</div>
      <div className="text-sm font-bold text-blue-400 font-mono tabular-nums">
        {formattedVal} {unit ? `(${unit})` : ""}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

export function InteractiveChart({ item, lang, locale = "es" }: InteractiveChartProps) {
  const activeLang = lang || locale || "es";
  const { chartType, unit } = item;

  const rawPoints = (item as any).dataPoints || (item as any).points || [];

  const data = useMemo(() => {
    return rawPoints.map((pt: any) => ({
      period: pt.year || pt.period || "",
      value: typeof pt.value === "number" ? pt.value : Number(pt.value) || 0,
    }));
  }, [rawPoints]);

  if (!data || data.length === 0) {
    return (
      <div className="bg-slate-900 border border-slate-800 rounded-2xl p-12 text-center text-slate-400 text-xs">
        No hay datos suficientes para mostrar la gráfica.
      </div>
    );
  }

  const isBar = chartType === "bar";

  return (
    <div className="bg-slate-900 border border-slate-800 rounded-2xl p-4 sm:p-6 shadow-xl space-y-3">
      <div className="w-full h-[360px] sm:h-[420px]">
        <ResponsiveContainer width="100%" height="100%">
          {isBar ? (
            <BarChart data={data} margin={{ top: 12, right: 16, left: -10, bottom: 8 }}>
              <CartesianGrid strokeDasharray="3 3" stroke="#1E293B" vertical={false} />
              <XAxis
                dataKey="period"
                stroke="#64748B"
                tick={{ fill: "#94A3B8", fontSize: 11, fontFamily: "monospace" }}
                axisLine={{ stroke: "#334155" }}
                tickLine={false}
              />
              <YAxis
                stroke="#64748B"
                tick={{ fill: "#94A3B8", fontSize: 11, fontFamily: "monospace" }}
                axisLine={{ stroke: "#334155" }}
                domain={[0, (dataMax: number) => Math.ceil(dataMax * 1.1)]}
                tickFormatter={(v: number) => formatNumber(v, activeLang)}
              />
              <Tooltip
                content={<StandardTooltip unit={unit} lang={activeLang} />}
              />
              <Bar dataKey="value" fill="#3B82F6" radius={[4, 4, 0, 0]} />
            </BarChart>
          ) : (
            <LineChart data={data} margin={{ top: 12, right: 16, left: -10, bottom: 8 }}>
              <CartesianGrid strokeDasharray="3 3" stroke="#1E293B" vertical={false} />
              <XAxis
                dataKey="period"
                stroke="#64748B"
                tick={{ fill: "#94A3B8", fontSize: 11, fontFamily: "monospace" }}
                axisLine={{ stroke: "#334155" }}
                tickLine={false}
              />
              <YAxis
                stroke="#64748B"
                tick={{ fill: "#94A3B8", fontSize: 11, fontFamily: "monospace" }}
                axisLine={{ stroke: "#334155" }}
                domain={[
                  (dataMin: number) => (dataMin < 0 ? Math.floor(dataMin * 1.1) : 0),
                  (dataMax: number) => Math.ceil(dataMax * 1.1),
                ]}
                tickFormatter={(v: number) => formatNumber(v, activeLang)}
              />
              <Tooltip
                content={<StandardTooltip unit={unit} lang={activeLang} />}
              />
              <Line
                type="monotone"
                dataKey="value"
                stroke="#3B82F6"
                strokeWidth={3}
                dot={{ r: 3, fill: "#3B82F6" }}
                activeDot={{ r: 6, fill: "#60a5fa" }}
              />
            </LineChart>
          )}
        </ResponsiveContainer>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

export default InteractiveChart;

--- FILE: frontend/components/shared/MethodologicalNotes.tsx ---
import type { Locale } from "@/lib/types";
import { t } from "@/lib/i18n";

interface MethodologicalNotesProps {
  notes: string[];
  locale: Locale;
}

export function MethodologicalNotes({ notes, locale }: MethodologicalNotesProps) {
  if (!notes || notes.length === 0) return null;

  return (
    <section className="bg-slate-900 rounded-2xl border border-slate-800 p-5 space-y-3 shadow-xl">
      <h3 className="text-xs font-bold text-slate-300 uppercase tracking-wider flex items-center gap-2">
        <span className="w-2 h-2 rounded-full bg-blue-500" />
        {t("methodologicalNotesTitle", locale)}
      </h3>
      <ul className="space-y-2 text-xs text-slate-400">
        {notes.map((note, idx) => (
          <li key={idx} className="flex items-start gap-2">
            <span className="text-blue-400 font-mono mt-0.5">•</span>
            <span className="leading-relaxed">{note}</span>
          </li>
        ))}
      </ul>
    </section>
  );
}

export default MethodologicalNotes;

--- FILE: frontend/components/shared/SourceCard.tsx ---
import { ShieldCheck, Calendar, ExternalLink, AlertCircle } from "lucide-react";
import type { Indicator, Locale, Lang } from "@/lib/types";
import { sanitizeHttpUrl } from "@/lib/url";
import { t } from "@/lib/i18n";

interface SourceCardProps {
  sources?: Indicator["sources"] | any[];
  lastUpdated?: string;
  locale?: Locale;
  lang?: Lang;
}

export function SourceCard({ sources, lastUpdated, locale, lang }: SourceCardProps) {
  const activeLocale: Locale = (lang || locale || "es") as Locale;

  if (!sources || sources.length === 0) {
    return (
      <div className="bg-slate-900 border border-slate-800 rounded-2xl p-5 shadow-sm space-y-4">
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between flex-wrap gap-2">
          <span className="text-[11px] uppercase tracking-wider font-bold text-slate-400 flex items-center gap-1.5">
            <ShieldCheck className="w-4 h-4 text-blue-400" />
            {t("sourceTitle", activeLocale)}
          </span>
        </div>
        <div className="p-5 rounded-xl border border-dashed border-slate-800 bg-slate-950/50 text-center space-y-2">
          <AlertCircle className="w-5 h-5 text-amber-400 mx-auto" />
          <p className="text-xs font-semibold text-white">
            {t("noDataYet", activeLocale)}
          </p>
        </div>
      </div>
    );
  }

  const uniqueInstitutions = Array.from(
    new Set(
      sources.map((s: any) =>
        typeof s.institution === "object"
          ? s.institution[activeLocale] || s.institution.es
          : s.institution
      ).filter(Boolean)
    )
  );
  const headerInstitution =
    uniqueInstitutions.length > 1
      ? uniqueInstitutions.join(" / ")
      : typeof sources[0]?.institution === "object"
      ? sources[0]?.institution[activeLocale] || sources[0]?.institution.es
      : sources[0]?.institution ?? "";

  const subtitleText =
    sources.length === 1
      ? t("sourcesSubtitleSingle", activeLocale)
      : t("sourcesSubtitleMultiple", activeLocale);

  return (
    <section className="bg-slate-900 border border-slate-800 rounded-2xl p-5 shadow-xl space-y-4">
      <div className="flex items-center justify-between flex-wrap gap-2">
        <span className="text-[11px] uppercase tracking-wider font-bold text-slate-400 flex items-center gap-1.5">
          <ShieldCheck className="w-4 h-4 text-blue-400" />
          {sources.length > 1 ? t("sourcesTitle", activeLocale) : t("sourceTitle", activeLocale)}
        </span>
      </div>

      <div className="space-y-1">
        <p className="text-sm font-semibold text-white">{headerInstitution}</p>
        <p className="text-xs text-slate-400">{subtitleText}</p>
      </div>

      <div className="space-y-3">
        {sources.map((s: any, idx: number) => {
          const safeUrl = sanitizeHttpUrl(s.url);
          const instText =
            typeof s.institution === "object"
              ? s.institution[activeLocale] || s.institution.es
              : s.institution;
          const titleText =
            typeof s.title === "object"
              ? s.title[activeLocale] || s.title.es
              : s.title;
          const shortName =
            typeof s.shortName === "object"
              ? s.shortName?.[activeLocale] || s.shortName?.es || titleText
              : s.shortName || titleText;

          const effectiveDate = s.lastUpdated ?? lastUpdated;

          return (
            <div
              key={`${s.tableId ?? "src"}-${idx}`}
              className="p-4 rounded-xl border transition-all flex flex-col sm:flex-row items-start sm:items-center justify-between gap-4 bg-slate-950/50 border-slate-800 hover:border-slate-700"
            >
              <div className="space-y-1.5">
                <div className="flex items-center gap-2 flex-wrap">
                  {uniqueInstitutions.length > 1 && (
                    <span className="text-[11px] font-semibold text-amber-400 bg-amber-950/80 px-2 py-0.5 rounded border border-amber-800/80">
                      {instText}
                    </span>
                  )}
                  <span className="text-xs font-bold text-white">{titleText}</span>
                  {s.period && (
                    <span className="text-[11px] font-semibold text-blue-400 bg-blue-950 px-2 py-0.5 rounded border border-blue-800/80 font-mono">
                      {s.period}
                    </span>
                  )}
                </div>

                <div className="flex items-center gap-3 text-[11px] text-slate-400 flex-wrap">
                  {s.tableId && (
                    <span className="font-mono text-slate-300 font-semibold">
                      {t("officialTable", activeLocale)} {s.tableId}
                    </span>
                  )}
                  {effectiveDate && (
                    <span className="flex items-center gap-1">
                      <Calendar className="w-3 h-3 text-slate-500" />
                      {t("lastUpdated", activeLocale)}: {effectiveDate}
                    </span>
                  )}
                </div>
              </div>

              {safeUrl ? (
                <a
                  href={safeUrl}
                  target="_blank"
                  rel="noopener noreferrer"
                  className="inline-flex items-center gap-2 text-xs font-semibold text-blue-300 hover:text-white bg-blue-950 hover:bg-blue-900 px-4 py-2.5 rounded-xl transition-colors border border-blue-800/80 shrink-0 w-full sm:w-auto justify-center"
                >
                  <span>{shortName}</span>
                  <ExternalLink className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                </a>
              ) : (
                <span className="text-xs text-slate-500 italic">URL no disponible</span>
              )}
            </div>
          );
        })}
      </div>
    </section>
  );
}

export default SourceCard;

--- FILE: frontend/components/shared/TimeSeriesChart.tsx ---
"use client";

import { useMemo } from "react";
import {
  ResponsiveContainer,
  LineChart,
  Line,
  BarChart,
  Bar,
  XAxis,
  YAxis,
  Tooltip,
  CartesianGrid,
  type TooltipProps,
} from "recharts";
import type { TimeSeriesIndicator, Locale } from "@/lib/types";
import { ChartCard, CHART_THEME } from "./ChartCard";
import { formatIndicatorValue } from "@/lib/format";

interface TimeSeriesChartProps {
  indicator: TimeSeriesIndicator;
  locale: Locale;
}

interface CustomTooltipProps extends TooltipProps<number, string> {
  unit: string;
  format: TimeSeriesIndicator["valueFormat"];
  locale: Locale;
}

function SeriesTooltip({ active, payload, label, unit, format, locale }: CustomTooltipProps) {
  if (!active || !payload || !payload.length) return null;
  const entry = payload[0];
  const val = Number(entry.value) || 0;

  return (
    <div className="bg-slate-900 text-white p-3 rounded-xl shadow-2xl border border-slate-700 text-xs space-y-1">
      <div className="font-semibold text-slate-300">{label}</div>
      <div className="text-sm font-bold text-blue-400 font-mono tabular-nums">
        {formatIndicatorValue(val, format || "number", locale)} {unit ? `(${unit})` : ""}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

export default function TimeSeriesChart({ indicator, locale }: TimeSeriesChartProps) {
  const { points, chartStyle, unit, valueFormat } = indicator;

  const data = useMemo(
    () =>
      points.map((pt) => ({
        period: pt.period,
        value: pt.value,
      })),
    [points]
  );

  const unitStr = unit[locale];

  return (
    <ChartCard>
      <div className="w-full h-[360px] sm:h-[420px]">
        <ResponsiveContainer width="100%" height="100%">
          {chartStyle === "bar" ? (
            <BarChart data={data} margin={{ top: 12, right: 16, left: 0, bottom: 8 }}>
              <CartesianGrid {...CHART_THEME.grid} />
              <XAxis dataKey="period" {...CHART_THEME.axis} />
              <YAxis
                {...CHART_THEME.axis}
                domain={[0, (dataMax: number) => Math.ceil(dataMax * 1.1)]}
                tickFormatter={(v: number) => formatIndicatorValue(v, valueFormat, locale, true)}
              />
              <Tooltip
                content={<SeriesTooltip unit={unitStr} format={valueFormat} locale={locale} />}
              />
              <Bar dataKey="value" fill={CHART_THEME.colors.primary} radius={[4, 4, 0, 0]} />
            </BarChart>
          ) : (
            <LineChart data={data} margin={{ top: 12, right: 16, left: 0, bottom: 8 }}>
              <CartesianGrid {...CHART_THEME.grid} />
              <XAxis dataKey="period" {...CHART_THEME.axis} />
              <YAxis
                {...CHART_THEME.axis}
                domain={[
                  (dataMin: number) => (dataMin < 0 ? Math.floor(dataMin * 1.1) : 0),
                  (dataMax: number) => Math.ceil(dataMax * 1.1),
                ]}
                tickFormatter={(v: number) => formatIndicatorValue(v, valueFormat, locale, true)}
              />
              <Tooltip
                content={<SeriesTooltip unit={unitStr} format={valueFormat} locale={locale} />}
              />
              <Line
                type="monotone"
                dataKey="value"
                stroke={CHART_THEME.colors.primary}
                strokeWidth={3}
                dot={{ r: 3, fill: CHART_THEME.colors.primary }}
                activeDot={{ r: 6, fill: "#60a5fa" }}
              />
            </LineChart>
          )}
        </ResponsiveContainer>
      </div>
    </ChartCard>
  );
}

--- FILE: frontend/components/shared/immigration/ChoroplethMap.tsx ---
"use client";

import React, { useState, useMemo, useRef, useCallback } from "react";
import projectedWorld from "@/data/geo/projected-world.json";
import { classifyChoropleth } from "./jenks";
import type { Locale } from "@/lib/types";
import { getCountryName } from "@/lib/geography";
import { formatNumber, formatCompactNumber } from "@/lib/format";
import { t } from "@/lib/i18n";
import { ZoomIn, ZoomOut, Maximize2, Globe } from "lucide-react";

interface ChoroplethMapProps {
  timeframeLabel: string;
  volumeByCountryIso2: Record<string, number>;
  totalVolume: number;
  hoveredIso2: string | null;
  onHoverIso2: (iso: string | null) => void;
  pinnedIso2: string | null;
  onPinIso2: (iso: string | null) => void;
  locale: Locale;
}

const PALETTE = ["#0284c7", "#2563eb", "#7c3aed", "#c026d3", "#db2777"] as const;

export default function ChoroplethMap({
  timeframeLabel,
  volumeByCountryIso2,
  hoveredIso2,
  onHoverIso2,
  pinnedIso2,
  onPinIso2,
  locale,
}: ChoroplethMapProps) {
  const [zoom, setZoom] = useState(1);
  const [pan, setPan] = useState({ x: 0, y: 0 });
  const [isPointerDown, setIsPointerDown] = useState(false);
  const pointerStartRef = useRef({ x: 0, y: 0, panX: 0, panY: 0 });
  const svgRef = useRef<SVGSVGElement | null>(null);

  const { viewBox, countries, graticulePath, width, height } = projectedWorld;

  // Compute Natural Breaks Classification
  const values = useMemo(
    () =>
      Object.entries(volumeByCountryIso2)
        .filter(([iso, val]) => iso !== "ES" && val > 0)
        .map(([_, val]) => val),
    [volumeByCountryIso2]
  );

  const classification = useMemo(
    () => classifyChoropleth(values, PALETTE),
    [values]
  );

  const activeSelectedIso = pinnedIso2 || hoveredIso2;

  // Pointer drag handling with screenCTM or rect scaling
  const handlePointerDown = (e: React.PointerEvent<SVGSVGElement>) => {
    setIsPointerDown(true);
    e.currentTarget.setPointerCapture(e.pointerId);
    pointerStartRef.current = {
      x: e.clientX,
      y: e.clientY,
      panX: pan.x,
      panY: pan.y,
    };
  };

  const handlePointerMove = (e: React.PointerEvent<SVGSVGElement>) => {
    if (!isPointerDown || !svgRef.current) return;
    const dx = e.clientX - pointerStartRef.current.x;
    const dy = e.clientY - pointerStartRef.current.y;

    const rect = svgRef.current.getBoundingClientRect();
    const factorX = width / rect.width;
    const factorY = height / rect.height;

    setPan({
      x: pointerStartRef.current.panX + dx * factorX,
      y: pointerStartRef.current.panY + dy * factorY,
    });
  };

  const handlePointerUp = (e: React.PointerEvent<SVGSVGElement>) => {
    setIsPointerDown(false);
    try {
      e.currentTarget.releasePointerCapture(e.pointerId);
    } catch {
      // Ignore if pointer capture already lost
    }
  };

  const handleWheel = useCallback((e: React.WheelEvent<SVGSVGElement>) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    const delta = e.deltaY < 0 ? 0.15 : -0.15;
    setZoom((prev) => Math.min(3.5, Math.max(0.8, prev + delta)));
  }, []);

  const handleResetZoom = () => {
    setZoom(1);
    setPan({ x: 0, y: 0 });
  };

  return (
    <div className="relative bg-[#060913] border border-slate-800 rounded-2xl overflow-hidden shadow-2xl flex flex-col justify-between select-none">
      {/* Top Map Control Bar Overlay */}
      <div className="absolute top-4 left-4 z-10 flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2 bg-slate-900/95 backdrop-blur-md p-1.5 rounded-xl border border-slate-700/80 shadow-xl">
        <button
          type="button"
          onClick={() => setZoom((z) => Math.min(3.5, z + 0.25))}
          className="p-1.5 rounded-lg bg-slate-800 hover:bg-slate-700 text-slate-300 hover:text-white transition-colors"
          title={t("zoomIn", locale)}
          aria-label={t("zoomIn", locale)}
        >
          <ZoomIn className="w-4 h-4" />
        </button>
        <button
          type="button"
          onClick={() => setZoom((z) => Math.max(0.8, z - 0.25))}
          className="p-1.5 rounded-lg bg-slate-800 hover:bg-slate-700 text-slate-300 hover:text-white transition-colors"
          title={t("zoomOut", locale)}
          aria-label={t("zoomOut", locale)}
        >
          <ZoomOut className="w-4 h-4" />
        </button>
        <button
          type="button"
          onClick={handleResetZoom}
          className="p-1.5 rounded-lg bg-slate-800 hover:bg-slate-700 text-slate-300 hover:text-white transition-colors"
          title={t("resetZoom", locale)}
          aria-label={t("resetZoom", locale)}
        >
          <Maximize2 className="w-4 h-4" />
        </button>

        <div className="w-px h-5 bg-slate-700 my-auto mx-1" />

        <div className="px-2.5 py-1 rounded-lg text-xs font-bold bg-blue-600 text-white shadow-xs flex items-center gap-1.5">
          <Globe className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
          <span>{t("choroplethMapTitle", locale)}</span>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Main SVG Choropleth Canvas with GPU-friendly pointer pan/zoom */}
      <div className="w-full h-[480px] sm:h-[540px] cursor-grab active:cursor-grabbing relative z-0">
        <svg
          ref={svgRef}
          viewBox={viewBox}
          onPointerDown={handlePointerDown}
          onPointerMove={handlePointerMove}
          onPointerUp={handlePointerUp}
          onPointerCancel={handlePointerUp}
          onWheel={handleWheel}
          className="w-full h-full overflow-hidden bg-[#060913] touch-none"
        >
          {/* Oceans background */}
          <rect width={width} height={height} fill="#060913" />

          {/* Map Content Group Transform */}
          <g
            transform={`translate(${pan.x} ${pan.y}) scale(${zoom})`}
            style={{
              transformOrigin: "center",
              transition: isPointerDown ? "none" : "transform 0.15s ease-out",
            }}
          >
            {/* Graticule lines */}
            {graticulePath && (
              <path
                d={graticulePath}
                fill="none"
                stroke="#1e293b"
                strokeWidth={0.5}
                strokeDasharray="2 4"
                opacity={0.7}
              />
            )}

            {/* Country Polygons */}
            {countries.map((c) => {
              const iso = c.iso2;
              const isSpain = iso === "ES";
              const volume = volumeByCountryIso2[iso] ?? 0;
              const isHovered = activeSelectedIso === iso;

              let fillColor = "#141c2e";
              if (isSpain) {
                fillColor = "#f59e0b"; // Gold destination anchor
              } else if (volume > 0) {
                fillColor = classification.getColor(volume);
              }

              return (
                <path
                  key={iso}
                  d={c.path}
                  fill={fillColor}
                  stroke={isHovered ? "#ffffff" : isSpain ? "#fbbf24" : "#0f172a"}
                  strokeWidth={isHovered ? 1.5 : 0.6}
                  className="transition-colors duration-150 cursor-pointer"
                  onPointerEnter={() => onHoverIso2(iso)}
                  onPointerLeave={() => onHoverIso2(null)}
                  onClick={(e) => {
                    e.stopPropagation();
                    onPinIso2(pinnedIso2 === iso ? null : iso);
                  }}
                >
                  <title>
                    {isSpain
                      ? `${getCountryName(iso, locale)} (Destino)`
                      : `${getCountryName(iso, locale)}: ${
                          volume > 0
                            ? `${formatNumber(volume, locale)} ${t("people", locale)}`
                            : t("noRecords", locale)
                        }`}
                  </title>
                </path>
              );
            })}
          </g>
        </svg>

        {/* Hovered / Pinned Country Tooltip Float */}
        {activeSelectedIso && (
          <div className="absolute bottom-16 left-4 z-20 bg-slate-900/95 backdrop-blur-md p-3 rounded-xl border border-slate-700 shadow-2xl text-xs space-y-1 max-w-xs pointer-events-none">
            <div className="font-bold text-white flex items-center justify-between gap-2">
              <span>{getCountryName(activeSelectedIso, locale)}</span>
              {activeSelectedIso === "ES" ? (
                <span className="text-[10px] bg-amber-500/20 text-amber-300 px-2 py-0.5 rounded border border-amber-500/40">
                  Destino
                </span>
              ) : null}
            </div>
            {activeSelectedIso !== "ES" && (
              <div className="text-slate-300">
                <span className="text-blue-400 font-bold font-mono text-sm">
                  {formatNumber(volumeByCountryIso2[activeSelectedIso] ?? 0, locale)}
                </span>{" "}
                {t("people", locale)} ({timeframeLabel})
              </div>
            )}
          </div>
        )}
      </div>

      {/* Bottom Choropleth Dynamic Legend */}
      <div className="p-3 bg-slate-950/90 border-t border-slate-800 flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-3 text-xs">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2 flex-wrap">
          <span className="text-[11px] font-semibold text-slate-400 uppercase tracking-wider">
            {timeframeLabel}:
          </span>
          {classification.classes.map((cls, idx) => {
            const minFormatted = formatCompactNumber(cls.min, locale);
            const maxFormatted = formatCompactNumber(cls.max, locale);
            return (
              <div key={idx} className="flex items-center gap-1.5">
                <span
                  className="w-3.5 h-3.5 rounded-sm shrink-0"
                  style={{ backgroundColor: cls.color }}
                />
                <span className="text-slate-300 font-mono text-[11px]">
                  {idx === classification.classes.length - 1 && cls.min !== cls.max
                    ? `> ${minFormatted}`
                    : `${minFormatted} – ${maxFormatted}`}
                </span>
              </div>
            );
          })}
        </div>
        <div className="flex items-center gap-3 text-[11px] text-slate-400">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5">
            <span className="w-3 h-3 rounded-sm bg-[#f59e0b]" />
            <span>España (Destino)</span>
          </div>
          <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5">
            <span className="w-3 h-3 rounded-sm bg-[#141c2e]" />
            <span>{t("noRecords", locale)}</span>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

--- FILE: frontend/components/shared/immigration/ContinentCountrySplitView.tsx ---
"use client";

import { useState, useMemo } from "react";
import type { Locale } from "@/lib/types";
import {
  REGIONS,
  COUNTRIES,
  getCountryName,
  getRegionName,
  type RegionId,
} from "@/lib/geography";
import { formatNumber, formatPercent } from "@/lib/format";
import { t } from "@/lib/i18n";
import { Search, ChevronDown, ChevronUp } from "lucide-react";

interface ContinentCountrySplitViewProps {
  volumeByCountryIso2: Record<string, number>;
  residualByRegion: Record<string, number>;
  totalArrivals: number;
  hoveredIso2: string | null;
  onHoverIso2: (iso: string | null) => void;
  pinnedIso2: string | null;
  onPinIso2: (iso: string | null) => void;
  selectedTimeframeLabel: string;
  locale: Locale;
}

export default function ContinentCountrySplitView({
  volumeByCountryIso2,
  residualByRegion,
  totalArrivals,
  hoveredIso2,
  onHoverIso2,
  pinnedIso2,
  onPinIso2,
  selectedTimeframeLabel,
  locale,
}: ContinentCountrySplitViewProps) {
  const [searchQuery, setSearchQuery] = useState("");
  const [selectedRegionFilter, setSelectedRegionFilter] = useState<RegionId | null>(null);
  const [isExpanded, setIsExpanded] = useState(false);

  // Continent Totals
  const continentTotals = useMemo(() => {
    const totals: Record<RegionId, number> = {
      "america-hispanohablante": 0,
      "america-no-hispanohablante": 0,
      europa: 0,
      africa: 0,
      asia: 0,
      oceania: 0,
    };

    // Country volumes
    for (const [iso, val] of Object.entries(volumeByCountryIso2)) {
      const meta = COUNTRIES[iso];
      if (meta && meta.region) {
        totals[meta.region] += val;
      }
    }

    // Residual regional volumes
    for (const [rId, val] of Object.entries(residualByRegion)) {
      if (rId in totals) {
        totals[rId as RegionId] += val;
      }
    }

    return totals;
  }, [volumeByCountryIso2, residualByRegion]);

  // Country Ranking List
  const countryList = useMemo(() => {
    return Object.entries(volumeByCountryIso2)
      .map(([iso, volume]) => ({
        iso,
        name: getCountryName(iso, locale),
        region: COUNTRIES[iso]?.region ?? ("europa" as RegionId),
        volume,
        pct: totalArrivals > 0 ? (volume / totalArrivals) * 100 : 0,
      }))
      .filter((c) => c.volume > 0)
      .sort((a, b) => b.volume - a.volume);
  }, [volumeByCountryIso2, totalArrivals, locale]);

  // Filtered Country List
  const filteredCountries = useMemo(() => {
    return countryList.filter((c) => {
      if (selectedRegionFilter && c.region !== selectedRegionFilter) return false;
      if (!searchQuery) return true;
      const q = searchQuery.toLowerCase().trim();
      return c.name.toLowerCase().includes(q) || c.iso.toLowerCase().includes(q);
    });
  }, [countryList, selectedRegionFilter, searchQuery]);

  const displayedCountries = isExpanded ? filteredCountries : filteredCountries.slice(0, 10);
  const activeSelectedIso = pinnedIso2 || hoveredIso2;

  return (
    <div className="grid grid-cols-1 lg:grid-cols-12 gap-6 pt-2">
      {/* Left Column: Continent Breakdown */}
      <div className="lg:col-span-5 bg-slate-900 border border-slate-800 rounded-2xl p-5 shadow-xl space-y-4">
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between border-b border-slate-800 pb-3">
          <h3 className="text-xs font-bold text-slate-200 uppercase tracking-wider flex items-center gap-2">
            <span className="w-2 h-2 rounded-full bg-blue-500" />
            {t("continentBreakdown", locale)}
          </h3>
          <span className="text-[11px] font-mono text-slate-400">
            {formatNumber(totalArrivals, locale)} {t("people", locale)}
          </span>
        </div>

        <div className="space-y-3">
          {(Object.keys(REGIONS) as RegionId[]).map((rId) => {
            const reg = REGIONS[rId];
            const volume = continentTotals[rId] ?? 0;
            const pct = totalArrivals > 0 ? (volume / totalArrivals) * 100 : 0;
            const isFilterActive = selectedRegionFilter === rId;

            return (
              <button
                type="button"
                key={rId}
                onClick={() => setSelectedRegionFilter(isFilterActive ? null : rId)}
                className={`w-full text-left p-3 rounded-xl border transition-all ${
                  isFilterActive
                    ? "bg-slate-800 border-blue-500/80 shadow-md ring-1 ring-blue-500/50"
                    : "bg-slate-950/50 border-slate-800 hover:border-slate-700"
                }`}
              >
                <div className="flex items-center justify-between text-xs font-semibold mb-1.5">
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                    <span
                      className="w-2.5 h-2.5 rounded-full shrink-0"
                      style={{ backgroundColor: reg.color }}
                    />
                    <span className="text-white">{reg.name[locale]}</span>
                  </div>
                  <span className="text-slate-300 font-mono">
                    {formatPercent(pct, locale, 1)}
                  </span>
                </div>
                <div className="w-full bg-slate-800 rounded-full h-1.5 overflow-hidden">
                  <div
                    className="h-full rounded-full transition-all duration-300"
                    style={{
                      width: `${Math.min(100, Math.max(0, pct))}%`,
                      backgroundColor: reg.color,
                    }}
                  />
                </div>
                <div className="text-[11px] text-slate-400 mt-1 font-mono">
                  {formatNumber(volume, locale)} {t("people", locale)}
                </div>
              </button>
            );
          })}
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Right Column: Country Detailed Ranking */}
      <div className="lg:col-span-7 bg-slate-900 border border-slate-800 rounded-2xl p-5 shadow-xl space-y-4">
        <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-center justify-between gap-3 border-b border-slate-800 pb-3">
          <div>
            <h3 className="text-xs font-bold text-slate-200 uppercase tracking-wider flex items-center gap-2">
              <span className="w-2 h-2 rounded-full bg-emerald-500" />
              {t("originRankingTitle", locale)}
            </h3>
            <span className="text-[11px] text-slate-400">{selectedTimeframeLabel}</span>
          </div>

          {/* Search Box */}
          <div className="relative w-full sm:w-48">
            <Search className="w-3.5 h-3.5 absolute left-3 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 text-slate-400" />
            <input
              type="text"
              value={searchQuery}
              onChange={(e) => setSearchQuery(e.target.value)}
              placeholder={t("searchCountryPlaceholder", locale)}
              className="w-full bg-slate-950 border border-slate-800 rounded-lg pl-8 pr-3 py-1.5 text-xs text-white placeholder-slate-500 focus:outline-hidden focus:border-blue-500 transition-colors"
            />
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Countries Ranking Table/List */}
        <div className="space-y-1.5 max-h-[460px] overflow-y-auto pr-1">
          {displayedCountries.map((c, idx) => {
            const isHovered = activeSelectedIso === c.iso;
            const regionMeta = REGIONS[c.region];

            return (
              <div
                key={c.iso}
                onMouseEnter={() => onHoverIso2(c.iso)}
                onMouseLeave={() => onHoverIso2(null)}
                onClick={() => onPinIso2(pinnedIso2 === c.iso ? null : c.iso)}
                className={`p-2.5 rounded-xl border flex items-center justify-between text-xs cursor-pointer transition-all ${
                  isHovered
                    ? "bg-blue-600/20 border-blue-500 text-white shadow-md ring-1 ring-blue-500/50"
                    : "bg-slate-950/40 border-slate-800 hover:border-slate-700 text-slate-300"
                }`}
              >
                <div className="flex items-center gap-2.5 min-w-0">
                  <span className="text-[10px] font-mono text-slate-500 w-5 text-right shrink-0">
                    {idx + 1}
                  </span>
                  <span
                    className="w-2 h-2 rounded-full shrink-0"
                    style={{ backgroundColor: regionMeta?.color ?? "#94a3b8" }}
                  />
                  <span className="font-semibold text-white truncate">{c.name}</span>
                  <span className="text-[10px] font-mono text-slate-400 uppercase bg-slate-800 px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded shrink-0">
                    {c.iso}
                  </span>
                </div>

                <div className="flex items-center gap-3 shrink-0 font-mono text-right">
                  <span className="text-white font-bold">{formatNumber(c.volume, locale)}</span>
                  <span className="text-slate-400 text-[11px] w-12">
                    {formatPercent(c.pct, locale, 1)}
                  </span>
                </div>
              </div>
            );
          })}
        </div>

        {filteredCountries.length > 10 && (
          <div className="pt-2 text-center">
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => setIsExpanded(!isExpanded)}
              className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 text-xs font-semibold text-blue-400 hover:text-blue-300 transition-colors"
            >
              <span>{isExpanded ? t("collapse", locale) : t("showMore", locale)}</span>
              {isExpanded ? <ChevronUp className="w-3.5 h-3.5" /> : <ChevronDown className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />}
            </button>
          </div>
        )}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

--- FILE: frontend/components/shared/immigration/ImmigrationExplorer.tsx ---
"use client";

import { useState, useMemo, useEffect } from "react";
import type { ImmigrationFlowIndicator, Locale } from "@/lib/types";
import TimelineScrubber from "./TimelineScrubber";
import ChoroplethMap from "./ChoroplethMap";
import ContinentCountrySplitView from "./ContinentCountrySplitView";
import { t } from "@/lib/i18n";

interface ImmigrationExplorerProps {
  indicator: ImmigrationFlowIndicator;
  locale: Locale;
}

const PLAYBACK_INTERVAL_MS = 1000;

export default function ImmigrationExplorer({
  indicator,
  locale,
}: ImmigrationExplorerProps) {
  const { years } = indicator;
  const yearStrings = useMemo(() => years.map((y) => y.year), [years]);

  const maxIndex = Math.max(0, years.length - 1);
  const defaultRangeStartIndex = Math.max(0, years.length - 5);

  const [currentIndex, setCurrentIndex] = useState<number>(maxIndex);
  const [rangeIndices, setRangeIndices] = useState<[number, number]>([
    defaultRangeStartIndex,
    maxIndex,
  ]);
  const [mode, setMode] = useState<"single" | "range">("single");
  const [isPlaying, setIsPlaying] = useState<boolean>(false);

  const [hoveredIso2, setHoveredIso2] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [pinnedIso2, setPinnedIso2] = useState<string | null>(null);

  // Playback timer
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!isPlaying || mode !== "single") return;

    if (currentIndex >= maxIndex) {
      setIsPlaying(false);
      return;
    }

    const timer = window.setTimeout(() => {
      setCurrentIndex((prev) => {
        if (prev >= maxIndex - 1) {
          setIsPlaying(false);
          return maxIndex;
        }
        return prev + 1;
      });
    }, PLAYBACK_INTERVAL_MS);

    return () => window.clearTimeout(timer);
  }, [isPlaying, mode, currentIndex, maxIndex]);

  // Aggregate metrics based on mode
  const {
    volumeByCountryIso2,
    residualByRegion,
    totalArrivals,
    timeframeLabel,
  } = useMemo(() => {
    const byCountry: Record<string, number> = {};
    const byRegion: Record<string, number> = {};
    let total = 0;

    let targetYears: typeof years = [];

    if (mode === "single") {
      const singleYear = years[currentIndex];
      targetYears = singleYear ? [singleYear] : [];
    } else {
      const [start, end] = rangeIndices;
      targetYears = years.slice(Math.min(start, end), Math.max(start, end) + 1);
    }

    for (const y of targetYears) {
      total += y.declaredTotal;
      for (const [iso, val] of Object.entries(y.byCountryIso2)) {
        byCountry[iso] = (byCountry[iso] ?? 0) + val;
      }
      for (const [rId, val] of Object.entries(y.residualByRegion)) {
        byRegion[rId] = (byRegion[rId] ?? 0) + val;
      }
    }

    const label =
      mode === "single"
        ? `${t("yearLabel", locale)} ${yearStrings[currentIndex] ?? ""}`
        : `${yearStrings[rangeIndices[0]] ?? ""}–${yearStrings[rangeIndices[1]] ?? ""}`;

    return {
      volumeByCountryIso2: byCountry,
      residualByRegion: byRegion,
      totalArrivals: total,
      timeframeLabel: label,
    };
  }, [years, mode, currentIndex, rangeIndices, yearStrings, locale]);

  return (
    <div className="space-y-6">
      {/* 1. Top Timeline Scrubber */}
      <TimelineScrubber
        years={yearStrings}
        currentIndex={currentIndex}
        onIndexChange={setCurrentIndex}
        rangeIndices={rangeIndices}
        onRangeChange={setRangeIndices}
        mode={mode}
        onModeChange={setMode}
        isPlaying={isPlaying}
        onPlayPause={setIsPlaying}
        locale={locale}
      />

      {/* 2. Interactive Natural Earth Choropleth Map */}
      <ChoroplethMap
        timeframeLabel={timeframeLabel}
        volumeByCountryIso2={volumeByCountryIso2}
        totalVolume={totalArrivals}
        hoveredIso2={hoveredIso2}
        onHoverIso2={setHoveredIso2}
        pinnedIso2={pinnedIso2}
        onPinIso2={setPinnedIso2}
        locale={locale}
      />

      {/* 3. Continent Donut & Country Ranking Split View */}
      <ContinentCountrySplitView
        volumeByCountryIso2={volumeByCountryIso2}
        residualByRegion={residualByRegion}
        totalArrivals={totalArrivals}
        hoveredIso2={hoveredIso2}
        onHoverIso2={setHoveredIso2}
        pinnedIso2={pinnedIso2}
        onPinIso2={setPinnedIso2}
        selectedTimeframeLabel={timeframeLabel}
        locale={locale}
      />
    </div>
  );
}

--- FILE: frontend/components/shared/immigration/TimelineScrubber.tsx ---
"use client";

import { Play, Pause, ChevronLeft, ChevronRight, Calendar, Layers } from "lucide-react";
import type { Locale } from "@/lib/types";
import { t } from "@/lib/i18n";

interface TimelineScrubberProps {
  years: string[];
  currentIndex: number;
  onIndexChange: (index: number) => void;
  rangeIndices: [number, number];
  onRangeChange: (indices: [number, number]) => void;
  mode: "single" | "range";
  onModeChange: (mode: "single" | "range") => void;
  isPlaying: boolean;
  onPlayPause: (playing: boolean) => void;
  locale: Locale;
}

export default function TimelineScrubber({
  years,
  currentIndex,
  onIndexChange,
  rangeIndices,
  onRangeChange,
  mode,
  onModeChange,
  isPlaying,
  onPlayPause,
  locale,
}: TimelineScrubberProps) {
  const maxIdx = Math.max(0, years.length - 1);
  const activeYear = years[currentIndex] ?? years[0] ?? "";
  const startYear = years[rangeIndices[0]] ?? years[0] ?? "";
  const endYear = years[rangeIndices[1]] ?? years[maxIdx] ?? "";

  const handleStepPrev = () => {
    onPlayPause(false);
    if (currentIndex > 0) {
      onIndexChange(currentIndex - 1);
    }
  };

  const handleStepNext = () => {
    onPlayPause(false);
    if (currentIndex < maxIdx) {
      onIndexChange(currentIndex + 1);
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className="bg-slate-900 border border-slate-800 rounded-2xl p-4 sm:p-5 text-white shadow-xl space-y-4">
      <div className="flex flex-col md:flex-row items-stretch md:items-center justify-between gap-4">
        {/* Playback Controls & Mode Switcher */}
        <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
          {/* Step Prev */}
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={handleStepPrev}
            disabled={currentIndex <= 0}
            className="w-8 h-8 rounded-lg bg-slate-800 hover:bg-slate-700 text-slate-300 hover:text-white disabled:opacity-40 flex items-center justify-center transition-colors"
            title={t("previousYear", locale)}
            aria-label={t("previousYear", locale)}
          >
            <ChevronLeft className="w-4 h-4" />
          </button>

          {/* Play / Pause */}
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => onPlayPause(!isPlaying)}
            className="w-10 h-10 rounded-xl bg-blue-600 hover:bg-blue-500 text-white flex items-center justify-center shadow-lg transition-all active:scale-95"
            title={isPlaying ? t("pauseAnimation", locale) : t("playChronology", locale)}
            aria-label={isPlaying ? t("pauseAnimation", locale) : t("playChronology", locale)}
          >
            {isPlaying ? (
              <Pause className="w-5 h-5 fill-current" />
            ) : (
              <Play className="w-5 h-5 fill-current ml-0.5" />
            )}
          </button>

          {/* Step Next */}
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={handleStepNext}
            disabled={currentIndex >= maxIdx}
            className="w-8 h-8 rounded-lg bg-slate-800 hover:bg-slate-700 text-slate-300 hover:text-white disabled:opacity-40 flex items-center justify-center transition-colors"
            title={t("nextYear", locale)}
            aria-label={t("nextYear", locale)}
          >
            <ChevronRight className="w-4 h-4" />
          </button>

          {/* Year Display Badge */}
          <div className="pl-2 border-l border-slate-800">
            <span className="text-[10px] font-semibold text-slate-400 uppercase tracking-wider block">
              {mode === "single" ? t("activeYear", locale) : t("accumulatedRange", locale)}
            </span>
            <span className="text-lg font-black font-mono text-blue-400">
              {mode === "single" ? activeYear : `${startYear} – ${endYear}`}
            </span>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Mode Selector Toggle */}
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2 bg-slate-800/80 p-1 rounded-xl border border-slate-700/60 self-start md:self-auto">
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => {
              onPlayPause(false);
              onModeChange("single");
            }}
            className={`px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg text-xs font-bold transition-all flex items-center gap-1.5 ${
              mode === "single"
                ? "bg-blue-600 text-white shadow-xs"
                : "text-slate-400 hover:text-white"
            }`}
          >
            <Calendar className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
            <span>{t("singleYear", locale)}</span>
          </button>
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => {
              onPlayPause(false);
              onModeChange("range");
            }}
            className={`px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg text-xs font-bold transition-all flex items-center gap-1.5 ${
              mode === "range"
                ? "bg-blue-600 text-white shadow-xs"
                : "text-slate-400 hover:text-white"
            }`}
          >
            <Layers className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
            <span>{t("yearRange", locale)}</span>
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Index-Driven Slider */}
      {mode === "single" ? (
        <div className="space-y-2">
          <input
            type="range"
            min={0}
            max={maxIdx}
            step={1}
            value={currentIndex}
            onChange={(e) => {
              onPlayPause(false);
              onIndexChange(parseInt(e.target.value, 10));
            }}
            className="w-full h-2.5 bg-slate-800 rounded-lg appearance-none cursor-pointer accent-blue-500"
            aria-label={t("activeYear", locale)}
          />
          <div className="flex justify-between text-[11px] font-mono text-slate-400 px-1 overflow-x-auto no-scrollbar">
            {years.map((y, idx) => (
              <button
                type="button"
                key={y}
                onClick={() => {
                  onPlayPause(false);
                  onIndexChange(idx);
                }}
                className={`hover:text-blue-400 transition-colors px-1 ${
                  currentIndex === idx ? "text-blue-400 font-bold" : ""
                }`}
              >
                {y}
              </button>
            ))}
          </div>
        </div>
      ) : (
        <div className="space-y-3">
          <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-4">
            <div>
              <label className="text-[10px] text-slate-400 block mb-1">
                {t("period", locale)} Start: {startYear}
              </label>
              <input
                type="range"
                min={0}
                max={rangeIndices[1]}
                step={1}
                value={rangeIndices[0]}
                onChange={(e) => {
                  onPlayPause(false);
                  onRangeChange([parseInt(e.target.value, 10), rangeIndices[1]]);
                }}
                className="w-full h-2.5 bg-slate-800 rounded-lg appearance-none cursor-pointer accent-blue-500"
              />
            </div>
            <div>
              <label className="text-[10px] text-slate-400 block mb-1">
                {t("period", locale)} End: {endYear}
              </label>
              <input
                type="range"
                min={rangeIndices[0]}
                max={maxIdx}
                step={1}
                value={rangeIndices[1]}
                onChange={(e) => {
                  onPlayPause(false);
                  onRangeChange([rangeIndices[0], parseInt(e.target.value, 10)]);
                }}
                className="w-full h-2.5 bg-slate-800 rounded-lg appearance-none cursor-pointer accent-blue-500"
              />
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

--- FILE: frontend/components/shared/immigration/jenks.ts ---
export interface ChoroplethClass {
  classIndex: number;
  min: number;
  max: number;
  color: string;
}

export interface ChoroplethClassification {
  classes: ChoroplethClass[];
  minVal: number;
  maxVal: number;
  getClass(value: number): ChoroplethClass | null;
  getColor(value: number, fallbackColor?: string): string;
}

/**
 * Jenks natural breaks algorithm returning cut points.
 */
function getJenksCutPoints(sortedData: number[], numClasses: number): number[] {
  const n = sortedData.length;
  if (n === 0) return [];
  if (numClasses >= n) {
    return Array.from(new Set(sortedData));
  }

  const mat1: number[][] = Array(n + 1)
    .fill(0)
    .map(() => Array(numClasses + 1).fill(0));
  const mat2: number[][] = Array(n + 1)
    .fill(0)
    .map(() => Array(numClasses + 1).fill(0));

  for (let i = 1; i <= numClasses; i++) {
    mat1[1][i] = 1;
    mat2[1][i] = 0;
    for (let j = 2; j <= n; j++) {
      mat2[j][i] = Infinity;
    }
  }

  for (let l = 2; l <= n; l++) {
    let sum = 0;
    let sumSquares = 0;
    let w = 0;
    for (let m = 1; m <= l; m++) {
      const val = sortedData[l - m];
      w++;
      sum += val;
      sumSquares += val * val;
      const variance = sumSquares - (sum * sum) / w;
      const i4 = l - m;
      if (i4 !== 0) {
        for (let j = 2; j <= numClasses; j++) {
          if (mat2[l][j] >= variance + mat2[i4][j - 1]) {
            mat1[l][j] = i4;
            mat2[l][j] = variance + mat2[i4][j - 1];
          }
        }
      }
    }
    mat1[l][1] = 1;
    mat2[l][1] = sumSquares - (sum * sum) / w;
  }

  let k = n;
  const kclass: number[] = Array(numClasses + 1).fill(0);
  kclass[numClasses] = sortedData[n - 1];
  kclass[0] = sortedData[0];

  let countNum = numClasses;
  while (countNum >= 2) {
    const idx = Math.round(mat1[k][countNum]);
    kclass[countNum - 1] = sortedData[idx - 1];
    k = idx;
    countNum--;
  }

  const cutPoints: number[] = [];
  for (let i = 1; i < kclass.length; i++) {
    cutPoints.push(kclass[i]);
  }

  return Array.from(new Set(cutPoints)).sort((a, b) => a - b);
}

/**
 * Pure, hardened choropleth classifier with graceful degradation.
 */
export function classifyChoropleth(
  values: number[],
  palette: readonly string[]
): ChoroplethClassification {
  if (!palette || palette.length === 0) {
    throw new Error("Palette must contain at least one color");
  }

  const positive = values
    .filter((v) => typeof v === "number" && Number.isFinite(v) && v > 0)
    .sort((a, b) => a - b);

  if (positive.length === 0) {
    return {
      classes: [],
      minVal: 0,
      maxVal: 0,
      getClass: () => null,
      getColor: (_val, fallbackColor = "#1e293b") => fallbackColor,
    };
  }

  const minVal = positive[0];
  const maxVal = positive[positive.length - 1];
  const uniqueValues = Array.from(new Set(positive));

  // Case: Only 1 unique value
  if (uniqueValues.length === 1) {
    const singleClass: ChoroplethClass = {
      classIndex: 0,
      min: minVal,
      max: maxVal,
      color: palette[0],
    };
    return {
      classes: [singleClass],
      minVal,
      maxVal,
      getClass: (val) => (val > 0 ? singleClass : null),
      getColor: (val, fallback = "#1e293b") => (val > 0 ? palette[0] : fallback),
    };
  }

  // Gracefully degrade target classes count
  const targetClassesCount = Math.min(palette.length, uniqueValues.length);
  const cutPoints = getJenksCutPoints(positive, targetClassesCount);

  // Build contiguous classes
  const classes: ChoroplethClass[] = [];
  let prevUpper = minVal;

  for (let i = 0; i < cutPoints.length; i++) {
    const upper = cutPoints[i];
    classes.push({
      classIndex: i,
      min: prevUpper,
      max: upper,
      color: palette[i] ?? palette[palette.length - 1],
    });
    prevUpper = upper;
  }

  const getClass = (value: number): ChoroplethClass | null => {
    if (typeof value !== "number" || !Number.isFinite(value) || value <= 0) {
      return null;
    }
    for (const c of classes) {
      if (value <= c.max) {
        return c;
      }
    }
    return classes[classes.length - 1] ?? null;
  };

  const getColor = (value: number, fallbackColor = "#1e293b"): string => {
    const matched = getClass(value);
    return matched ? matched.color : fallbackColor;
  };

  return {
    classes,
    minVal,
    maxVal,
    getClass,
    getColor,
  };
}

import React from "react";

export const CHART_THEME = {
  grid: {
    stroke: "#334155",
    strokeDasharray: "3 3",

interface ChartCardProps {
  children: React.ReactNode;
  title?: React.ReactNode;
  subtitle?: React.ReactNode;
  headerRight?: React.ReactNode;
  className?: string;
}

export function ChartCard({
  children,
  title,
  subtitle,
  headerRight,
  className = "",
}: ChartCardProps) {
  return (
    <div
      className={`bg-slate-900 border border-slate-800 rounded-2xl p-4 sm:p-6 shadow-xl space-y-4 ${className}`}
    >
      {(title || subtitle || headerRight) && (
        <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-center justify-between gap-2 border-b border-slate-800/80 pb-3">
          <div>
            {title && <h3 className="text-sm font-bold text-white tracking-tight">{title}</h3>}
            {subtitle && <p className="text-xs text-slate-400 mt-0.5">{subtitle}</p>}
          </div>
          {headerRight && <div className="flex items-center gap-2">{headerRight}</div>}
        </div>
      )}
      <div>{children}</div>
    </div>
  );
}
