--- FILE: frontend/components/visualizations/FiscalBalanceDashboard.tsx ---
"use client";

import { useState, useMemo } from "react";
import {
  ResponsiveContainer,
  BarChart,
  Bar,
  LineChart,
  Line,
  XAxis,
  YAxis,
  Tooltip,
  CartesianGrid,
  ReferenceLine,
  type TooltipProps,
} from "recharts";
import type { FiscalBalanceIndicator, Locale } from "@/lib/types";
import { ChartCard, CHART_THEME } from "./ChartCard";
import { t } from "@/lib/i18n";
import { formatNumber, formatPercent } from "@/lib/format";

interface FiscalBalanceDashboardProps {
  indicator: FiscalBalanceIndicator;
  locale: Locale;
}

type TabMode = "ratio" | "capita" | "total";

interface FiscalTooltipProps extends TooltipProps<number, string> {
  activeTab: TabMode;
  locale: Locale;
}

function FiscalTooltip({ active, payload, label, activeTab, locale }: FiscalTooltipProps) {
  if (!active || !payload || !payload.length) return null;

  return (
    <div className="bg-slate-900 text-white p-3.5 rounded-xl shadow-2xl border border-slate-700 text-xs max-w-xs space-y-2">
      <div className="flex items-center justify-between border-b border-slate-800 pb-1.5 font-bold">
        <span>
          {t("yearLabel", locale)} {label}
        </span>
        <span className="text-blue-400 font-semibold">
          {activeTab === "ratio"
            ? t("fiscalTabRatio", locale)
            : activeTab === "capita"
            ? t("fiscalTabCapita", locale)
            : t("fiscalTabTotal", locale)}
        </span>
      </div>
      <div className="space-y-1 text-[11px]">
        {payload.map((entry, idx) => {
          const val = Number(entry.value) || 0;
          let displayVal = "";
          if (activeTab === "ratio") {
            const isSurplus = val >= 100;
            displayVal = `${formatPercent(val, locale)} (${
              isSurplus ? t("fiscalSurplus", locale) : t("fiscalDeficit", locale)
            })`;
          } else if (activeTab === "capita") {
            displayVal = `${val >= 0 ? "+" : ""}${formatNumber(val, locale)} €/hab`;
          } else {
            displayVal = `${formatNumber(val, locale)} MM€`;
          }

          return (
            <div key={idx} className="flex items-center justify-between gap-3">
              <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 truncate">
                <span
                  className="w-2.5 h-2.5 rounded-full flex-shrink-0"
                  style={{ backgroundColor: entry.color }}
                />
                <span className="truncate text-slate-300">{entry.name}</span>
              </div>
              <span className="font-semibold text-white tabular-nums font-mono">
                {displayVal}
              </span>
            </div>
          );
        })}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

export default function FiscalBalanceDashboard({
  indicator,
  locale,
}: FiscalBalanceDashboardProps) {
  const [activeTab, setActiveTab] = useState<TabMode>("ratio");

  const points = indicator.points || [];

  const ratioData = useMemo(
    () =>
      points.map((p) => ({
        year: p.year,
        foreignRatio: p.ratio.foreign,
        spanishRatio: p.ratio.spanish,
      })),
    [points]
  );

  const capitaData = useMemo(
    () =>
      points.map((p) => ({
        year: p.year,
        foreignCapita: p.perCapitaEur.foreign,
        spanishCapita: p.perCapitaEur.spanish,
      })),
    [points]
  );

  const totalData = useMemo(
    () =>
      points.map((p) => ({
        year: p.year,
        totalRevenue: p.totalRevenueMmEur,
        totalSpending: p.totalSpendingMmEur,
        deficit: p.deficitMmEur,
      })),
    [points]
  );

  const tabSubtitle =
    activeTab === "ratio"
      ? t("fiscalRatioDesc", locale)
      : activeTab === "capita"
      ? t("fiscalCapitaDesc", locale)
      : t("fiscalTotalDesc", locale);

  return (
    <ChartCard
      title={
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
          <span className="text-base">⚖️</span>
          <span>{indicator.question[locale]}</span>
        </div>
      }
      subtitle={tabSubtitle}
      headerRight={
        <div className="flex items-center gap-1 bg-slate-800/80 p-1 rounded-xl border border-slate-700/60 text-xs font-bold">
          <button
            onClick={() => setActiveTab("ratio")}
            className={`px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg transition-colors ${
              activeTab === "ratio"
                ? "bg-blue-600 text-white shadow-xs"
                : "text-slate-400 hover:text-white"
            }`}
          >
            {t("fiscalTabRatio", locale)}
          </button>
          <button
            onClick={() => setActiveTab("capita")}
            className={`px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg transition-colors ${
              activeTab === "capita"
                ? "bg-blue-600 text-white shadow-xs"
                : "text-slate-400 hover:text-white"
            }`}
          >
            {t("fiscalTabCapita", locale)}
          </button>
          <button
            onClick={() => setActiveTab("total")}
            className={`px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg transition-colors ${
              activeTab === "total"
                ? "bg-blue-600 text-white shadow-xs"
                : "text-slate-400 hover:text-white"
            }`}
          >
            {t("fiscalTabTotal", locale)}
          </button>
        </div>
      }
    >
      <div className="w-full h-[380px] sm:h-[440px] pt-2">
        <ResponsiveContainer width="100%" height="100%">
          {activeTab === "ratio" ? (
            <BarChart data={ratioData} margin={{ top: 12, right: 16, left: 0, bottom: 8 }}>
              <CartesianGrid {...CHART_THEME.grid} />
              <XAxis dataKey="year" {...CHART_THEME.axis} />
              <YAxis
                {...CHART_THEME.axis}
                domain={[0, (dataMax: number) => Math.ceil(dataMax * 1.15)]}
                tickFormatter={(v: number) => `${v}%`}
              />
              <Tooltip content={<FiscalTooltip activeTab="ratio" locale={locale} />} />
              <ReferenceLine
                y={100}
                stroke="#ef4444"
                strokeDasharray="4 4"
                label={{
                  value: "100% (Equilibrio)",
                  fill: "#ef4444",
                  fontSize: 10,
                  position: "top",
                }}
              />
              <Bar
                dataKey="foreignRatio"
                name={t("fiscalForeignRatio", locale)}
                fill="#10b981"
                radius={[4, 4, 0, 0]}
              />
              <Bar
                dataKey="spanishRatio"
                name={t("fiscalSpanishRatio", locale)}
                fill="#64748b"
                radius={[4, 4, 0, 0]}
              />
            </BarChart>
          ) : activeTab === "capita" ? (
            <BarChart data={capitaData} margin={{ top: 12, right: 16, left: 0, bottom: 8 }}>
              <CartesianGrid {...CHART_THEME.grid} />
              <XAxis dataKey="year" {...CHART_THEME.axis} />
              <YAxis
                {...CHART_THEME.axis}
                domain={[
                  (dataMin: number) => Math.floor(dataMin * 1.15),
                  (dataMax: number) => Math.ceil(dataMax * 1.15),
                ]}
                tickFormatter={(v: number) => `${v} €`}
              />
              <Tooltip content={<FiscalTooltip activeTab="capita" locale={locale} />} />
              <ReferenceLine y={0} stroke="#94a3b8" />
              <Bar
                dataKey="foreignCapita"
                name={t("fiscalForeignCapita", locale)}
                fill="#10b981"
                radius={[4, 4, 0, 0]}
              />
              <Bar
                dataKey="spanishCapita"
                name={t("fiscalSpanishCapita", locale)}
                fill="#64748b"
                radius={[4, 4, 0, 0]}
              />
            </BarChart>
          ) : (
            <BarChart data={totalData} margin={{ top: 12, right: 16, left: 0, bottom: 8 }}>
              <CartesianGrid {...CHART_THEME.grid} />
              <XAxis dataKey="year" {...CHART_THEME.axis} />
              <YAxis
                {...CHART_THEME.axis}
                domain={[0, (dataMax: number) => Math.ceil(dataMax * 1.15)]}
                tickFormatter={(v: number) => `${v} MM€`}
              />
              <Tooltip content={<FiscalTooltip activeTab="total" locale={locale} />} />
              <Bar
                dataKey="totalRevenue"
                name={t("fiscalTotalRevenue", locale)}
                fill="#3b82f6"
                radius={[4, 4, 0, 0]}
              />
              <Bar
                dataKey="totalSpending"
                name={t("fiscalTotalSpending", locale)}
                fill="#8b5cf6"
                radius={[4, 4, 0, 0]}
              />
            </BarChart>
          )}
        </ResponsiveContainer>
      </div>
    </ChartCard>
  );
}

--- FILE: frontend/components/visualizations/IndicatorVisualization.tsx ---
import dynamic from "next/dynamic";
import type { Indicator, Locale } from "@/lib/types";
import { isFiscalBalanceItem, isNationalityIndicatorsItem } from "@/lib/types";
import { ChartSkeleton } from "./ChartSkeleton";

const TimeSeriesChart = dynamic(() => import("./TimeSeriesChart"), {
  loading: () => <ChartSkeleton />,
});
const FiscalBalanceDashboard = dynamic(() => import("./FiscalBalanceDashboard"), {
  loading: () => <ChartSkeleton />,
});
const FiscalBalanceView = dynamic(() => import("../fiscal/FiscalBalanceView"), {
  loading: () => <ChartSkeleton />,
});
const NationalityIndicatorsView = dynamic(() => import("../nationality/NationalityIndicatorsView"), {
  loading: () => <ChartSkeleton />,
});
const AdminRegistersChart = dynamic(() => import("../admin-registers/AdminRegistersChart"), {
  loading: () => <ChartSkeleton />,
});
const ImmigrationExplorer = dynamic(() => import("./immigration/ImmigrationExplorer"), {
  loading: () => <ChartSkeleton />,
});

export function IndicatorVisualization({
  indicator,
  locale,
}: {
  indicator: Indicator | any;
  locale: Locale;
}) {
  if (isFiscalBalanceItem(indicator as any) || (indicator && "annualBalances" in indicator)) {
    return <FiscalBalanceView item={indicator as any} lang={locale} locale={locale} />;
  }

  if (indicator?.kind === "admin-registers-nationality") {
    return <AdminRegistersChart records={indicator.annualRecords || []} lang={locale} />;
  }

  if (
    isNationalityIndicatorsItem(indicator as any) ||
    indicator?.kind === "nationality-indicators" ||
    indicator?.chartType === "nationality-indicators" ||
    (indicator && "annualRecords" in indicator)
  ) {
    return <NationalityIndicatorsView item={indicator as any} lang={locale} locale={locale} />;
  }

  switch (indicator.kind) {
    case "time-series":
      return <TimeSeriesChart indicator={indicator} locale={locale} />;
    case "fiscal-balance":
      return <FiscalBalanceDashboard indicator={indicator} locale={locale} />;
    case "immigration-flow":
      return <ImmigrationExplorer indicator={indicator} locale={locale} />;
    default: {
      return <TimeSeriesChart indicator={indicator} locale={locale} />;
    }
  }
}

--- FILE: frontend/components/visualizations/TimeSeriesChart.tsx ---
"use client";

import { useMemo } from "react";
import {
  ResponsiveContainer,
  LineChart,
  Line,
  BarChart,
  Bar,
  XAxis,
  YAxis,
  Tooltip,
  CartesianGrid,
  type TooltipProps,
} from "recharts";
import type { TimeSeriesIndicator, Locale } from "@/lib/types";
import { ChartCard, CHART_THEME } from "./ChartCard";
import { formatIndicatorValue } from "@/lib/format";

interface TimeSeriesChartProps {
  indicator: TimeSeriesIndicator;
  locale: Locale;
}

interface CustomTooltipProps extends TooltipProps<number, string> {
  unit: string;
  format: TimeSeriesIndicator["valueFormat"];
  locale: Locale;
}

function SeriesTooltip({ active, payload, label, unit, format, locale }: CustomTooltipProps) {
  if (!active || !payload || !payload.length) return null;
  const entry = payload[0];
  const val = Number(entry.value) || 0;

  return (
    <div className="bg-slate-900 text-white p-3 rounded-xl shadow-2xl border border-slate-700 text-xs space-y-1">
      <div className="font-semibold text-slate-300">{label}</div>
      <div className="text-sm font-bold text-blue-400 font-mono tabular-nums">
        {formatIndicatorValue(val, format || "number", locale)} {unit ? `(${unit})` : ""}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

export default function TimeSeriesChart({ indicator, locale }: TimeSeriesChartProps) {
  const { points, chartStyle, unit, valueFormat } = indicator;

  const data = useMemo(
    () =>
      points.map((pt) => ({
        period: pt.period,
        value: pt.value,
      })),
    [points]
  );

  const unitStr = unit[locale];

  return (
    <ChartCard>
      <div className="w-full h-[360px] sm:h-[420px]">
        <ResponsiveContainer width="100%" height="100%">
          {chartStyle === "bar" ? (
            <BarChart data={data} margin={{ top: 12, right: 16, left: 0, bottom: 8 }}>
              <CartesianGrid {...CHART_THEME.grid} />
              <XAxis dataKey="period" {...CHART_THEME.axis} />
              <YAxis
                {...CHART_THEME.axis}
                domain={[0, (dataMax: number) => Math.ceil(dataMax * 1.1)]}
                tickFormatter={(v: number) => formatIndicatorValue(v, valueFormat, locale, true)}
              />
              <Tooltip
                content={<SeriesTooltip unit={unitStr} format={valueFormat} locale={locale} />}
              />
              <Bar dataKey="value" fill={CHART_THEME.colors.primary} radius={[4, 4, 0, 0]} />
            </BarChart>
          ) : (
            <LineChart data={data} margin={{ top: 12, right: 16, left: 0, bottom: 8 }}>
              <CartesianGrid {...CHART_THEME.grid} />
              <XAxis dataKey="period" {...CHART_THEME.axis} />
              <YAxis
                {...CHART_THEME.axis}
                domain={[
                  (dataMin: number) => (dataMin < 0 ? Math.floor(dataMin * 1.1) : 0),
                  (dataMax: number) => Math.ceil(dataMax * 1.1),
                ]}
                tickFormatter={(v: number) => formatIndicatorValue(v, valueFormat, locale, true)}
              />
              <Tooltip
                content={<SeriesTooltip unit={unitStr} format={valueFormat} locale={locale} />}
              />
              <Line
                type="monotone"
                dataKey="value"
                stroke={CHART_THEME.colors.primary}
                strokeWidth={3}
                dot={{ r: 3, fill: CHART_THEME.colors.primary }}
                activeDot={{ r: 6, fill: "#60a5fa" }}
              />
            </LineChart>
          )}
        </ResponsiveContainer>
      </div>
    </ChartCard>
  );
}

--- FILE: frontend/components/visualizations/immigration/ChoroplethMap.tsx ---
"use client";

import React, { useState, useMemo, useRef, useCallback } from "react";
import projectedWorld from "@/data/geo/projected-world.json";
import { classifyChoropleth } from "./jenks";
import type { Locale } from "@/lib/types";
import { getCountryName } from "@/lib/geography";
import { formatNumber, formatCompactNumber } from "@/lib/format";
import { t } from "@/lib/i18n";
import { ZoomIn, ZoomOut, Maximize2, Globe } from "lucide-react";

interface ChoroplethMapProps {
  timeframeLabel: string;
  volumeByCountryIso2: Record<string, number>;
  totalVolume: number;
  hoveredIso2: string | null;
  onHoverIso2: (iso: string | null) => void;
  pinnedIso2: string | null;
  onPinIso2: (iso: string | null) => void;
  locale: Locale;
}

const PALETTE = ["#0284c7", "#2563eb", "#7c3aed", "#c026d3", "#db2777"] as const;

export default function ChoroplethMap({
  timeframeLabel,
  volumeByCountryIso2,
  hoveredIso2,
  onHoverIso2,
  pinnedIso2,
  onPinIso2,
  locale,
}: ChoroplethMapProps) {
  const [zoom, setZoom] = useState(1);
  const [pan, setPan] = useState({ x: 0, y: 0 });
  const [isPointerDown, setIsPointerDown] = useState(false);
  const pointerStartRef = useRef({ x: 0, y: 0, panX: 0, panY: 0 });
  const svgRef = useRef<SVGSVGElement | null>(null);

  const { viewBox, countries, graticulePath, width, height } = projectedWorld;

  // Compute Natural Breaks Classification
  const values = useMemo(
    () =>
      Object.entries(volumeByCountryIso2)
        .filter(([iso, val]) => iso !== "ES" && val > 0)
        .map(([_, val]) => val),
    [volumeByCountryIso2]
  );

  const classification = useMemo(
    () => classifyChoropleth(values, PALETTE),
    [values]
  );

  const activeSelectedIso = pinnedIso2 || hoveredIso2;

  // Pointer drag handling with screenCTM or rect scaling
  const handlePointerDown = (e: React.PointerEvent<SVGSVGElement>) => {
    setIsPointerDown(true);
    e.currentTarget.setPointerCapture(e.pointerId);
    pointerStartRef.current = {
      x: e.clientX,
      y: e.clientY,
      panX: pan.x,
      panY: pan.y,
    };
  };

  const handlePointerMove = (e: React.PointerEvent<SVGSVGElement>) => {
    if (!isPointerDown || !svgRef.current) return;
    const dx = e.clientX - pointerStartRef.current.x;
    const dy = e.clientY - pointerStartRef.current.y;

    const rect = svgRef.current.getBoundingClientRect();
    const factorX = width / rect.width;
    const factorY = height / rect.height;

    setPan({
      x: pointerStartRef.current.panX + dx * factorX,
      y: pointerStartRef.current.panY + dy * factorY,
    });
  };

  const handlePointerUp = (e: React.PointerEvent<SVGSVGElement>) => {
    setIsPointerDown(false);
    try {
      e.currentTarget.releasePointerCapture(e.pointerId);
    } catch {
      // Ignore if pointer capture already lost
    }
  };

  const handleWheel = useCallback((e: React.WheelEvent<SVGSVGElement>) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    const delta = e.deltaY < 0 ? 0.15 : -0.15;
    setZoom((prev) => Math.min(3.5, Math.max(0.8, prev + delta)));
  }, []);

  const handleResetZoom = () => {
    setZoom(1);
    setPan({ x: 0, y: 0 });
  };

  return (
    <div className="relative bg-[#060913] border border-slate-800 rounded-2xl overflow-hidden shadow-2xl flex flex-col justify-between select-none">
      {/* Top Map Control Bar Overlay */}
      <div className="absolute top-4 left-4 z-10 flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2 bg-slate-900/95 backdrop-blur-md p-1.5 rounded-xl border border-slate-700/80 shadow-xl">
        <button
          type="button"
          onClick={() => setZoom((z) => Math.min(3.5, z + 0.25))}
          className="p-1.5 rounded-lg bg-slate-800 hover:bg-slate-700 text-slate-300 hover:text-white transition-colors"
          title={t("zoomIn", locale)}
          aria-label={t("zoomIn", locale)}
        >
          <ZoomIn className="w-4 h-4" />
        </button>
        <button
          type="button"
          onClick={() => setZoom((z) => Math.max(0.8, z - 0.25))}
          className="p-1.5 rounded-lg bg-slate-800 hover:bg-slate-700 text-slate-300 hover:text-white transition-colors"
          title={t("zoomOut", locale)}
          aria-label={t("zoomOut", locale)}
        >
          <ZoomOut className="w-4 h-4" />
        </button>
        <button
          type="button"
          onClick={handleResetZoom}
          className="p-1.5 rounded-lg bg-slate-800 hover:bg-slate-700 text-slate-300 hover:text-white transition-colors"
          title={t("resetZoom", locale)}
          aria-label={t("resetZoom", locale)}
        >
          <Maximize2 className="w-4 h-4" />
        </button>

        <div className="w-px h-5 bg-slate-700 my-auto mx-1" />

        <div className="px-2.5 py-1 rounded-lg text-xs font-bold bg-blue-600 text-white shadow-xs flex items-center gap-1.5">
          <Globe className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
          <span>{t("choroplethMapTitle", locale)}</span>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Main SVG Choropleth Canvas with GPU-friendly pointer pan/zoom */}
      <div className="w-full h-[480px] sm:h-[540px] cursor-grab active:cursor-grabbing relative z-0">
        <svg
          ref={svgRef}
          viewBox={viewBox}
          onPointerDown={handlePointerDown}
          onPointerMove={handlePointerMove}
          onPointerUp={handlePointerUp}
          onPointerCancel={handlePointerUp}
          onWheel={handleWheel}
          className="w-full h-full overflow-hidden bg-[#060913] touch-none"
        >
          {/* Oceans background */}
          <rect width={width} height={height} fill="#060913" />

          {/* Map Content Group Transform */}
          <g
            transform={`translate(${pan.x} ${pan.y}) scale(${zoom})`}
            style={{
              transformOrigin: "center",
              transition: isPointerDown ? "none" : "transform 0.15s ease-out",
            }}
          >
            {/* Graticule lines */}
            {graticulePath && (
              <path
                d={graticulePath}
                fill="none"
                stroke="#1e293b"
                strokeWidth={0.5}
                strokeDasharray="2 4"
                opacity={0.7}
              />
            )}

            {/* Country Polygons */}
            {countries.map((c) => {
              const iso = c.iso2;
              const isSpain = iso === "ES";
              const volume = volumeByCountryIso2[iso] ?? 0;
              const isHovered = activeSelectedIso === iso;

              let fillColor = "#141c2e";
              if (isSpain) {
                fillColor = "#f59e0b"; // Gold destination anchor
              } else if (volume > 0) {
                fillColor = classification.getColor(volume);
              }

              return (
                <path
                  key={iso}
                  d={c.path}
                  fill={fillColor}
                  stroke={isHovered ? "#ffffff" : isSpain ? "#fbbf24" : "#0f172a"}
                  strokeWidth={isHovered ? 1.5 : 0.6}
                  className="transition-colors duration-150 cursor-pointer"
                  onPointerEnter={() => onHoverIso2(iso)}
                  onPointerLeave={() => onHoverIso2(null)}
                  onClick={(e) => {
                    e.stopPropagation();
                    onPinIso2(pinnedIso2 === iso ? null : iso);
                  }}
                >
                  <title>
                    {isSpain
                      ? `${getCountryName(iso, locale)} (Destino)`
                      : `${getCountryName(iso, locale)}: ${
                          volume > 0
                            ? `${formatNumber(volume, locale)} ${t("people", locale)}`
                            : t("noRecords", locale)
                        }`}
                  </title>
                </path>
              );
            })}
          </g>
        </svg>

        {/* Hovered / Pinned Country Tooltip Float */}
        {activeSelectedIso && (
          <div className="absolute bottom-16 left-4 z-20 bg-slate-900/95 backdrop-blur-md p-3 rounded-xl border border-slate-700 shadow-2xl text-xs space-y-1 max-w-xs pointer-events-none">
            <div className="font-bold text-white flex items-center justify-between gap-2">
              <span>{getCountryName(activeSelectedIso, locale)}</span>
              {activeSelectedIso === "ES" ? (
                <span className="text-[10px] bg-amber-500/20 text-amber-300 px-2 py-0.5 rounded border border-amber-500/40">
                  Destino
                </span>
              ) : null}
            </div>
            {activeSelectedIso !== "ES" && (
              <div className="text-slate-300">
                <span className="text-blue-400 font-bold font-mono text-sm">
                  {formatNumber(volumeByCountryIso2[activeSelectedIso] ?? 0, locale)}
                </span>{" "}
                {t("people", locale)} ({timeframeLabel})
              </div>
            )}
          </div>
        )}
      </div>

      {/* Bottom Choropleth Dynamic Legend */}
      <div className="p-3 bg-slate-950/90 border-t border-slate-800 flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-3 text-xs">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2 flex-wrap">
          <span className="text-[11px] font-semibold text-slate-400 uppercase tracking-wider">
            {timeframeLabel}:
          </span>
          {classification.classes.map((cls, idx) => {
            const minFormatted = formatCompactNumber(cls.min, locale);
            const maxFormatted = formatCompactNumber(cls.max, locale);
            return (
              <div key={idx} className="flex items-center gap-1.5">
                <span
                  className="w-3.5 h-3.5 rounded-sm shrink-0"
                  style={{ backgroundColor: cls.color }}
                />
                <span className="text-slate-300 font-mono text-[11px]">
                  {idx === classification.classes.length - 1 && cls.min !== cls.max
                    ? `> ${minFormatted}`
                    : `${minFormatted} – ${maxFormatted}`}
                </span>
              </div>
            );
          })}
        </div>
        <div className="flex items-center gap-3 text-[11px] text-slate-400">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5">
            <span className="w-3 h-3 rounded-sm bg-[#f59e0b]" />
            <span>España (Destino)</span>
          </div>
          <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5">
            <span className="w-3 h-3 rounded-sm bg-[#141c2e]" />
            <span>{t("noRecords", locale)}</span>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

--- FILE: frontend/components/visualizations/immigration/ContinentCountrySplitView.tsx ---
"use client";

import { useState, useMemo } from "react";
import type { Locale } from "@/lib/types";
import {
  REGIONS,
  COUNTRIES,
  getCountryName,
  getRegionName,
  type RegionId,
} from "@/lib/geography";
import { formatNumber, formatPercent } from "@/lib/format";
import { t } from "@/lib/i18n";
import { Search, ChevronDown, ChevronUp } from "lucide-react";

interface ContinentCountrySplitViewProps {
  volumeByCountryIso2: Record<string, number>;
  residualByRegion: Record<string, number>;
  totalArrivals: number;
  hoveredIso2: string | null;
  onHoverIso2: (iso: string | null) => void;
  pinnedIso2: string | null;
  onPinIso2: (iso: string | null) => void;
  selectedTimeframeLabel: string;
  locale: Locale;
}

export default function ContinentCountrySplitView({
  volumeByCountryIso2,
  residualByRegion,
  totalArrivals,
  hoveredIso2,
  onHoverIso2,
  pinnedIso2,
  onPinIso2,
  selectedTimeframeLabel,
  locale,
}: ContinentCountrySplitViewProps) {
  const [searchQuery, setSearchQuery] = useState("");
  const [selectedRegionFilter, setSelectedRegionFilter] = useState<RegionId | null>(null);
  const [isExpanded, setIsExpanded] = useState(false);

  // Continent Totals
  const continentTotals = useMemo(() => {
    const totals: Record<RegionId, number> = {
      "america-hispanohablante": 0,
      "america-no-hispanohablante": 0,
      europa: 0,
      africa: 0,
      asia: 0,
      oceania: 0,
    };

    // Country volumes
    for (const [iso, val] of Object.entries(volumeByCountryIso2)) {
      const meta = COUNTRIES[iso];
      if (meta && meta.region) {
        totals[meta.region] += val;
      }
    }

    // Residual regional volumes
    for (const [rId, val] of Object.entries(residualByRegion)) {
      if (rId in totals) {
        totals[rId as RegionId] += val;
      }
    }

    return totals;
  }, [volumeByCountryIso2, residualByRegion]);

  // Country Ranking List
  const countryList = useMemo(() => {
    return Object.entries(volumeByCountryIso2)
      .map(([iso, volume]) => ({
        iso,
        name: getCountryName(iso, locale),
        region: COUNTRIES[iso]?.region ?? ("europa" as RegionId),
        volume,
        pct: totalArrivals > 0 ? (volume / totalArrivals) * 100 : 0,
      }))
      .filter((c) => c.volume > 0)
      .sort((a, b) => b.volume - a.volume);
  }, [volumeByCountryIso2, totalArrivals, locale]);

  // Filtered Country List
  const filteredCountries = useMemo(() => {
    return countryList.filter((c) => {
      if (selectedRegionFilter && c.region !== selectedRegionFilter) return false;
      if (!searchQuery) return true;
      const q = searchQuery.toLowerCase().trim();
      return c.name.toLowerCase().includes(q) || c.iso.toLowerCase().includes(q);
    });
  }, [countryList, selectedRegionFilter, searchQuery]);

  const displayedCountries = isExpanded ? filteredCountries : filteredCountries.slice(0, 10);
  const activeSelectedIso = pinnedIso2 || hoveredIso2;

  return (
    <div className="grid grid-cols-1 lg:grid-cols-12 gap-6 pt-2">
      {/* Left Column: Continent Breakdown */}
      <div className="lg:col-span-5 bg-slate-900 border border-slate-800 rounded-2xl p-5 shadow-xl space-y-4">
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between border-b border-slate-800 pb-3">
          <h3 className="text-xs font-bold text-slate-200 uppercase tracking-wider flex items-center gap-2">
            <span className="w-2 h-2 rounded-full bg-blue-500" />
            {t("continentBreakdown", locale)}
          </h3>
          <span className="text-[11px] font-mono text-slate-400">
            {formatNumber(totalArrivals, locale)} {t("people", locale)}
          </span>
        </div>

        <div className="space-y-3">
          {(Object.keys(REGIONS) as RegionId[]).map((rId) => {
            const reg = REGIONS[rId];
            const volume = continentTotals[rId] ?? 0;
            const pct = totalArrivals > 0 ? (volume / totalArrivals) * 100 : 0;
            const isFilterActive = selectedRegionFilter === rId;

            return (
              <button
                type="button"
                key={rId}
                onClick={() => setSelectedRegionFilter(isFilterActive ? null : rId)}
                className={`w-full text-left p-3 rounded-xl border transition-all ${
                  isFilterActive
                    ? "bg-slate-800 border-blue-500/80 shadow-md ring-1 ring-blue-500/50"
                    : "bg-slate-950/50 border-slate-800 hover:border-slate-700"
                }`}
              >
                <div className="flex items-center justify-between text-xs font-semibold mb-1.5">
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                    <span
                      className="w-2.5 h-2.5 rounded-full shrink-0"
                      style={{ backgroundColor: reg.color }}
                    />
                    <span className="text-white">{reg.name[locale]}</span>
                  </div>
                  <span className="text-slate-300 font-mono">
                    {formatPercent(pct, locale, 1)}
                  </span>
                </div>
                <div className="w-full bg-slate-800 rounded-full h-1.5 overflow-hidden">
                  <div
                    className="h-full rounded-full transition-all duration-300"
                    style={{
                      width: `${Math.min(100, Math.max(0, pct))}%`,
                      backgroundColor: reg.color,
                    }}
                  />
                </div>
                <div className="text-[11px] text-slate-400 mt-1 font-mono">
                  {formatNumber(volume, locale)} {t("people", locale)}
                </div>
              </button>
            );
          })}
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Right Column: Country Detailed Ranking */}
      <div className="lg:col-span-7 bg-slate-900 border border-slate-800 rounded-2xl p-5 shadow-xl space-y-4">
        <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-center justify-between gap-3 border-b border-slate-800 pb-3">
          <div>
            <h3 className="text-xs font-bold text-slate-200 uppercase tracking-wider flex items-center gap-2">
              <span className="w-2 h-2 rounded-full bg-emerald-500" />
              {t("originRankingTitle", locale)}
            </h3>
            <span className="text-[11px] text-slate-400">{selectedTimeframeLabel}</span>
          </div>

          {/* Search Box */}
          <div className="relative w-full sm:w-48">
            <Search className="w-3.5 h-3.5 absolute left-3 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 text-slate-400" />
            <input
              type="text"
              value={searchQuery}
              onChange={(e) => setSearchQuery(e.target.value)}
              placeholder={t("searchCountryPlaceholder", locale)}
              className="w-full bg-slate-950 border border-slate-800 rounded-lg pl-8 pr-3 py-1.5 text-xs text-white placeholder-slate-500 focus:outline-hidden focus:border-blue-500 transition-colors"
            />
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Countries Ranking Table/List */}
        <div className="space-y-1.5 max-h-[460px] overflow-y-auto pr-1">
          {displayedCountries.map((c, idx) => {
            const isHovered = activeSelectedIso === c.iso;
            const regionMeta = REGIONS[c.region];

            return (
              <div
                key={c.iso}
                onMouseEnter={() => onHoverIso2(c.iso)}
                onMouseLeave={() => onHoverIso2(null)}
                onClick={() => onPinIso2(pinnedIso2 === c.iso ? null : c.iso)}
                className={`p-2.5 rounded-xl border flex items-center justify-between text-xs cursor-pointer transition-all ${
                  isHovered
                    ? "bg-blue-600/20 border-blue-500 text-white shadow-md ring-1 ring-blue-500/50"
                    : "bg-slate-950/40 border-slate-800 hover:border-slate-700 text-slate-300"
                }`}
              >
                <div className="flex items-center gap-2.5 min-w-0">
                  <span className="text-[10px] font-mono text-slate-500 w-5 text-right shrink-0">
                    {idx + 1}
                  </span>
                  <span
                    className="w-2 h-2 rounded-full shrink-0"
                    style={{ backgroundColor: regionMeta?.color ?? "#94a3b8" }}
                  />
                  <span className="font-semibold text-white truncate">{c.name}</span>
                  <span className="text-[10px] font-mono text-slate-400 uppercase bg-slate-800 px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded shrink-0">
                    {c.iso}
                  </span>
                </div>

                <div className="flex items-center gap-3 shrink-0 font-mono text-right">
                  <span className="text-white font-bold">{formatNumber(c.volume, locale)}</span>
                  <span className="text-slate-400 text-[11px] w-12">
                    {formatPercent(c.pct, locale, 1)}
                  </span>
                </div>
              </div>
            );
          })}
        </div>

        {filteredCountries.length > 10 && (
          <div className="pt-2 text-center">
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => setIsExpanded(!isExpanded)}
              className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 text-xs font-semibold text-blue-400 hover:text-blue-300 transition-colors"
            >
              <span>{isExpanded ? t("collapse", locale) : t("showMore", locale)}</span>
              {isExpanded ? <ChevronUp className="w-3.5 h-3.5" /> : <ChevronDown className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />}
            </button>
          </div>
        )}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

--- FILE: frontend/components/visualizations/immigration/ImmigrationExplorer.tsx ---
"use client";

import { useState, useMemo, useEffect } from "react";
import type { ImmigrationFlowIndicator, Locale } from "@/lib/types";
import TimelineScrubber from "./TimelineScrubber";
import ChoroplethMap from "./ChoroplethMap";
import ContinentCountrySplitView from "./ContinentCountrySplitView";
import { t } from "@/lib/i18n";

interface ImmigrationExplorerProps {
  indicator: ImmigrationFlowIndicator;
  locale: Locale;
}

const PLAYBACK_INTERVAL_MS = 1000;

export default function ImmigrationExplorer({
  indicator,
  locale,
}: ImmigrationExplorerProps) {
  const { years } = indicator;
  const yearStrings = useMemo(() => years.map((y) => y.year), [years]);

  const maxIndex = Math.max(0, years.length - 1);
  const defaultRangeStartIndex = Math.max(0, years.length - 5);

  const [currentIndex, setCurrentIndex] = useState<number>(maxIndex);
  const [rangeIndices, setRangeIndices] = useState<[number, number]>([
    defaultRangeStartIndex,
    maxIndex,
  ]);
  const [mode, setMode] = useState<"single" | "range">("single");
  const [isPlaying, setIsPlaying] = useState<boolean>(false);

  const [hoveredIso2, setHoveredIso2] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [pinnedIso2, setPinnedIso2] = useState<string | null>(null);

  // Playback timer
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!isPlaying || mode !== "single") return;

    if (currentIndex >= maxIndex) {
      setIsPlaying(false);
      return;
    }

    const timer = window.setTimeout(() => {
      setCurrentIndex((prev) => {
        if (prev >= maxIndex - 1) {
          setIsPlaying(false);
          return maxIndex;
        }
        return prev + 1;
      });
    }, PLAYBACK_INTERVAL_MS);

    return () => window.clearTimeout(timer);
  }, [isPlaying, mode, currentIndex, maxIndex]);

  // Aggregate metrics based on mode
  const {
    volumeByCountryIso2,
    residualByRegion,
    totalArrivals,
    timeframeLabel,
  } = useMemo(() => {
    const byCountry: Record<string, number> = {};
    const byRegion: Record<string, number> = {};
    let total = 0;

    let targetYears: typeof years = [];

    if (mode === "single") {
      const singleYear = years[currentIndex];
      targetYears = singleYear ? [singleYear] : [];
    } else {
      const [start, end] = rangeIndices;
      targetYears = years.slice(Math.min(start, end), Math.max(start, end) + 1);
    }

    for (const y of targetYears) {
      total += y.declaredTotal;
      for (const [iso, val] of Object.entries(y.byCountryIso2)) {
        byCountry[iso] = (byCountry[iso] ?? 0) + val;
      }
      for (const [rId, val] of Object.entries(y.residualByRegion)) {
        byRegion[rId] = (byRegion[rId] ?? 0) + val;
      }
    }

    const label =
      mode === "single"
        ? `${t("yearLabel", locale)} ${yearStrings[currentIndex] ?? ""}`
        : `${yearStrings[rangeIndices[0]] ?? ""}–${yearStrings[rangeIndices[1]] ?? ""}`;

    return {
      volumeByCountryIso2: byCountry,
      residualByRegion: byRegion,
      totalArrivals: total,
      timeframeLabel: label,
    };
  }, [years, mode, currentIndex, rangeIndices, yearStrings, locale]);

  return (
    <div className="space-y-6">
      {/* 1. Top Timeline Scrubber */}
      <TimelineScrubber
        years={yearStrings}
        currentIndex={currentIndex}
        onIndexChange={setCurrentIndex}
        rangeIndices={rangeIndices}
        onRangeChange={setRangeIndices}
        mode={mode}
        onModeChange={setMode}
        isPlaying={isPlaying}
        onPlayPause={setIsPlaying}
        locale={locale}
      />

      {/* 2. Interactive Natural Earth Choropleth Map */}
      <ChoroplethMap
        timeframeLabel={timeframeLabel}
        volumeByCountryIso2={volumeByCountryIso2}
        totalVolume={totalArrivals}
        hoveredIso2={hoveredIso2}
        onHoverIso2={setHoveredIso2}
        pinnedIso2={pinnedIso2}
        onPinIso2={setPinnedIso2}
        locale={locale}
      />

      {/* 3. Continent Donut & Country Ranking Split View */}
      <ContinentCountrySplitView
        volumeByCountryIso2={volumeByCountryIso2}
        residualByRegion={residualByRegion}
        totalArrivals={totalArrivals}
        hoveredIso2={hoveredIso2}
        onHoverIso2={setHoveredIso2}
        pinnedIso2={pinnedIso2}
        onPinIso2={setPinnedIso2}
        selectedTimeframeLabel={timeframeLabel}
        locale={locale}
      />
    </div>
  );
}

--- FILE: frontend/components/visualizations/immigration/TimelineScrubber.tsx ---
"use client";

import { Play, Pause, ChevronLeft, ChevronRight, Calendar, Layers } from "lucide-react";
import type { Locale } from "@/lib/types";
import { t } from "@/lib/i18n";

interface TimelineScrubberProps {
  years: string[];
  currentIndex: number;
  onIndexChange: (index: number) => void;
  rangeIndices: [number, number];
  onRangeChange: (indices: [number, number]) => void;
  mode: "single" | "range";
  onModeChange: (mode: "single" | "range") => void;
  isPlaying: boolean;
  onPlayPause: (playing: boolean) => void;
  locale: Locale;
}

export default function TimelineScrubber({
  years,
  currentIndex,
  onIndexChange,
  rangeIndices,
  onRangeChange,
  mode,
  onModeChange,
  isPlaying,
  onPlayPause,
  locale,
}: TimelineScrubberProps) {
  const maxIdx = Math.max(0, years.length - 1);
  const activeYear = years[currentIndex] ?? years[0] ?? "";
  const startYear = years[rangeIndices[0]] ?? years[0] ?? "";
  const endYear = years[rangeIndices[1]] ?? years[maxIdx] ?? "";

  const handleStepPrev = () => {
    onPlayPause(false);
    if (currentIndex > 0) {
      onIndexChange(currentIndex - 1);
    }
  };

  const handleStepNext = () => {
    onPlayPause(false);
    if (currentIndex < maxIdx) {
      onIndexChange(currentIndex + 1);
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className="bg-slate-900 border border-slate-800 rounded-2xl p-4 sm:p-5 text-white shadow-xl space-y-4">
      <div className="flex flex-col md:flex-row items-stretch md:items-center justify-between gap-4">
        {/* Playback Controls & Mode Switcher */}
        <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
          {/* Step Prev */}
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={handleStepPrev}
            disabled={currentIndex <= 0}
            className="w-8 h-8 rounded-lg bg-slate-800 hover:bg-slate-700 text-slate-300 hover:text-white disabled:opacity-40 flex items-center justify-center transition-colors"
            title={t("previousYear", locale)}
            aria-label={t("previousYear", locale)}
          >
            <ChevronLeft className="w-4 h-4" />
          </button>

          {/* Play / Pause */}
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => onPlayPause(!isPlaying)}
            className="w-10 h-10 rounded-xl bg-blue-600 hover:bg-blue-500 text-white flex items-center justify-center shadow-lg transition-all active:scale-95"
            title={isPlaying ? t("pauseAnimation", locale) : t("playChronology", locale)}
            aria-label={isPlaying ? t("pauseAnimation", locale) : t("playChronology", locale)}
          >
            {isPlaying ? (
              <Pause className="w-5 h-5 fill-current" />
            ) : (
              <Play className="w-5 h-5 fill-current ml-0.5" />
            )}
          </button>

          {/* Step Next */}
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={handleStepNext}
            disabled={currentIndex >= maxIdx}
            className="w-8 h-8 rounded-lg bg-slate-800 hover:bg-slate-700 text-slate-300 hover:text-white disabled:opacity-40 flex items-center justify-center transition-colors"
            title={t("nextYear", locale)}
            aria-label={t("nextYear", locale)}
          >
            <ChevronRight className="w-4 h-4" />
          </button>

          {/* Year Display Badge */}
          <div className="pl-2 border-l border-slate-800">
            <span className="text-[10px] font-semibold text-slate-400 uppercase tracking-wider block">
              {mode === "single" ? t("activeYear", locale) : t("accumulatedRange", locale)}
            </span>
            <span className="text-lg font-black font-mono text-blue-400">
              {mode === "single" ? activeYear : `${startYear} – ${endYear}`}
            </span>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Mode Selector Toggle */}
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2 bg-slate-800/80 p-1 rounded-xl border border-slate-700/60 self-start md:self-auto">
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => {
              onPlayPause(false);
              onModeChange("single");
            }}
            className={`px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg text-xs font-bold transition-all flex items-center gap-1.5 ${
              mode === "single"
                ? "bg-blue-600 text-white shadow-xs"
                : "text-slate-400 hover:text-white"
            }`}
          >
            <Calendar className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
            <span>{t("singleYear", locale)}</span>
          </button>
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => {
              onPlayPause(false);
              onModeChange("range");
            }}
            className={`px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg text-xs font-bold transition-all flex items-center gap-1.5 ${
              mode === "range"
                ? "bg-blue-600 text-white shadow-xs"
                : "text-slate-400 hover:text-white"
            }`}
          >
            <Layers className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
            <span>{t("yearRange", locale)}</span>
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Index-Driven Slider */}
      {mode === "single" ? (
        <div className="space-y-2">
          <input
            type="range"
            min={0}
            max={maxIdx}
            step={1}
            value={currentIndex}
            onChange={(e) => {
              onPlayPause(false);
              onIndexChange(parseInt(e.target.value, 10));
            }}
            className="w-full h-2.5 bg-slate-800 rounded-lg appearance-none cursor-pointer accent-blue-500"
            aria-label={t("activeYear", locale)}
          />
          <div className="flex justify-between text-[11px] font-mono text-slate-400 px-1 overflow-x-auto no-scrollbar">
            {years.map((y, idx) => (
              <button
                type="button"
                key={y}
                onClick={() => {
                  onPlayPause(false);
                  onIndexChange(idx);
                }}
                className={`hover:text-blue-400 transition-colors px-1 ${
                  currentIndex === idx ? "text-blue-400 font-bold" : ""
                }`}
              >
                {y}
              </button>
            ))}
          </div>
        </div>
      ) : (
        <div className="space-y-3">
          <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-4">
            <div>
              <label className="text-[10px] text-slate-400 block mb-1">
                {t("period", locale)} Start: {startYear}
              </label>
              <input
                type="range"
                min={0}
                max={rangeIndices[1]}
                step={1}
                value={rangeIndices[0]}
                onChange={(e) => {
                  onPlayPause(false);
                  onRangeChange([parseInt(e.target.value, 10), rangeIndices[1]]);
                }}
                className="w-full h-2.5 bg-slate-800 rounded-lg appearance-none cursor-pointer accent-blue-500"
              />
            </div>
            <div>
              <label className="text-[10px] text-slate-400 block mb-1">
                {t("period", locale)} End: {endYear}
              </label>
              <input
                type="range"
                min={rangeIndices[0]}
                max={maxIdx}
                step={1}
                value={rangeIndices[1]}
                onChange={(e) => {
                  onPlayPause(false);
                  onRangeChange([rangeIndices[0], parseInt(e.target.value, 10)]);
                }}
                className="w-full h-2.5 bg-slate-800 rounded-lg appearance-none cursor-pointer accent-blue-500"
              />
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

--- FILE: frontend/components/visualizations/immigration/jenks.ts ---
export interface ChoroplethClass {
  classIndex: number;
  min: number;
  max: number;
  color: string;
}

export interface ChoroplethClassification {
  classes: ChoroplethClass[];
  minVal: number;
  maxVal: number;
  getClass(value: number): ChoroplethClass | null;
  getColor(value: number, fallbackColor?: string): string;
}

/**
 * Jenks natural breaks algorithm returning cut points.
 */
function getJenksCutPoints(sortedData: number[], numClasses: number): number[] {
  const n = sortedData.length;
  if (n === 0) return [];
  if (numClasses >= n) {
    return Array.from(new Set(sortedData));
  }

  const mat1: number[][] = Array(n + 1)
    .fill(0)
    .map(() => Array(numClasses + 1).fill(0));
  const mat2: number[][] = Array(n + 1)
    .fill(0)
    .map(() => Array(numClasses + 1).fill(0));

  for (let i = 1; i <= numClasses; i++) {
    mat1[1][i] = 1;
    mat2[1][i] = 0;
    for (let j = 2; j <= n; j++) {
      mat2[j][i] = Infinity;
    }
  }

  for (let l = 2; l <= n; l++) {
    let sum = 0;
    let sumSquares = 0;
    let w = 0;
    for (let m = 1; m <= l; m++) {
      const val = sortedData[l - m];
      w++;
      sum += val;
      sumSquares += val * val;
      const variance = sumSquares - (sum * sum) / w;
      const i4 = l - m;
      if (i4 !== 0) {
        for (let j = 2; j <= numClasses; j++) {
          if (mat2[l][j] >= variance + mat2[i4][j - 1]) {
            mat1[l][j] = i4;
            mat2[l][j] = variance + mat2[i4][j - 1];
          }
        }
      }
    }
    mat1[l][1] = 1;
    mat2[l][1] = sumSquares - (sum * sum) / w;
  }

  let k = n;
  const kclass: number[] = Array(numClasses + 1).fill(0);
  kclass[numClasses] = sortedData[n - 1];
  kclass[0] = sortedData[0];

  let countNum = numClasses;
  while (countNum >= 2) {
    const idx = Math.round(mat1[k][countNum]);
    kclass[countNum - 1] = sortedData[idx - 1];
    k = idx;
    countNum--;
  }

  const cutPoints: number[] = [];
  for (let i = 1; i < kclass.length; i++) {
    cutPoints.push(kclass[i]);
  }

  return Array.from(new Set(cutPoints)).sort((a, b) => a - b);
}

/**
 * Pure, hardened choropleth classifier with graceful degradation.
 */
export function classifyChoropleth(
  values: number[],
  palette: readonly string[]
): ChoroplethClassification {
  if (!palette || palette.length === 0) {
    throw new Error("Palette must contain at least one color");
  }

  const positive = values
    .filter((v) => typeof v === "number" && Number.isFinite(v) && v > 0)
    .sort((a, b) => a - b);

  if (positive.length === 0) {
    return {
      classes: [],
      minVal: 0,
      maxVal: 0,
      getClass: () => null,
      getColor: (_val, fallbackColor = "#1e293b") => fallbackColor,
    };
  }

  const minVal = positive[0];
  const maxVal = positive[positive.length - 1];
  const uniqueValues = Array.from(new Set(positive));

  // Case: Only 1 unique value
  if (uniqueValues.length === 1) {
    const singleClass: ChoroplethClass = {
      classIndex: 0,
      min: minVal,
      max: maxVal,
      color: palette[0],
    };
    return {
      classes: [singleClass],
      minVal,
      maxVal,
      getClass: (val) => (val > 0 ? singleClass : null),
      getColor: (val, fallback = "#1e293b") => (val > 0 ? palette[0] : fallback),
    };
  }

  // Gracefully degrade target classes count
  const targetClassesCount = Math.min(palette.length, uniqueValues.length);
  const cutPoints = getJenksCutPoints(positive, targetClassesCount);

  // Build contiguous classes
  const classes: ChoroplethClass[] = [];
  let prevUpper = minVal;

  for (let i = 0; i < cutPoints.length; i++) {
    const upper = cutPoints[i];
    classes.push({
      classIndex: i,
      min: prevUpper,
      max: upper,
      color: palette[i] ?? palette[palette.length - 1],
    });
    prevUpper = upper;
  }

  const getClass = (value: number): ChoroplethClass | null => {
    if (typeof value !== "number" || !Number.isFinite(value) || value <= 0) {
      return null;
    }
    for (const c of classes) {
      if (value <= c.max) {
        return c;
      }
    }
    return classes[classes.length - 1] ?? null;
  };

  const getColor = (value: number, fallbackColor = "#1e293b"): string => {
    const matched = getClass(value);
    return matched ? matched.color : fallbackColor;
  };

  return {
    classes,
    minVal,
    maxVal,
    getClass,
    getColor,
  };
}

--- FILE: frontend/lib/conceptGuide.test.ts ---
import { describe, it, expect } from "vitest";
import fs from "node:fs";
import path from "node:path";
import type { ConceptGuide, GlossaryTerm } from "@/lib/types";

const GUIDE_PATH = path.join(process.cwd(), "data", "locales", "es", "cuentas-publicas-espana.json");

function normalizeText(text: string): string {
  return text
    .normalize("NFD")
    .replace(/[\u0300-\u036f]/g, "")
    .toLowerCase();
}

function filterTerms(
  terms: GlossaryTerm[],
  categoryId: string,
  query: string
): GlossaryTerm[] {
  const normQuery = normalizeText(query.trim());
  return terms.filter((term) => {
    if (categoryId !== "all" && term.categoryId !== categoryId) {
      return false;
    }
    if (normQuery) {
      const c = normalizeText(term.code);
      const dc = normalizeText(term.displayCode);
      const t = normalizeText(term.title);
      const s = normalizeText(term.shortDefinition);
      const e = normalizeText(term.explanation);
      return (
        c.includes(normQuery) ||
        dc.includes(normQuery) ||
        t.includes(normQuery) ||
        s.includes(normQuery) ||
        e.includes(normQuery)
      );
    }
    return true;
  });
}

describe("Concept Guide & Technical Glossary", () => {
  const guideJson: ConceptGuide = JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(GUIDE_PATH, "utf-8")).conceptGuide;

  it("loads valid guide JSON structure", () => {
    expect(guideJson.title).toBeTruthy();
    expect(guideJson.subtitle).toBeTruthy();
    expect(Array.isArray(guideJson.quickKeys)).toBe(true);
    expect(guideJson.quickKeys.length).toBeGreaterThan(0);
    expect(Array.isArray(guideJson.categories)).toBe(true);
    expect(Array.isArray(guideJson.terms)).toBe(true);
  });

  it("has zero duplicate terms[].code values", () => {
    const seen = new Set<string>();
    for (const term of guideJson.terms) {
      expect(seen.has(term.code)).toBe(false);
      seen.add(term.code);
    }
  });

  it("ensures every term.categoryId exists in categories", () => {
    const validCategories = new Set(guideJson.categories.map((c) => c.id));
    for (const term of guideJson.terms) {
      expect(validCategories.has(term.categoryId)).toBe(true);
    }
  });

  it("ensures every relatedCodes entry points to an existing term.code", () => {
    const validCodes = new Set(guideJson.terms.map((t) => t.code));
    for (const term of guideJson.terms) {
      if (term.relatedCodes) {
        for (const relCode of term.relatedCodes) {
          expect(validCodes.has(relCode)).toBe(true);
        }
      }
    }
  });

  it("includes glossary entries for all expected fiscal codes", () => {
    const validCodes = new Set(guideJson.terms.map((t) => t.code));
    const requiredCodes = [
      "SEC2010",
      "S13",
      "PC_GDP",
      "TR",
      "TE",
      "B9",
      "D2",
      "D5",
      "D61",
      "D91",
      "NON_TAX_RESIDUAL",
      "GF01",
      "GF02",
      "GF03",
      "GF04",
      "GF05",
      "GF06",
      "GF07",
      "GF08",
      "GF09",
      "GF10",
      "GF0107",
    ];

    for (const code of requiredCodes) {
      expect(validCodes.has(code)).toBe(true);
    }
  });

  describe("Filtering & Lookup Logic", () => {
    it("filters terms by category correctly", () => {
      const revenueTerms = filterTerms(guideJson.terms, "revenues", "");
      expect(revenueTerms.length).toBeGreaterThan(0);
      revenueTerms.forEach((t) => {
        expect(t.categoryId).toBe("revenues");
      });
    });

    it("filters terms by query with case-insensitive and accent-insensitive matching", () => {
      // Searching "b.9"
      const b9Results = filterTerms(guideJson.terms, "all", "b.9");
      expect(b9Results.some((t) => t.code === "B9")).toBe(true);

      // Searching "proteccion" (without accent) matches "Protección"
      const accentResults = filterTerms(guideJson.terms, "all", "proteccion");
      expect(accentResults.some((t) => t.code === "GF10")).toBe(true);
    });
  });
});

--- FILE: frontend/lib/data-integrity.test.ts ---
import { describe, it, expect } from "vitest";
import fs from "node:fs";
import path from "node:path";
import { IndicatorSchema } from "./types";
import { loadIndicatorSync } from "./data";

function getDataDir(): string {
  const cwd = process.cwd();
  if (fs.existsSync(path.join(cwd, "frontend", "data", "datasets"))) {
    return path.join(cwd, "frontend", "data");
  }
  if (fs.existsSync(path.join(cwd, "data", "datasets"))) {
    return path.join(cwd, "data");
  }
  return path.join(cwd, "frontend", "data");
}

describe("Data Integrity & Invariants", () => {
  const dataDir = getDataDir();
  const datasetsDir = path.join(dataDir, "datasets");
  const localesEsDir = path.join(dataDir, "locales", "es");
  const localesEnDir = path.join(dataDir, "locales", "en");

  const files = fs.existsSync(datasetsDir) ? fs.readdirSync(datasetsDir).filter((f) => f.endsWith(".json")) : [];

  it("should have at least one indicator file in datasets", () => {
    expect(files.length).toBeGreaterThan(0);
  });

  it("asserts every dataset has matching localization files in both es and en", () => {
    files.forEach((file) => {
      const slug = file.replace(/\.json$/, "");
      const esFile = path.join(localesEsDir, file);
      const enFile = path.join(localesEnDir, file);

      expect(fs.existsSync(esFile), `Missing ES locale for ${slug}`).toBe(true);
      expect(fs.existsSync(enFile), `Missing EN locale for ${slug}`).toBe(true);

      const esData = JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(esFile, "utf-8"));
      const enData = JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(enFile, "utf-8"));

      expect(esData.question).toBeTruthy();
      expect(enData.question).toBeTruthy();
      expect(esData.unit).toBeTruthy();
      expect(enData.unit).toBeTruthy();
    });
  });

  files.forEach((file) => {
    const slug = file.replace(/\.json$/, "");
    describe(`Indicator: ${file}`, () => {
      const raw = loadIndicatorSync(slug, "es");

      it("conforms strictly to IndicatorSchema", () => {
        expect(raw).not.toBeNull();
        const result = IndicatorSchema.safeParse(raw);
        if (!result.success) {
          console.error(`Validation errors in ${file}:`, result.error.format());
        }
        expect(result.success).toBe(true);
      });

      if (raw && raw.kind === "immigration-flow" && (raw as any).years) {
        it("reconciles immigration numbers: sum(byCountryIso2) + sum(residualByRegion) === declaredTotal", () => {
          for (const yearData of (raw as any).years) {
            const countrySum = Object.values(yearData.byCountryIso2 as Record<string, number>).reduce(
              (acc, v) => acc + v,
              0
            );
            const residualSum = Object.values(
              yearData.residualByRegion as Record<string, number>
            ).reduce((acc, v) => acc + v, 0);
            const totalSum = countrySum + residualSum;

            expect(Math.abs(totalSum - yearData.declaredTotal)).toBeLessThanOrEqual(2);
          }
        });
      }

      if (raw && raw.kind === "fiscal-balance" && raw.points) {
        it("reconciles fiscal figures: sum(stateRevenue) ≈ totalRevenue and sum(stateSpending) ≈ totalSpending", () => {
          for (const point of raw.points) {
            const revSum = Object.values(point.stateRevenueMmEur as Record<string, number>).reduce(
              (acc, v) => acc + v,
              0
            );
            const spendSum = Object.values(point.stateSpendingMmEur as Record<string, number>).reduce(
              (acc, v) => acc + v,
              0
            );

            expect(Math.abs(revSum - point.totalRevenueMmEur)).toBeLessThanOrEqual(0.1);
            expect(Math.abs(spendSum - point.totalSpendingMmEur)).toBeLessThanOrEqual(0.1);
            expect(
              Math.abs(point.totalRevenueMmEur - point.totalSpendingMmEur - point.deficitMmEur)
            ).toBeLessThanOrEqual(0.1);
          }
        });
      }
    });
  });
});

--- FILE: frontend/lib/data.ts ---
import fs from "node:fs";
import path from "node:path";
import {
  IndicatorSchema,
  type Indicator,
  type DirectoryIndexItem,
  type Locale,
  type DatoItem,
  type ItemIndexItem,
  type GlobalMetadata,
  type Category,
  type SourceInfo,
} from "./types";

function getDataDir(): string {
  const cwd = process.cwd();
  if (fs.existsSync(path.join(cwd, "frontend", "data", "datasets"))) {
    return path.join(cwd, "frontend", "data");
  }
  if (fs.existsSync(path.join(cwd, "data", "datasets"))) {
    return path.join(cwd, "data");
  }
  return path.join(cwd, "frontend", "data");
}

export function loadIndicatorSync(slug: string, requestedLang?: Locale): Indicator | null {
  if (!slug || slug === "_none" || !/^[a-z0-9-]+$/.test(slug)) {
    return null;
  }

  const dataDir = getDataDir();
  const datasetPath = path.join(dataDir, "datasets", `${slug}.json`);
  if (!fs.existsSync(datasetPath)) {
    return null;
  }

  try {
    const rawData = JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(datasetPath, "utf-8"));
    const esPath = path.join(dataDir, "locales", "es", `${slug}.json`);
    const enPath = path.join(dataDir, "locales", "en", `${slug}.json`);

    const esText = fs.existsSync(esPath) ? JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(esPath, "utf-8")) : {};
    const enText = fs.existsSync(enPath) ? JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(enPath, "utf-8")) : {};

    const sources = (rawData.sources || []).map((src: any, idx: number) => {
      const esSrc = Array.isArray(esText.sources) ? esText.sources[idx] : esText.sources?.[src.id];
      const enSrc = Array.isArray(enText.sources) ? enText.sources[idx] : enText.sources?.[src.id];
      return {
        ...src,
        institution: {
          es: esSrc?.institution || (typeof src.institution === "string" ? src.institution : "Fuente Oficial"),
          en: enSrc?.institution || esSrc?.institution || (typeof src.institution === "string" ? src.institution : "Official Source"),
        },
        title: {
          es: esSrc?.title || (typeof src.title === "string" ? src.title : "Fuente Oficial"),
          en: enSrc?.title || esSrc?.title || (typeof src.title === "string" ? src.title : "Official Source"),
        },
        shortName: (esSrc?.shortName || enSrc?.shortName || src.shortName) ? {
          es: esSrc?.shortName || (typeof src.shortName === "string" ? src.shortName : esSrc?.title || "Fuente Oficial"),
          en: enSrc?.shortName || enSrc?.title || esSrc?.shortName || (typeof src.shortName === "string" ? src.shortName : "Official Source"),
        } : undefined,
      };
    });

    const merged = {
      ...rawData,
      question: {
        es: esText.question || rawData.question?.es || "",
        en: enText.question || esText.question || rawData.question?.en || "",
      },
      unit: {
        es: esText.unit || rawData.unit?.es || "",
        en: enText.unit || esText.unit || rawData.unit?.en || "",
      },
      contextNotes: {
        es: esText.contextNotes || rawData.contextNotes?.es || [],
        en: enText.contextNotes || esText.contextNotes || rawData.contextNotes?.en || [],
      },
      sources,
      conceptGuide: esText.conceptGuide || enText.conceptGuide || rawData.conceptGuide,
    };

    const result = IndicatorSchema.safeParse(merged);
    if (!result.success) {
      console.error(`[loadIndicatorSync] Invalid schema for indicator ${slug}:`, result.error);
      return null;
    }

    return result.data;
  } catch (err) {
    console.error(`[loadIndicatorSync] Failed to load indicator ${slug}:`, err);
    return null;
  }
}

export async function getAllIndicatorSlugs(): Promise<string[]> {
  try {
    const dataDir = getDataDir();
    const datasetsDir = path.join(dataDir, "datasets");
    if (fs.existsSync(datasetsDir)) {
      const files = fs.readdirSync(datasetsDir).filter((f) => f.endsWith(".json"));
      return files.map((f) => f.replace(/\.json$/, ""));
    }
  } catch (err) {
    console.error("[getAllIndicatorSlugs] Error listing indicator files:", err);
  }
  return [];
}

export async function getDirectoryIndex(lang: Locale = "es"): Promise<DirectoryIndexItem[]> {
  const slugs = await getAllIndicatorSlugs();
  const items: DirectoryIndexItem[] = [];

  for (const slug of slugs) {
    const indicator = loadIndicatorSync(slug, lang);
    if (!indicator) continue;

    let latestValue = 0;
    let latestPeriod = "";
    let timeHorizon = "";
    let sparkline: number[] = [];

    if (indicator.kind === "time-series") {
      const first = indicator.points[0];
      const last = indicator.points[indicator.points.length - 1];
      latestPeriod = last.period;
      latestValue = last.value;
      timeHorizon = indicator.points.length > 1 ? `${first.period}–${last.period}` : last.period;
      sparkline = indicator.points.map((p) => p.value);
    } else if (indicator.kind === "fiscal-balance") {
      if ((indicator as any).annualBalances && (indicator as any).annualBalances.length > 0) {
        const balances = (indicator as any).annualBalances;
        const first = balances[0];
        const last = balances[balances.length - 1];
        latestPeriod = last.year;
        latestValue = last.metrics?.PC_GDP?.totals?.balance ?? 0;
        timeHorizon = balances.length > 1 ? `${first.year}–${last.year}` : last.year;
        sparkline = balances.map((b: any) => b.metrics?.PC_GDP?.totals?.balance ?? 0);
      }
    } else if (indicator.kind === "immigration-flow") {
      if ((indicator as any).years && (indicator as any).years.length > 0) {
        const first = (indicator as any).years[0];
        const last = (indicator as any).years[(indicator as any).years.length - 1];
        latestPeriod = last.year;
        latestValue = last.declaredTotal;
        timeHorizon = (indicator as any).years.length > 1 ? `${first.year}–${last.year}` : last.year;
        sparkline = (indicator as any).years.map((y: any) => y.declaredTotal);
      } else if ((indicator as any).annualBlocks && (indicator as any).annualBlocks.length > 0) {
        const blocks = (indicator as any).annualBlocks;
        const first = blocks[0];
        const last = blocks[blocks.length - 1];
        latestPeriod = last.year;
        latestValue = last.total;
        timeHorizon = blocks.length > 1 ? `${first.year}–${last.year}` : last.year;
        sparkline = blocks.map((b: any) => b.total);
      }
    } else if (indicator.kind === "nationality-indicators") {
      const records = (indicator as any).annualRecords;
      const first = records[0];
      const last = records[records.length - 1];
      latestPeriod = last.year;
      latestValue = last.definitions?.citizenship?.demographics?.foreigners?.shareOfTotalPct ?? 0;
      timeHorizon = records.length > 1 ? `${first.year}–${last.year}` : last.year;
      sparkline = records.map((r: any) => r.definitions?.citizenship?.demographics?.foreigners?.shareOfTotalPct ?? 0);
    } else if (indicator.kind === "admin-registers-nationality") {
      const pts = indicator.dataPoints ?? [];
      const records = indicator.annualRecords;
      if (pts.length > 0) {
        const first = pts[0];
        const last = pts[pts.length - 1];
        latestPeriod = last.year;
        latestValue = last.value;
        timeHorizon = pts.length > 1 ? `${first.year}–${last.year}` : last.year;
        sparkline = pts.map((p) => p.value);
      } else if (records && records.length > 0) {
        latestPeriod = records[records.length - 1].year;
        timeHorizon = records.length > 1 ? `${records[0].year}–${latestPeriod}` : latestPeriod;
      }
    }

    items.push({
      id: indicator.id,
      kind: indicator.kind,
      category: indicator.category,
      question: indicator.question,
      unit: indicator.unit,
      valueFormat: indicator.valueFormat,
      latestValue,
      latestPeriod,
      timeHorizon,
      primarySource: {
        es: indicator.sources[0]?.institution.es || "",
        en: indicator.sources[0]?.institution.en || "",
      },
      searchSummary: {
        es: indicator.contextNotes.es[0] ?? indicator.question.es,
        en: indicator.contextNotes.en[0] ?? indicator.question.en,
      },
      sparkline,
    });
  }

  return items;
}

export async function getIndicatorBySlug(slug: string, lang: Locale = "es"): Promise<Indicator | null> {
  return loadIndicatorSync(slug, lang);
}

export async function fetchItemDetail(id: string, lang: Locale = "es"): Promise<DatoItem | null> {
  const indicator = loadIndicatorSync(id, lang);
  if (!indicator) return null;

  const sourcesList: SourceInfo[] = indicator.sources.map((s) => ({
    institution: s.institution[lang] || s.institution.es,
    title: s.title[lang] || s.title.es,
    url: s.url || "#",
    shortName: s.shortName ? (s.shortName[lang] || s.shortName.es) : undefined,
    tableId: s.tableId,
    period: s.period,
    lastUpdated: s.lastUpdated,
  }));

  return {
    ...indicator,
    question: indicator.question[lang] || indicator.question.es,
    unit: indicator.unit[lang] || indicator.unit.es,
    contextNotes: indicator.contextNotes[lang] || indicator.contextNotes.es,
    chartType: indicator.kind as any,
    sources: sourcesList,
    dataPoints: (indicator as any).dataPoints || [],
  } as DatoItem;
}

export async function fetchMetadata(lang: Locale = "es"): Promise<GlobalMetadata> {
  const slugs = await getAllIndicatorSlugs();
  return {
    last_updated: new Date().toISOString().split("T")[0],
    total_items: slugs.length,
  };
}

export async function fetchItems(lang: Locale = "es"): Promise<ItemIndexItem[]> {
  const dirIndex = await getDirectoryIndex(lang);
  return dirIndex.map((item) => ({
    id: item.id,
    category: item.category,
    question: item.question[lang] || item.question.es,
    unit: item.unit[lang] || item.unit.es,
    latestValue: item.latestValue,
    latestYear: item.latestPeriod,
    lastUpdated: new Date().toISOString().split("T")[0],
  }));
}

--- FILE: frontend/lib/format.test.ts ---
import { describe, it, expect } from "vitest";
import {
  formatNumber,
  formatCompactNumber,
  formatPercent,
  formatCurrency,
  formatIndicatorValue,
} from "./format";

describe("Locale-Aware Number Formatters", () => {
  describe("formatNumber", () => {
    it("formats thousands separator in Spanish (period)", () => {
      expect(formatNumber(1250000, "es")).toBe("1.250.000");
    });

    it("formats thousands separator in English (comma)", () => {
      expect(formatNumber(1250000, "en")).toBe("1,250,000");
    });

    it("handles 0 and non-finite values safely", () => {
      expect(formatNumber(0, "es")).toBe("0");
      expect(formatNumber(NaN, "es")).toBe("0");
      expect(formatNumber(Infinity, "es")).toBe("0");
    });
  });

  describe("formatCompactNumber", () => {
    it("formats compact notation in Spanish", () => {
      expect(formatCompactNumber(500, "es")).toBe("500");
      expect(formatCompactNumber(1500, "es")).toBe("2 mil");
      expect(formatCompactNumber(1500000, "es")).toBe("1,5 M");
      expect(formatCompactNumber(2500000000, "es")).toBe("2,5 MM");
    });

    it("formats compact notation in English", () => {
      expect(formatCompactNumber(500, "en")).toBe("500");
      expect(formatCompactNumber(1500, "en")).toBe("1.5K");
      expect(formatCompactNumber(1500000, "en")).toBe("1.5M");
      expect(formatCompactNumber(2500000000, "en")).toBe("2.5B");
    });
  });

  describe("formatPercent", () => {
    it("formats percentages with 1 decimal by default", () => {
      expect(formatPercent(187.0, "es")).toBe("187,0%");
      expect(formatPercent(187.0, "en")).toBe("187.0%");
    });

    it("formats percentages with custom decimal places", () => {
      expect(formatPercent(45.678, "es", 2)).toBe("45,68%");
      expect(formatPercent(45.678, "en", 2)).toBe("45.68%");
    });
  });

  describe("formatCurrency", () => {
    it("formats currency in EUR", () => {
      expect(formatCurrency(1500, "es", "eur")).toBe("1.500 €");
      expect(formatCurrency(1500, "en", "eur")).toBe("1,500 €");
    });

    it("formats currency in MM€", () => {
      expect(formatCurrency(661.5, "es", "mmeur")).toBe("661,5 MM€");
      expect(formatCurrency(661.5, "en", "mmeur")).toBe("661.5 MM€");
    });
  });

  describe("formatIndicatorValue", () => {
    it("formats according to ValueFormat enum", () => {
      expect(formatIndicatorValue(187, "percent", "es")).toBe("187,0%");
      expect(formatIndicatorValue(1288562, "number", "es", false)).toBe("1.288.562");
      expect(formatIndicatorValue(1288562, "number", "es", true)).toBe("1,3 M");
    });
  });
});

--- FILE: frontend/lib/format.ts ---
import type { Locale, ValueFormat } from "./types";

const LOCALE_TAG: Record<Locale, string> = {
  es: "es-ES",
  en: "en-US",
};

/**
 * Formats a regular number with locale-accurate separators.
 */
export function formatNumber(
  val: number,
  locale: Locale = "es",
  options?: Intl.NumberFormatOptions
): string {
  if (!Number.isFinite(val)) return "0";
  return new Intl.NumberFormat(LOCALE_TAG[locale], options).format(val);
}

/**
 * Formats a compact number (K/M/B in English, mil/M/MM in Spanish).
 */
export function formatCompactNumber(val: number, locale: Locale = "es"): string {
  if (!Number.isFinite(val)) return "0";
  const abs = Math.abs(val);

  if (locale === "en") {
    if (abs >= 1e9) return `${(val / 1e9).toFixed(1)}B`;
    if (abs >= 1e6) return `${(val / 1e6).toFixed(1)}M`;
    if (abs >= 1e3) return `${(val / 1e3).toFixed(1)}K`;
    return val.toLocaleString("en-US");
  }

  // Spanish compact standards
  if (abs >= 1e9) return `${(val / 1e9).toFixed(1).replace(".", ",")} MM`;
  if (abs >= 1e6) return `${(val / 1e6).toFixed(1).replace(".", ",")} M`;
  if (abs >= 1e3) return `${Math.round(val / 1e3).toLocaleString("es-ES")} mil`;
  return val.toLocaleString("es-ES");
}

/**
 * Formats a percentage value.
 */
export function formatPercent(
  val: number,
  locale: Locale = "es",
  decimals = 1
): string {
  if (!Number.isFinite(val)) return "0%";
  const formatted = val.toLocaleString(LOCALE_TAG[locale], {
    minimumFractionDigits: decimals,
    maximumFractionDigits: decimals,
  });
  return `${formatted}%`;
}

/**
 * Formats currency values in EUR or MM€ (millions of EUR).
 */
export function formatCurrency(
  val: number,
  locale: Locale = "es",
  unit: "eur" | "mmeur" = "eur"
): string {
  if (!Number.isFinite(val)) return "0 €";
  const formatted = new Intl.NumberFormat(LOCALE_TAG[locale], {
    maximumFractionDigits: unit === "mmeur" ? 2 : 0,
    useGrouping: true,
  }).format(val);
  return `${formatted} ${unit === "mmeur" ? "MM€" : "€"}`;
}

/**
 * High-level formatter based on ValueFormat enum.
 */
export function formatIndicatorValue(
  val: number,
  format: ValueFormat,
  locale: Locale = "es",
  compact = false
): string {
  switch (format) {
    case "percent":
      return formatPercent(val, locale);
    case "currency-eur":
      return formatCurrency(val, locale, "eur");
    case "currency-mmeur":
      return formatCurrency(val, locale, "mmeur");
    case "number":
    default:
      return compact ? formatCompactNumber(val, locale) : formatNumber(val, locale);
  }
}

import type { Locale } from "./types";

export type RegionId =
  | "america-hispanohablante"
  | "america-no-hispanohablante"
  | "europa"
  | "africa"
  | "asia"
  | "oceania";

export interface RegionMeta {
  id: RegionId;
  name: Record<Locale, string>;
  color: string;
}

export const REGIONS: Record<RegionId, RegionMeta> = {
  "america-hispanohablante": {
    id: "america-hispanohablante",
    name: {
      es: "América (Hispanohablante)",
      en: "Americas (Spanish-speaking)",
    },
    color: "#2563EB",
  },
  "america-no-hispanohablante": {
    id: "america-no-hispanohablante",
    name: {
      es: "América (No Hispanohablante)",
      en: "Americas (Other)",
    },
    color: "#7C3AED",
  },
  europa: {
    id: "europa",
    name: {
      es: "Europa",
      en: "Europe",
    },
    color: "#0891B2",
  },
  africa: {
    id: "africa",
    name: {
      es: "África",
      en: "Africa",
    },
    color: "#059669",
  },
  asia: {
    id: "asia",
    name: {
      es: "Asia",
      en: "Asia",
    },
    color: "#D97706",
  },
  oceania: {
    id: "oceania",
    name: {
      es: "Oceanía",
      en: "Oceania",

export interface CountryMeta {
