export function getCountryMeta(iso2: string): CountryMeta | undefined {
  return COUNTRIES[iso2.toUpperCase()];
}

export function getCountryName(iso2: string, locale: Locale): string {
  const meta = getCountryMeta(iso2);
  return meta?.name[locale] ?? iso2;
}

export function getCountryRegion(iso2: string): RegionId {
  return getCountryMeta(iso2)?.region ?? "europa";
}

export function getRegionMeta(regionId: RegionId): RegionMeta {
  return REGIONS[regionId] ?? REGIONS.europa;
}

export function getRegionName(regionId: RegionId, locale: Locale): string {
  return getRegionMeta(regionId).name[locale];
}

--- FILE: frontend/lib/i18n.ts ---
import type { Category, Locale } from "./types";
import esCommon from "../data/locales/es/common.json";
import enCommon from "../data/locales/en/common.json";
export { formatNumber, formatCompactNumber } from "./format";

const DICTIONARY: Record<Locale, Record<string, string>> = {
  es: esCommon,
  en: enCommon,
};

export const routes = {
  home: (locale: Locale = "es"): string => (locale === "en" ? "/en" : "/es"),
  indicator: (locale: Locale = "es", slug: string): string =>
    locale === "en" ? `/en/dato/${slug}` : `/es/dato/${slug}`,
  toggleLocale: (pathname: string, targetLocale: Locale): string => {
    const clean = pathname.replace(/\/$/, "");
    if (targetLocale === "en") {
      if (!clean || clean === "" || clean === "/es") return "/en";
      if (clean === "/en") return "/en";
      if (clean.startsWith("/en/")) return clean;
      if (clean.startsWith("/es/")) return clean.replace(/^\/es/, "/en");
      return `/en${clean}`;
    } else {
      if (clean === "/en" || !clean || clean === "") return "/es";
      if (clean.startsWith("/en/")) {
        return clean.replace(/^\/en/, "/es");
      }
      if (clean.startsWith("/es")) return clean;
      return `/es${clean}`;
    }
  },
};

export const CATEGORY_LABELS: Record<Category | "all", Record<Locale, string>> = {
  all: {
    es: "Todas",
    en: "All",
  },
  "pensions-demography": {
    es: "Pensiones y Demografía",
    en: "Pensions & Demography",
  },
  "economy-employment": {
    es: "Economía y Empleo",
    en: "Economy & Employment",
  },
  housing: {
    es: "Vivienda",
    en: "Housing",
  },
  healthcare: {
    es: "Sanidad",
    en: "Healthcare",
  },
};

export const CATEGORY_ICONS: Record<Category | "all", string> = {
  all: "📊",
  housing: "🏠",
  "economy-employment": "📈",
  healthcare: "🏥",
  "pensions-demography": "👥",
};

export function t(key: string, locale: Locale = "es"): string {
  return DICTIONARY[locale]?.[key] ?? DICTIONARY.es[key] ?? key;
}

export function translateCategory(category: Category | "all", locale: Locale = "es"): string {
  return CATEGORY_LABELS[category]?.[locale] ?? category;
}

--- FILE: frontend/lib/sanitize-url.test.ts ---
import { describe, it, expect } from "vitest";
import { sanitizeHttpUrl } from "./url";

describe("sanitizeHttpUrl Security Function", () => {
  it("permits standard https:// URLs", () => {
    const url = "https://www.ine.es/jaxiT3/Tabla.htm?t=56951";
    expect(sanitizeHttpUrl(url)).toBe(url);
  });

  it("permits standard http:// URLs", () => {
    const url = "http://datos.gob.es/catalogo";
    expect(sanitizeHttpUrl(url)).toBe(url);
  });

  it("blocks javascript: XSS attack vectors", () => {
    expect(sanitizeHttpUrl("javascript:alert(1)")).toBeNull();
    expect(sanitizeHttpUrl("JAVASCRIPT:document.location='http://evil.com'")).toBeNull();
  });

  it("blocks data: URI attack vectors", () => {
    expect(sanitizeHttpUrl("data:text/html,<script>alert(1)</script>")).toBeNull();
  });

  it("blocks file: and vbscript: URIs", () => {
    expect(sanitizeHttpUrl("file:///etc/passwd")).toBeNull();
    expect(sanitizeHttpUrl("vbscript:msgbox(1)")).toBeNull();
  });

  it("handles null, undefined, whitespace, and non-URL strings safely", () => {
    expect(sanitizeHttpUrl(null)).toBeNull();
    expect(sanitizeHttpUrl(undefined)).toBeNull();
    expect(sanitizeHttpUrl("")).toBeNull();
    expect(sanitizeHttpUrl("   ")).toBeNull();
    expect(sanitizeHttpUrl("not a valid url")).toBeNull();
  });
});

--- FILE: frontend/lib/types.ts ---
import { z } from "zod";

export const HttpUrlSchema = z
  .string()
  .url()
  .refine((u) => u.startsWith("https://") || u.startsWith("http://"), {
    message: "Only http(s) URLs are allowed",
  });

export const LocalizedTextSchema = z.object({
  es: z.string().min(1),
  en: z.string().min(1),
});

export const SourceSchema = z.object({
  institution: LocalizedTextSchema,
  title: LocalizedTextSchema,
  shortName: LocalizedTextSchema.optional(),
  tableId: z.string().optional(),
  period: z.string().optional(),
  lastUpdated: z.string().regex(/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/).optional(),
  url: HttpUrlSchema.optional(),
});

export const CategorySchema = z.enum([
  "housing",
  "economy-employment",
  "healthcare",
  "pensions-demography",
]);
export type Category = z.infer<typeof CategorySchema>;

export const ValueFormatSchema = z.enum([
  "number",
  "percent",
  "currency-eur",
  "currency-mmeur",
]);
export type ValueFormat = z.infer<typeof ValueFormatSchema>;

export const ConceptGuideSchema = z.object({
  title: z.string().min(1),
  subtitle: z.string().min(1),
  quickKeys: z.array(
    z.object({
      id: z.string(),
      badge: z.string(),
      title: z.string(),
      summary: z.string(),
      example: z.string().optional(),
    })
  ),
  categories: z.array(
    z.object({
      id: z.string(),
      label: z.string(),
    })
  ),
  terms: z.array(
    z.object({
      code: z.string(),
      displayCode: z.string(),
      categoryId: z.string(),
      title: z.string(),
      shortDefinition: z.string(),
      explanation: z.string(),
      keyNote: z.string().optional(),
      relatedCodes: z.array(z.string()).optional(),
    })
  ),
});

export const BaseIndicatorSchema = z.object({
  id: z.string().regex(/^[a-z0-9-]+$/),
  category: CategorySchema.default("economy-employment"),
  question: LocalizedTextSchema,
  unit: LocalizedTextSchema,
  valueFormat: ValueFormatSchema.default("number"),
  contextNotes: z.object({
    es: z.array(z.string().min(1)),
    en: z.array(z.string().min(1)),
  }),
  conceptGuide: ConceptGuideSchema.optional(),
  sources: z.array(SourceSchema).min(1),
  lastUpdated: z.string().regex(/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/).default("2026-01-01"),
});

export const TimeSeriesIndicatorSchema = BaseIndicatorSchema.extend({
  kind: z.literal("time-series"),
  chartStyle: z.enum(["line", "bar"]),
  points: z.array(z.object({ period: z.string(), value: z.number() })).min(1),
});

export const FiscalBalanceIndicatorSchema = BaseIndicatorSchema.extend({
  kind: z.literal("fiscal-balance"),
  availableUnits: z
    .array(
      z.object({
        id: z.string(),
        label: z.string(),
        decimals: z.number().optional(),
      })
    )
    .optional(),
  taxonomy: z
    .object({
      revenues: z.array(z.any()),
      expenditures: z.array(z.any()),
    })
    .optional(),
  annualBalances: z.array(z.any()).optional(),
  points: z
    .array(
      z.object({
        year: z.string(),
        ratio: z.object({ foreign: z.number(), spanish: z.number() }),
        perCapitaEur: z.object({ foreign: z.number(), spanish: z.number() }),
        stateRevenueMmEur: z.record(z.string(), z.number()),
        stateSpendingMmEur: z.record(z.string(), z.number()),
        totalRevenueMmEur: z.number(),
        totalSpendingMmEur: z.number(),
        deficitMmEur: z.number(),
      })
    )
    .optional(),
});

export const ImmigrationFlowIndicatorSchema = BaseIndicatorSchema.extend({
  kind: z.literal("immigration-flow"),
  years: z
    .array(
      z.object({
        year: z.string(),
        declaredTotal: z.number().nonnegative(),
        byCountryIso2: z.record(z.string().length(2), z.number().nonnegative()),
        residualByRegion: z.record(z.string(), z.number().nonnegative()),
      })
    )
    .min(1),
});

export const NationalityIndicatorsIndicatorSchema = BaseIndicatorSchema.extend({
  kind: z.literal("nationality-indicators"),
  annualRecords: z.array(z.any()),
  dataPoints: z.array(z.object({ year: z.string(), value: z.number() })).optional(),
});

export const AdminRegistersNationalityIndicatorSchema = BaseIndicatorSchema.extend({
  kind: z.literal("admin-registers-nationality"),
  annualRecords: z.array(z.any()).default([]),
  dataPoints: z.array(z.object({ year: z.string(), value: z.number() })).optional(),
});

export const IndicatorSchema = z.discriminatedUnion("kind", [
  TimeSeriesIndicatorSchema,
  FiscalBalanceIndicatorSchema,
  ImmigrationFlowIndicatorSchema,
  NationalityIndicatorsIndicatorSchema,
  AdminRegistersNationalityIndicatorSchema,
]);

export type Indicator = z.infer<typeof IndicatorSchema>;
export type TimeSeriesIndicator = z.infer<typeof TimeSeriesIndicatorSchema>;
export type FiscalBalanceIndicator = z.infer<typeof FiscalBalanceIndicatorSchema>;
export type ImmigrationFlowIndicator = z.infer<typeof ImmigrationFlowIndicatorSchema>;
export type NationalityIndicatorsIndicator = z.infer<typeof NationalityIndicatorsIndicatorSchema>;
export type AdminRegistersNationalityIndicator = z.infer<typeof AdminRegistersNationalityIndicatorSchema>;

export type Locale = "es" | "en";
export type Lang = Locale;

  id: string;
  kind: Indicator["kind"];
  category: Category;
  question: { es: string; en: string };
  unit: { es: string; en: string };

  latestValue: number;
  latestPeriod: string;
  timeHorizon: string;
  primarySource: { es: string; en: string };
  searchSummary: { es: string; en: string };
  sparkline: number[];
}

export function isFiscalBalanceItem(item: DatoItem): item is FiscalBalanceItem {
  return (
    item.chartType === "fiscal-balance" &&
    "annualBalances" in item &&
    Array.isArray((item as Partial<FiscalBalanceItem>).annualBalances)
  );
}

export interface NationalityIndicatorsItem extends DatoItem {
  chartType: "nationality-indicators";
  annualRecords: NationalityAnnualRecord[];
}

export function isNationalityIndicatorsItem(
  item: DatoItem
): item is NationalityIndicatorsItem {
  return (
    item.chartType === "nationality-indicators" &&
    "annualRecords" in item &&
    Array.isArray((item as Partial<NationalityIndicatorsItem>).annualRecords)
  );
}

--- FILE: frontend/lib/url.ts ---
/**
 * URL Sanitizer to prevent XSS and malicious javascript: or data: URIs.
 * Only http and https protocols are permitted.
 */
export function sanitizeHttpUrl(rawUrl: string | null | undefined): string | null {
  if (!rawUrl || typeof rawUrl !== "string") {
    return null;
  }

  const trimmed = rawUrl.trim();
  if (!trimmed) {
    return null;
  }

  try {
    const parsed = new URL(trimmed);
    if (parsed.protocol === "http:" || parsed.protocol === "https:") {
      return parsed.toString();
    }
    return null;
  } catch {
    return null;
  }
}
